# Complete Penumbra configuration

Every authored text file of the bundle: the installer, the palette, build tools and tests, helper
scripts, configuration, login screen and wallpaper SVGs. Generated by `tools/build-configuration.py`;
the files themselves are the source, so edit those and run it again. Images, compiled cursors,
the aurora shader's compiled form and the animated wallpapers' generated `layers.js` are only in
the bundle.

## `install.sh`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Penumbra installer. Run it as your regular desktop user: ./install.sh
# Run ./install.sh --help for the options.
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)
STAMP=$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)
SETTINGS_FILE=${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}/penumbra/settings.conf
XKB_RULES=/usr/share/X11/xkb/rules/base.lst

# Configuration folders of the desktop Penumbra replaced (Waybar and Mako); the packages are in the
# distribution's LEGACY_PACKAGES.
LEGACY_CONFIGS=(waybar mako)

usage() {
    cat <<EOF
Usage: ./install.sh [part] [options]

Parts (default: all of them):
  --user-only          Only copy your configuration; no sudo, no packages.
  --system-only        Only the system part: packages, login screen, services.
                       Needs root; normally started by the full install.
  --boot-only          Only the optional GRUB, Limine and Plymouth themes.
  --uninstall          Undo every Penumbra install: put back the files each run
                       replaced and remove the ones it added. Packages stay.
                       With --user-only, only your account's files.

Distribution (detected from /etc/os-release; supported: $(supported_distros)):
  --distro NAME        Use distro/NAME/ instead of the detected one.

Settings (detected on the first run, then remembered in $SETTINGS_FILE):
  --layout LAYOUT      Keyboard layout, e.g. us, gb, fr, de, or "us,fr" for several.
  --variant VARIANT    Layout variant, e.g. intl, dvorak, colemak. Empty for none.
  --kb-options OPTS    XKB options, e.g. caps:escape,grp:alt_shift_toggle.
  --scale SCALE        Display scale: auto, 1, 1.25, 1.5, 2...
  --numlock on|off     Turn Num Lock on when you log in.
  --shell fish|keep    Make fish your login shell, or keep the current one.

Other options:
  -y, --yes            Don't ask about the settings; use the saved or detected ones.
  --skip-packages      Don't install packages (or PENUMBRA_SKIP_PACKAGES=1).
  --remove-legacy yes|no|ask
                       Whether to remove the packages Penumbra replaced (Waybar,
                       Mako, swaybg) if they are installed (or
                       PENUMBRA_REMOVE_LEGACY=1/0). Default: ask.
  --user NAME          Desktop user for --system-only (default: \$SUDO_USER).
  -h, --help           Show this help.
EOF
}

# ─── Output and questions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

step() { printf '\n\033[1;35m==>\033[0m \033[1m%s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
warn() { printf '\033[1;33mwarning:\033[0m %s\n' "$*" >&2; }
die() { printf '\033[1;31merror:\033[0m %s\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

interactive() { [[ -t 0 ]]; }

# ask "Question?" → true on yes. Defaults to no, and to no without a terminal.
ask() {
    if ! interactive; then
        printf '%s [y/N] skipped (noninteractive)\n' "$1"
        return 1
    fi
    local answer
    read -r -p "$1 [y/N] " answer
    [[ ${answer,,} == y || ${answer,,} == yes ]]
}

# ─── Distribution ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Everything that differs between distributions (package names, the package manager, the logo,
# update checks) lives in distro/NAME/. distro.sh is sourced here; its config/, bin/, share/ and
# system/ folders are installed over the common ones. See EXTENDING.md.

supported_distros() {
    local dir names=()
    for dir in "$ROOT"/distro/*/distro.sh; do names+=("$(basename "$(dirname "$dir")")"); done
    local IFS=,
    echo "${names[*]}"
}

# Pick DISTRO: --distro, else the first of /etc/os-release's ID and ID_LIKE (e.g. "endeavouros"
# then "arch") that has a folder.
detect_distro() {
    local id
    if [[ -z $DISTRO ]]; then
        for id in $( . /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null; echo "${ID:-} ${ID_LIKE:-}"); do
            [[ -r $ROOT/distro/$id/distro.sh ]] && { DISTRO=$id; break; }
        done
        [[ -n $DISTRO ]] || die "Penumbra has no support for this distribution yet (supported: $(supported_distros)). See EXTENDING.md."
    fi
    [[ $DISTRO =~ ^[a-z0-9_-]+$ && -r $ROOT/distro/$DISTRO/distro.sh ]] \
        || die "No distro/$DISTRO/distro.sh (supported: $(supported_distros))."
}

load_distro() {
    detect_distro
    DISTRO_DIR=$ROOT/distro/$DISTRO
    LEGACY_PACKAGES=() PLYMOUTH_PACKAGES=(plymouth)
    # shellcheck source=distro/arch/distro.sh
    source "$DISTRO_DIR/distro.sh"
    local name
    for name in pkg_install pkg_installed pkg_versions pkg_remove; do
        declare -F "$name" >/dev/null || die "distro/$DISTRO/distro.sh does not define $name."
    done
    for name in DISTRO_NAME DISTRO_GLYPH DISTRO_UPDATE_COMMAND PACKAGES; do
        [[ -v $name ]] || die "distro/$DISTRO/distro.sh does not set $name."
    done
}

# ─── Settings ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Each setting comes from, in order: a command-line option, the saved settings
# file, then what this machine already uses. Templates get each one as @@KEY@@ (kb_layout →
# @@KB_LAYOUT@@). A new setting needs a line in SETTING_KEYS, SETTING_OPTIONS and SETTING_LABELS,
# a default in detect_settings and a check in valid_setting.

declare -A settings=()
SETTING_KEYS=(kb_layout kb_variant kb_options scale numlock shell)
declare -A SETTING_OPTIONS=(
    [kb_layout]=--layout
    [kb_variant]=--variant
    [kb_options]=--kb-options
    [scale]=--scale
    [numlock]=--numlock
    [shell]=--shell
)
declare -A SETTING_LABELS=(
    [kb_layout]='Keyboard layout'
    [kb_variant]='Layout variant'
    [kb_options]='Keyboard options'
    [scale]='Display scale'
    [numlock]='Num Lock at login (on/off)'
    [shell]='Login shell (fish/keep)'
)

# option_setting --layout → kb_layout; false for any other option.
option_setting() {
    local key
    for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do
        [[ $1 == "${SETTING_OPTIONS[$key]}" ]] && { echo "$key"; return 0; }
    done
    return 1
}

# Set a setting unless something with higher priority already did.
set_default() { [[ -v settings[$1] ]] || settings[$1]=$2; }

load_saved_settings() {
    [[ -r $SETTINGS_FILE ]] || return 0
    local key value
    while IFS='=' read -r key value; do
        if [[ $key =~ ^[a-z_]+$ && -v SETTING_LABELS[$key] ]]; then set_default "$key" "$value"; fi
    done < "$SETTINGS_FILE"
}

save_settings() {
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SETTINGS_FILE")"
    {
        echo '# Penumbra settings, written by install.sh.'
        echo '# Edit them, then run ./install.sh --user-only to apply.'
        local key
        for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do printf '%s=%s\n' "$key" "${settings[$key]}"; done
    } > "$SETTINGS_FILE"
}

# localectl_field "X11 Layout" → that line's value from `localectl status`.
localectl_field() {
    command -v localectl >/dev/null || return 0
    localectl status 2>/dev/null | sed -n "s/^ *$1: //p"
}

known_layout() {
    [[ -r $XKB_RULES ]] || return 0  # Can't check; trust the user.
    awk -v want="$1" '/^!/ { section = $2 } section == "layout" && $1 == want { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "$XKB_RULES"
}

# Default to the keyboard this machine already uses.
detect_settings() {
    local layout variant='' options='' keymap
    layout=$(localectl_field 'X11 Layout')
    if [[ -n $layout ]]; then
        variant=$(localectl_field 'X11 Variant')
        options=$(localectl_field 'X11 Options')
    else
        # No X11 layout configured: derive one from the console keymap, e.g. "de-latin1" → "de".
        keymap=$(localectl_field 'VC Keymap')
        keymap=${keymap%%-*}
        case $keymap in uk) keymap=gb ;; sv) keymap=se ;; esac
        [[ $keymap =~ ^[a-z]+$ ]] && known_layout "$keymap" && layout=$keymap
    fi
    set_default kb_layout "${layout:-us}"
    set_default kb_variant "$variant"
    set_default kb_options "$options"
    set_default scale auto
    set_default numlock on
    set_default shell fish
}

# valid_setting KEY VALUE → true if VALUE is acceptable, otherwise explains why.
valid_setting() {
    local key=$1 value=$2 layout
    case $key in
        kb_layout)
            [[ $value =~ ^[a-z0-9_]+(,[a-z0-9_]+)*$ ]] || { warn "Layout should look like 'us' or 'us,fr'."; return 1; }
            for layout in ${value//,/ }; do
                known_layout "$layout" || { warn "Unknown keyboard layout '$layout' (see $XKB_RULES)."; return 1; }
            done ;;
        kb_variant) [[ $value =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_,-]*$ ]] || { warn "Invalid layout variant '$value'."; return 1; } ;;
        kb_options) [[ $value =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_,:+-]*$ ]] || { warn "Invalid keyboard options '$value'."; return 1; } ;;
        scale) [[ $value =~ ^(auto|[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?)$ ]] || { warn "Scale should be 'auto' or a number such as 1.25."; return 1; } ;;
        numlock) [[ $value == on || $value == off ]] || { warn "Num Lock should be 'on' or 'off'."; return 1; } ;;
        shell) [[ $value == fish || $value == keep ]] || { warn "Login shell should be 'fish' or 'keep'."; return 1; } ;;
    esac
}

show_settings() {
    local key
    for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do
        printf '  %-28s %s\n' "${SETTING_LABELS[$key]}" "${settings[$key]:-(none)}"
    done
}

# Show the settings and let the user change them before anything is installed.
confirm_settings() {
    local key value
    step 'Settings'
    show_settings
    interactive && [[ ${ASSUME_YES:-0} != 1 ]] || return 0
    ask 'Change any of these?' || return 0
    echo "Press Enter to keep a value, or type '-' to clear it."
    for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do
        while true; do
            read -r -p "  ${SETTING_LABELS[$key]} [${settings[$key]}]: " value
            case $value in
                '') value=${settings[$key]} ;;
                -) value='' ;;
            esac
            valid_setting "$key" "$value" && break
        done
        settings[$key]=$value
    done
}

# Virtual machines get software cursors and a software-rendered login screen; their virtual GPUs
# often draw hardware cursors and accelerated QML wrongly.
is_vm() { systemd-detect-virt --vm --quiet 2>/dev/null; }

# ─── Backups and copying ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Every run keeps the files it replaces under $BACKUP, and lists the files it
# creates in $BACKUP/new-files.txt.

# backup_file PATH NAME: keep PATH as $BACKUP/NAME, or record it as new.
backup_file() {
    local path=$1 name=$2
    if [[ -e $path || -L $path ]]; then
        mkdir -p "$BACKUP/$(dirname "$name")"
        cp -a "$path" "$BACKUP/$name"
    else
        printf '%s\n' "$path" >> "$BACKUP/new-files.txt"
    fi
}

# The values templates can use besides the settings, as PLACEHOLDER=value.
declare -A placeholders=()
set_placeholders() {
    placeholders=(
        [HOME]=$HOME
        [DISTRO_GLYPH]=$DISTRO_GLYPH
        [DISTRO_UPDATE_COMMAND]=$DISTRO_UPDATE_COMMAND
        # Hyprland's no_hardware_cursors: 1 forces software cursors, 2 decides per GPU.
        [SOFTWARE_CURSOR]=$(is_vm && echo 1 || echo 2)
    )
    local key
    for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do placeholders[${key^^}]=${settings[$key]:-}; done
}

# Print a file with its @@PLACEHOLDERS@@ replaced by this user's values.
render_template() {
    local text name
    text=$(<"$1")
    for name in "${!placeholders[@]}"; do text=${text//@@$name@@/"${placeholders[$name]}"}; done
    [[ $text =~ @@[A-Z_]+@@ ]] && warn "${1#"$ROOT/"}: unknown placeholder ${BASH_REMATCH[0]}"
    printf '%s\n' "$text"
}

# install_tree FROM TO: copy every file under FROM into TO, filling in templates.
install_tree() {
    local from=$1 to=$2 source dest
    while IFS= read -r -d '' source; do
        dest=$to/${source#"$from/"}
        backup_file "$dest" "${dest#"$HOME/"}"
        mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dest")"
        if grep -Iq '@@[A-Z_]*@@' "$source"; then
            render_template "$source" > "$dest"
        else
            cp "$source" "$dest"
        fi
    done < <(find "$from" -type f -print0)
}

# prune_tree FROM TO: remove files under TO that FROM no longer has, keeping them in the backup.
# Only for folders Penumbra owns outright: Quickshell loads every .qml it finds, so a renamed or
# deleted component would otherwise linger.
prune_tree() {
    local from=$1 to=$2 dest
    [[ -d $to ]] || return 0
    while IFS= read -r -d '' dest; do
        [[ -e $from/${dest#"$to/"} ]] && continue
        backup_file "$dest" "${dest#"$HOME/"}"
        rm -f "$dest"
        echo "Removed ${dest/#"$HOME"/\~}; Penumbra no longer uses it."
    done < <(find "$to" \( -type f -o -type l \) -print0)
}

# Starter files for your own settings, created once and never replaced: Hyprland, Kitty and
# Neovim read them after Penumbra's configuration.
create_user_files() {
    local path
    declare -A starters=(
        [$HOME/.config/hypr/user.lua]='-- Your own Hyprland settings, read after Penumbra'"'"'s hyprland.lua. ./install.sh never replaces this file.
-- hl.config({ general = { gaps_out = 8 } })
-- hl.bind("SUPER + T", hl.dsp.exec_cmd("thunar"))'
        [$HOME/.config/kitty/user.conf]='# Your own Kitty settings, read after Penumbra'"'"'s kitty.conf. ./install.sh never replaces this file.
# font_size 12.0'
        [$HOME/.config/nvim/lua/user.lua]='-- Your own Neovim settings, run after Penumbra'"'"'s init.lua. ./install.sh never replaces this file.
-- vim.opt.relativenumber = false'
    )
    for path in "${!starters[@]}"; do
        [[ -e $path ]] && continue
        backup_file "$path" "${path#"$HOME/"}"
        mkdir -p "$(dirname "$path")"
        printf '%s\n' "${starters[$path]}" > "$path"
    done
}
# ─── System part (root) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

install_packages() {
    if [[ $SKIP_PACKAGES == 1 ]]; then
        echo 'Skipping packages.'
        return
    fi
    pkg_install "${PACKAGES[@]}"
}

remove_legacy_packages() {
    local installed
    (( ${#LEGACY_PACKAGES[@]} )) || return 0
    mapfile -t installed < <(pkg_installed "${LEGACY_PACKAGES[@]}")
    (( ${#installed[@]} )) || return 0
    case $REMOVE_LEGACY in
        yes) ;;
        ask) ask "Remove packages Penumbra no longer uses (${installed[*]})?" || return 0 ;;
        *) return 0 ;;
    esac
    pkg_versions "${installed[@]}" > "$BACKUP/removed-packages.txt"
    pkg_remove "${installed[@]}" \
        || warn "Could not remove ${installed[*]}; another package may still depend on them."
}

# The common system/ files, then the distribution's, which can replace them.
install_system_files() {
    local source rel from
    for from in "$ROOT/system" "$DISTRO_DIR/system"; do
        [[ -d $from ]] || continue
        while IFS= read -r -d '' source; do
            rel=${source#"$from/"}
            backup_file "/$rel" "$rel"
            install -Dm644 "$source" "/$rel"
        done < <(find "$from" -type f -print0)
    done
    configure_vm_greeter
}

# In a virtual machine the login screen renders in software at scale 1; elsewhere it uses the GPU
# and the screen's own scale.
configure_vm_greeter() {
    local conf=/etc/sddm.conf.d/91-penumbra-vm.conf
    if is_vm; then
        backup_file "$conf" "${conf#/}"
        printf '%s\n' '# Written by the Penumbra installer because this is a virtual machine.' '[General]' \
            'GreeterEnvironment=QT_SCREEN_SCALE_FACTORS=1,QT_QUICK_CONTROLS_STYLE=Basic,QT_QUICK_BACKEND=software' > "$conf"
    elif [[ -e $conf ]]; then
        backup_file "$conf" "${conf#/}"
        rm -f "$conf"
    fi
}

set_login_shell() {
    local user=$1 fish
    [[ ${settings[shell]:-fish} == fish ]] || return 0
    fish=$(command -v fish) || { warn 'fish is not installed; the login shell stays as it is.'; return 0; }
    getent passwd "$user" | cut -d: -f7 > "$BACKUP/previous-shell.txt"
    usermod -s "$fish" "$user"
}

# Give the login screen the same keyboard layout as the desktop.
set_login_keyboard() {
    [[ -v settings[kb_layout] ]] && command -v localectl >/dev/null || return 0
    local layout=${settings[kb_layout]} variant=${settings[kb_variant]:-} options=${settings[kb_options]:-}
    if [[ $(localectl_field 'X11 Layout') == "$layout" && $(localectl_field 'X11 Variant') == "$variant" ]]; then
        return 0
    fi
    localectl status > "$BACKUP/localectl-status.txt" 2>&1 || true
    # --no-convert leaves the text console keymap alone.
    if localectl --no-convert set-x11-keymap "$layout" "$(localectl_field 'X11 Model')" "$variant" "$options"; then
        echo "Login screen keyboard layout set to $layout${variant:+ ($variant)}."
    else
        warn 'Could not set the login screen keyboard layout.'
    fi
}

# The keyring unlocks with your password when you sign in through SDDM. Arch's SDDM PAM file
# already lists gnome-keyring (as optional, skipped while it isn't installed); other, older or
# edited files get the two lines added.
enable_keyring_unlock() {
    local pam=/etc/pam.d/sddm
    [[ -f $pam ]] || return 0
    grep -Eq '^-?auth[[:space:]].*pam_gnome_keyring\.so' "$pam" \
        && grep -Eq '^-?session[[:space:]].*pam_gnome_keyring\.so' "$pam" && return 0
    backup_file "$pam" "${pam#/}"
    grep -Eq '^-?auth[[:space:]].*pam_gnome_keyring\.so' "$pam" \
        || printf '%s\n' '-auth       optional    pam_gnome_keyring.so' >> "$pam"
    grep -Eq '^-?session[[:space:]].*pam_gnome_keyring\.so' "$pam" \
        || printf '%s\n' '-session    optional    pam_gnome_keyring.so    auto_start' >> "$pam"
    echo "Added gnome-keyring to $pam, so the keyring unlocks when you sign in."
}

enable_services() {
    local unit
    for unit in "${SERVICES[@]}" sddm; do
        systemctl is-enabled "$unit" > "$BACKUP/$unit.enabled" 2>&1 || true
    done
    systemctl enable --now "${SERVICES[@]}"
    # Not started now: that would end the current session.
    systemctl enable sddm
}

run_system_part() {
    [[ $EUID == 0 ]] || die 'The system part needs root: sudo ./install.sh --system-only'
    local user=${TARGET_USER:-${SUDO_USER:-}}
    [[ -n $user && $user != root ]] || die 'Name the desktop user with --user NAME.'
    BACKUP=/var/lib/penumbra/backups/$STAMP
    mkdir -p "$BACKUP"

    step 'Installing packages'
    install_packages
    remove_legacy_packages

    step 'Installing the login screen, cursors and system files'
    install_system_files
    set_login_keyboard
    set_login_shell "$user"
    enable_keyring_unlock
    enable_services

    step 'Boot themes (optional)'
    run_boot_part || warn 'Optional boot theming did not complete; see the messages above.'

    echo "System backup: $BACKUP"
}

# Boot assets are deliberately outside system/: deploy only after opt-in. The package comes from
# the distribution; tools/install-boot.py does the rest.
run_boot_part() {
    if ! command -v plymouth-set-default-theme >/dev/null \
        && ask "Install Plymouth (${PLYMOUTH_PACKAGES[*]}) for the boot splash?"; then
        pkg_install "${PLYMOUTH_PACKAGES[@]}" || warn 'Plymouth was not installed.'
    fi
    python3 "$ROOT/tools/install-boot.py"
}

# ─── User part ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

install_wallpapers() {
    local dir=$HOME/.local/share/penumbra wallpaper
    mkdir -p "$dir"
    for wallpaper in "$ROOT"/assets/wallpaper*.png; do
        backup_file "$dir/${wallpaper##*/}" ".local/share/penumbra/${wallpaper##*/}"
        cp "$wallpaper" "$dir/"
    done
    # The lock screen follows the chosen wallpaper; penumbra-wallpaper repoints this link.
    [[ -e $dir/lock.png ]] || ln -sfn "$dir/wallpaper-clean.png" "$dir/lock.png"
}

# Stop the previous bar, notification daemon and wallpaper setter, and move their configs into the backup.
retire_legacy_desktop() {
    local name
    for name in "${LEGACY_PACKAGES[@]}"; do pkill -u "$UID" -x "$name" 2>/dev/null || true; done
    for name in "${LEGACY_CONFIGS[@]}"; do
        [[ -e $HOME/.config/$name ]] || continue
        mkdir -p "$BACKUP/.config"
        mv "$HOME/.config/$name" "$BACKUP/.config/$name"
        echo "Moved ~/.config/$name to the backup; it is no longer used."
    done
}

apply_gtk_settings() {
    command -v gsettings >/dev/null || return 0
    gsettings list-recursively org.gnome.desktop.interface > "$BACKUP/gsettings-interface.txt"
    gsettings list-recursively org.gnome.desktop.wm.preferences > "$BACKUP/gsettings-wm.txt"
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface color-scheme prefer-dark
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface gtk-theme Breeze-Dark
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface icon-theme Penumbra
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface cursor-theme Penumbra-Cursors
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface cursor-size 28
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.interface font-name 'Inter 10'
    # Only a close button on title bars: Hyprland tiles, so minimize and maximize do nothing.
    gsettings set org.gnome.desktop.wm.preferences button-layout ':close'
}

# gnome-keyring keeps the passwords apps save (Secret Service). KWallet comes with Dolphin and can
# claim the same D-Bus name, so it is told not to; Dolphin's own KWallet use is unchanged.
configure_keyring() {
    local rc=$HOME/.config/kwalletrc
    if command -v kwriteconfig6 >/dev/null; then
        backup_file "$rc" .config/kwalletrc
        kwriteconfig6 --file "$rc" --group org.freedesktop.secrets --key apiEnabled false
    fi
    systemctl --user enable gnome-keyring-daemon.socket 2>/dev/null || true
}

# The Penumbra shell is the notification daemon; mask Dunst so it can't compete.
disable_dunst() {
    systemctl --user cat dunst.service >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
    systemctl --user is-enabled dunst.service > "$BACKUP/dunst.enabled" 2>&1 || true
    backup_file "$HOME/.config/systemd/user/dunst.service" .config/systemd/user/dunst.service
    systemctl --user mask --now dunst.service
    systemctl --user daemon-reload
}

run_user_part() {
    BACKUP=$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/backups/$STAMP
    mkdir -p "$BACKUP"

    step 'Installing your configuration'
    prune_tree "$ROOT/config/quickshell/penumbra" "$HOME/.config/quickshell/penumbra"
    prune_tree "$ROOT/config/nvim/lua/penumbra" "$HOME/.config/nvim/lua/penumbra"
    local script from
    for script in "$HOME"/.local/bin/penumbra-*; do
        [[ -e $script && ! -e $ROOT/bin/${script##*/} && ! -e $DISTRO_DIR/bin/${script##*/} ]] || continue
        backup_file "$script" "${script#"$HOME/"}"
        rm -f "$script"
        echo "Removed ~/.local/bin/${script##*/}; Penumbra no longer uses it."
    done
    set_placeholders
    # The common files, then the distribution's, which can add to or replace them.
    for from in "$ROOT" "$DISTRO_DIR"; do
        [[ ! -d $from/config ]] || install_tree "$from/config" "$HOME/.config"
        [[ ! -d $from/bin ]] || install_tree "$from/bin" "$HOME/.local/bin"
        [[ ! -d $from/share ]] || install_tree "$from/share" "$HOME/.local/share"
    done
    create_user_files
    chmod +x "$HOME"/.local/bin/penumbra-*
    # The configs above come rounded; put back sharp corners if that was the choice.
    "$HOME/.local/bin/penumbra-corners" restore
    install_wallpapers
    retire_legacy_desktop

    step 'Applying themes and starting services'
    apply_gtk_settings
    configure_keyring
    disable_dunst
    systemctl --user daemon-reload
    systemctl --user enable --now pipewire.socket pipewire-pulse.socket wireplumber.service
    if [[ -n ${HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE:-} ]]; then
        hyprctl reload
        hyprctl dispatch "hl.dsp.exec_cmd('~/.local/bin/penumbra-session restart')"
    fi

    printf '\nPenumbra installed. User backup: %s\n' "$BACKUP"
    echo 'Log out when convenient to apply every environment setting.'
}

# ─── Uninstall ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Every installer run left a backup; undo them newest first: remove the files a run added, then put
# back the ones it replaced. What is there now is kept in an uninstall-STAMP backup first, in case
# you changed something since.

# backup_runs DIR → the run backups in DIR (named YYYYmmdd-HHMMSS), newest first.
backup_runs() {
    find "$1" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -regextype posix-extended -regex '.*/[0-9]{8}-[0-9]{6}' \
        -printf '%f\n' 2>/dev/null | sort -r
}

# keep_once PATH NAME: back PATH up unless an earlier step of this uninstall already did; the first
# copy is what you had before uninstalling.
keep_once() { [[ -e $BACKUP/$2 || -L $BACKUP/$2 ]] || backup_file "$1" "$2"; }

# undo_runs DIR TARGET TOPS...: undo every run in DIR. Saved files live under TOPS (e.g. ".config",
# or "etc"), relative to TARGET ($HOME or /); new-files.txt paths must be under TARGET.
undo_runs() {
    local dir=$1 target=$2 run path rel top
    shift 2
    local runs
    mapfile -t runs < <(backup_runs "$dir")
    for run in "${runs[@]}"; do
        if [[ -r $dir/$run/new-files.txt ]]; then
            while IFS= read -r path; do
                [[ $path == "${target%/}"/* ]] && [[ -e $path || -L $path ]] || continue
                rel=${path#"${target%/}/"}
                [[ " $* " == *" ${rel%%/*} "* ]] || continue  # Only under TOPS.
                keep_once "$path" "$rel"
                rm -f "$path"
            done < "$dir/$run/new-files.txt"
        fi
        for top in "$@"; do
            [[ -d $dir/$run/$top ]] || continue
            while IFS= read -r -d '' path; do
                rel=${path#"$dir/$run/"}
                keep_once "${target%/}/$rel" "$rel"
                mkdir -p "$(dirname "${target%/}/$rel")"
                rm -f "${target%/}/$rel"
                cp -a "$path" "${target%/}/$rel"
            done < <(find "$dir/$run/$top" \( -type f -o -type l \) -print0)
        done
        echo "Undid the install of $run."
    done
}

# oldest_backup DIR FILE → FILE from the oldest run that has it: the state before Penumbra.
oldest_backup() {
    local run
    while IFS= read -r run; do
        [[ -e $1/$run/$2 ]] && { printf '%s\n' "$1/$run/$2"; return 0; }
    done < <(backup_runs "$1" | sort)
    return 1
}

uninstall_user() {
    local dir=$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/backups file key value schema
    [[ -n $(backup_runs "$dir") ]] || die "No Penumbra backups in $dir; nothing to undo."
    BACKUP=$dir/uninstall-$STAMP
    mkdir -p "$BACKUP"
    step 'Removing Penumbra from your account'
    systemctl --user stop penumbra-shell.service 2>/dev/null || true
    undo_runs "$dir" "$HOME" .config .local
    # The lock screen's link to the current wallpaper, and folders left empty.
    [[ -L $HOME/.local/share/penumbra/lock.png ]] && rm -f "$HOME/.local/share/penumbra/lock.png"
    find "$HOME/.config/quickshell/penumbra" "$HOME/.local/share/penumbra" "$HOME/.config/nvim/lua/penumbra" \
        -depth -type d -empty -delete 2>/dev/null || true
    systemctl --user daemon-reload
    # GSettings Penumbra changed, as they were before the first install.
    if command -v gsettings >/dev/null; then
        for file in gsettings-interface.txt gsettings-wm.txt; do
            file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" "$file") || continue
            while read -r schema key value; do
                case $key in
                    color-scheme|gtk-theme|icon-theme|cursor-theme|cursor-size|font-name|button-layout)
                        gsettings set "$schema" "$key" "$value" || true ;;
                esac
            done < "$file"
        done
    fi
    if file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" dunst.enabled); then
        systemctl --user unmask dunst.service 2>/dev/null || true
        [[ $(<"$file") == enabled ]] && systemctl --user enable dunst.service 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    echo "Your files from before the uninstall are in $BACKUP."
}

uninstall_system() {
    [[ $EUID == 0 ]] || die 'The system part needs root: sudo ./install.sh --uninstall --system-only'
    local user=${TARGET_USER:-${SUDO_USER:-}} dir=/var/lib/penumbra/backups file shell layout variant
    [[ -n $(backup_runs "$dir") ]] || { echo "No Penumbra system backups in $dir; nothing to undo."; return 0; }
    BACKUP=$dir/uninstall-$STAMP
    mkdir -p "$BACKUP"
    step 'Removing the login screen, cursors and system files'
    undo_runs "$dir" / etc usr
    if [[ -n $user ]] && file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" previous-shell.txt); then
        shell=$(<"$file")
        if [[ -n $shell && -x $shell ]]; then usermod -s "$shell" "$user" && echo "Login shell of $user is $shell again."; fi
    fi
    if file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" localectl-status.txt); then
        layout=$(sed -n 's/^ *X11 Layout: //p' "$file")
        variant=$(sed -n 's/^ *X11 Variant: //p' "$file")
        if [[ -n $layout ]]; then
            localectl --no-convert set-x11-keymap "$layout" "$(localectl_field 'X11 Model')" "$variant" \
                "$(sed -n 's/^ *X11 Options: //p' "$file")" && echo "Login screen keyboard layout is $layout again."
        fi
    fi
    if file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" sddm.enabled) && [[ $(<"$file") != enabled ]]; then
        warn 'SDDM was not enabled before Penumbra. It stays enabled so you can still sign in; enable your'
        warn 'previous display manager, then run: sudo systemctl disable sddm'
    fi
    if file=$(oldest_backup "$dir" removed-packages.txt); then
        echo "Penumbra removed these packages; reinstall them if you want them back: $(cut -d' ' -f1 "$file" | tr '\n' ' ')"
    fi
    echo 'Packages Penumbra installed stay installed. Boot themes have their own undo:'
    echo '  sudo python3 tools/install-boot.py --restore /var/lib/penumbra/boot-backups/DIRECTORY'
    echo "System files from before the uninstall are in $BACKUP."
}

# sudo resets the environment, so hand the system part its options explicitly.
run_system_part_with_sudo() {
    local args=(--system-only --user "$USER" --distro "$DISTRO" --remove-legacy "$REMOVE_LEGACY") key
    for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do args+=("${SETTING_OPTIONS[$key]}" "${settings[$key]}"); done
    [[ $SKIP_PACKAGES == 1 ]] && args+=(--skip-packages)
    sudo "$ROOT/install.sh" "${args[@]}"
}

# ─── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Sourced (by the tests): only define the functions. `return` only works in a sourced file.
if (return 0 2>/dev/null); then return 0; fi

PART=all
UNINSTALL=0
ASSUME_YES=0
SKIP_PACKAGES=${PENUMBRA_SKIP_PACKAGES:-0}
case ${PENUMBRA_REMOVE_LEGACY:-ask} in 1) REMOVE_LEGACY=yes ;; 0) REMOVE_LEGACY=no ;; *) REMOVE_LEGACY=ask ;; esac
TARGET_USER=''
DISTRO=''
# Started and enabled by the system part, besides SDDM; a distribution's distro.sh may change them.
SERVICES=(bluetooth NetworkManager)

while (( $# )); do
    case $1 in
        --user-only) PART=user ;;
        --system-only) PART=system ;;
        --boot-only) PART=boot ;;
        --uninstall) UNINSTALL=1 ;;
        --distro) DISTRO=${2?--distro needs a name}; shift ;;
        --skip-packages) SKIP_PACKAGES=1 ;;
        --remove-legacy) REMOVE_LEGACY=${2?--remove-legacy needs yes, no or ask}; shift ;;
        --user) TARGET_USER=${2?--user needs a name}; shift ;;
        -y|--yes) ASSUME_YES=1 ;;
        -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
        -*)
            key=$(option_setting "$1") || { usage >&2; exit 2; }
            settings[$key]=${2?$1 needs a value}
            shift ;;
        *) TARGET_USER=$1 ;;  # Older form: --system-only NAME
    esac
    shift
done

[[ $REMOVE_LEGACY =~ ^(yes|no|ask)$ ]] || die '--remove-legacy should be yes, no or ask.'
for key in "${!settings[@]}"; do
    valid_setting "$key" "${settings[$key]}" || exit 2
done

if (( UNINSTALL )); then
    case $PART in
        system) uninstall_system ;;
        user|all)
            [[ $EUID != 0 ]] || die 'Run the uninstall as your regular desktop user, not root.'
            if interactive && [[ $ASSUME_YES != 1 ]]; then
                ask 'Remove Penumbra and put back the files it replaced?' || exit 0
            fi
            uninstall_user
            [[ $PART == all ]] && sudo "$ROOT/install.sh" --uninstall --system-only --user "$USER"
            echo 'Penumbra is uninstalled. Log out to leave the running desktop.'
            ;;
        boot) die 'Undo boot themes with: sudo python3 tools/install-boot.py --restore DIRECTORY' ;;
    esac
    exit 0
fi

load_distro
case $PART in
    boot)
        [[ $EUID == 0 ]] || exec sudo "$ROOT/install.sh" --boot-only --distro "$DISTRO"
        run_boot_part
        ;;
    system)
        run_system_part
        ;;
    user|all)
        [[ $EUID != 0 ]] || die 'Run this installer as your regular desktop user, not root.'
        load_saved_settings
        detect_settings
        for key in "${SETTING_KEYS[@]}"; do
            valid_setting "$key" "${settings[$key]}" || die "Fix '$key' with its option (see --help) or in $SETTINGS_FILE."
        done
        confirm_settings
        save_settings
        [[ $PART == all ]] && run_system_part_with_sudo
        run_user_part
        ;;
esac
```

## `distro/arch/distro.sh`

```bash
# Penumbra on Arch Linux and the distributions built on it (EndeavourOS, CachyOS, Manjaro...).
# install.sh sources this file; EXTENDING.md lists what a distribution's file must define.

DISTRO_NAME='Arch Linux'
# The bar's launcher button: a JetBrainsMono Nerd Font glyph (nf-md-arch).
DISTRO_GLYPH='󰣇'
# What fish's `update` abbreviation runs.
DISTRO_UPDATE_COMMAND='sudo pacman -Syu'

PACKAGES=(
    # Desktop
    hyprland quickshell awww sddm hyprlock hypridle hyprsunset rofi
    xdg-desktop-portal-hyprland xdg-desktop-portal-gtk
    # Terminal and editor
    kitty fish starship fastfetch htop neovim git ripgrep unzip nodejs npm tree-sitter-cli gcc
    # Applications
    dolphin pavucontrol blueman network-manager-applet xdg-utils
    # Themes and fonts
    breeze breeze-cursors breeze-icons breeze-gtk papirus-icon-theme
    qt5ct qt6ct qt5-wayland qt5-quickcontrols2 ttf-jetbrains-mono-nerd inter-font
    # Hardware and services
    networkmanager bluez bluez-utils pipewire-pulse wireplumber brightnessctl playerctl cava
    # Passwords apps save, unlocked when you sign in
    gnome-keyring seahorse
    # Screenshots, screen recording, clipboard, color picker, emoji and symbols
    grim slurp satty wf-recorder hyprpicker cliphist wl-clipboard wtype unicode-emoji noto-fonts-emoji
    # Used by the helper scripts and build tools
    pacman-contrib flatpak libnotify librsvg python-pillow xorg-xcursorgen
)
# Replaced by the Quickshell shell (bar, notifications, OSD) and awww (wallpaper).
LEGACY_PACKAGES=(waybar mako swaybg)
# The boot splash, offered by ./install.sh --boot-only.
PLYMOUTH_PACKAGES=(plymouth)

# pkg_install PACKAGE...: install what is missing. Uses the currently synced repositories; never
# refresh the database alone. pacman asks about conflicts and replacements itself, unless nobody is
# there to answer.
pkg_install() {
    local confirm=()
    interactive || confirm=(--noconfirm)
    pacman -S --needed "${confirm[@]}" "$@"
}

# pkg_installed PACKAGE... → the ones that are installed, one per line.
pkg_installed() { pacman -Qq "$@" 2>/dev/null || true; }

# pkg_versions PACKAGE... → "name version" per line, kept in the backup before removing them.
pkg_versions() { pacman -Q "$@"; }

# pkg_remove PACKAGE...: remove them without asking (the installer already asked). -s also drops
# their now-unneeded dependencies, never explicitly installed packages.
pkg_remove() { pacman -Rns --noconfirm "$@"; }
```

## `distro/arch/share/penumbra/updates.py`

```python
"""Arch's package sources for penumbra-updates: the official repositories, and the AUR through
Paru or Yay when one is installed. Flatpak is handled by penumbra-updates itself."""
import shutil


def aur_helper():
    return shutil.which("paru") or shutil.which("yay")


def sources():
    """The checks to run. Each lists one pending update per line, "name old -> new"; `empty` are
    exit codes that mean nothing to update, `empty_if_silent` the same when nothing is printed."""
    # checkupdates uses a separate database, so it never refreshes the live pacman one.
    found = [{"name": "Arch", "command": ["checkupdates", "--nocolor"], "empty": (2,)}]
    aur = aur_helper()
    if aur:
        # Yay returns 1 with no output when no AUR upgrades are available.
        found.append({"name": "AUR", "command": [aur, "-Qua", "--color", "never"], "empty_if_silent": (1,)})
    return found


def notes():
    """Extra lines for the bar's tooltip."""
    return [] if aur_helper() else ["AUR: install yay or paru to enable"]


def upgrades():
    """(name, command) for each upgrade step, run in order in a terminal."""
    aur = aur_helper()
    return [("Arch + AUR", [aur, "-Syu"]) if aur else ("Arch", ["sudo", "pacman", "-Syu"])]
```

## `palette/palette.toml`

```toml
# PENUMBRA palette: the one place the desktop's colours are defined.
# After changing a colour, run `python tools/build-palette.py` to rewrite every generated
# colour file (the templates are in palette/templates/), then `./install.sh --user-only`.

[colors]
# Surfaces, darkest first.
shade = "#07070b"         # screen dimming behind overlays, the greeter's backdrop
base = "#0d0a12"          # desktop, terminal and editor background
surface = "#100d18"       # windows, panels and glass
viewAlt = "#140f1c"       # alternate list rows
surfaceAlt = "#16111f"    # header bars, tooltips
raised = "#1c1528"        # fields and buttons on a surface
button = "#1f182b"        # Qt/KDE buttons
buttonAlt = "#271e34"
glassRaised = "#281e3a"   # the shell's raised glass, used translucent
border = "#33263f"        # borders on solid surfaces
hover = "#38264f"         # hovered and selected rows
light = "#42344f"         # bevel light, the greeter's card edge
mid = "#59446a"           # bevel mid-tone

# Selection.
textSelection = "#563b7e" # selected text in the terminal and editor
selectionAlt = "#7655ae"
selection = "#916dcf"     # selected items, with selectionText on top
selectionText = "#ffffff"

# The accent.
violetInk = "#100b1b"     # text on a violet fill
violetDeep = "#6d28d9"
violetSoft = "#9c87c6"    # visited links
violet = "#b69af7"
violetBright = "#c4a5ff"

# Text, brightest first.
white = "#faf5ff"
text = "#e9e1f5"
textSoft = "#c8bbde"
subtle = "#aa9cbd"        # quiet labels on the bar
muted = "#8f809f"
dim = "#71637f"

# Signal and ANSI colours.
black = "#231d2e"
rose = "#ef8fa4"
green = "#a5ccb3"
amber = "#e5c89a"
blue = "#a1a5e9"
pink = "#d5a4dc"
cyan = "#9ccbd2"
silver = "#ded5e8"
roseBright = "#f7a3b6"
greenBright = "#b9e0c6"
amberBright = "#f4dab0"
blueBright = "#b8bdf5"
cyanBright = "#b4e0e5"

# Editor diff backgrounds.
diffAdd = "#1c3029"
diffDelete = "#38202d"
diffChange = "#27243f"
diffText = "#403457"
```

## `tools/build-boot-assets.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build original boot artwork from Penumbra's wallpaper and palette.
Needs Pillow, rsvg-convert (librsvg) and the Inter font, as the wallpapers do."""
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
import math
import subprocess

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
grub, ply, limine = root / 'boot/grub', root / 'boot/plymouth', root / 'boot/limine'
limine.mkdir(exist_ok=True)


def render(svg, path):
    subprocess.run(['rsvg-convert', '-o', str(path)], input=svg.encode(), check=True)


def svg(width, height, body, defs=''):
    return (f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{width}" height="{height}" '
            f'viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}"><defs>{defs}</defs>{body}</svg>')


# The wallpaper's sky, rim light and planet shading, reused by every scene below.
RIM = ('<stop stop-color="#2b1547"/><stop offset=".37" stop-color="#7c3cc7"/>'
       '<stop offset=".65" stop-color="#dabaff"/><stop offset=".8" stop-color="#8651c0"/>'
       '<stop offset="1" stop-color="#1d102a"/>')
PLANET = ('<radialGradient id="planet" cx="74%" cy="26%" r="75%"><stop stop-color="#41245c"/>'
          '<stop offset=".3" stop-color="#1b102b"/><stop offset=".75" stop-color="#08080e"/>'
          '<stop offset="1" stop-color="#08070c"/></radialGradient>')
EDGE = '#07070b'


def sky(cx, cy):
    # Fades to EDGE before every border, so a centred image meets the backdrop seamlessly.
    return (f'<radialGradient id="sky" cx="{cx}" cy="{cy}" r=".5" fx="{cx}" fy="{cy}">'
            '<stop stop-color="#1d0f2d"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#0f0a17"/>'
            f'<stop offset=".92" stop-color="{EDGE}"/></radialGradient>')


def stars(points):
    return ''.join(f'<circle cx="{x}" cy="{y}" r="{r}" fill="#c8a2ef" opacity="{o}"/>' for x, y, r, o in points)


def planet(cx, cy, r, rx, ry, tilt, glow=1.0, ring_width=None, behind='', before=''):
    """Planet with a tilted ring; `behind` is drawn between the far half of the ring and the
    planet, `before` over everything."""
    rw = ring_width or max(2, r / 40)
    back = f'M {-rx} 0 A {rx} {ry} 0 0 1 {rx} 0'
    front = f'M {rx} 0 A {rx} {ry} 0 0 1 {-rx} 0'
    def ring(path):
        return (f'<g transform="translate({cx} {cy}) rotate({tilt})">'
                f'<path d="{path}" fill="none" stroke="#a66ce9" stroke-width="{rw * 3}" filter="url(#ringglow)" opacity="{.5 * glow}"/>'
                f'<path d="{path}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="{rw}" stroke-linecap="round"/>'
                f'<path d="{path}" fill="none" stroke="url(#rim)" stroke-width="{rw / 4}" opacity=".4" transform="scale({1 + 11 / 615} {1 + 11 / 188})"/>'
                '</g>')
    return (ring(back) + behind
            + f'<circle cx="{cx}" cy="{cy}" r="{r}" fill="url(#planet)" stroke="#362047" stroke-width="1"/>'
            # Atmosphere: a thin violet limb, brightest towards the light.
            + f'<circle cx="{cx}" cy="{cy}" r="{r - 0.5}" fill="none" stroke="url(#limb)" stroke-width="{max(1, r / 50)}"/>'
            + ring(front) + before)


def planet_defs(rx, ry, glow_blur):
    return (f'<linearGradient id="rim" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x1="{-rx}" y1="{ry}" x2="{rx}" y2="{-ry}">{RIM}</linearGradient>'
            '<linearGradient id="limb" x1="0" y1="1" x2="1" y2="0"><stop stop-color="#7c3cc7" stop-opacity="0"/>'
            '<stop offset=".7" stop-color="#9b6ae0" stop-opacity=".18"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#dabaff" stop-opacity=".4"/></linearGradient>'
            f'{PLANET}<filter id="ringglow" x="-20%" y="-60%" width="140%" height="220%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="{glow_blur}"/></filter>'
            f'<filter id="soft" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="{glow_blur * 6}"/></filter>')


# ── GRUB ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Image.open(root / 'assets/wallpaper.png').convert('RGB').resize((1600, 1000)).save(grub / 'background.png')
# GRUB selected-entry nine-slice frame, entirely original artwork.
for name, size in [('c', (8, 8)), ('n', (8, 3)), ('s', (8, 3)), ('e', (3, 8)), ('w', (3, 8)),
                   ('nw', (3, 3)), ('ne', (3, 3)), ('sw', (3, 3)), ('se', (3, 3))]:
    Image.new('RGBA', size, '#352348' if name == 'c' else '#78579b').save(grub / f'select_{name}.png')

# ── Limine ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Shown with `wallpaper_style: centered` on an EDGE backdrop: never stretched, so the planet
# stays round at any aspect ratio. The menu is drawn in the middle; the planet sits to its
# right and stays inside the 1280x800 centre crop of smaller screens.
W, H = 1920, 1080
cx, cy, r = 1300, 560, 150
rx, ry = r * 615 / 370, r * 188 / 370
render(svg(W, H,
           f'<rect width="{W}" height="{H}" fill="url(#sky)"/>'
           f'<ellipse cx="{cx + 12}" cy="{cy + 18}" rx="{r * 1.5}" ry="{r * 1.3}" fill="#6e2cb9" opacity=".11" filter="url(#soft)"/>'
           f'<g transform="translate({cx} {cy}) rotate(-28)" fill="none" stroke="#7e4ab4" stroke-width="1">'
           f'<ellipse rx="{rx * 1.17}" ry="{ry * 1.3}" opacity=".14"/><ellipse rx="{rx * 1.27}" ry="{ry * 1.54}" opacity=".07"/></g>'
           + planet(cx, cy, r, rx, ry, -28, ring_width=2.4)
           + stars([(1640, 300, 2, .55), (560, 820, 1.5, .4), (1500, 860, 1.5, .3), (420, 250, 1.2, .3), (1120, 230, 1.2, .25)]),
           sky(.62, .52) + planet_defs(rx, ry, 9)),
       limine / 'background.png')

# ── Plymouth ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Throbber: the wallpaper's planet in miniature, with a moonlet riding the ring and a ring
# glow that breathes. 30 fps (Plymouth's rate), one orbit every four seconds. The soft halo
# lives in the background: in every frame it would cost the initramfs megabytes.
FW, FH, FRAMES = 320, 200, 120
pr = 60
prx, pry = pr * 2.05, pr * .62
tilt = -28
for path in list(ply.glob('throbber-*.png')) + list(ply.glob('animation-*.png')) + [ply / 'keymap-render.png']:
    path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
for frame in range(FRAMES):
    phase = frame / FRAMES
    breathe = .5 + .5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * phase)
    def moon(part):
        dots = []
        for i in range(16, -1, -1):
            t = 2 * math.pi * phase - i * .045
            if (math.sin(t) > 0) != (part == 'front'):
                continue
            x, y = prx * math.cos(t), pry * math.sin(t)
            depth = 1 if part == 'front' else .55
            if i == 0:
                dots.append(f'<circle cx="{x:.2f}" cy="{y:.2f}" r="6" fill="#dac5ff" opacity="{.45 * depth:.2f}" filter="url(#moonglow)"/>'
                            f'<circle cx="{x:.2f}" cy="{y:.2f}" r="2.8" fill="#f6efff" opacity="{depth:.2f}"/>')
            else:
                dots.append(f'<circle cx="{x:.2f}" cy="{y:.2f}" r="{2.2 - i * .08:.2f}" fill="#cdb4fb" opacity="{.42 * (1 - i / 17) * depth:.2f}"/>')
        return f'<g transform="translate({FW / 2} {FH / 2}) rotate({tilt})">{"".join(dots)}</g>'
    body = planet(FW / 2, FH / 2, pr, prx, pry, tilt, glow=.8 + .4 * breathe, ring_width=2,
                  behind=moon('back'), before=moon('front'))
    path = ply / f'throbber-{frame + 1:04}.png'
    render(svg(FW, FH, body, planet_defs(prx, pry, 3)
               + '<filter id="moonglow" x="-100%" y="-100%" width="300%" height="300%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2.5"/></filter>'),
           path)
    # Every frame goes into the initramfs; a 256-colour palette keeps them a third the size,
    # indistinguishable on this artwork. Plymouth expands palette PNGs when it loads them.
    if Image.core.HAVE_LIBIMAGEQUANT:
        Image.open(path).quantize(256, method=Image.Quantize.LIBIMAGEQUANT).save(path, optimize=True)

# Background: the wallpaper's sky, centred on the planet. Plymouth stretches it to the screen,
# so it holds nothing that would show the distortion.
render(svg(1280, 800, '<rect width="1280" height="800" fill="url(#sky)"/>'
           '<ellipse cx="644" cy="358" rx="150" ry="125" fill="#6e2cb9" opacity=".2" filter="url(#wide)"/>',
           sky(.5, .44).replace('r=".5"', 'r=".62"')
           + '<filter id="wide" x="-100%" y="-100%" width="300%" height="300%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="45"/></filter>'),
       ply / 'background.png')


def wordmark(width, title, subtitle, size, spacing):
    # Letter spacing trails the last glyph too; shift by half of it to centre the word.
    mid = width / 2
    return (f'<path d="M{mid - 22} 2h44" stroke="#ad83e2" stroke-width="2"/>'
            f'<text x="{mid + spacing / 2}" y="{14 + size * 1.6}" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter" font-size="{size}" '
            f'letter-spacing="{spacing}" fill="#b8a4cf">{title}</text>'
            f'<text x="{mid + 1.25}" y="{14 + size * 1.6 + 22}" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter" font-size="{size * .6:.1f}" '
            f'letter-spacing="2.5" fill="#635570">{subtitle}</text>')


render(svg(360, 72, wordmark(360, 'PENUMBRA', 'A QUIETER ORBIT.', 15, 7)), ply / 'watermark.png')

# Password dialog: a frosted card like the shell's, holding the lock and the field.
render(svg(420, 88, '<rect x=".5" y=".5" width="419" height="87" rx="20" fill="#100d18" fill-opacity=".88" '
           'stroke="#b69af7" stroke-opacity=".22"/><path d="M190 1h40" stroke="#ad83e2" stroke-opacity=".7" stroke-width="2"/>'),
       ply / 'box.png')
render(svg(324, 44, '<rect x="1" y="1" width="322" height="42" rx="13" fill="#1a1424" stroke="#b69af7" '
           'stroke-opacity=".6" stroke-width="1.5"/>'), ply / 'entry.png')
render(svg(18, 18, '<circle cx="9" cy="9" r="4" fill="#dac5ff"/>'), ply / 'bullet.png')
# The field follows the lock directly, so its right-hand gap is part of the image.
render(svg(52, 44, '<path d="M14 19v-5a8 8 0 0 1 16 0v5" fill="none" stroke="#b69af7" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round"/>'
           '<rect x="9" y="18" width="26" height="20" rx="5" fill="#b69af7"/><circle cx="22" cy="27" r="2.6" fill="#100d18"/>'
           '<path d="M22 28.5v4" stroke="#100d18" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>'), ply / 'lock.png')
render(svg(24, 24, '<path d="M12 3l9 9h-5v5H8v-5H3z" fill="none" stroke="#b69af7" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'
           '<path d="M8 20.5h8" stroke="#b69af7" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>'), ply / 'capslock.png')
# keymap-render.png must match the layout table compiled into Plymouth; the installer copies
# the packaged one next to this icon.
render(svg(32, 22, '<rect x="1" y="1" width="30" height="20" rx="4" fill="none" stroke="#b69af7" stroke-width="1.5"/>'
           + ''.join(f'<rect x="{x}" y="{y}" width="3" height="3" rx=".8" fill="#b69af7"/>' for x in range(6, 26, 5) for y in (6, 11))
           + '<rect x="9" y="15.5" width="14" height="2.5" rx="1" fill="#b69af7"/>'), ply / 'keyboard.png')
```

## `tools/build-configuration.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write CONFIGURATION.md: every authored text file of the bundle in one Markdown document.

Run it after changing any file; `--check` only reports whether CONFIGURATION.md is out of date
(exit status 1), which tests/test_configuration.py uses.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUTPUT = ROOT / 'CONFIGURATION.md'

# Sections in reading order; each is a list of glob patterns relative to the bundle.
SECTIONS = [
    ['install.sh'],
    ['distro/**/*'],
    ['palette/palette.toml'],
    ['tools/*'],
    ['tests/*'],
    ['bin/*'],
    ['config/**/*'],
    ['share/color-schemes/*', 'share/icons/*/index.theme'],
    ['system/**/*'],
    ['boot/grub/theme.txt', 'boot/plymouth/*.plymouth'],
    ['assets/wallpaper*.svg'],
]
# Generated data and binary assets, which stay files of their own.
SKIP = [
    re.compile(r'.*/layers\.js$'),
    re.compile(r'^system/usr/share/icons/Penumbra-Cursors/cursors/'),
]
LANGUAGES = {
    '.sh': 'bash', '.py': 'python', '.lua': 'lua', '.qml': 'qml', '.js': 'javascript', '.toml': 'toml',
    '.css': 'css', '.ini': 'ini', '.conf': 'ini', '.jsonc': 'jsonc', '.json': 'json', '.fish': 'fish',
    '.rasi': 'css', '.svg': 'xml', '.frag': 'glsl', '.service': 'ini', '.desktop': 'ini', '.theme': 'ini',
    '.colors': 'ini', '.plymouth': 'ini', '.txt': 'text',
}

INTRO = '''# Complete Penumbra configuration

Every authored text file of the bundle: the installer, the palette, build tools and tests, helper
scripts, configuration, login screen and wallpaper SVGs. Generated by `tools/build-configuration.py`;
the files themselves are the source, so edit those and run it again. Images, compiled cursors,
the aurora shader's compiled form and the animated wallpapers' generated `layers.js` are only in
the bundle.
'''


def text_of(path):
    """The file's text, or None for binary files."""
    data = path.read_bytes()
    if b'\0' in data:
        return None
    try:
        return data.decode('utf-8')
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return None


def language(path, text):
    if path.suffix in LANGUAGES:
        return LANGUAGES[path.suffix]
    first = text.split('\n', 1)[0]
    if first.startswith('#!'):
        return 'python' if 'python' in first else 'bash'
    return 'text' if path.suffix == '' else ''


def files():
    seen = set()
    for patterns in SECTIONS:
        found = set()
        for pattern in patterns:
            found.update(p for p in ROOT.glob(pattern) if p.is_file())
        for path in sorted(found):
            rel = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
            if rel in seen or any(skip.match(rel) for skip in SKIP):
                continue
            seen.add(rel)
            yield rel, path


def build():
    parts = [INTRO]
    for rel, path in files():
        text = text_of(path)
        if text is None:
            continue
        longest = max((len(run) for run in re.findall(r'`+', text)), default=0)
        fence = '`' * max(3, longest + 1)
        parts.append(f'\n## `{rel}`\n\n{fence}{language(path, text)}\n{text.rstrip(chr(10))}\n{fence}\n')
    return ''.join(parts)


def main():
    text = build()
    if '--check' in sys.argv[1:]:
        current = OUTPUT.read_text() if OUTPUT.exists() else ''
        if current != text:
            print('out of date: CONFIGURATION.md (run tools/build-configuration.py)')
            return 1
        return 0
    OUTPUT.write_text(text)
    print(f'wrote {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)} ({len(text) // 1024} KiB)')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build original SVG cursor artwork into a complete multi-size Xcursor theme.
Requires Python 3, librsvg (rsvg-convert), and xorg-xcursorgen.
Run from any directory. No downloaded cursor artwork is used.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import math, shutil, subprocess, tempfile
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ASSETS=ROOT/'assets/cursors'
THEME=ROOT/'system/usr/share/icons/Penumbra-Cursors'
for stale in ASSETS.glob('*.svg'):stale.unlink()
shutil.rmtree(THEME/'cursors',ignore_errors=True)
ASSETS.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
(THEME/'cursors').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
# Palette: midnight outline, moonlit lavender body, Penumbra violet accents.
D='#100b1b'; F='url(#body)'; V='#b69af7'; A='#8a63e8'; C='#9a82c9'; R='#ef8fa4'; O=1.6
DEFS=('<defs><linearGradient id="body" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x1="0" y1="2" x2="0" y2="30">'
 '<stop offset="0" stop-color="#faf6ff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#d4c2ff"/></linearGradient>'
 '<filter id="shadow" x="-30%" y="-30%" width="160%" height="160%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation=".9"/></filter></defs>')
def svg(*layers):
 # Every part gets a midnight rim; each layer's rims sit under its fills so overlapping parts merge into one silhouette.
 rims=''.join(r for layer in layers for r,_ in layer)
 body=''.join(''.join(r for r,_ in layer)+''.join(f for _,f in layer) for layer in layers)
 return (f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="32" height="32" viewBox="0 0 32 32">{DEFS}'
  f'<g filter="url(#shadow)" opacity=".42" transform="translate(.4 1.1)">{rims}</g>'
  f'<g stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round">{body}</g></svg>')
def shape(d,fill=F):return (f'<path d="{d}" fill="{D}" stroke="{D}" stroke-width="{2*O}"/>',f'<path d="{d}" fill="{fill}"/>')
def cap(x1,y1,x2,y2,w,fill=F):
 line=f'<path d="M{x1:.2f} {y1:.2f} L{x2:.2f} {y2:.2f}" fill="none" stroke="{{}}" stroke-width="{{}}"/>'
 return (line.format(D,w+2*O),line.format(fill,w))
def disc(cx,cy,r,fill=F):return shape(f'M{cx-r} {cy} a{r} {r} 0 1 0 {2*r} 0 a{r} {r} 0 1 0 {-2*r} 0 Z',fill)
def ring(cx,cy,r,w,fill=F):
 circle=f'<circle cx="{cx}" cy="{cy}" r="{r}" fill="none" stroke="{{}}" stroke-width="{{}}"/>'
 return (circle.format(D,w+2*O),circle.format(fill,w))
def mark(d,color=D,w=1.7):return ('',f'<path d="{d}" fill="none" stroke="{color}" stroke-width="{w}"/>')
def rounded(points,r):
 """Closed polygon with every corner softened by a quadratic of radius r."""
 d='';n=len(points)
 for i,(x,y) in enumerate(points):
  ends=[]
  for px,py in (points[i-1],points[(i+1)%n]):
   k=r/math.hypot(px-x,py-y);ends.append((x+(px-x)*k,y+(py-y)*k))
  d+=('M' if not i else 'L')+f'{ends[0][0]:.2f} {ends[0][1]:.2f} Q{x} {y} {ends[1][0]:.2f} {ends[1][1]:.2f} '
 return d+'Z'
def turn(points,deg,cx=16,cy=16):
 a=math.radians(deg);c,s=math.cos(a),math.sin(a)
 return [(round(cx+(x-cx)*c-(y-cy)*s,3),round(cy+(x-cx)*s+(y-cy)*c,3)) for x,y in points]
# Soft sail-shaped arrow: straight left edge, gently scooped underside.
POINTER='M5 4.3 Q5 2.6 6.3 3.7 L19.2 14.9 Q20.6 16.3 18.6 16.6 Q12 17 8.1 22.6 Q5 26 5 22 Z'
pointer=[shape(POINTER)]
def hand(fingers,thumb,palm_top):
 parts=[cap(*f) for f in fingers]+[cap(*thumb),shape(f'M9.8 {palm_top} H27 V21.8 Q27 29.6 19.8 29.6 H15.2 Q12.2 29.6 10.2 26.6 Z')]
 # Creases run from the valley between two fingers down into the palm.
 for a,b in zip(fingers,fingers[1:]):
  top=max(a[1],b[1])+1.2
  parts.append(mark(f'M{(a[0]+b[0])/2:.2f} {top:.2f} V{max(top+1.6,palm_top+2.4):.2f}',C,.9))
 return parts
point=hand([(12.6,4.6,12.6,18,5.2),(17.4,12.6,17.4,18,4.8),(21.6,14,21.6,18,4.4),(25.1,15.8,25.1,18,3.8)],(6,19.4,10.6,24.6,5),16.4)
grab=hand([(12,7.4,12,17,4.6),(16.6,5,16.6,17,4.6),(21,6.4,21,17,4.4),(25,9.4,25,17.6,3.8)],(6.2,15.6,10.4,21.4,4.6),15)
fist=hand([(12,12.8,12,17,4.6),(16.6,11.8,16.6,17,4.6),(21,12.4,21,17,4.4),(25,13.8,25,17.6,3.8)],(8.6,19.2,13.4,21.4,4.4),15)
UP=[(16,3.6),(22.2,11.6),(9.8,11.6)]
def arrows(*angles,points=UP):return [shape(rounded(turn(points,a),1.9)) for a in angles]+[disc(16,16,1.7,V)]
SMALL=[(16,2.6),(21,8.6),(11,8.6)]
text=[cap(16,5.5,16,26.5,2.8)]
target=[ring(16,16,7.4,1.8)]+[cap(*(c for xy in turn([(16,2.6),(16,7.6)],a) for c in xy),1.8) for a in (0,90,180,270)]+[disc(16,16,2,V)]
def badge(symbol,fill=V):return [disc(23.2,23.2,6.2,fill),mark(symbol)]
marks={'copy':'M20 23.2 H26.4 M23.2 20 V26.4','alias':'M20.6 25.8 L25.4 21 M21.8 20.8 H25.6 V24.6',
 'help':'M21.1 21.8 Q21.4 19.4 23.4 19.4 Q25.6 19.5 25.5 21.4 Q25.3 22.8 23.4 23.6 V24.2 M23.4 26.6 V26.7','context-menu':'M20.4 21 H26 M20.4 23.2 H26 M20.4 25.4 H26'}
def lens(symbol):return [cap(19.6,18.6,27.2,26.2,4.2),disc(14,12.6,8.4),ring(14,12.6,8.4,0.01,A),mark(symbol,D,2)]
shapes={'left_ptr':([pointer],5,3),'hand2':([point],12.6,2.4),'xterm':([text],16,16),
 'ns-resize':([arrows(0,180)],16,16),'ew-resize':([arrows(90,270)],16,16),
 'nwse-resize':([arrows(-45,135)],16,16),'nesw-resize':([arrows(45,225)],16,16),
 'fleur':([arrows(0,90,180,270,points=SMALL)],16,16),'crosshair':([target],16,16),
 'openhand':([grab],16,16),'closedhand':([fist],16,16),'forbidden':([pointer,badge('M20.9 20.9 L25.5 25.5 M26.4 23.2 a3.2 3.2 0 1 0 -6.4 0 a3.2 3.2 0 1 0 6.4 0',R)],5,3),
 'vertical-text':([[cap(5.5,16,26.5,16,2.8)]],16,16),'cell':([[cap(16,5,16,27,5),cap(5,16,27,16,5)]],16,16),
 'zoom-in':([lens('M14 8.6 V16.6 M10 12.6 H18')],14,12.6),'zoom-out':([lens('M10 12.6 H18')],14,12.6)}
for kind,symbol in marks.items():shapes[kind]=([pointer,badge(symbol)],5,3)
def spinner(t,cx=16,cy=16,r=8.6,w=4.4):
 """Violet arc chasing round a lavender track; it stretches and shrinks once per turn and loops seamlessly."""
 length=24+232*math.sin(math.pi*t)**2;mid=720*t
 head,tail=[math.radians(mid+s*length/2) for s in (1,-1)]
 x1,y1,x2,y2=cx+r*math.sin(tail),cy-r*math.cos(tail),cx+r*math.sin(head),cy-r*math.cos(head)
 arc=f'<path d="M{x1:.3f} {y1:.3f} A{r} {r} 0 {int(length>180)} 1 {x2:.3f} {y2:.3f}" fill="none" stroke="{A}" stroke-width="{w-.6}"/>'
 track=ring(cx,cy,r,w)
 return [(track[0],track[1]+arc)]
FRAMES=24
frames={name:([svg(*layers)],x,y) for name,(layers,x,y) in shapes.items()}
frames['watch']=([svg(spinner(i/FRAMES)) for i in range(FRAMES)],16,16)
frames['left_ptr_watch']=([svg(pointer,spinner(i/FRAMES,23.4,23,5,3.2)) for i in range(FRAMES)],5,3)
aliases={
 'left_ptr':'default arrow top_left_arrow center_ptr draft right_ptr',
 'hand2':'pointer pointing_hand hand hand1 link 9d800788f1b08800ae810202380a0822 e29285e634086352946a0e7090d73106',
 'xterm':'text ibeam', 'ns-resize':'n-resize s-resize top_side bottom_side v_double_arrow sb_v_double_arrow row-resize size_ver split_v double_arrow 00008160000006810000408080010102',
 'ew-resize':'e-resize w-resize left_side right_side h_double_arrow sb_h_double_arrow col-resize size_hor split_h 028006030e0e7ebffc7f7070c0600140',
 'nwse-resize':'nw-resize se-resize top_left_corner bottom_right_corner size_fdiag bd_double_arrow c7088f0f3e6c8088236ef8e1e3e70000',
 'nesw-resize':'ne-resize sw-resize top_right_corner bottom_left_corner size_bdiag fd_double_arrow fcf1c3c7cd4491d801f1e1c78f100000',
 'fleur':'move all-scroll size_all dnd-move 4498f0e0c1937ffe01fd06f973665830 9081237383d90e509aa00f00170e968f',
 'crosshair':'cross tcross diamond_cross plus color-picker', 'openhand':'grab 5c6cd98b3f3ebcb1f9c7f1c204630408',
 'closedhand':'grabbing dnd-none 208530c400c041818281048008011002',
 'forbidden':'not-allowed no-drop dnd-no-drop crossed_circle circle X_cursor 03b6e0fcb3499374a867c041f52298f0',
 'copy':'dnd-copy 1081e37283d90000800003c07f3ef6bf 6407b0e94181790501fd1e167b474872',
 'alias':'dnd-link 3085a0e285430894940527032f8b26df 640fb0e74195791501fd1ed57b41487f',
 'help':'question_arrow left_ptr_help whats_this d9ce0ab605698f320427677b458ad60b',
 'watch':'wait', 'left_ptr_watch':'progress half-busy 08e8e1c95fe2fc01f976f1e063a24ccd',
}
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='penumbra-cursors-') as tmp:
 tmp=Path(tmp)
 for name,(images,hx,hy) in frames.items():
  lines=[]
  for i,source in enumerate(images):
   src=ASSETS/f'{name}-{i:02}.svg';src.write_text(source)
   for size in [24,28,32,48,64]:
    png=tmp/f'{name}-{i}-{size}.png'
    subprocess.run(['rsvg-convert','-w',str(size),'-h',str(size),str(src),'-o',str(png)],check=True)
    lines.append(f'{size} {round(hx*size/32)} {round(hy*size/32)} {png} {40 if len(images)>1 else 0}')
  cfg=tmp/(name+'.conf');cfg.write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
  subprocess.run(['xcursorgen',str(cfg),str(THEME/'cursors'/name)],check=True)
 for canonical,names in aliases.items():
  for alias in names.split():shutil.copyfile(THEME/'cursors'/canonical,THEME/'cursors'/alias)
(THEME/'index.theme').write_text('[Icon Theme]\nName=Penumbra Cursors\nComment=Soft lavender cursors with midnight outlines and a violet ring spinner\nInherits=breeze_cursors\n')
# SVG contact sheet, keeping the artwork editable and inspectable.
parts=['<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="960" height="570" viewBox="0 0 960 570"><rect width="960" height="570" rx="24" fill="#100d18"/><text x="40" y="50" fill="#e4dced" font-family="Inter" font-size="24">PENUMBRA / CURSORS</text><text x="40" y="79" fill="#8f809f" font-family="Inter" font-size="13">Soft lavender silhouettes · midnight outlines · violet ring spinner</text>']
for n,(name,(images,_,_)) in enumerate(frames.items()):
 x=40+(n%6)*150;y=110+(n//6)*110
 inner=images[len(images)//3].split('>',1)[1].rsplit('</svg>',1)[0]
 parts.append(f'<rect x="{x}" y="{y}" width="132" height="82" rx="12" fill="#1a1324"/><g transform="translate({x+42} {y+7}) scale(1.5)">{inner}</g><text x="{x+66}" y="{y+99}" fill="#a99abb" font-family="Inter" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle">{name}</text>')
parts.append('</svg>')
p=ROOT/'previews/cursors.svg';p.write_text(''.join(parts))
subprocess.run(['rsvg-convert',str(p),'-o',str(p.with_suffix('.png'))],check=True)
print(f'Built {len(frames)} original shapes / {len(list((THEME/"cursors").iterdir()))} cursor names, five sizes each.')
```
