## `tools/build-palette.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write every PENUMBRA colour file from palette/palette.toml.

Each file under palette/templates/ is rendered to the same path in the repository, with
{{name}} replaced by that colour as #rrggbb, {{name:hex}} as rrggbb and {{name:rgb}} as r,g,b.
`--check` only reports generated files that are out of date (exit status 1).
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import tomllib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
PALETTE = ROOT / 'palette' / 'palette.toml'
TEMPLATES = ROOT / 'palette' / 'templates'
TOKEN = re.compile(r'\{\{\s*([A-Za-z]+)(?::(hex|rgb))?\s*\}\}')


def load_palette():
    colors = tomllib.loads(PALETTE.read_text())['colors']
    for name, value in colors.items():
        if not re.fullmatch(r'#[0-9a-f]{6}', value):
            sys.exit(f'{PALETTE}: {name} should be a lowercase #rrggbb colour, not {value!r}')
    return colors


def render(template, colors):
    def color(match):
        name, form = match.groups()
        if name not in colors:
            raise KeyError(f'{template.relative_to(ROOT)}: unknown colour {name!r}')
        value = colors[name]
        if form == 'hex':
            return value[1:]
        if form == 'rgb':
            return ','.join(str(int(value[i:i + 2], 16)) for i in (1, 3, 5))
        return value
    return TOKEN.sub(color, template.read_text())


def main():
    check = '--check' in sys.argv[1:]
    colors = load_palette()
    stale = []
    for template in sorted(p for p in TEMPLATES.rglob('*') if p.is_file()):
        target = ROOT / template.relative_to(TEMPLATES)
        try:
            text = render(template, colors)
        except KeyError as error:
            sys.exit(error.args[0])
        if target.exists() and target.read_text() == text:
            continue
        stale.append(target.relative_to(ROOT))
        if not check:
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_text(text)
    for path in stale:
        print(f'{"out of date" if check else "wrote"}: {path}')
    return 1 if check and stale else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

## `tools/build-wallpapers.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Render each wallpaper and a wordmark-free variant for the lock screen and greeter,
then split the dawn and eclipse scenes into the layers the animated Quickshell wallpaper moves."""
from pathlib import Path
import copy
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

from PIL import Image

root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
assets = root / 'assets'
for name in ('wallpaper', 'wallpaper-dawn', 'wallpaper-eclipse'):
    source = (assets / f'{name}.svg').read_text()
    # The wordmark blurs into a smudge on hyprlock and crowds the SDDM card.
    clean = re.sub(r'<g id="wordmark">.*?</g>\n', '', source, flags=re.S)
    assert clean.count('<text') == 0 and clean != source, f'wordmark group not found in {name}.svg'
    (assets / f'{name}-clean.svg').write_text(clean)
    for variant in (name, f'{name}-clean'):
        subprocess.run(['rsvg-convert', str(assets / f'{variant}.svg'), '-o', str(assets / f'{variant}.png')], check=True)
shutil.copyfile(assets / 'wallpaper-clean.png', root / 'system/usr/share/sddm/themes/penumbra/wallpaper.png')

# Animated scenes: every top-level `layer-*` element becomes its own cropped PNG, drawn in
# document order; whatever is left (sky gradient, wordmark) is the backdrop. Stars are drawn
# by QML so they can twinkle, and glows are rendered brighter so they can breathe both ways.
ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'
ET.register_namespace('', ns)
boost = {'glow': 1.6, 'halo': 1.6, 'nebula': 1.6}


def belongs(child, layer):
    child_id = child.get('id') or ''
    if child_id.startswith('layer-'):
        return child_id == f'layer-{layer}'
    return layer is None or child.tag == f'{{{ns}}}defs'


def render(scene, out, layer, name):
    svg = copy.deepcopy(scene)
    for child in list(svg):
        if not belongs(child, layer):
            svg.remove(child)
    for element in svg.iter():
        if name in boost and element.get('opacity'):
            element.set('opacity', f"{float(element.get('opacity')) * boost[name]:.3f}")
    png = subprocess.run(['rsvg-convert'], input=ET.tostring(svg), capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
    image = Image.open(io.BytesIO(png))
    left, top, right, bottom = image.getbbox()
    image.crop((left, top, right, bottom)).save(out / f'{name}.png', optimize=True)
    return {'name': name, 'x': left, 'y': top, 'width': right - left, 'height': bottom - top, 'boost': boost.get(name, 1)}


for name, folder in (('wallpaper-dawn', 'dawn'), ('wallpaper-eclipse', 'eclipse')):
    scene = ET.parse(assets / f'{name}.svg').getroot()
    out = root / 'config/quickshell/penumbra' / folder
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    names = [child.get('id')[6:] for child in scene if (child.get('id') or '').startswith('layer-')]
    # The lock screen animates the scene too, over a backdrop without the wordmark.
    clean = copy.deepcopy(scene)
    clean.remove(clean.find(f"{{{ns}}}g[@id='wordmark']"))
    layers = [render(scene, out, None, 'sky'), render(clean, out, None, 'sky-clean')]
    layers += [render(scene, out, layer, layer) for layer in names if layer != 'stars']
    stars = [{'x': float(star.get('cx')), 'y': float(star.get('cy')), 'r': float(star.get('r')),
              'opacity': float(star.get('opacity')), 'glint': star.get('class') == 'glint'}
             for star in scene.find(f".//*[@id='layer-stars']")]
    (out / 'layers.js').write_text(
        '.pragma library\n'
        f'// Generated by tools/build-wallpapers.py from assets/{name}.svg; do not edit.\n'
        f"var width = {scene.get('width')};\nvar height = {scene.get('height')};\n"
        f'var layers = {json.dumps(layers)};\nvar stars = {json.dumps(stars)};\n')
    # Shaders ship compiled; rebuilding them needs qsb from qt6-shadertools.
    for shader in out.glob('*.frag'):
        subprocess.run([shutil.which('qsb') or '/usr/lib/qt6/bin/qsb', '--qt6', '-o', f'{shader}.qsb', str(shader)], check=True)
```

## `tools/install-boot.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Opt-in boot themes. Never installs GRUB or Limine, or writes boot sectors/EFI entries."""
import argparse
import datetime
import fcntl
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BACKUPS = Path('/var/lib/penumbra/boot-backups')


def run(*args):
    subprocess.run(args, check=True)


def ask(question):
    # No implicit yes, including when run without a terminal or at EOF.
    if not os.isatty(0):
        print(f'{question} [y/N] — skipped (noninteractive)')
        return False
    try:
        return input(f'{question} [y/N] ').strip().lower() in ('y', 'yes')
    except EOFError:
        return False


def literal(value):
    if any(c in value for c in '$`\\\n;<>|&'):
        raise ValueError('Dynamic shell configuration requires manual setup.')
    words = shlex.split(value, comments=True)
    if len(words) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected one literal shell value.')
    return words[0]


def splash_defaults(text):
    matches = list(re.finditer(r'^GRUB_CMDLINE_LINUX_DEFAULT=(.*)$', text, re.M))
    if len(matches) > 1:
        raise ValueError('Multiple kernel command-line assignments require manual setup.')
    value = literal(matches[0][1]) if matches else ''
    words = value.split()
    if any(w in ('plymouth.enable=0', 'rd.plymouth=0', 'nosplash') for w in words):
        raise ValueError('Plymouth is explicitly disabled in the kernel command line.')
    if 'splash' not in words:
        words.append('splash')
    replacement = 'GRUB_CMDLINE_LINUX_DEFAULT=' + shlex.quote(' '.join(words))
    if matches:
        return text[:matches[0].start()] + replacement + text[matches[0].end():]
    return text + '\n' + replacement + '\n'


def plymouth_hooks(text):
    matches = list(re.finditer(r'^HOOKS=\(([^)]*)\)', text, re.M))
    if len(matches) != 1 or len(re.findall(r'^\s*HOOKS[+]?=', text, re.M)) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected one plain HOOKS array; custom configuration needs manual setup.')
    hooks = shlex.split(matches[0][1], comments=True)
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r'[\w-]+', h) for h in hooks):
        raise ValueError('Dynamic HOOKS array is unsupported.')
    if 'plymouth-encrypt' in hooks or 'sd-plymouth' in hooks:
        raise ValueError('Legacy Plymouth hooks require manual migration.')
    anchors = [h for h in ('udev', 'systemd') if h in hooks]
    if len(anchors) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected either udev or systemd in HOOKS.')
    hooks = [h for h in hooks if h != 'plymouth']
    hooks.insert(hooks.index(anchors[0]) + 1, 'plymouth')
    # Preserve keyboard, encryption, root filesystem and all other hooks.
    return text[:matches[0].start()] + 'HOOKS=(' + ' '.join(hooks) + ')' + text[matches[0].end():]


def preset_jobs(directory):
    """Accept conventional Arch presets only, without executing shell input."""
    jobs = []
    for path in sorted(directory.glob('*.preset')):
        values = {}
        for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
            line = line.strip()
            if not line or line.startswith('#'):
                continue
            match = re.fullmatch(r'(\w+)=(.*)', line)
            if not match or match[1] in values:
                raise ValueError(f'Custom preset requires manual setup: {path}')
            key, value = match.groups()
            if key == 'PRESETS':
                if not re.fullmatch(r'\([\w\s\'"-]+\)', value):
                    raise ValueError(f'Unsupported PRESETS array: {path}')
                values[key] = shlex.split(value[1:-1])
            else:
                values[key] = literal(value)
        presets = values.get('PRESETS', [])
        if not presets or not all(re.fullmatch(r'\w+', p) for p in presets):
            raise ValueError(f'No supported presets: {path}')
        allowed = {'PRESETS', 'ALL_kver', 'ALL_config'}
        for p in presets:
            allowed.update({p + '_image', p + '_kver', p + '_config', p + '_options', p + '_uki'})
        # Presets may retain assignments for disabled fallback entries.
        allowed.update(k for k in values if re.fullmatch(r'\w+_(image|uki|kver|config|options)', k))
        if set(values) - allowed:
            raise ValueError(f'UKI/custom preset keys require manual setup: {path}')
        for p in presets:
            config = values.get(p + '_config', values.get('ALL_config', '/etc/mkinitcpio.conf'))
            if p + '_image' in values and p + '_uki' in values:
                raise ValueError('Combined initramfs/UKI outputs require manual setup.')
            uki = p + '_uki' in values
            image = Path(values.get(p + ('_uki' if uki else '_image'), ''))
            kernel = Path(values.get(p + '_kver', values.get('ALL_kver', '')))
            options = shlex.split(values.get(p + '_options', ''))
            checked_options = options[:]
            if uki and '--splash' in checked_options:
                index = checked_options.index('--splash')
                if index + 1 >= len(checked_options) or not Path(checked_options[index + 1]).is_file():
                    raise ValueError('UKI splash bitmap is missing.')
                del checked_options[index:index + 2]
            if config != '/etc/mkinitcpio.conf' or checked_options not in ([], ['-S', 'autodetect']):
                raise ValueError(f'Custom config/options require manual setup: {path}')
            valid_image = (image.parent == Path('/boot/EFI/Linux') and image.suffix == '.efi') if uki else (image.parent == Path('/boot') and image.name.startswith('initramfs-') and image.suffix == '.img')
            if not valid_image:
                raise ValueError(f'Nonstandard initramfs destination: {path}')
            if kernel.parent != Path('/boot') or not kernel.name.startswith('vmlinuz-') or not kernel.is_file():
                raise ValueError(f'Unsupported kernel location: {path}')
            if image.is_symlink() or not image.is_file():
                raise ValueError(f'Existing regular initramfs required: {image}')
            jobs.append((kernel, image, options))
    if not jobs or len({j[1] for j in jobs}) != len(jobs):
        raise ValueError('Missing presets or duplicate initramfs destinations.')
    return jobs


def loader_info():
    """The Boot Loader Interface name of the UEFI loader that ran, e.g. 'Limine 12.9.4'."""
    info = Path('/sys/firmware/efi/efivars/LoaderInfo-4a67b082-0a4c-41cf-b6c7-440b29bb8c4f')
    try:
        return info.read_bytes()[4:].decode('utf-16-le').rstrip('\0')
    except (OSError, UnicodeError):
        return ''


def grub_detected():
    required = ('grub-mkconfig', 'grub-script-check', 'grub-probe', 'grub-mkrelpath', 'findmnt')
    if not all(shutil.which(c) for c in required):
        return False
    mounts = subprocess.check_output(['findmnt', '--fstab', '--noheadings', '--output', 'TARGET'], text=True).splitlines()
    for mount in mounts:
        if mount in ('/boot', '/boot/grub') and not os.path.ismount(mount):
            print(f'Required boot filesystem is not mounted: {mount}')
            return False
    if not Path('/etc/default/grub').is_file() or not Path('/boot/grub/grub.cfg').is_file():
        return False
    if Path('/boot/grub/grub.cfg').is_symlink() or Path('/etc/default/grub').is_symlink():
        return False
    if Path('/sys/firmware/efi').exists():
        if not shutil.which('efibootmgr'):
            print('Cannot verify the active EFI boot entry without efibootmgr.')
            return False
        result = subprocess.run(['efibootmgr', '-v'], text=True, capture_output=True)
        current = re.search(r'^BootCurrent: ([0-9A-Fa-f]{4})$', result.stdout, re.M)
        if result.returncode or not current:
            return False
        entry = re.search(r'^Boot' + current[1] + r'\*?\s+(.+)$', result.stdout, re.M)
        if not entry or not re.search(r'grub[^\\/\s]*\.efi', entry[1], re.I):
            # Firmware fallback entries often name only the physical disk.
            # LoaderInfo identifies the loader that ran, independently of its filename.
            loader = loader_info()
            if not loader.startswith('GRUB '):
                print('Active EFI entry is not clearly GRUB and firmware LoaderInfo does not identify GRUB.')
                return False
            print(f'Firmware reports {loader}; EFI entry uses a fallback/custom path.')
            if not ask('Confirm this boot uses /boot/grub/grub.cfg (fallback/custom EFI path)'):
                return False
    else:
        if not Path('/boot/grub/i386-pc/core.img').is_file():
            return False
        if not ask('Legacy BIOS GRUB files found; confirm this machine actually boots with GRUB'):
            return False
    run('grub-script-check', '/boot/grub/grub.cfg')
    run('grub-probe', '--target=fs', '/boot/grub')
    return True


class Transaction:
    def __init__(self, name):
        BACKUPS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
        self.directory = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-') + name + '-', dir=BACKUPS))
        self.paths = []
        self.temporary = []
        print(f'Boot backup: {self.directory}', flush=True)

    def save(self, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if str(path) in self.paths:
            return
        destination = self.directory / 'files' / str(path).lstrip('/')
        destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if path.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError(f'Refusing to replace symlink: {path}')
        if path.is_dir():
            shutil.copytree(path, destination, symlinks=True)
        elif path.exists():
            shutil.copy2(path, destination)
        self.paths.append(str(path))
        (self.directory / 'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(self.paths, indent=2))

    def stage(self, destination):
        fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.penumbra-', dir=Path(destination).parent)
        os.close(fd)
        self.temporary.append(Path(name))
        return Path(name)

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, typ, value, trace):
        if typ is not None:
            print('Boot theme failed; restoring previous files.', flush=True)
            restore(self.directory)
        for path in self.temporary:
            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        return False


def restore(directory):
    for name in reversed(json.loads((directory / 'manifest.json').read_text())):
        dest = Path(name)
        source = directory / 'files' / name.lstrip('/')
        if dest.is_dir() and not dest.is_symlink():
            shutil.rmtree(dest)
        elif dest.exists() or dest.is_symlink():
            dest.unlink()
        if source.is_dir():
            shutil.copytree(source, dest, symlinks=True)
        elif source.exists():
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(source, dest)
    print(f'Restored files from {directory}. Installed packages were retained.')


def deploy(tx, source, dest):
    tx.save(dest)
    # Replace rather than merge, so files a previous version shipped do not linger.
    if dest.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(dest)
    shutil.copytree(source, dest)


def generate_grub(tx):
    destination = Path('/boot/grub/grub.cfg')
    tx.save(destination)
    staged = tx.stage(destination)
    # stdout mode avoids grub-mkconfig writing the active configuration itself.
    with staged.open('w') as output:
        subprocess.run(['grub-mkconfig'], stdout=output, check=True)
    run('grub-script-check', str(staged))
    text = staged.read_text()
    if not re.search(r'^\s*menuentry\s', text, re.M) or not re.search(r'^\s*(?:linux(?:efi)?|chainloader)\s', text, re.M):
        raise ValueError('Generated GRUB config has no Linux boot entry.')
    # Theming must not alter the generated kernel/initrd boot commands.
    pattern = r'^\s*(?:linux(?:efi)?|initrd(?:efi)?|chainloader)\s+.*$'
    old = re.findall(pattern, destination.read_text(), re.M)
    new = re.findall(pattern, text, re.M)
    normalize = lambda lines: [' '.join(w for w in line.split() if w != 'splash') for line in lines]
    if normalize(old) != normalize(new):
        raise ValueError('Generated kernel/initrd commands changed beyond splash; review manually.')
    os.chmod(staged, destination.stat().st_mode & 0o777)
    os.replace(staged, destination)


def theme_existing_grub(tx):
    """Append graphics setup while retaining the working boot menu verbatim."""
    destination = Path('/boot/grub/grub.cfg')
    original = destination.read_text()
    begin = '# BEGIN PENUMBRA GRAPHICS'
    end = '# END PENUMBRA GRAPHICS'
    # The theme was called Nocturne before; replace a block left by that name too.
    for name in ('PENUMBRA', 'NOCTURNE'):
        old_begin, old_end = f'# BEGIN {name} GRAPHICS', f'# END {name} GRAPHICS'
        if old_begin in original or old_end in original:
            # Only replace our own final block; never remove intervening user edits.
            pattern = r'\n' + old_begin + r'\n.*?\n' + old_end + r'\n\Z'
            if original.count(old_begin) != 1 or original.count(old_end) != 1 or not re.search(pattern, original, re.S):
                raise ValueError('Penumbra graphics block was edited/moved; review manually.')
            original = re.sub(pattern, '', original, flags=re.S)
    uuid = subprocess.check_output(['grub-probe', '--target=fs_uuid', '/boot/grub'], text=True).strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9-]+', uuid):
        raise ValueError('Cannot determine a safe GRUB filesystem UUID.')
    paths = []
    for name in ('/boot/grub/fonts/unicode.pf2', '/boot/grub/themes/penumbra/theme.txt'):
        value = subprocess.check_output(['grub-mkrelpath', name], text=True).strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r'/[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+', value):
            raise ValueError('Unsupported GRUB filesystem-relative theme path.')
        paths.append(value)
    font, theme = paths
    block = f"""
{begin}
# Keep root, boot entries, default selection and timeout unchanged.
search --no-floppy --fs-uuid --set=penumbra_root {uuid}
if loadfont ($penumbra_root){font}; then
    insmod all_video
    insmod gfxterm
    insmod gfxmenu
    insmod png
    if [ -z "$gfxmode" ]; then
        set gfxmode=auto
    fi
    terminal_output gfxterm
    set theme=($penumbra_root){theme}
    export theme
fi
{end}
"""
    tx.save(destination)
    staged = tx.stage(destination)
    staged.write_text(original + block)
    run('grub-script-check', str(staged))
    os.chmod(staged, destination.stat().st_mode & 0o777)
    os.replace(staged, destination)


def apply_grub():
    if not Path('/boot/grub/fonts/unicode.pf2').is_file():
        raise ValueError('GRUB Unicode font missing; graphical theme skipped.')
    if re.search(r'^\s*GRUB_TERMINAL=', Path('/etc/default/grub').read_text(), re.M):
        raise ValueError('GRUB_TERMINAL overrides graphical output; review this custom terminal setting manually.')
    with Transaction('grub') as tx:
        defaults = Path('/etc/default/grub')
        tx.save(defaults)
        deploy(tx, ROOT / 'boot/grub', Path('/boot/grub/themes/penumbra'))
        # Last shell assignments win; preserve all original lines and boot arguments.
        text = defaults.read_text()
        text = re.sub(r'\n# BEGIN (PENUMBRA|NOCTURNE) GRUB\n.*?# END \1 GRUB\n', '', text, flags=re.S)
        text += '\n# BEGIN PENUMBRA GRUB\nGRUB_THEME="/boot/grub/themes/penumbra/theme.txt"\nGRUB_TERMINAL_OUTPUT="gfxterm"\n# END PENUMBRA GRUB\n'
        defaults.write_text(text)
        theme_existing_grub(tx)
        if 'themes/penumbra/theme.txt' not in Path('/boot/grub/grub.cfg').read_text():
            raise ValueError('Generated GRUB configuration did not activate the theme.')
    print('Penumbra GRUB theme applied. Boot installation and timeout were preserved.')


LIMINE_BEGIN = '# BEGIN PENUMBRA LIMINE'
LIMINE_END = '# END PENUMBRA LIMINE'
# Prefix for the user's own copies of the options the block sets: Limine reads the first
# copy of an option, and picks a random wallpaper when there are several.
LIMINE_OFF = '#penumbra-off# '
LIMINE_KEYS = {'wallpaper', 'wallpaper_style', 'backdrop', 'interface_branding',
               'interface_branding_colour', 'interface_branding_color', 'interface_help_colour',
               'interface_help_color', 'interface_help_colour_bright', 'interface_help_color_bright',
               'term_palette', 'term_palette_bright', 'term_background', 'term_foreground',
               'term_background_bright', 'term_foreground_bright', 'term_margin', 'term_margin_gradient'}
# Where Limine looks for its configuration on each volume, in its own order.
LIMINE_CONFIGS = ('boot/limine/limine.conf', 'boot/limine.conf', 'limine/limine.conf', 'limine.conf')
LIMINE_EFI_CONFIGS = ('EFI/BOOT/limine.conf', 'EFI/limine/limine.conf')
LIMINE_BIOS_STAGE = ('limine-bios.sys', 'limine/limine-bios.sys', 'boot/limine-bios.sys', 'boot/limine/limine-bios.sys')


def boot_volumes():
    """Mounted FAT volumes where Limine's configuration and artwork can live."""
    volumes = []
    for mount in ('/boot', '/efi', '/boot/efi'):
        result = subprocess.run(['findmnt', '--noheadings', '--output', 'FSTYPE', '--mountpoint', mount],
                                text=True, capture_output=True)
        if result.returncode == 0 and result.stdout.strip() == 'vfat':
            volumes.append(Path(mount))
    return volumes


def mount_of(path):
    return Path(subprocess.check_output(['findmnt', '--noheadings', '--output', 'TARGET', '--target', str(path)], text=True).strip())


def limine_version(volume):
    """Version of the deployed Limine: firmware LoaderInfo on UEFI, the stage file on BIOS."""
    if Path('/sys/firmware/efi').exists():
        match = re.fullmatch(r'Limine (\d+)\.(\d+)\S*', loader_info())
        return (int(match[1]), int(match[2])) if match else None
    for name in LIMINE_BIOS_STAGE:
        stage = volume / name
        if stage.is_file():
            match = re.search(rb'Limine (\d+)\.(\d+)\.\d+', stage.read_bytes())
            return (int(match[1]), int(match[2])) if match else None
    return None


def limine_detected():
    """Return the limine.conf of a confirmed Limine 12+ boot, else None."""
    if not shutil.which('findmnt'):
        return None
    efi = Path('/sys/firmware/efi').exists()
    if efi and not loader_info().startswith('Limine '):
        return None
    names = (LIMINE_EFI_CONFIGS if efi else ()) + LIMINE_CONFIGS
    found = []
    for volume in boot_volumes():
        for name in names:
            path = volume / name
            # FAT names are case-insensitive, so one file can show up under two spellings.
            if path.is_file() and not any(os.path.samefile(path, other) for other in found):
                found.append(path)
    if not found:
        return None
    if len(found) > 1:
        print('Several limine.conf files found; which one Limine reads needs manual review: '
              + ', '.join(map(str, found)))
        return None
    config = found[0]
    if config.is_symlink():
        return None
    version = limine_version(mount_of(config))
    if version is None or version < (12, 0):
        # Limine 12 takes RRGGBB interface colours; older releases took a 0-7 index.
        print('Limine 12 or newer is required for its theme (deploy the updated Limine files first).')
        return None
    print(f'Limine {version[0]}.{version[1]} configuration found: {config}')
    if not ask(f'Confirm this machine boots with Limine and reads {config}'):
        return None
    return config


def limine_theme(text, wallpaper):
    """Put the managed appearance block first; switch off other copies of its options."""
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    begins = [i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.rstrip('\r\n') == LIMINE_BEGIN]
    ends = [i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.rstrip('\r\n') == LIMINE_END]
    if begins or ends:
        # Only replace our own leading block; never remove intervening user edits.
        if begins != [0] or len(ends) != 1:
            raise ValueError('Penumbra Limine block was edited/moved; review manually.')
        lines = lines[ends[0] + 1:]
        if lines and not lines[0].strip():
            lines = lines[1:]
    for index, line in enumerate(lines):
        match = re.match(r'\s*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*:', line)
        if match and match[1].lower() in LIMINE_KEYS:
            lines[index] = LIMINE_OFF + line
    block = f"""{LIMINE_BEGIN}
# Appearance only: entries, timeout and default entry are unchanged.
wallpaper: {wallpaper}
wallpaper_style: centered
backdrop: 07070b
interface_branding: P E N U M B R A
interface_branding_colour: b8a4cf
interface_help_colour: b69af7
interface_help_colour_bright: dac5ff
term_palette: 0d0a12;ef8fa4;a5ccb3;e5c89a;a1a5e9;b69af7;9ccbd2;ded5e8
term_palette_bright: 71637f;f7a3b6;b9e0c6;f4dab0;b8bdf5;ceb4ff;b4e0e5;faf5ff
term_foreground: c8bbde
term_foreground_bright: f3eaff
term_background: ff000000
term_background_bright: ff000000
term_margin: 0
term_margin_gradient: 0
{LIMINE_END}

"""
    return block + ''.join(lines)


def limine_entries(text):
    """Yield (protocol, [line indexes of cmdline options]) for each menu entry."""
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    starts = [i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if re.match(r'\s*/', line)] + [len(lines)]
    for start, stop in zip(starts, starts[1:]):
        protocol, cmdlines = None, []
        for index in range(start + 1, stop):
            match = re.match(r'\s*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*:\s*(.*?)\s*$', lines[index])
            if not match:
                continue
            key = match[1].lower()
            if key == 'protocol':
                protocol = match[2].lower()
            elif key in ('cmdline', 'kernel_cmdline'):
                cmdlines.append(index)
        yield protocol, cmdlines


def limine_splash(text, uki=False):
    """Append splash to every Linux entry's command line; other entries stay as they are."""
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    for protocol, cmdlines in limine_entries(text):
        if uki:
            # Limine hands an EFI entry's cmdline to the UKI, which then ignores its own.
            if protocol in ('efi', 'uefi', 'efi_chainload') and any('splash' not in lines[i].split(':', 1)[1].split() for i in cmdlines):
                raise ValueError('A Limine EFI entry passes its own command line without splash; add it there manually.')
            continue
        if protocol != 'linux':
            continue
        if not cmdlines:
            raise ValueError('A Limine Linux entry has no cmdline; add splash manually.')
        for index in cmdlines:
            key, value = lines[index].split(':', 1)
            ending = value[len(value.rstrip('\r\n')):]
            words = value.split()
            if '${' in value:
                raise ValueError('Limine command lines built from macros require manual setup.')
            if any(w in ('plymouth.enable=0', 'rd.plymouth=0', 'nosplash') for w in words):
                raise ValueError('Plymouth is explicitly disabled in a Limine command line.')
            if 'splash' not in words:
                lines[index] = key + ':' + value.rstrip() + ' splash' + ending
    return ''.join(lines)


def limine_entry_tool():
    # limine-entry-tool (limine-mkinitcpio-hook, limine-snapper-sync) rewrites entries from
    # /etc/default/limine, so command lines belong there rather than in limine.conf.
    return Path('/etc/default/limine').exists() or bool(shutil.which('limine-entry-tool'))


def replace_text(tx, destination, text):
    """Swap in new contents through a staged file, so a failed write leaves the original."""
    tx.save(destination)
    staged = tx.stage(destination)
    staged.write_text(text)
    os.replace(staged, destination)


def apply_limine(config):
    volume = mount_of(config)
    artwork = config.parent / 'penumbra'
    wallpaper = 'boot():/' + str((artwork / 'background.png').relative_to(volume))
    if not re.fullmatch(r'boot\(\):/[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+', wallpaper):
        raise ValueError('Unsupported Limine artwork path.')
    with Transaction('limine') as tx:
        deploy(tx, ROOT / 'boot/limine', artwork)
        replace_text(tx, config, limine_theme(config.read_text(), wallpaper))
    print('Penumbra Limine theme applied. Entries, timeout and default entry were preserved.')
    if limine_entry_tool():
        print('limine-entry-tool keeps global options; if an update drops the theme, run --boot-only again.')


def uki_section(image, section):
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
        output = Path(directory) / 'section'
        # Supply an output copy: objcopy must never rewrite the source EFI image.
        run('objcopy', '--dump-section', f'{section}={output}', str(image), str(Path(directory) / 'copy.efi'))
        return output.read_bytes()


def check_uki(image):
    if not shutil.which('objcopy'):
        raise ValueError('UKI validation requires objcopy (binutils).')
    # Signing and custom ukify settings need a separate integration path.
    for name in ('/etc/kernel/uki.conf', '/usr/lib/kernel/uki.conf'):
        path = Path(name)
        if path.exists() and any(line.strip() and not line.lstrip().startswith(('#', ';')) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()):
            raise ValueError('Custom UKI/signing configuration requires manual setup.')
    for directory in ('/etc/kernel/uki.conf.d', '/usr/lib/kernel/uki.conf.d', '/etc/cmdline.d'):
        if list(Path(directory).glob('*.conf')):
            raise ValueError('UKI/cmdline drop-ins require manual setup.')
    secure = Path('/sys/firmware/efi/efivars/SecureBoot-8be4df61-93ca-11d2-aa0d-00e098032b8c')
    if not secure.is_file() or secure.read_bytes()[4:] != b'\x00':
        raise ValueError('Automatic UKI rebuild requires verified disabled Secure Boot.')
    # The PE certificate table must be empty; do not silently remove an existing signature.
    data = image.read_bytes()
    offset = int.from_bytes(data[60:64], 'little')
    optional = offset + 24
    if data[:2] != b'MZ' or data[offset:offset + 4] != b'PE\0\0' or data[optional:optional + 2] != b'\x0b\x02':
        raise ValueError('Expected a PE32+ UKI.')
    certificate = optional + 112 + 4 * 8
    if len(data) < certificate + 8 or any(data[certificate:certificate + 8]):
        raise ValueError('Signed or malformed UKI requires manual setup.')
    embedded = uki_section(image, '.cmdline').rstrip(b'\0').decode().strip()
    source = Path('/etc/kernel/cmdline')
    if not source.is_file() or source.read_text().strip() != embedded:
        raise ValueError('/etc/kernel/cmdline must match the existing UKI before rebuilding.')
    words = embedded.split()
    if any(w in words for w in ('nosplash', 'plymouth.enable=0', 'rd.plymouth=0')):
        raise ValueError('Plymouth is explicitly disabled in the UKI kernel command line.')


def uki_splash_cmdline(text):
    """Append only splash; retain quoting and spacing of existing arguments."""
    value = text.strip()
    if '\n' in value or '\r' in value or '#' in value or '--' in value.split():
        raise ValueError('Custom UKI kernel command line requires manual setup.')
    if any(w in value.split() for w in ('nosplash', 'plymouth.enable=0', 'rd.plymouth=0')):
        raise ValueError('Plymouth is explicitly disabled in the UKI kernel command line.')
    return value if 'splash' in value.split() else value + (' ' if value else '') + 'splash'


def without_stock_splash(options):
    """Remove Arch's pre-kernel logo; retain custom bitmaps and other options."""
    result = options[:]
    for index in range(len(result) - 1):
        if result[index:index + 2] == ['--splash', '/usr/share/systemd/bootctl/splash-arch.bmp']:
            del result[index:index + 2]
            break
    return result


def stock_splash_preset_updates(directory):
    updates = {}
    for path in sorted(directory.glob('*.preset')):
        original = path.read_text()
        def replace(match):
            options = shlex.split(literal(match[2]))
            updated = without_stock_splash(options)
            if updated == options:
                return match[0]
            return match[1] + shlex.quote(shlex.join(updated))
        updated = re.sub(r'^(\s*\w+_options=)([^\n]*)', replace, original, flags=re.M)
        if updated != original:
            if path.is_symlink():
                raise ValueError('Symlinked mkinitcpio preset requires manual setup.')
            updates[path] = updated
    return updates


def apply_plymouth(limine=None):
    """Install the splash; `limine` is the confirmed limine.conf, otherwise GRUB boots."""
    if not shutil.which('mkinitcpio') or not Path('/usr/lib/initcpio/install/plymouth').is_file():
        raise ValueError('Supported mkinitcpio Plymouth hook is missing.')
    if not Path('/usr/lib/plymouth/two-step.so').is_file():
        raise ValueError('Plymouth two-step plugin missing.')
    if any(Path(d).exists() and any(Path(d).iterdir()) for d in ('/etc/initcpio/post', '/usr/lib/initcpio/post')):
        raise ValueError('Custom mkinitcpio post hooks require manual setup to avoid extra boot writes.')
    if list(Path('/etc/mkinitcpio.conf.d').glob('*.conf')):
        raise ValueError('mkinitcpio drop-ins require manual setup; no hooks changed.')
    config = Path('/etc/mkinitcpio.conf')
    # The boot loader's own copy of the kernel command line, which gains splash.
    defaults = limine or Path('/etc/default/grub')
    hooks = plymouth_hooks(config.read_text())
    jobs = preset_jobs(Path('/etc/mkinitcpio.d'))
    uki_jobs = [job for job in jobs if job[1].suffix == '.efi']
    if uki_jobs and len(uki_jobs) != len(jobs):
        raise ValueError('Mixed UKI/initramfs presets require manual setup.')
    for _, image, _ in uki_jobs:
        check_uki(image)
    source = Path('/etc/kernel/cmdline')
    if uki_jobs:
        cmdline = uki_splash_cmdline(source.read_text())
    elif not limine:
        cmdline = splash_defaults(defaults.read_text())
    if limine:
        entries = limine_splash(defaults.read_text(), uki=bool(uki_jobs))
        if entries != defaults.read_text() and limine_entry_tool():
            raise ValueError('limine-entry-tool manages these entries: add splash to KERNEL_CMDLINE '
                             'in /etc/default/limine, regenerate them, then retry.')
    preset_updates = stock_splash_preset_updates(Path('/etc/mkinitcpio.d')) if uki_jobs else {}
    if uki_jobs:
        jobs = [(kernel, image, without_stock_splash(options)) for kernel, image, options in jobs]
    # Keep originals plus new staged images; allow ample room for Plymouth/fonts.
    needed = sum(image.stat().st_size for _, image, _ in jobs) + 256 * 1024 * 1024
    if shutil.disk_usage('/boot').free < needed:
        raise ValueError('Insufficient free /boot space for safe staged initramfs builds.')
    with Transaction('plymouth') as tx:
        for path in (config, defaults, Path('/etc/plymouth/plymouthd.conf'),
                     Path('/usr/share/plymouth/themes/default.plymouth')):
            # Theme selector can remove this legacy symlink; preserve it separately.
            if path.is_symlink():
                raise ValueError(f'Legacy/symlink configuration needs manual setup: {path}')
            tx.save(path)
        for _, image, _ in jobs:
            tx.save(image)
        if uki_jobs:
            tx.save(source)
            source.write_text(cmdline + '\n')
        for path, updated in preset_updates.items():
            tx.save(path)
            path.write_text(updated)
        theme = Path('/usr/share/plymouth/themes/penumbra')
        deploy(tx, ROOT / 'boot/plymouth', theme)
        # Keyboard layout names are drawn from a sprite sheet whose offsets are compiled into
        # Plymouth, so only the packaged sheet can match; without one the layout is not shown.
        keymaps = Path('/usr/share/plymouth/themes/spinner/keymap-render.png')
        if keymaps.is_file():
            shutil.copy2(keymaps, theme / 'keymap-render.png')
        config.write_text(hooks)
        if limine:
            if entries != defaults.read_text():
                replace_text(tx, defaults, entries)
        elif not uki_jobs:
            defaults.write_text(cmdline)
        run('plymouth-set-default-theme', 'penumbra')
        staged_images = []
        for kernel, image, options in jobs:
            stage = tx.stage(image)
            uki = image.suffix == '.efi'
            uki_options = ['--cmdline', '/etc/kernel/cmdline'] if uki else []
            run('mkinitcpio', '-c', str(config), '-k', str(kernel), '-U' if uki else '-g', str(stage), *options, *uki_options)
            if uki:
                if uki_section(stage, '.cmdline').rstrip(b'\0').decode().strip() != cmdline:
                    raise ValueError('Rebuilt UKI changed the embedded kernel command line.')
                with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                    initrd = Path(directory) / 'initrd'
                    initrd.write_bytes(uki_section(stage, '.initrd'))
                    listing = subprocess.check_output(['lsinitcpio', str(initrd)], text=True)
            else:
                listing = subprocess.check_output(['lsinitcpio', str(stage)], text=True)
            if 'usr/bin/plymouth' not in listing or 'penumbra.plymouth' not in listing:
                raise ValueError('Generated initramfs does not contain Plymouth and Penumbra.')
            os.chmod(stage, image.stat().st_mode & 0o777)
            staged_images.append((stage, image))
        # UKIs carry their command line and initrd; keep their existing GRUB chainload entries.
        # Limine entries name the images directly, so they need no regeneration.
        if not uki_jobs and not limine:
            generate_grub(tx)
        for stage, image in staged_images:
            os.replace(stage, image)
    print('Penumbra Plymouth theme applied and initramfs rebuilt. Reboot when convenient.')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--restore', type=Path, help='restore a recorded boot transaction (newest first)')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if os.geteuid() != 0:
        parser.error('Run through ./install.sh --boot-only or sudo.')
    with open('/run/penumbra-boot.lock', 'w') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        if args.restore:
            directory = args.restore.resolve()
            if directory.parent != BACKUPS or not (directory / 'manifest.json').is_file():
                parser.error('Choose a backup directory under ' + str(BACKUPS))
            restore(directory)
            return
        limine = limine_detected()
        print('Limine: ' + ('verified/confirmed.' if limine else 'not verified; its theme will be skipped.'))
        # A confirmed Limine boot settles which loader ran; GRUB files may still be installed.
        available = not limine and grub_detected()
        print('GRUB: ' + ('verified/confirmed.' if available else 'not in use or not verified; its theme will be skipped.'))
        if limine and ask('Apply the Penumbra Limine theme (back up limine.conf; entries unchanged)'):
            apply_limine(limine)
        if available and ask('Apply the Penumbra GRUB theme (back up and preserve existing menu)'):
            apply_grub()
        # ./install.sh --boot-only offers to install it, with the distribution's package manager.
        installed = bool(shutil.which('plymouth-set-default-theme'))
        print('Plymouth: ' + ('installed.' if installed else 'not installed.'))
        if installed and (available or limine) and ask('Apply Penumbra to Plymouth (add splash/hook and rebuild initramfs)'):
            apply_plymouth(limine)
        elif installed and not (available or limine):
            print('Plymouth theme is in boot/plymouth; boot integration requires a verified GRUB or Limine setup.')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    def interrupted(signum, frame):
        raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'Signal {signum}')
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, interrupted)
    try:
        main()
    except (ValueError, OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError, KeyboardInterrupt) as error:
        print(f'Boot theming stopped: {error}', flush=True)
        raise SystemExit(1)
```

## `tools/install-neovim.sh`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install only the Neovim configuration; no desktop or system changes.
set -euo pipefail
ROOT=$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)
command -v nvim >/dev/null
command -v git >/dev/null
config_root=${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}
state_root=${XDG_STATE_HOME:-$HOME/.local/state}
destination="$config_root/nvim"
backup=$(mktemp -d "$state_root/penumbra/backups/neovim-XXXXXXXX" 2>/dev/null) || {
    mkdir -p "$state_root/penumbra/backups"
    backup=$(mktemp -d "$state_root/penumbra/backups/neovim-XXXXXXXX")
}
if [[ -e "$destination" || -L "$destination" ]]; then
    mv -- "$destination" "$backup/nvim"
else
    printf '%s\n' "$destination" > "$backup/new-files.txt"
fi
mkdir -p "$config_root"
cp -a "$ROOT/config/nvim" "$destination"
printf 'Neovim configuration installed. Backup record: %s\n' "$backup"
nvim --headless '+Lazy! restore' +qa
printf 'Open a project with: nvim /path/to/project\n'
```

## `tests/test_boot.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Isolated tests: no root and no host boot writes."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('boot', Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'tools/install-boot.py')
boot = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(boot)


class ConfigTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_splash_preserves_arguments_and_is_idempotent(self):
        text = '# comment\nGRUB_CMDLINE_LINUX_DEFAULT="quiet rootflags=subvol=@ cryptdevice=UUID=x:root"\nGRUB_TIMEOUT=5\n'
        updated = boot.splash_defaults(text)
        self.assertIn('quiet rootflags=subvol=@ cryptdevice=UUID=x:root splash', updated)
        self.assertEqual(updated, boot.splash_defaults(updated))
        self.assertTrue(updated.endswith('GRUB_TIMEOUT=5\n'))

    def test_dynamic_or_disabled_cmdline_rejected(self):
        for value in ('"$FLAGS"', '"quiet $(touch /tmp/bad)"', '"plymouth.enable=0"'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                boot.splash_defaults('GRUB_CMDLINE_LINUX_DEFAULT=' + value)

    def test_hook_order_and_idempotence(self):
        for init, crypt in [('udev', 'encrypt'), ('systemd', 'sd-encrypt')]:
            text = f'HOOKS=(base {init} autodetect kms keyboard keymap {crypt} filesystems fsck)\n'
            updated = boot.plymouth_hooks(text)
            self.assertIn(f'{init} plymouth autodetect', updated)
            self.assertEqual(updated, boot.plymouth_hooks(updated))
            self.assertIn(f'keyboard keymap {crypt} filesystems fsck', updated)

    def test_uki_splash_preserves_quoted_arguments(self):
        original = 'quiet  root=UUID=example param="two words"'
        updated = boot.uki_splash_cmdline(original + '\n')
        self.assertEqual(updated, original + ' splash')
        self.assertEqual(boot.uki_splash_cmdline(updated), updated)
        for value in ('quiet nosplash', 'plymouth.enable=0', 'rd.plymouth=0',
                      'quiet -- initarg', 'quiet\nroot=example', '# comment'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                boot.uki_splash_cmdline(value)

    def test_ambiguous_hooks_rejected(self):
        for text in ('HOOKS=($MYHOOKS)', 'HOOKS=(base)', 'HOOKS=(base udev)\nHOOKS+=(encrypt)',
                     'HOOKS=(base udev plymouth-encrypt)', 'HOOKS=(base udev systemd)'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                boot.plymouth_hooks(text)

    def test_remove_only_stock_uki_splash(self):
        stock = ['-S', 'autodetect', '--splash', '/usr/share/systemd/bootctl/splash-arch.bmp']
        self.assertEqual(boot.without_stock_splash(stock), ['-S', 'autodetect'])
        custom = ['--splash', '/custom/logo.bmp']
        self.assertEqual(boot.without_stock_splash(custom), custom)
        self.assertEqual(len(stock), 4)

    def test_limine_theme_leads_and_is_idempotent(self):
        text = ('timeout: 3\nwallpaper: boot():/old.png\nTERM_PALETTE: 000000\n\n'
                '/Arch Linux\n    protocol: linux\n    cmdline: quiet\n')
        themed = boot.limine_theme(text, 'boot():/limine/penumbra/background.png')
        self.assertTrue(themed.startswith(boot.LIMINE_BEGIN + '\n'))
        self.assertIn('wallpaper: boot():/limine/penumbra/background.png\n', themed)
        # The user's copies stay visible but no longer override the block.
        self.assertIn(boot.LIMINE_OFF + 'wallpaper: boot():/old.png\n', themed)
        self.assertIn(boot.LIMINE_OFF + 'TERM_PALETTE: 000000\n', themed)
        rest = text.replace('wallpaper', boot.LIMINE_OFF + 'wallpaper').replace('TERM_PALETTE', boot.LIMINE_OFF + 'TERM_PALETTE')
        self.assertEqual(themed.split(boot.LIMINE_END + '\n\n', 1)[1], rest)
        self.assertEqual(boot.limine_theme(themed, 'boot():/limine/penumbra/background.png'), themed)
        moved = themed.replace(boot.LIMINE_BEGIN + '\n', '', 1) + boot.LIMINE_BEGIN + '\n'
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            boot.limine_theme(moved, 'boot():/x.png')

    def test_limine_splash_only_touches_linux_entries(self):
        text = ('timeout: 5\n/Arch Linux\n    protocol: linux\n    cmdline: root=UUID=x rw quiet\n'
                '/+Tools\n//Memtest\n    protocol: efi\n    path: boot():/memtest.efi\n'
                '/Other kernel\n    PROTOCOL: Linux\n    kernel_cmdline: root=UUID=y splash\r\n')
        updated = boot.limine_splash(text)
        self.assertIn('    cmdline: root=UUID=x rw quiet splash\n', updated)
        self.assertIn('kernel_cmdline: root=UUID=y splash\r\n', updated)
        self.assertEqual(updated.replace(' splash\n', '\n'), text.replace(' splash\n', '\n'))
        self.assertEqual(boot.limine_splash(updated), updated)
        for bad in ('/A\n protocol: linux\n cmdline: ${ARGS}\n', '/A\n protocol: linux\n cmdline: quiet nosplash\n',
                    '/A\n protocol: linux\n path: boot():/vmlinuz\n'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                boot.limine_splash(bad)
        uki = '/Arch\n protocol: efi\n path: boot():/EFI/Linux/arch.efi\n cmdline: quiet\n'
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            boot.limine_splash(uki, uki=True)
        self.assertEqual(boot.limine_splash(uki.replace('quiet', 'quiet splash'), uki=True), uki.replace('quiet', 'quiet splash'))

    def test_noninteractive_defaults_no(self):
        with patch.object(boot.os, 'isatty', return_value=False), patch('builtins.input') as read:
            self.assertFalse(boot.ask('Apply?'))
            read.assert_not_called()


class IntegrationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        original_path = Path
        def mapped(value):
            value = str(value)
            if value.startswith(('/etc/', '/boot/', '/usr/', '/sys/')) or value in ('/boot', '/etc'):
                return self.root / value.lstrip('/')
            return original_path(value)
        self.mapping = patch.object(boot, 'Path', side_effect=mapped)
        self.mapping.start()
        self.backups = patch.object(boot, 'BACKUPS', self.root / 'backups')
        self.backups.start()
        self.write('/etc/default/grub', 'GRUB_CMDLINE_LINUX_DEFAULT="quiet"\n')
        self.write('/etc/mkinitcpio.conf', 'HOOKS=(base udev autodetect keyboard filesystems fsck)\n')
        self.write('/etc/mkinitcpio.d/linux.preset', "ALL_kver='/boot/vmlinuz-linux'\nPRESETS=('default')\ndefault_image='/boot/initramfs-linux.img'\n")
        self.write('/boot/vmlinuz-linux', 'kernel')
        self.write('/boot/initramfs-linux.img', 'old image')
        self.write('/boot/grub/grub.cfg', "menuentry 'Arch' {\n linux /vmlinuz-linux root=UUID=example quiet\n initrd /initramfs-linux.img\n}\n")
        self.write('/usr/lib/initcpio/install/plymouth', 'hook')
        self.write('/usr/lib/plymouth/two-step.so', 'plugin')
        self.write('/boot/grub/fonts/unicode.pf2', 'font')
        self.original = self.snapshot()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.mapping.stop()
        self.backups.stop()
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def write(self, name, content):
        path = self.root / name.lstrip('/')
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(content)

    def snapshot(self):
        return {str(p.relative_to(self.root)): p.read_bytes() for p in self.root.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and 'backups' not in p.parts}

    def fake_run(self, *args):
        if args[0] == 'mkinitcpio':
            Path(args[args.index('-U' if '-U' in args else '-g') + 1]).write_text('new image')
        elif args[0] == 'plymouth-set-default-theme':
            self.write('/etc/plymouth/plymouthd.conf', '[Daemon]\nTheme=penumbra\n')

    def fake_subprocess(self, args, **kwargs):
        if args == ['grub-mkconfig']:
            old = (self.root / 'boot/grub/grub.cfg').read_text()
            kwargs['stdout'].write(old.replace(' quiet', ' quiet splash'))
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(args, 0)

    def test_success_stages_images_and_keeps_backup(self):
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=self.fake_run), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'run', side_effect=self.fake_subprocess), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', return_value='usr/bin/plymouth\npenumbra.plymouth'):
            boot.apply_plymouth()
        self.assertEqual((self.root / 'boot/initramfs-linux.img').read_text(), 'new image')
        self.assertIn('splash', (self.root / 'boot/grub/grub.cfg').read_text())
        backup = next((self.root / 'backups').iterdir())
        boot.restore(backup)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)

    def test_build_failure_rolls_back_everything(self):
        def fail(*args):
            self.fake_run(*args)
            if args[0] == 'mkinitcpio':
                raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(1, args)
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=fail):
            with self.assertRaises(subprocess.CalledProcessError):
                boot.apply_plymouth()
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)

    def grub_path(self, args, **kwargs):
        if args[0] == 'grub-probe':
            return 'ABCD-1234\n'
        return args[1].removeprefix('/boot') + '\n'

    def test_grub_preserves_dynamic_uki_menu_and_is_idempotent(self):
        text = '# Custom UKI discovery\nset timeout=7\nset default=saved\ninsmod bls\nblscfg\n'
        self.write('/boot/grub/grub.cfg', text)
        before = self.snapshot()
        with patch.object(boot, 'run'), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'run') as generate, patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', side_effect=self.grub_path):
            boot.apply_grub()
            first = (self.root / 'boot/grub/grub.cfg').read_text()
            self.assertTrue(first.startswith(text))
            self.assertIn('set theme=($penumbra_root)/grub/themes/penumbra/theme.txt', first)
            boot.apply_grub()
            self.assertEqual((self.root / 'boot/grub/grub.cfg').read_text(), first)
            generate.assert_not_called()
        for backup in sorted((self.root / 'backups').iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime_ns, reverse=True):
            boot.restore(backup)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), before)

    def test_grub_success_and_restore(self):
        with patch.object(boot, 'run'), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', side_effect=self.grub_path):
            boot.apply_grub()
        self.assertIn('GRUB_THEME=', (self.root / 'etc/default/grub').read_text())
        boot.restore(next((self.root / 'backups').iterdir()))
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)

    def test_grub_syntax_failure_restores_original(self):
        def fail(*args):
            if args[0] == 'grub-script-check':
                raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(1, args)
        with patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=fail), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', side_effect=self.grub_path):
            with self.assertRaises(subprocess.CalledProcessError):
                boot.apply_grub()
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)

    def uki_fixture(self):
        self.write('/etc/kernel/cmdline', 'quiet\n')
        self.write('/etc/mkinitcpio.d/linux.preset', "ALL_kver='/boot/vmlinuz-linux'\nPRESETS=('default')\ndefault_uki='/boot/EFI/Linux/arch-linux.efi'\ndefault_options='--splash /usr/share/systemd/bootctl/splash-arch.bmp'\nfallback_uki='/boot/EFI/Linux/arch-linux-fallback.efi'\n")
        self.write('/usr/share/systemd/bootctl/splash-arch.bmp', 'bitmap')
        self.write('/boot/EFI/Linux/arch-linux.efi', 'old uki')

    def test_uki_preset_accepts_disabled_fallback_and_splash(self):
        self.uki_fixture()
        jobs = boot.preset_jobs(self.root / 'etc/mkinitcpio.d')
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), 1)
        self.assertEqual(jobs[0][1].name, 'arch-linux.efi')
        self.assertEqual(jobs[0][2], ['--splash', '/usr/share/systemd/bootctl/splash-arch.bmp'])

    def test_uki_preflight_rejects_signature_and_changed_arguments(self):
        self.uki_fixture()
        image = self.root / 'boot/EFI/Linux/arch-linux.efi'
        data = bytearray(512)
        data[:2] = b'MZ'
        data[60:64] = (128).to_bytes(4, 'little')
        data[128:132] = b'PE\0\0'
        data[152:154] = b'\x0b\x02'
        image.write_bytes(data)
        secure = self.root / 'sys/firmware/efi/efivars/SecureBoot-8be4df61-93ca-11d2-aa0d-00e098032b8c'
        secure.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        secure.write_bytes(b'\x07\0\0\0\0')
        self.write('/etc/kernel/cmdline', 'quiet splash\n')
        self.write('/usr/lib/kernel/uki.conf', '# Stock commented template\n#SecureBootPrivateKey=example\n')
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot, 'uki_section', return_value=b'quiet splash\0'):
            boot.check_uki(image)
            data[296] = 1
            image.write_bytes(data)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Signed or malformed'):
                boot.check_uki(image)
            data[296] = 0
            image.write_bytes(data)
            self.write('/etc/kernel/cmdline', 'quiet\n')
            with patch.object(boot, 'uki_section', return_value=b'quiet\0'):
                boot.check_uki(image)
            self.write('/etc/kernel/cmdline', 'quiet nosplash\n')
            with patch.object(boot, 'uki_section', return_value=b'quiet nosplash\0'):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'explicitly disabled'):
                    boot.check_uki(image)
            self.write('/etc/kernel/cmdline', 'different splash')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'must match'):
                boot.check_uki(image)

    def test_uki_success_preserves_grub_and_restores(self):
        self.uki_fixture()
        before = self.snapshot()
        def build(*args):
            if args[0] == 'mkinitcpio':
                self.assertIn('-U', args)
                self.assertNotIn('--splash', args)
                self.assertEqual((self.root / 'etc/kernel/cmdline').read_text(), 'quiet splash\n')
                Path(args[args.index('-U') + 1]).write_text('new uki')
            else:
                self.fake_run(*args)
        def section(image, name):
            return b'quiet splash' if name == '.cmdline' else b'initrd'
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot, 'check_uki'), patch.object(boot, 'uki_section', side_effect=section), patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=build), patch.object(boot, 'generate_grub') as generate, patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', return_value='usr/bin/plymouth\npenumbra.plymouth'):
            boot.apply_plymouth()
            generate.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual((self.root / 'boot/EFI/Linux/arch-linux.efi').read_text(), 'new uki')
        self.assertNotIn('--splash', (self.root / 'etc/mkinitcpio.d/linux.preset').read_text())
        self.assertEqual((self.root / 'etc/default/grub').read_bytes(), before['etc/default/grub'])
        boot.restore(next((self.root / 'backups').iterdir()))
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), before)

    def test_uki_commandline_change_rolls_back(self):
        self.uki_fixture()
        before = self.snapshot()
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot, 'check_uki'), patch.object(boot, 'uki_section', side_effect=[b'wrong', b'quiet splash']), patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=self.fake_run):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'embedded kernel command line'):
                boot.apply_plymouth()
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), before)

    def test_fallback_grub_requires_loader_evidence_and_confirmation(self):
        self.write('/sys/firmware/efi/efivars/LoaderInfo-4a67b082-0a4c-41cf-b6c7-440b29bb8c4f', '')
        info = self.root / 'sys/firmware/efi/efivars/LoaderInfo-4a67b082-0a4c-41cf-b6c7-440b29bb8c4f'
        info.write_bytes(b'\x07\0\0\0' + 'GRUB 2.16\0'.encode('utf-16-le'))
        result = subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 0, stdout='BootCurrent: 0008\nBoot0008* Solid State Disk\n')
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', return_value=''), patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'run', return_value=result), patch.object(boot, 'run'), patch.object(boot, 'ask', return_value=True) as ask:
            self.assertTrue(boot.grub_detected())
            ask.assert_called_once()
            ask.return_value = False
            self.assertFalse(boot.grub_detected())
            info.write_bytes(b'\x07\0\0\0' + 'systemd-boot\0'.encode('utf-16-le'))
            ask.return_value = True
            self.assertFalse(boot.grub_detected())

    def limine_fixture(self, entries='/Arch Linux\n    protocol: linux\n    path: boot():/vmlinuz-linux\n    cmdline: root=UUID=x rw quiet\n    module_path: boot():/initramfs-linux.img\n'):
        self.write('/boot/limine/limine.conf', 'timeout: 5\n\n' + entries)
        self.write('/boot/limine/limine-bios.sys', 'stage\0Limine 12.9.4 (x86-64, BIOS)\0')
        (self.root / 'boot/grub/grub.cfg').unlink()
        (self.root / 'etc/default/grub').unlink()
        self.original = self.snapshot()
        return self.root / 'boot/limine/limine.conf'

    def limine_patches(self):
        return (patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', side_effect=lambda c: None if c == 'limine-entry-tool' else '/bin/mock'),
                patch.object(boot, 'boot_volumes', return_value=[self.root / 'boot']),
                patch.object(boot, 'mount_of', return_value=self.root / 'boot'))

    def test_limine_detection_needs_version_single_config_and_confirmation(self):
        config = self.limine_fixture()
        which, volumes, mount = self.limine_patches()
        with which, volumes, mount, patch.object(boot, 'ask', return_value=True) as ask:
            self.assertEqual(boot.limine_detected(), config)
            ask.assert_called_once()
            ask.return_value = False
            self.assertIsNone(boot.limine_detected())
            ask.return_value = True
            self.write('/boot/limine/limine-bios.sys', 'stage\0Limine 11.4.1 (x86-64, BIOS)\0')
            self.assertIsNone(boot.limine_detected())
            self.write('/boot/limine/limine-bios.sys', 'stage\0Limine 12.0.0 (x86-64, BIOS)\0')
            self.write('/boot/limine.conf', 'timeout: 1\n')
            self.assertIsNone(boot.limine_detected())

    def test_limine_theme_success_and_restore(self):
        config = self.limine_fixture()
        which, volumes, mount = self.limine_patches()
        with which, volumes, mount:
            boot.apply_limine(config)
            first = config.read_text()
            self.assertIn('wallpaper: boot():/limine/penumbra/background.png\n', first)
            self.assertTrue((self.root / 'boot/limine/penumbra/background.png').is_file())
            self.assertTrue(first.endswith(self.original['boot/limine/limine.conf'].decode()))
            boot.apply_limine(config)
            self.assertEqual(config.read_text(), first)
        for backup in sorted((self.root / 'backups').iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime_ns, reverse=True):
            boot.restore(backup)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / 'boot/limine/penumbra').exists())

    def test_limine_plymouth_adds_splash_without_grub(self):
        config = self.limine_fixture()
        which, volumes, mount = self.limine_patches()
        with which, volumes, mount, patch.object(boot, 'run', side_effect=self.fake_run), patch.object(boot, 'generate_grub') as generate, patch.object(boot.subprocess, 'check_output', return_value='usr/bin/plymouth\npenumbra.plymouth'):
            boot.apply_plymouth(config)
            generate.assert_not_called()
        self.assertIn('cmdline: root=UUID=x rw quiet splash\n', config.read_text())
        self.assertEqual((self.root / 'boot/initramfs-linux.img').read_text(), 'new image')
        self.assertFalse((self.root / 'etc/default/grub').exists())
        boot.restore(next((self.root / 'backups').iterdir()))
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), self.original)

    def test_limine_entry_tool_needs_its_own_cmdline(self):
        config = self.limine_fixture()
        self.write('/etc/default/limine', 'KERNEL_CMDLINE[default]="quiet"\n')
        before = self.snapshot()
        _, volumes, mount = self.limine_patches()
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'), volumes, mount, patch.object(boot, 'run') as run:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'KERNEL_CMDLINE'):
                boot.apply_plymouth(config)
            run.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), before)

    def test_combined_uki_and_initramfs_preset_is_rejected(self):
        with (self.root / 'etc/mkinitcpio.d/linux.preset').open('a') as f:
            f.write("default_uki='/efi/EFI/Linux/arch.efi'\n")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            boot.preset_jobs(self.root / 'etc/mkinitcpio.d')

    def test_dropin_is_rejected_before_changes(self):
        self.write('/etc/mkinitcpio.conf.d/custom.conf', 'HOOKS=(custom)')
        before = self.snapshot()
        with patch.object(boot.shutil, 'which', return_value='/bin/mock'):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                boot.apply_plymouth()
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot(), before)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
```

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


class GeneratedFiles(unittest.TestCase):
    def check(self, tool):
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'tools' / tool), '--check'],
                                capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)

    def test_palette_files_are_current(self):

if __name__ == '__main__':

## `tests/test_install.py`

```python
"""Every distribution folder gives install.sh what it needs, and every template fills in."""
import importlib.util
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DISTROS = sorted(p.parent.name for p in (ROOT / 'distro').glob('*/distro.sh'))
SETTINGS = 'settings=([kb_layout]=us [kb_variant]= [kb_options]= [scale]=auto [numlock]=on [shell]=fish)'


def bash(script, distro, *args):
    """Run `script` with install.sh's functions loaded for `distro`; `args` are "$@". HOME is a
    throwaway folder, so nothing can reach your own configuration."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as home:
        return subprocess.run(['bash', '-c', f'source "$0"; DISTRO=$1; shift; load_distro; {script}',
                               str(ROOT / 'install.sh'), distro, *args],
                              capture_output=True, text=True, env={**os.environ, 'HOME': home})


def templates(distro):
    """The text files install.sh fills in for `distro`."""
    files = [p for folder in (ROOT / 'config', ROOT / 'bin', ROOT / 'share', ROOT / 'distro' / distro)
             for p in folder.rglob('*') if p.is_file()]
    return [str(p) for p in files if b'@@' in p.read_bytes() and b'\0' not in p.read_bytes()]


class Distributions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_arch_is_supported(self):
        self.assertIn('arch', DISTROS)

    def test_distro_sh_defines_everything(self):
        for distro in DISTROS:
            with self.subTest(distro=distro):
                result = bash('(( ${#PACKAGES[@]} ))', distro)
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)

    def test_updates_module(self):
        for distro in DISTROS:
            path = ROOT / 'distro' / distro / 'share/penumbra/updates.py'
            if not path.exists():
                continue
            with self.subTest(distro=distro):
                spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('updates', path)
                module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
                spec.loader.exec_module(module)
                for source in module.sources():
                    self.assertTrue(source['name'] and source['command'], source)
                self.assertIsInstance(module.notes(), list)
                self.assertTrue(all(name and command for name, command in module.upgrades()))

    def test_templates_fill_in(self):
        for distro in DISTROS:
            with self.subTest(distro=distro):
                result = bash(f'{SETTINGS}; set_placeholders; for f; do render_template "$f"; done',
                              distro, *templates(distro))
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
                self.assertEqual(result.stderr, '')
                self.assertNotRegex(result.stdout, r'@@[A-Z_]+@@')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
```

## `bin/penumbra-clipboard`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Clipboard history from cliphist, in the shell's launcher; this rofi menu is the fallback without it.
set -euo pipefail
if [[ ${1:-} = clear ]]; then
 cliphist wipe
 rm -rf "${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/tmp}/penumbra/clipboard"
 notify-send -a Penumbra 'Clipboard cleared' 'History has been wiped.'
 exit
fi
quickshell -c penumbra ipc call launcher clipboard >/dev/null 2>&1 && exit
choice=$(cliphist list | rofi -dmenu -i -p CLIPBOARD -display-columns 2 \
 -theme-str 'entry { placeholder: "Search clipboard history…"; } listview { lines: 8; } element { padding: 9px 12px; }') || exit 0
cliphist decode <<<"$choice" | wl-copy
```

## `bin/penumbra-corners`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Switch Penumbra between rounded and sharp corners: the shell, windows, rofi, hyprlock, Satty
# and the Fastfetch greeting. The shell and Hyprland read the choice back on their next start.
set -euo pipefail
state="$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/corners"
config=${XDG_CONFIG_HOME:-$HOME/.config}
current=$(cat "$state" 2>/dev/null || echo rounded)
[[ $current = sharp ]] || current=rounded
case "${1:-toggle}" in
 toggle) [[ $current = sharp ]] && current=rounded || current=sharp;;
 rounded|sharp) current=$1;;
 restore) ;;
 get) echo "$current"; exit 0;;
 *) echo "Usage: penumbra-corners [toggle|rounded|sharp|restore|get]" >&2; exit 2;;
esac
mkdir -p "${state%/*}"
printf '%s\n' "$current" > "$state"

if [[ $current = sharp ]]; then
 window=0 lock=0 annotate=0
 boxes='s/╭/┌/g; s/╮/┐/g; s/╰/└/g; s/╯/┘/g'
else
 window=16 lock=14 annotate=12
 boxes='s/┌/╭/g; s/┐/╮/g; s/└/╰/g; s/┘/╯/g'
fi

# Windows, at once; hyprland.lua reads the same file when it loads.
if [[ -n ${HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE:-} ]]; then
 hyprctl eval "hl.config({decoration = {rounding = $window}})" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
fi
# Rofi imports corners.rasi after its own theme.
if [[ -d $config/rofi ]]; then
 if [[ $current = sharp ]]; then
  echo 'window, inputbar, element, button { border-radius: 0; }' > "$config/rofi/corners.rasi"
 else
  echo '/* Rounded: penumbra.rasi as it is. Written by penumbra-corners. */' > "$config/rofi/corners.rasi"
 fi
fi
edit() { [[ -f $1 ]] && sed -i "$2" "$1" || true; }
edit "$config/hypr/hyprlock.conf" "s/^\(\s*rounding = \).*/\1$lock/"
edit "$config/satty/config.toml" "s/^corner-roundness = .*/corner-roundness = $annotate/"
edit "$config/fastfetch/config.jsonc" "$boxes"
```

## `bin/penumbra-gpus`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# GPU readings for the shell's system monitor.
#   penumbra-gpus list     one line per GPU: card, driver, model name from the hardware database
#   penumbra-gpus sample   "now <uptime>", then one line per GPU: card and key=value readings
# A GPU that is runtime-suspended (a hybrid laptop's discrete card, most of the time) only
# reports state=asleep: reading its counters, or running nvidia-smi, would power it up.
set -u
shopt -s nullglob

# Cards, not their connectors (card1-eDP-1).
cards() {
    local card
    for card in /sys/class/drm/card[0-9]*; do
        [[ ${card##*/} == *-* || ! -e $card/device/driver ]] || echo "$card"
    done
}

driver() {
    local path
    path=$(readlink -f "$1/device/driver")
    echo "${path##*/}"
}

# The first line of a file, or nothing.
get() {
    local value=
    [[ -r $1 ]] && read -r value < "$1" 2>/dev/null
    echo "$value"
}

list() {
    local card model
    for card in $(cards); do
        model=$(udevadm info -q property -p "$(readlink -f "$card/device")" 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/^ID_MODEL_FROM_DATABASE=//p')
        printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "${card##*/}" "$(driver "$card")" "$model"
    done
}

# Busy time comes as idle counters (Intel) or a percentage (AMD, NVIDIA); memory in MiB,
# temperature in °C, power in W.
sample() {
    local card device out hwmon used total temp power
    read -r now _ < /proc/uptime
    echo "now $now"
    for card in $(cards); do
        device=$(readlink -f "$card/device")
        out="${card##*/}"
        if [[ $(get "$device/power/runtime_status") == suspended ]]; then
            printf '%s\tstate=asleep\n' "$out"
            continue
        fi
        out+=$'\tstate=active'
        case $(driver "$card") in
        i915)
            out+=$'\tidle_ms='$(get "$card/gt/gt0/rc6_residency_ms")
            out+=$'\tfreq='$(get "$card/gt_act_freq_mhz")$'\tmax_freq='$(get "$card/gt_RP0_freq_mhz")
            ;;
        xe)
            out+=$'\tidle_ms='$(get "$device/tile0/gt0/gtidle/idle_residency_ms")
            out+=$'\tfreq='$(get "$device/tile0/gt0/freq0/act_freq")$'\tmax_freq='$(get "$device/tile0/gt0/freq0/rp0_freq")
            ;;
        amdgpu)
            out+=$'\tbusy='$(get "$device/gpu_busy_percent")
            used=$(get "$device/mem_info_vram_used") total=$(get "$device/mem_info_vram_total")
            [[ -n $used && -n $total ]] && out+=$'\tmem_used='$((used / 1048576))$'\tmem_total='$((total / 1048576))
            for hwmon in "$device"/hwmon/hwmon*; do
                temp=$(get "$hwmon/temp1_input") power=$(get "$hwmon/power1_average")
                [[ -n $power ]] || power=$(get "$hwmon/power1_input")
                [[ -n $temp ]] && out+=$'\ttemp='$((temp / 1000))
                [[ -n $power ]] && out+=$'\tpower='$((power / 1000000))
            done
            ;;
        nvidia)
            if command -v nvidia-smi > /dev/null; then
                IFS=', ' read -r busy used total temp power < <(nvidia-smi --id="${device##*/}" \
                    --query-gpu=utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total,temperature.gpu,power.draw \
                    --format=csv,noheader,nounits 2>/dev/null)
                out+=$'\tbusy='${busy:-}$'\tmem_used='${used:-}$'\tmem_total='${total:-}$'\ttemp='${temp:-}$'\tpower='${power%.*}
            fi
            ;;
        esac
        echo "$out"
    done
}

case ${1:-} in
list) list ;;
sample) sample ;;
*)
    echo "usage: ${0##*/} list|sample" >&2
    exit 2
    ;;
esac
```

## `bin/penumbra-osd`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Adjust desktop controls and show a single, replacing Penumbra popup."""
import fcntl
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys


def run(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(args, text=True, env={**os.environ, 'LC_ALL': 'C'}).strip()


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) != 3 or sys.argv[1] not in ('volume', 'microphone', 'brightness') or sys.argv[2] not in ('up', 'down', 'mute', 'show'):
        sys.exit('Usage: penumbra-osd volume|microphone|brightness up|down|mute|show')
    control, action = sys.argv[1:]
    if control == 'brightness' and action == 'mute':
        sys.exit('Brightness cannot be muted')
    # The Penumbra shell changes the value and draws the popup itself; the rest of this script
    # is the fallback for when it isn't running. `quickshell ipc` exits 0 even when the call
    # fails, so only a printed "true" counts; it also reads a "show" argument as its own word.
    try:
        answer = subprocess.run(['quickshell', '-c', 'penumbra', 'ipc', 'call', 'osd', 'adjust', control,
                                 'current' if action == 'show' else action],
                                capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
        if answer == 'true':
            return
    except OSError:
        pass
    directory = Path(os.environ.get('XDG_RUNTIME_DIR', f'/run/user/{os.getuid()}')) / 'penumbra'
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # Serialize repeat events so the last popup always reflects the latest value.
    with (directory / 'osd.lock').open('w') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        muted = False
        if control == 'brightness':
            if action != 'show':
                run('brightnessctl', '--class=backlight', '--min-value=1', 'set', '+5%' if action == 'up' else '5%-')
            current = int(run('brightnessctl', '--class=backlight', 'get'))
            maximum = int(run('brightnessctl', '--class=backlight', 'max'))
            value = round(current * 100 / maximum)
            title = f'Brightness · {value}%'
        else:
            device = '@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SOURCE@' if control == 'microphone' else '@DEFAULT_AUDIO_SINK@'
            if action == 'mute':
                run('wpctl', 'set-mute', device, 'toggle')
            elif action != 'show':
                run('wpctl', 'set-volume', '-l', '1', device, '5%+' if action == 'up' else '5%-')
            status = run('wpctl', 'get-volume', device)
            value = round(float(status.split()[1]) * 100)
            label = 'Microphone' if control == 'microphone' else 'Volume'
            muted = '[MUTED]' in status
            title = f'{label} · Muted' if muted else f'{label} · {value}%'
        value = min(100, max(0, value))
        # The Penumbra shell draws the popup; fall back to a plain notification without it.
        try:
            shown = subprocess.run(['quickshell', '-c', 'penumbra', 'ipc', 'call', 'osd', 'flash',
                                    control, str(value), 'true' if muted else 'false'],
                                   capture_output=True).returncode == 0
        except OSError:
            shown = False
        if not shown:
            run('notify-send', '--app-name=Penumbra OSD', '--urgency=low', '--expire-time=1500',
                '--hint=int:value:' + str(0 if muted else value),
                '--hint=string:x-canonical-private-synchronous:penumbra-osd', title)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    try:
        main()
    except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as error:
        sys.exit(f'penumbra-osd: {error}')
```

set -euo pipefail
color=$(hyprpicker --autocopy --format=hex --no-fancy --quiet 2>/dev/null) || exit 0
