## `bin/penumbra-power`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# The Penumbra shell draws the session overlay; this rofi menu is the fallback without it.
quickshell -c penumbra ipc call panel session >/dev/null 2>&1 && exit
# One row of large icon tiles; rofi returns the selected index, so labels can change freely.
tiles='window { width: 620px; padding: 22px; } mainbox { children: [ listview ]; }
listview { columns: 5; lines: 1; spacing: 10px; fixed-columns: true; fixed-height: true; }
element { orientation: vertical; padding: 16px 0 10px; border-radius: 16px; }
element-text { horizontal-align: 0.5; font: "Inter 10"; }'
menu() {
 local -n labels=$1 icons=$2; local i
 for i in "${!labels[@]}"; do
  printf "<span font_desc='JetBrainsMono Nerd Font 26'>%s</span>\n<span alpha='75%%'>%s</span>|" "${icons[i]}" "${labels[i]}"
 done
}
pick() { rofi -dmenu -sep '|' -eh 4 -markup-rows -no-custom -format i -no-show-icons -p "$1" -theme-str "$tiles $2"; }
names=(Lock Suspend 'Log out' Restart 'Shut down')
glyphs=(󰌾 󰤄 󰍃 󰜉 󰐥)
choice=$(menu names glyphs | pick SESSION '') || exit 0
case "$choice" in
 0) loginctl lock-session; exit;;
 1) systemctl suspend; exit;;
 2) action=logout;;
 3) action=reboot;;
 4) action=poweroff;;
 *) exit;;
esac
confirm=(Cancel "${names[choice]}")
confirm_glyphs=(󰜺 "${glyphs[choice]}")
answer=$(menu confirm confirm_glyphs | pick CONFIRM 'window { width: 300px; } listview { columns: 2; }') || exit 0
[[ "$answer" = 1 ]] || exit 0
case "$action" in
 logout) hyprctl dispatch 'hl.dsp.exit()';;
 reboot) systemctl reboot;;
 poweroff) systemctl poweroff;;
esac
```

## `bin/penumbra-record`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Screen recording to ~/Videos/Screencasts with wf-recorder. The shell's overlay picks the area and
# runs `start --geometry "X,Y WxH"` (--sound adds what the computer plays); `stop` finishes the
# recording. With no argument (Ctrl+Print), it stops a recording or opens the overlay to start one;
# without the shell, slurp asks for a region.
set -euo pipefail
state=${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run/user/$(id -u)}/penumbra/recording
pidfile=$state.pid
log=$state.log

# The shell shows a recording in the bar; it reads $state again when told.
refresh() { quickshell -c penumbra ipc call recorder refresh >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
recording() { [[ -f $pidfile ]] && kill -0 "$(<"$pidfile")" 2>/dev/null; }
stop() { recording && kill -INT "$(<"$pidfile")" || true; }
usage() { echo 'Usage: penumbra-record [toggle|stop|start [--geometry "X,Y WxH"] [--sound]]' >&2; exit 2; }

case ${1:-toggle} in
 stop) stop; exit 0;;
 toggle)
  if recording; then stop; exit 0; fi
  quickshell -c penumbra ipc call shot record >/dev/null 2>&1 && exit 0
  exec "$0" start;;
 start) shift;;
 *) usage;;
esac

geometry= sound=false
while (( $# )); do
 case $1 in
  --geometry) geometry=${2?--geometry needs "X,Y WxH"}; shift;;
  --sound) sound=true;;
  *) usage;;
 esac
 shift
done
recording && exit 0
[[ -n $geometry ]] || geometry=$(slurp -b 100d18aa -c b69af7 -w 2) || exit 0
# H.264 in yuv420p needs an even width and height.
if [[ $geometry =~ ^(-?[0-9]+),(-?[0-9]+)\ ([0-9]+)x([0-9]+)$ ]]; then
 geometry="${BASH_REMATCH[1]},${BASH_REMATCH[2]} $(( BASH_REMATCH[3] / 2 * 2 ))x$(( BASH_REMATCH[4] / 2 * 2 ))"
fi

dir=$HOME/Videos/Screencasts
mkdir -p "$dir" "${state%/*}"
file=$dir/$(date +%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S).mp4
args=(-g "$geometry" -f "$file" -c libx264 -p preset=veryfast -p crf=23 -x yuv420p)
# The default output's monitor carries what the computer plays.
if $sound; then args+=("--audio=$(pactl get-default-sink).monitor"); fi

# This script stays until the recording ends, so it can report it. Job control keeps the recorder
# from inheriting "ignore SIGINT", the signal `stop` finishes it with.
set -m
wf-recorder "${args[@]}" >"$log" 2>&1 &
pid=$!
set +m
echo "$pid" > "$pidfile"
printf '%s\n%s\n' "$(date +%s)" "$file" > "$state"
refresh
wait "$pid" || true
rm -f "$state" "$pidfile"
refresh

if [[ -s $file ]]; then
 # Copied as a file, so it can be pasted into a chat or a file manager.
 printf 'file://%s\n' "$file" | wl-copy --type text/uri-list
 action=$(notify-send -a Penumbra -i video-x-generic --action=open=Play 'Recording saved' \
  "Copied to clipboard · ${file/#$HOME/\~}") || true
 [[ $action = open ]] && xdg-open "$file"
else
 rm -f "$file"
 notify-send -a Penumbra -u critical 'Recording failed' "$(tail -n 3 "$log")"
fi
exit 0
```

## `bin/penumbra-session`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Start the Penumbra desktop components when Hyprland starts; `restart` restarts the shell.
set -u
export QT_QPA_PLATFORMTHEME=qt6ct XCURSOR_THEME=Penumbra-Cursors XCURSOR_SIZE=28
mkdir -p "$HOME/.local/state/penumbra"
exec 9>"${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run/user/$(id -u)}/penumbra-session.lock"
flock -n 9 || exit 0
# The shell runs as a systemd user service, so it needs this session's environment. PATH lets
# commands from the launcher find ~/.local/bin.
dbus-update-activation-environment --systemd WAYLAND_DISPLAY DISPLAY XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP \
 HYPRLAND_INSTANCE_SIGNATURE PATH QT_QPA_PLATFORMTHEME XCURSOR_THEME XCURSOR_SIZE
# Older Penumbra versions exported GTK_THEME, which breaks libadwaita apps.
systemctl --user unset-environment GTK_THEME
# A shell started by hand (or by an older Penumbra) would hold the notification and polkit names.
pkill -u "$UID" -f '^quickshell -c penumbra$' || true
if [[ ${1:-} = restart ]]; then
 systemctl --user reset-failed penumbra-shell.service 2>/dev/null
 systemctl --user restart penumbra-shell.service
 exit
fi
start() {
 local process=$1; shift
 if ! pgrep -u "$UID" -x "$process" >/dev/null; then
  nohup "$@" >"$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/$process.log" 2>&1 9>&- &
 fi
}
hyprctl setcursor Penumbra-Cursors 28 >/dev/null
start awww-daemon awww-daemon
# The shell is the polkit agent too; an older KDE agent would hold the registration.
pkill -u "$UID" -f '^/usr/lib/(.*/)?polkit-kde-authentication-agent-1' || true
# Bar, notifications, OSD, control center, launcher, overlays, lock screen and polkit agent;
# systemd restarts it if it dies (see ~/.config/systemd/user/penumbra-shell.service).
systemctl --user reset-failed penumbra-shell.service 2>/dev/null
systemctl --user restart penumbra-shell.service
start hypridle hypridle
# Clipboard history for text and images.
if ! pgrep -u "$UID" -f "^wl-paste --type text --watch cliphist store" >/dev/null; then
 nohup wl-paste --type text --watch cliphist store >/dev/null 2>&1 9>&- &
 nohup wl-paste --type image --watch cliphist store >/dev/null 2>&1 9>&- &
fi
nohup "$HOME/.local/bin/penumbra-wallpaper" restore >"$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/wallpaper.log" 2>&1 9>&- &
exec 9>&-
```

## `bin/penumbra-shot`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Screenshot to ~/Pictures/Screenshots, saved and copied; "annotate" opens it in Satty first.
# The shell's overlay picks the area (region, window or screen) and passes it back with
# --geometry "X,Y WxH"; without the shell, slurp asks for a region.
set -euo pipefail
annotate=false
region=
while (( $# )); do
 case $1 in
  annotate) annotate=true;;
  --geometry) region=${2?--geometry needs "X,Y WxH"}; shift;;
  *) echo 'Usage: penumbra-shot [annotate] [--geometry "X,Y WxH"]' >&2; exit 2;;
 esac
 shift
done
if [[ -z $region ]]; then
 quickshell -c penumbra ipc call shot open "$annotate" >/dev/null 2>&1 && exit
 region=$(slurp -b 100d18aa -c b69af7 -w 2) || exit 0
fi
mkdir -p "$HOME/Pictures/Screenshots"
file="$HOME/Pictures/Screenshots/$(date +%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S).png"
if [[ $annotate = true ]]; then
 grim -g "$region" - | satty --filename - --output-filename "$file"
 exit
fi
grim -g "$region" "$file"
wl-copy < "$file"
notify-send -a Penumbra -i "$file" 'Screenshot saved' "Copied to clipboard · ${file/#$HOME/\~}"
```

## `bin/penumbra-updates`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Update checks for the Penumbra bar and an interactive Kitty update session."""
import fcntl
import html
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

# The distribution's package sources, installed from distro/<name>/share/penumbra/updates.py.
DISTRO = Path.home() / ".local/share/penumbra/updates.py"


def distro():
    """The distribution's updates module, or None when there is none."""
    if not DISTRO.is_file():
        return None
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("penumbra_distro_updates", DISTRO)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def check(command, empty=(), empty_if_silent=()):
    """The pending updates a command lists, one per line, or None when the check failed."""
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                timeout=180, env={**os.environ, "LC_ALL": "C"})
        if result.returncode in empty:
            return []
        if (result.returncode in empty_if_silent
                and not result.stdout.strip() and not result.stderr.strip()):
            return []
        if result.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError(result.stderr.strip() or "check failed")
        return sorted({line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()})
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired, RuntimeError) as error:
        print(f"{command[0]}: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return None


def status():
    packages = distro()
    sources = packages.sources() if packages else []
    if shutil.which("flatpak"):
        sources.extend({"name": f"Flatpak ({scope})",
                        "command": ["flatpak", "remote-ls", f"--{scope}", "--updates",
                                    "--all", "--arch=*", "--columns=ref"]}
                       for scope in ("user", "system"))
    found = [(source["name"], check(source["command"], source.get("empty", ()),
                                    source.get("empty_if_silent", ())))
             for source in sources]
    total = sum(len(lines) for _, lines in found if lines is not None)
    tooltip = [f"{name}: {len(lines) if lines is not None else 'check unavailable'}"
               for name, lines in found]
    tooltip.extend(packages.notes() if packages else ["System packages: run ./install.sh to enable"])
    tooltip.append("Click for the list · checked every 15 minutes")
    # Each line is what the distribution's check prints (pacman: "name old -> new") or a Flatpak ref.
    print(json.dumps({"text": f"󰚰 {total}" if total else "",
                      "tooltip": html.escape("\n".join(tooltip)),
                      "class": "available",
                      "sources": [{"name": name, "packages": lines}
                                  for name, lines in found if lines]}))


def lock(name):
    directory = Path(os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR",
                     os.environ.get("XDG_CACHE_HOME", str(Path.home() / ".cache")))) / "penumbra"
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    handle = (directory / name).open("w")
    try:
        fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    except BlockingIOError:
        handle.close()
        return None
    return handle


def update():
    handle = lock("update.lock")
    if handle is None:
        return 0
    failed = False
    try:
        packages = distro()
        commands = packages.upgrades() if packages else []
        if shutil.which("flatpak"):
            commands.extend((f"Flatpak ({scope})", ["flatpak", "update", f"--{scope}"])
                            for scope in ("user", "system"))
        for name, command in commands:
            print(f"\n── {name} ──\n", flush=True)
            if subprocess.run(command).returncode:
                failed = True
                break
    except (OSError, KeyboardInterrupt) as error:
        print(f"\nUpdate interrupted: {error}", flush=True)
        failed = True
    finally:
        # Refresh the bar's update count, including after a partial upgrade.
        subprocess.run(["quickshell", "-c", "penumbra", "ipc", "call", "updates", "refresh"],
                       capture_output=True, check=False)
        handle.close()
    if failed:
        try:
            input("\nUpdates did not finish. Press Enter to close…")
        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
            pass
    return int(failed)


def main():
    mode = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status"
    if mode == "status":
        status()
    elif mode == "open":
        handle = lock("terminal.lock")
        if handle is not None:
            try:
                return subprocess.call(["kitty", "--class", "penumbra-updates",
                                        "--title", "Penumbra · Updates",
                                        str(Path(__file__).resolve()), "run"])
            finally:
                handle.close()
    elif mode == "run":
        return update()
    else:
        sys.exit("Usage: penumbra-updates [status|open|run]")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## `bin/penumbra-wallpaper`

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Switch Penumbra wallpapers (the next one, or one by name) with an awww transition;
# the lock screen follows the choice.
set -euo pipefail
dir="$HOME/.local/share/penumbra"
state="$HOME/.local/state/penumbra/wallpaper"
names=(wallpaper wallpaper-dawn wallpaper-eclipse)
current=$(cat "$state" 2>/dev/null || echo wallpaper)
[[ -e "$dir/$current.png" ]] || current=wallpaper
transition=(--transition-type grow --transition-duration 1.4 --transition-fps 120)
case "${1:-next}" in
 next)
  for i in "${!names[@]}"; do [[ ${names[i]} = "$current" ]] && break; done
  current=${names[(i + 1) % ${#names[@]}]};;
 wallpaper|wallpaper-dawn|wallpaper-eclipse) current=$1;;
 restore) transition=(--transition-type none);;
 *) echo "Usage: penumbra-wallpaper [next|restore|wallpaper|wallpaper-dawn|wallpaper-eclipse]" >&2; exit 2;;
esac
# Grow each scene out of its planet, or the eclipse.
if [[ ${1:-next} != restore ]]; then
 case $current in
  wallpaper-dawn) transition+=(--transition-pos 0.32,0.37);;
  wallpaper-eclipse) transition+=(--transition-pos 0.65,0.63);;
  *) transition+=(--transition-pos 0.68,0.51);;
 esac
fi
mkdir -p "${state%/*}"
printf '%s\n' "$current" > "$state"
ln -sfn "$dir/$current-clean.png" "$dir/lock.png"
# The shell animates dawn and eclipse over awww's still image, once the transition has finished.
[[ ${1:-next} = restore ]] && delay=0 || delay=1400
quickshell -c penumbra ipc call wallpaper set "$current" "$delay" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
for _ in {1..50}; do awww query >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 0.1; done
exec awww img "$dir/$current.png" --resize crop "${transition[@]}"
```

```fish
function fish_greeting
    if command -q fastfetch
        if test "$TERM" = xterm-kitty; and test "$COLUMNS" -ge 95
            fastfetch
        else
            fastfetch --logo none
        end
    else
        set_color $penumbra_violet
        printf '\n  ◈  P E N U M B R A\n'
        set_color normal
    end
end
```

## `config/hypr/hypridle.conf`

```ini
general {
    # The shell's lock screen; hyprlock when the shell isn't running.
    lock_cmd = quickshell -c penumbra ipc call lock lock || pidof hyprlock || hyprlock
    before_sleep_cmd = loginctl lock-session
    after_sleep_cmd = hyprctl dispatch 'hl.dsp.dpms("on")'
}
listener {
    timeout = 600
    on-timeout = loginctl lock-session
}
```

## `config/hypr/hyprland.lua`

```lua
-- PENUMBRA / Hyprland 0.56 Lua configuration
-- Keyboard, Num Lock, scale and the cursor mode come from ~/.config/penumbra/settings.conf (see
-- ./install.sh --help). Put your own settings in ~/.config/hypr/user.lua, which is read last and
-- survives ./install.sh.
local home = os.getenv("HOME")
-- Colours come from palette/palette.toml (tools/build-palette.py).
local c = dofile(home .. "/.config/hypr/colors.lua")
hl.monitor({ output = "", mode = "preferred", position = "auto", scale = "@@SCALE@@" })
hl.env("XCURSOR_THEME", "Penumbra-Cursors")
hl.env("XCURSOR_SIZE", "28")
hl.env("HYPRCURSOR_SIZE", "28")
hl.env("QT_QPA_PLATFORMTHEME", "qt6ct")
-- Rounded or sharp corners, as penumbra-corners records them.
local corners = io.open(home .. "/.local/state/penumbra/corners")
local sharp = corners ~= nil and corners:read("l") == "sharp"
if corners then corners:close() end
hl.config({
 xwayland = {
    force_zero_scaling = true
 },
 -- Software cursors in virtual machines, where hardware cursors often draw wrongly; automatic elsewhere.
 cursor = { no_hardware_cursors = @@SOFTWARE_CURSOR@@, enable_hyprcursor = false },
 general = { gaps_in = 6, gaps_out = 14, border_size = 2, resize_on_border = true, layout = "dwindle",
  col = { active_border = {colors = {"rgba(" .. c.violet .. "ee)", "rgba(" .. c.violetDeep .. "bb)"}, angle = 45}, inactive_border = "rgba(" .. c.black .. "ff)" } },
 decoration = { rounding = sharp and 0 or 16, rounding_power = 2, active_opacity = 1, inactive_opacity = 0.97,
  shadow = {enabled = true, range = 28, render_power = 3, color = "rgba(" .. c.violetInk .. "88)", color_inactive = "rgba(00000055)"},
  glow = {enabled = true, range = 18, render_power = 3, color = "rgba(" .. c.violetDeep .. "40)", color_inactive = "rgba(00000000)"},
  blur = {enabled = true, size = 6, passes = 3, vibrancy = 0.2, noise = 0.02, contrast = 1.1, brightness = 0.9, popups = true} },
 animations = {enabled = true},
 dwindle = {preserve_split = true},
 input = {kb_layout = "@@KB_LAYOUT@@", kb_variant = "@@KB_VARIANT@@", kb_options = "@@KB_OPTIONS@@", numlock_by_default = "@@NUMLOCK@@" == "on",
  follow_mouse = 1, sensitivity = 0, touchpad = {natural_scroll = true}},
 -- A fresh lock screen (hyprlock from a TTY) can take over if the shell's ever dies while locked.
 misc = {disable_hyprland_logo = true, disable_splash_rendering = true, force_default_wallpaper = 0, allow_session_lock_restore = true},
})
hl.curve("penumbra", {type = "bezier", points = {{0.16, 1}, {0.3, 1}}})
hl.curve("soft", {type = "bezier", points = {{0.4, 0}, {0.2, 1}}})
hl.curve("linear", {type = "bezier", points = {{0, 0}, {1, 1}}})
for _, a in ipairs({
 {"windows", 4.5, "penumbra", "slide"}, {"windowsIn", 4, "penumbra", "popin 94%"},
 {"windowsOut", 3, "soft", "popin 96%"}, {"border", 5, "penumbra", nil},
 {"borderangle", 60, "linear", "loop"},
 {"fade", 3, "soft", nil}, {"workspaces", 4.5, "penumbra", "slide"},
 {"layers", 3.5, "penumbra", "fade"}
}) do hl.animation({leaf=a[1], enabled=true, speed=a[2], bezier=a[3], style=a[4]}) end
local function exec(key, cmd) hl.bind(key, hl.dsp.exec_cmd(cmd)) end
exec("SUPER + Return", "kitty")
exec("SUPER + Q", "kitty")
exec("SUPER + E", "dolphin")
exec("SUPER + R", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-launcher")
exec("SUPER + space", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-launcher")
-- Your default web browser (xdg-settings set default-web-browser NAME.desktop to change it).
exec("SUPER + B", "gtk-launch \"$(xdg-settings get default-web-browser)\"")
exec("SUPER + M", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-power")
exec("SUPER + L", "loginctl lock-session")
exec("SUPER + N", "quickshell -c penumbra ipc call notifications clear")
exec("SUPER + A", "quickshell -c penumbra ipc call panel control")
exec("SUPER + comma", "quickshell -c penumbra ipc call panel settings")
exec("SUPER + SHIFT + V", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-clipboard")
exec("SUPER + semicolon", "quickshell -c penumbra ipc call launcher emoji")
exec("SUPER + SHIFT + C", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-picker")
exec("SUPER + W", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-wallpaper next")
exec("SUPER + SHIFT + W", "quickshell -c penumbra ipc call panel wallpapers")
exec("SUPER + SHIFT + R", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-session restart")
exec("Print", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-shot")
exec("SHIFT + Print", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-shot annotate")
-- Record the screen; press again (or click the red capsule) to stop.
exec("CTRL + Print", "~/.local/bin/penumbra-record")
hl.bind("SUPER + C", hl.dsp.window.close())
hl.bind("SUPER + V", hl.dsp.window.float({action="toggle"}))
hl.bind("SUPER + F", hl.dsp.window.fullscreen())
hl.bind("SUPER + J", hl.dsp.layout("togglesplit"))
for _, d in ipairs({"left", "right", "up", "down"}) do hl.bind("SUPER + "..d, hl.dsp.focus({direction=d})) end
-- Physical keycodes keep the number-row workspace shortcuts working on any layout (AZERTY included).
for i=1,10 do
 hl.bind("SUPER + code:"..(i+9), hl.dsp.focus({workspace=i}))
 hl.bind("SUPER + SHIFT + code:"..(i+9), hl.dsp.window.move({workspace=i}))
end
hl.bind("SUPER + mouse:272", hl.dsp.window.drag(), {mouse=true})
hl.bind("SUPER + mouse:273", hl.dsp.window.resize(), {mouse=true})
hl.bind("SUPER + mouse_down", hl.dsp.focus({workspace="e+1"}))
hl.bind("SUPER + mouse_up", hl.dsp.focus({workspace="e-1"}))
-- Volume and brightness repeat while held; toggles (mute, play/pause) fire once per press.
for key,cmd in pairs({
 XF86AudioRaiseVolume="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd volume up",
 XF86AudioLowerVolume="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd volume down",
 XF86MonBrightnessUp="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd brightness up", XF86MonBrightnessDown="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd brightness down"
}) do hl.bind(key, hl.dsp.exec_cmd(cmd), {locked=true, repeating=true}) end
for key,cmd in pairs({
 XF86AudioMute="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd volume mute",
 XF86AudioMicMute="~/.local/bin/penumbra-osd microphone mute",
 XF86AudioPlay="playerctl play-pause", XF86AudioPause="playerctl pause",
 XF86AudioNext="playerctl next", XF86AudioPrev="playerctl previous"
}) do hl.bind(key, hl.dsp.exec_cmd(cmd), {locked=true}) end
hl.window_rule({name="settings", match={class="^(org.pulseaudio.pavucontrol|pavucontrol|blueman-manager|nm-connection-editor|qt6ct)$"}, float=true, size="820 540", center=true})
hl.window_rule({name="annotate", match={class="^(com.gabm.satty)$"}, float=true, center=true})
-- Frosted glass behind every Penumbra shell surface and the launcher.
hl.layer_rule({name="glass", match={namespace="^(penumbra-.*|rofi)$"}, blur=true, ignore_alpha=0.3})
-- The screenshot overlay must be gone at once, before grim reads the screen.
hl.layer_rule({name="shot", match={namespace="^penumbra-shot$"}, no_anim=true})
hl.window_rule({name="suppress-maximize", match={class=".*"}, suppress_event="maximize"})
hl.on("hyprland.start", function() hl.exec_cmd("~/.local/bin/penumbra-session") end)
-- Your own settings, if any, override everything above.
local user = io.open(home .. "/.config/hypr/user.lua")
if user then user:close(); dofile(home .. "/.config/hypr/user.lua") end
```

## `config/hypr/hyprlock.conf`

```ini
# Colours come from hyprlock-colors.conf, generated from palette/palette.toml.
source = ~/.config/hypr/hyprlock-colors.conf
general {
    hide_cursor = true
    immediate_render = true
    screencopy_mode = 1
}
background {
    monitor =
    path = ~/.local/share/penumbra/lock.png
    blur_passes = 2
    blur_size = 3
    brightness = 0.7
}
label {
    monitor =
    text = $TIME
    color = $text
    font_size = 84
    font_family = Inter
    position = 0, 100
    halign = center
    valign = center
}
label {
    monitor =
    text = P E N U M B R A   /   $USER
    color = $violet
    font_size = 12
    font_family = Inter
    position = 0, 20
    halign = center
    valign = center
}
input-field {
    monitor =
    size = 310, 56
    outline_thickness = 2
    fade_on_empty = false
    dots_size = 0.2
    dots_spacing = 0.3
    outer_color = $outline
    inner_color = $field
    font_color = $text
    check_color = $check
    fail_color = $fail
    placeholder_text = Enter password
    fail_text = Try again ($ATTEMPTS)
    rounding = 14
    position = 0, -65
    halign = center
    valign = center
}
```

## `config/kitty/kitty.conf`

```ini
# PENUMBRA
shell /usr/bin/fish
font_family JetBrainsMono Nerd Font
bold_font auto
italic_font auto
font_size 11.0
window_padding_width 24
# Colours come from palette/palette.toml (tools/build-palette.py).
include penumbra-colors.conf
background_opacity 0.8
dynamic_background_opacity yes
cursor_shape beam
cursor_trail 3
cursor_trail_decay 0.12 0.35
cursor_blink_interval 0.6
tab_bar_edge bottom
tab_bar_style powerline
tab_powerline_style slanted
hide_window_decorations yes
enable_audio_bell no
confirm_os_window_close 0
remember_window_size yes
initial_window_width 100c
initial_window_height 28c
map ctrl+shift+enter new_window_with_cwd
map ctrl+shift+t new_tab_with_cwd
# Your own settings: ~/.config/kitty/user.conf is read last and survives ./install.sh.
globinclude user.conf
```

## `config/nvim/colors/penumbra.lua`

```lua
-- Original Penumbra palette, shared with the desktop (lua/penumbra/palette.lua is generated from
-- palette/palette.toml).
vim.cmd('highlight clear')
if vim.g.syntax_on then vim.cmd('syntax reset') end
vim.o.background = 'dark'
vim.g.colors_name = 'penumbra'
package.loaded['penumbra.palette'] = nil
local c = require('penumbra.palette')
local groups = {
  Normal = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.bg }, NormalNC = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.bg },
  NormalFloat = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.panel }, FloatBorder = { fg = c.violet, bg = c.panel },
  FloatTitle = { fg = c.violet, bg = c.panel, bold = true },
  CursorLine = { bg = c.raised }, CursorLineNr = { fg = c.violet, bold = true },
  LineNr = { fg = c.muted }, SignColumn = { bg = c.bg }, EndOfBuffer = { fg = c.border },
  WinSeparator = { fg = c.border }, Visual = { bg = c.selection },
  Search = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.gold }, IncSearch = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.violet }, CurSearch = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.violet },
  Pmenu = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.panel }, PmenuSel = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.violet },
  PmenuSbar = { bg = c.raised }, PmenuThumb = { bg = c.muted },
  StatusLine = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.panel }, StatusLineNC = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel },
  TabLine = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel }, TabLineSel = { fg = c.violet, bg = c.raised, bold = true },
  TabLineFill = { bg = c.panel }, Directory = { fg = c.violet },
  Title = { fg = c.violet, bold = true }, Comment = { fg = c.dim, italic = true },
  Constant = { fg = c.gold }, String = { fg = c.green }, Character = { fg = c.green },
  Number = { fg = c.gold }, Boolean = { fg = c.gold }, Float = { fg = c.gold },
  Identifier = { fg = c.fg }, Function = { fg = c.blue }, Statement = { fg = c.violet },
  Keyword = { fg = c.violet }, Operator = { fg = c.pink }, PreProc = { fg = c.pink },
  Type = { fg = c.gold }, Special = { fg = c.pink }, Delimiter = { fg = c.muted },
  Underlined = { fg = c.blue, underline = true }, Todo = { fg = c.gold, bold = true },
  Error = { fg = c.rose }, ErrorMsg = { fg = c.rose }, WarningMsg = { fg = c.gold },
  MatchParen = { fg = c.violet, bg = c.border, bold = true }, NonText = { fg = c.border },
  Folded = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel }, FoldColumn = { fg = c.muted },
  DiffAdd = { bg = c.diffAdd }, DiffDelete = { bg = c.diffDelete },
  DiffChange = { bg = c.diffChange }, DiffText = { bg = c.diffText },
  GitSignsAdd = { fg = c.green }, GitSignsChange = { fg = c.violet }, GitSignsDelete = { fg = c.rose },
  DiagnosticError = { fg = c.rose }, DiagnosticWarn = { fg = c.gold },
  DiagnosticInfo = { fg = c.blue }, DiagnosticHint = { fg = c.violet },
  NvimTreeNormal = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.panel }, NvimTreeNormalNC = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.panel },
  NvimTreeEndOfBuffer = { fg = c.panel, bg = c.panel },
  NvimTreeRootFolder = { fg = c.violet, bold = true }, NvimTreeFolderName = { fg = c.violet },
  NvimTreeOpenedFolderName = { fg = c.pink, bold = true }, NvimTreeFolderIcon = { fg = c.violet },
  TelescopeBorder = { fg = c.border }, TelescopeSelection = { bg = c.raised },
  TelescopeMatching = { fg = c.violet, bold = true },
  WhichKey = { fg = c.violet }, WhichKeyGroup = { fg = c.blue }, WhichKeyDesc = { fg = c.fg },

  -- Treesitter captures (`:Inspect` shows them under the cursor). Unlisted ones fall back to
  -- Neovim's links to the classic groups above.
  ['@variable'] = { fg = c.fg }, ['@variable.builtin'] = { fg = c.pink, italic = true },
  ['@variable.parameter'] = { fg = c.soft }, ['@variable.member'] = { fg = c.soft },
  ['@property'] = { fg = c.soft }, ['@constant'] = { fg = c.gold }, ['@constant.builtin'] = { fg = c.gold, italic = true },
  ['@module'] = { fg = c.cyan }, ['@label'] = { fg = c.cyan },
  ['@string'] = { fg = c.green }, ['@string.escape'] = { fg = c.pink }, ['@string.regexp'] = { fg = c.pink },
  ['@string.special.url'] = { fg = c.blue, underline = true },
  ['@character'] = { fg = c.green }, ['@number'] = { fg = c.gold }, ['@boolean'] = { fg = c.gold },
  ['@type'] = { fg = c.gold }, ['@type.builtin'] = { fg = c.gold, italic = true },
  ['@attribute'] = { fg = c.cyan }, ['@function'] = { fg = c.blue }, ['@function.builtin'] = { fg = c.blue, italic = true },
  ['@function.method'] = { fg = c.blue }, ['@constructor'] = { fg = c.gold },
  ['@keyword'] = { fg = c.violet }, ['@keyword.function'] = { fg = c.violet, italic = true },
  ['@keyword.return'] = { fg = c.violet, bold = true }, ['@keyword.import'] = { fg = c.pink },
  ['@operator'] = { fg = c.pink }, ['@punctuation'] = { fg = c.muted }, ['@punctuation.bracket'] = { fg = c.muted },
  ['@punctuation.delimiter'] = { fg = c.muted }, ['@punctuation.special'] = { fg = c.pink },
  ['@comment'] = { fg = c.dim, italic = true }, ['@comment.todo'] = { fg = c.gold, bold = true },
  ['@comment.error'] = { fg = c.rose, bold = true }, ['@comment.warning'] = { fg = c.gold, bold = true },
  ['@tag'] = { fg = c.violet }, ['@tag.attribute'] = { fg = c.cyan }, ['@tag.delimiter'] = { fg = c.muted },
  ['@markup.heading'] = { fg = c.violet, bold = true }, ['@markup.strong'] = { bold = true },
  ['@markup.italic'] = { italic = true }, ['@markup.link'] = { fg = c.blue }, ['@markup.link.url'] = { fg = c.blue, underline = true },
  ['@markup.raw'] = { fg = c.green }, ['@markup.list'] = { fg = c.pink }, ['@markup.quote'] = { fg = c.muted, italic = true },
  ['@diff.plus'] = { fg = c.green }, ['@diff.minus'] = { fg = c.rose }, ['@diff.delta'] = { fg = c.violet },
  -- Language servers' semantic tokens use the same colours.
  ['@lsp.type.namespace'] = { link = '@module' }, ['@lsp.type.parameter'] = { link = '@variable.parameter' },
  ['@lsp.type.property'] = { link = '@property' }, ['@lsp.type.enumMember'] = { link = '@constant' },
  ['@lsp.type.macro'] = { link = '@constant' }, ['@lsp.mod.deprecated'] = { strikethrough = true },
}
for name, value in pairs(groups) do vim.api.nvim_set_hl(0, name, value) end
for _, severity in ipairs({ 'Error', 'Warn', 'Info', 'Hint' }) do
  vim.api.nvim_set_hl(0, 'DiagnosticUnderline' .. severity, { sp = groups['Diagnostic' .. severity].fg, undercurl = true })
end
for i, color in ipairs(c.terminal) do vim.g['terminal_color_' .. (i - 1)] = color end
```

## `config/nvim/init.lua`

```lua
vim.g.mapleader = ' '
vim.g.maplocalleader = ' '
vim.g.loaded_netrw = 1
vim.g.loaded_netrwPlugin = 1
local o = vim.opt
o.termguicolors = true
o.number = true
o.relativenumber = true
o.mouse = 'a'
o.clipboard = 'unnamedplus'
o.expandtab = true
o.shiftwidth = 2
o.tabstop = 2
o.smartindent = true
o.ignorecase = true
o.smartcase = true
o.signcolumn = 'yes'
o.cursorline = true
o.wrap = false
o.scrolloff = 6
o.splitright = true
o.splitbelow = true
o.undofile = true
o.updatetime = 250
o.timeoutlen = 400
o.completeopt = { 'menu', 'menuone', 'noselect' }
o.laststatus = 3
vim.cmd.colorscheme('penumbra')
local path = vim.fn.stdpath('data') .. '/lazy/lazy.nvim'
if not vim.uv.fs_stat(path) then
  local result = vim.fn.system({ 'git', 'clone', '--filter=blob:none', '--branch=stable',
    'https://github.com/folke/lazy.nvim.git', path })
  if vim.v.shell_error ~= 0 then
    vim.notify('Plugin download failed. Check your connection and restart Neovim.\n' .. result, vim.log.levels.ERROR)
    return
  end
end
vim.opt.rtp:prepend(path)
require('lazy').setup('penumbra.plugins', {
  change_detection = { notify = false },
  checker = { enabled = false },
  local_spec = false,
  ui = { border = 'rounded' },
})
require('penumbra.keys')
vim.diagnostic.config({ virtual_text = false, float = { border = 'rounded' }, severity_sort = true })
vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd('VimEnter', {
  callback = function()
    if #vim.api.nvim_list_uis() == 0 or vim.fn.argc() > 1 then return end
    local arg = vim.fn.argv(0)
    if vim.fn.argc() == 0 or vim.fn.isdirectory(arg) == 1 then
      if vim.fn.isdirectory(arg) == 1 then vim.cmd.cd(vim.fn.fnameescape(arg)) end
      require('nvim-tree.api').tree.open()
    end
  end,
})
-- Your own settings: ~/.config/nvim/lua/user.lua runs last and survives ./install.sh.
local user = vim.fn.stdpath('config') .. '/lua/user.lua'
if vim.uv.fs_stat(user) then dofile(user) end
```

## `config/nvim/lua/penumbra/keys.lua`

```lua
local function map(key, action, desc, mode)
  vim.keymap.set(mode or 'n', key, action, { silent = true, desc = desc })
end
local function find(name)
  return function() require('telescope.builtin')[name]() end
end
map('<leader>e', '<cmd>NvimTreeToggle<cr>', 'Toggle file tree')
map('<leader>o', '<cmd>NvimTreeFindFile<cr>', 'Reveal current file')
map('<leader><space>', find('find_files'), 'Find project file')
map('<C-p>', find('find_files'), 'Find project file')
map('<leader>ff', find('find_files'), 'Find files')
map('<leader>fg', find('live_grep'), 'Search project text')
map('<leader>fr', find('oldfiles'), 'Recent files')
map('<leader>fb', find('buffers'), 'Open buffers')
map('<leader>fh', find('help_tags'), 'Search help')
map('<S-l>', '<cmd>bnext<cr>', 'Next file')
map('<S-h>', '<cmd>bprevious<cr>', 'Previous file')
map('<leader>bn', '<cmd>bnext<cr>', 'Next file')
map('<leader>bp', '<cmd>bprevious<cr>', 'Previous file')
map('<leader>bd', '<cmd>bdelete<cr>', 'Close file (protect unsaved changes)')
map('<leader>s', '<cmd>write<cr>', 'Save file')
map('<C-s>', '<cmd>write<cr>', 'Save file', { 'n', 'i' })
map('<leader>q', '<cmd>confirm qall<cr>', 'Quit (ask about unsaved files)')
map('<Esc>', '<cmd>nohlsearch<cr>', 'Clear search highlight')
map('<leader>?', function() require('which-key').show({ global = true }) end, 'Show shortcuts')
map('<leader>wl', '<C-w>l', 'Focus right window')
map('<leader>wh', '<C-w>h', 'Focus left window')
map('<leader>wj', '<C-w>j', 'Focus lower window')
map('<leader>wk', '<C-w>k', 'Focus upper window')
map('<leader>wv', '<cmd>vsplit<cr>', 'Split vertically')
map('<leader>ws', '<cmd>split<cr>', 'Split horizontally')
map('<leader>gg', find('git_status'), 'Git changed files')
map('<leader>gp', function() require('gitsigns').preview_hunk() end, 'Preview Git change')
map('<leader>cm', '<cmd>Mason<cr>', 'Install language tools')
map('<leader>cd', vim.diagnostic.open_float, 'Line diagnostics')
map('<leader>cl', find('diagnostics'), 'Project diagnostics')
map('<leader>cr', vim.lsp.buf.rename, 'Rename symbol')
map('<leader>ca', vim.lsp.buf.code_action, 'Code action')
map('<leader>cf', function() vim.lsp.buf.format({ async = true }) end, 'Format with language server')
map('gd', vim.lsp.buf.definition, 'Go to definition')
map('grr', vim.lsp.buf.references, 'Find references')
map('K', vim.lsp.buf.hover, 'Documentation')
map('<leader>t', '<cmd>botright 12split | terminal<cr>', 'Open terminal')
map('<Esc><Esc>', '<C-\\><C-n>', 'Leave terminal mode', 't')
map('<leader>p', '<cmd>Lazy<cr>', 'Manage plugins')
```

## `config/nvim/lua/penumbra/plugins.lua`

```lua
return {
  { 'nvim-tree/nvim-web-devicons', opts = {} },
  { 'nvim-tree/nvim-tree.lua', dependencies = { 'nvim-tree/nvim-web-devicons' }, opts = {
    view = { width = 32, side = 'left' },
    renderer = { group_empty = true, highlight_git = 'name' },
    update_focused_file = { enable = true },
    filters = { dotfiles = false, custom = { '^.git$' } },
    actions = { open_file = { quit_on_open = false } },
  } },
  { 'folke/which-key.nvim', opts = { preset = 'modern', delay = 350, spec = {
    { '<leader>f', group = 'Find' }, { '<leader>b', group = 'Buffers' },
    { '<leader>c', group = 'Code' }, { '<leader>g', group = 'Git' },
    { '<leader>w', group = 'Windows' },
  } } },
  { 'nvim-telescope/telescope.nvim', dependencies = { 'nvim-lua/plenary.nvim' }, opts = {
    defaults = { sorting_strategy = 'ascending', layout_config = { prompt_position = 'top' },
      file_ignore_patterns = { 'node_modules/', '%.git/' } },
    pickers = { find_files = { hidden = true } },
  } },
  { 'akinsho/bufferline.nvim', dependencies = { 'nvim-tree/nvim-web-devicons' }, opts = {
    options = { diagnostics = 'nvim_lsp', separator_style = 'thin',
      offsets = { { filetype = 'NvimTree', text = 'PENUMBRA · Files', text_align = 'center' } } },
  } },
  { 'nvim-lualine/lualine.nvim', opts = function()
    local c = require('penumbra.palette')
    return { options = { theme = {
      normal = { a = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.violet, gui = 'bold' }, b = { fg = c.fg, bg = c.raised }, c = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel } },
      insert = { a = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.green } },
      visual = { a = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.pink } },
      replace = { a = { fg = c.bg, bg = c.rose } },
      inactive = { a = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel }, b = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel }, c = { fg = c.muted, bg = c.panel } },
    }, globalstatus = true } }
  end },
  -- Treesitter parsers, so code is coloured by its structure. Building them needs tree-sitter-cli
  -- and a C compiler (both installed by ./install.sh); `:TSInstall LANGUAGE` adds more.
  { 'nvim-treesitter/nvim-treesitter', branch = 'main', lazy = false, build = ':TSUpdate', config = function()
    if vim.fn.executable('tree-sitter') == 1 then
      require('nvim-treesitter').install({ 'bash', 'c', 'css', 'go', 'html', 'javascript', 'json', 'lua',
        'markdown', 'markdown_inline', 'python', 'qmljs', 'rust', 'toml', 'typescript', 'yaml' })
    end
    vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd('FileType', { callback = function(ev)
      -- Treesitter colours and indents wherever a parser is installed; Vim's syntax files elsewhere.
      if pcall(vim.treesitter.start, ev.buf) then
        vim.bo[ev.buf].indentexpr = "v:lua.require'nvim-treesitter'.indentexpr()"
      end
    end })
  end },
  { 'lewis6991/gitsigns.nvim', opts = {} },
  { 'windwp/nvim-autopairs', opts = {} },
  { 'mason-org/mason.nvim', opts = { ui = { border = 'rounded' } } },
  { 'neovim/nvim-lspconfig', dependencies = { 'mason-org/mason.nvim' }, config = function()
    -- Enable only installed servers; Mason adds its executables to PATH.
    local servers = {
      lua_ls = 'lua-language-server', pyright = 'pyright-langserver',
      ts_ls = 'typescript-language-server', bashls = 'bash-language-server',
      clangd = 'clangd', rust_analyzer = 'rust-analyzer', gopls = 'gopls',
    }
    vim.lsp.config('lua_ls', { settings = { Lua = { diagnostics = { globals = { 'vim' } } } } })
    for server, executable in pairs(servers) do
      if vim.fn.executable(executable) == 1 then vim.lsp.enable(server) end
    end
    vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd('LspAttach', { callback = function(ev)
      local client = vim.lsp.get_client_by_id(ev.data.client_id)
      if client and client:supports_method('textDocument/completion') then
        vim.lsp.completion.enable(true, client.id, ev.buf, { autotrigger = true })
      end
    end })
  end },
}
```
