## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Avatar.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Widgets

// Your ~/.face when there is one, else your initial on a violet disc. The control center and
// the lock screen show the same one.
ClippingRectangle {
    id: root

    property int size: 42
    property bool ring: false

    implicitWidth: size
    implicitHeight: size
    radius: Theme.corner(size / 2)
    border.width: ring ? 2 : 0
    border.color: Theme.violet

    Rectangle {
        anchors.fill: parent
        gradient: Gradient {
            GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violet }
            GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.violetDeep }
        }
    }
    Label {
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        visible: face.status !== Image.Ready
        text: Quickshell.env("USER").charAt(0).toUpperCase()
        font.family: Theme.display
        font.pixelSize: Math.round(root.size * 0.4)
        font.weight: Font.Bold
        color: Theme.violetInk
    }
    Image {
        id: face
        anchors.fill: parent
        fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
        sourceSize: Qt.size(root.size * 2, root.size * 2)
        asynchronous: true
    }
    FileView {
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.face"
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: face.source = "file://" + path
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Bar.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Effects
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Services.SystemTray
import Quickshell.Wayland
import Quickshell.Widgets

// Floating capsules: launcher, clock, workspaces and media left; focused window centered; status right.
PanelWindow {
    id: bar
    required property var modelData
    screen: modelData

    anchors { top: true; left: true; right: true }
    margins { top: 10; left: Theme.edge; right: Theme.edge }
    implicitHeight: Theme.barHeight
    exclusiveZone: Theme.barHeight + 4
    color: "transparent"
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-bar"

    IdleInhibitor {
        window: bar
        enabled: Ui.caffeine
    }

    Item {
    id: content
    anchors.fill: parent
    HoverHandler {
        onPointChanged: bar.updateTip(point.position)
        onHoveredChanged: if (!hovered) bar.updateTip(null)
    }

    // ── Left ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    RowLayout {
        id: leftSide
        anchors { left: parent.left; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
        spacing: Theme.gap

        Capsule {
            padding: 14
            tip: "Applications · right-click for the clipboard"
            active: Ui.launcherOpen
            onClicked: m => Ui.toggleLauncher(m.button === Qt.RightButton ? "clipboard" : "apps")
            Glyph { text: Theme.distroGlyph; size: 20; color: Ui.launcherOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.violetBright }
        }

        Capsule {
            id: clockCapsule
            padding: 18
            active: Ui.calendarOpen
            tip: Qt.formatDateTime(Clock.date, "dddd d MMMM yyyy")
            onClicked: Ui.toggleCalendar()
            Label {
                text: Qt.formatDateTime(Clock.date, "HH:mm")
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                font.weight: Font.Bold
                color: Ui.calendarOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text
            }
        }

        // Workspaces sit right of the clock.
        RowLayout {
            spacing: Theme.gap
            Repeater {
                model: Hyprland.workspaces.values
                    .filter(w => w.id > 0 && w.monitor?.name === bar.screen.name && (w.toplevels.values.length > 0 || w.focused || w.active))
                    .sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
                delegate: Capsule {
                    id: workspace
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property bool current: modelData.active && modelData.monitor?.name === bar.screen.name
                    implicitWidth: Math.max(current ? 52 : Theme.barHeight, workspaceLabel.implicitWidth + 26)
                    padding: 0
                    active: current
                    color: current ? Theme.violet : modelData.urgent ? Theme.rose : (hovered ? Theme.hover : Theme.glass)
                    onClicked: modelData.activate()
                    Label {
                        id: workspaceLabel
                        text: workspace.modelData.name
                        color: workspace.current || workspace.modelData.urgent ? Theme.violetInk : workspace.hovered ? Theme.text : Theme.subtle
                        font.weight: workspace.current ? Font.Bold : Font.DemiBold
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Cava's spectrum while something plays (a note when cava is missing); hidden while paused.
        Capsule {
            shown: !!Media.playing
            padding: 14
            spacing: 10
            tip: Media.player ? [Media.player.trackTitle, Media.player.trackArtist].filter(t => t).join(" · ") + "\nMiddle-click to pause · scroll to skip" : ""
            onClicked: m => m.button === Qt.MiddleButton ? Media.player.togglePlaying() : Ui.toggleControl()
            onScrolled: step => step > 0 ? Media.player.previous() : Media.player.next()
            Row {
                visible: Ui.hasVisualizer
                spacing: 2
                Layout.preferredHeight: 18
                // Centred pills that stretch both ways, so a quiet passage is a row of short dashes.
                Repeater {
                    model: Media.bars
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property real modelData
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent?.verticalCenter
                        width: 3
                        height: 5 + modelData * 13
                        radius: Theme.corner(1.5)
                        color: Theme.violetBright
                        Behavior on height { NumberAnimation { duration: 60 } }
                    }
                }
            }
            Glyph { visible: !Ui.hasVisualizer; text: "󰎆"; color: Theme.violetBright }
        }
    }

    // ── Center ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // The focused window holds the exact center.
    RowLayout {
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        Capsule {
            id: title
            // The Wayland toplevel protocol knows the focused window from startup; nothing is
            // activated on an empty workspace. Show it on the bar of the screen it is on.
            readonly property var client: ToplevelManager.activeToplevel
            readonly property var entry: client ? DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(client.appId) : null
            // Most windows end their title with the app's name ("… — Mozilla Firefox"); the icon says that.
            readonly property string text: {
                const t = client?.title ?? "";
                const m = t.match(/^(.+)\s[—–-]\s(.+)$/);
                const names = [entry?.name, client?.appId].filter(n => n).map(n => n.toLowerCase());
                return m && names.some(n => m[2].toLowerCase().includes(n)) ? m[1] : t;
            }
            shown: !!client && client.activated && text !== "" && client.screens.some(s => s.name === bar.screen.name)
            interactive: false
            padding: 15
            spacing: 8
            tip: shown ? client.title : ""
            IconImage {
                visible: source !== ""
                implicitSize: 15
                source: title.entry?.icon ? Quickshell.iconPath(title.entry.icon, true) : ""
                asynchronous: true
            }
            Label {
                text: title.text
                // As wide as the room between the side capsules allows, up to a point.
                Layout.maximumWidth: Math.max(60, Math.min(300, 2 * Math.min(content.width / 2 - leftSide.x - leftSide.width, rightSide.x - content.width / 2) - 2 * Theme.gap - 60))
                color: Theme.textSoft
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Right ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    RowLayout {
        id: rightSide
        anchors { right: parent.right; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
        spacing: Theme.gap

        // A capture's timer counting down; click to cancel.
        Capsule {
            shown: Recorder.countdown > 0
            padding: 14
            tip: "Click to cancel"
            onClicked: Recorder.cancel()
            Glyph { text: "󰔛"; color: Theme.amber }
            Label { text: Recorder.countdown; font.weight: Font.Bold; color: Theme.amber }
        }

        // A screen recording and how long it has run; click to stop.
        Capsule {
            shown: Recorder.recording
            padding: 14
            tip: "Recording · click to stop (or Ctrl+Print)"
            color: hovered ? Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.3) : Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.16)
            border.color: Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.5)
            onClicked: Recorder.stop()
            Rectangle {
                implicitWidth: 9
                implicitHeight: 9
                radius: Theme.corner(4.5)
                color: Theme.rose
                SequentialAnimation on opacity {
                    running: Recorder.recording
                    loops: Animation.Infinite
                    NumberAnimation { to: 0.3; duration: 700; easing.type: Easing.InOutSine }
                    NumberAnimation { to: 1; duration: 700; easing.type: Easing.InOutSine }
                }
            }
            Label { text: Recorder.elapsedText; font.weight: Font.Bold; color: Theme.rose }
        }

        Capsule {
            shown: Devices.layouts.length > 1
            padding: 14
            tip: `${Devices.layoutName} · click to switch`
            onClicked: Devices.nextLayout()
            Glyph { text: "󰌌"; color: Theme.textSoft }
            Label { text: Devices.layoutCode; font.weight: Font.Bold; color: Theme.textSoft }
        }

        Capsule {
            shown: Sys.updates > 0
            padding: 12
            active: Ui.updatesOpen
            tip: Sys.updatesDetail
            onClicked: Ui.toggleUpdates()
            Glyph { text: "󰚰"; color: Ui.updatesOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.violetBright }
            Label { text: Sys.updates; color: Ui.updatesOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.violetBright }
        }

        Capsule {
            id: monitor
            padding: 12
            spacing: 12
            active: Ui.monitorOpen
            tip: `CPU ${Usage.cpu}% · Memory ${Usage.memoryDetail}`
            onClicked: m => m.button === Qt.RightButton ? Ui.run("kitty --class penumbra-monitor htop") : Ui.toggleMonitor()
            readonly property color tint: Ui.monitorOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.subtle
            Repeater {
                model: [{ name: "CPU", value: Usage.cpu }, { name: "RAM", value: Usage.memory }]
                delegate: RowLayout {
                    required property var modelData
                    spacing: 5
                    Label {
                        text: modelData.name
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                        font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                        font.letterSpacing: 0.6
                        color: Ui.monitorOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.dim
                    }
                    Label { text: modelData.value + "%"; color: monitor.tint }
                }
            }
        }

        Capsule {
            // nm-applet and blueman keep running for password and pairing prompts,
            // but the control center replaces their tray icons.
            readonly property var items: SystemTray.items.values.filter(i => !["nm-applet", "blueman"].includes(i.id))
            id: tray
            shown: items.length > 0
            interactive: false
            padding: 12
            spacing: 10
            Repeater {
                model: tray.items
                delegate: Item {
                    id: trayItem
                    required property var modelData
                    implicitWidth: 18
                    implicitHeight: 18
                    IconImage {
                        id: trayIcon
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        source: trayItem.modelData.icon
                        visible: false
                    }
                    // Tinted to match the bar; full color on hover.
                    MultiEffect {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        source: trayIcon
                        saturation: trayMouse.containsMouse ? 0 : -1
                        colorization: trayMouse.containsMouse ? 0 : 0.35
                        colorizationColor: Theme.violet
                        brightness: trayMouse.containsMouse ? 0 : 0.1
                        Behavior on saturation { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                        Behavior on colorization { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                    }
                    MouseArea {
                        id: trayMouse
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        acceptedButtons: Qt.LeftButton | Qt.RightButton | Qt.MiddleButton
                        onClicked: m => {
                            const item = trayItem.modelData;
                            if (m.button === Qt.MiddleButton)
                                item.secondaryActivate();
                            else if (m.button === Qt.RightButton || item.onlyMenu) {
                                if (item.hasMenu) {
                                    const p = trayItem.mapToItem(bar.contentItem, 0, trayItem.height + 14);
                                    item.display(bar, p.x, p.y);
                                }
                            } else
                                item.activate();
                        }
                        onWheel: w => trayItem.modelData.scroll(w.angleDelta.y, false)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Network, Bluetooth, audio, battery and notifications open the control center.
        Capsule {
            id: controls
            padding: 14
            spacing: 13
            active: Ui.controlOpen
            tip: `${Devices.networkName} · Bluetooth ${Devices.bluetoothName}` + (Devices.hasBattery ? `\n${Devices.batteryDetail}` : "")
            onClicked: m => m.button === Qt.RightButton ? Ui.run("pavucontrol") : Ui.toggleControl()
            onScrolled: step => Devices.adjust("volume", step > 0 ? "up" : "down")
            readonly property color tint: Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.textSoft

            Glyph { text: Devices.networkGlyph; color: Devices.networkName === "Offline" && !Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.dim : controls.tint }
            Glyph { text: Devices.bluetoothGlyph; color: !Devices.bluetoothOn && !Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.dim : controls.tint }
            RowLayout {
                spacing: 5
                Glyph { text: Devices.volumeGlyph; color: Devices.muted && !Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.dim : controls.tint }
                Label {
                    visible: !Devices.muted
                    text: Devices.volume + "%"
                    color: controls.tint
                }
            }
            RowLayout {
                visible: Devices.hasBattery
                spacing: 5
                Glyph { text: Devices.batteryGlyph; color: Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Devices.batteryColor }
                Label { text: Devices.batteryPercent + "%"; color: Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Devices.batteryColor }
            }
            Item {
                implicitWidth: bell.implicitWidth
                implicitHeight: bell.implicitHeight
                Glyph {
                    id: bell
                    text: Notifs.dnd ? "󰂛" : Notifs.count ? "󰂚" : "󰂜"
                    color: Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Notifs.dnd ? Theme.dim : Theme.textSoft
                }
                Rectangle {
                    visible: Notifs.count > 0 && !Notifs.dnd
                    width: 6; height: 6; radius: Theme.corner(3)
                    color: Ui.controlOpen ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.violetBright
                    anchors { right: bell.right; top: bell.top; rightMargin: -2; topMargin: 1 }
                }
            }
        }

        Capsule {
            padding: 14
            tip: "Session & power"
            onClicked: Ui.openSession()
            Glyph { text: "󰐥"; size: 16; color: Theme.violetBright }
        }
    }

    }

    // ── Tooltip ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    property Item tipTarget: null
    function updateTip(pos) {
        const target = pos ? capsuleAt(pos) : null;
        if (target !== tipTarget) {
            tipTarget = target;
            tipWindow.visible = false;
            tipDelay.restart();
        }
    }
    // Innermost item under the pointer that declares a tooltip.
    function capsuleAt(pos) {
        let found = null, item = content, p = pos;
        for (let child = item.childAt(p.x, p.y); child; child = item.childAt(p.x, p.y)) {
            p = item.mapToItem(child, p.x, p.y);
            if (child.tip !== undefined)
                found = child;
            item = child;
        }
        return found;
    }
    Timer {
        id: tipDelay
        interval: 550
        onTriggered: tipWindow.visible = !!bar.tipTarget && bar.tipTarget.tip !== ""
    }
    PopupWindow {
        id: tipWindow
        anchor.item: bar.tipTarget ?? bar.contentItem
        anchor.edges: Edges.Bottom
        anchor.gravity: Edges.Bottom
        anchor.margins.top: 8
        implicitWidth: tipText.implicitWidth + 24
        implicitHeight: tipText.implicitHeight + 16
        color: "transparent"
        Rectangle {
            anchors.fill: parent
            radius: Theme.radiusSmall
            color: Theme.glassStrong
            border.color: Theme.lineStrong
            Label {
                id: tipText
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                text: bar.tipTarget?.tip ?? ""
                color: Theme.text
                elide: Text.ElideNone
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/BluetoothPage.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell.Bluetooth

// Bluetooth devices: discover, pair, connect, disconnect and forget.
ColumnLayout {
    id: root

    property bool active: false
    readonly property var adapter: Devices.adapter
    readonly property bool powered: adapter?.enabled ?? false
    readonly property var all: Bluetooth.devices.values
    readonly property var paired: all.filter(d => d.paired || d.bonded)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.connected - a.connected) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    // Unnamed devices only advertise their address; leave them out.
    readonly property var available: all.filter(d => !d.paired && !d.bonded && d.deviceName && !/^([0-9A-F]{2}[:-]){5}[0-9A-F]{2}$/i.test(d.name))
        .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    property string expanded: ""
    property string pairingAddress: ""

    spacing: 12

    // Discover only while the page is on screen.
    function updateDiscovery() {
        if (adapter && adapter.enabled && adapter.discovering !== active)
            adapter.discovering = active;
    }
    onActiveChanged: { updateDiscovery(); if (!active) expanded = ""; }
    onPoweredChanged: updateDiscovery()

    function glyphFor(device) {
        const icon = device.icon ?? "";
        if (icon.includes("headset") || icon.includes("headphone"))
            return "󰋋";
        if (icon.includes("audio") || icon.includes("speaker"))
            return "󰓃";
        if (icon.includes("mouse"))
            return "󰍽";
        if (icon.includes("keyboard"))
            return "󰌌";
        if (icon.includes("gaming") || icon.includes("joystick"))
            return "󰊴";
        if (icon.includes("phone"))
            return "󰏲";
        if (icon.includes("computer"))
            return "󰌢";
        return "󰂯";
    }
    function statusFor(device) {
        if (device.state === BluetoothDeviceState.Connecting)
            return "Connecting…";
        if (device.state === BluetoothDeviceState.Disconnecting)
            return "Disconnecting…";
        if (device.connected)
            return device.batteryAvailable ? `Connected · ${Math.round(device.battery > 1 ? device.battery : device.battery * 100)}% battery` : "Connected";
        return "Not connected";
    }

    PageHeader {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        title: "Bluetooth"
        status: !root.adapter ? "No adapter" : !root.powered ? "Off" : root.adapter.discovering ? "On · searching for devices" : "On"
        showSwitch: !!root.adapter
        checked: root.powered
        onBack: Ui.controlPage = "main"
        onToggled: root.adapter.enabled = !root.adapter.enabled
    }

    component SectionTitle: Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
    }

    Flickable {
        id: scroller
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(contentHeight, 400)
        visible: root.powered
        contentHeight: sections.implicitHeight
        clip: true
        boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
        ScrollBar.vertical: ScrollBar { policy: scroller.contentHeight > scroller.height ? ScrollBar.AsNeeded : ScrollBar.AlwaysOff }

        ColumnLayout {
            id: sections
            width: scroller.width
            spacing: 4

            SectionTitle { visible: root.paired.length > 0; text: "My devices" }
            Repeater {
                model: root.paired
                delegate: ListRow {
                    id: row
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property var dev: modelData
                    readonly property bool changing: dev.state === BluetoothDeviceState.Connecting || dev.state === BluetoothDeviceState.Disconnecting
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: root.glyphFor(dev)
                    title: dev.name
                    subtitle: root.statusFor(dev)
                    highlighted: dev.connected
                    busy: changing
                    expanded: root.expanded === dev.address
                    onClicked: {
                        const open = root.expanded === dev.address;
                        root.expanded = open ? "" : dev.address;
                        // One click connects; the row stays open for Disconnect and Forget.
                        if (!open && !dev.connected && !changing)
                            dev.connect();
                    }
                    Flow {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        spacing: 6
                        PillButton {
                            text: row.dev.connected ? "Disconnect" : "Connect"
                            primary: !row.dev.connected
                            available: !row.changing
                            onClicked: row.dev.connected ? row.dev.disconnect() : row.dev.connect()
                        }
                        PillButton {
                            text: "Forget"
                            danger: true
                            onClicked: { root.expanded = ""; row.dev.forget(); }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            SectionTitle {
                Layout.topMargin: root.paired.length ? 10 : 2
                text: root.available.length ? "Available devices" : "Searching for devices…"
            }
            Repeater {
                model: root.available
                delegate: ListRow {
                    id: candidate
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property var dev: modelData
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: root.glyphFor(dev)
                    title: dev.name
                    subtitle: dev.pairing ? "Pairing…" : "Click to pair"
                    busy: dev.pairing
                    onClicked: {
                        if (dev.pairing)
                            return;
                        root.pairingAddress = dev.address;
                        dev.pair();
                    }
                    // Trust and connect as soon as pairing completes.
                    Connections {
                        target: candidate.dev
                        function onPairedChanged() {
                            if (candidate.dev.paired && root.pairingAddress === candidate.dev.address) {
                                root.pairingAddress = "";
                                candidate.dev.trusted = true;
                                candidate.dev.connect();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    ColumnLayout {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 6
        Layout.bottomMargin: 6
        visible: !root.powered
        spacing: 4
        Glyph { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: "󰂲"; size: 28; color: Theme.dim }
        Label {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
            text: !root.adapter ? "This computer has no Bluetooth adapter" : "Bluetooth is off"
            color: Theme.muted
        }
    }

    Label {
        Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignRight
        visible: !!root.adapter
        text: "Advanced Bluetooth settings"
        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
        color: advancedArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.muted
        MouseArea {
            id: advancedArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            anchors.margins: -4
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: { Ui.close(); Ui.run("blueman-manager"); }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/CalendarPanel.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts

// Month view under the clock.
Panel {
    id: root
    anchors { top: true; left: true }
    margins.left: Theme.edge - shadowRoom
    sheetWidth: 320
    open: Ui.calendarOpen
    onDismissed: Ui.dismiss()

    property date today: new Date()
    property int month: today.getMonth()
    property int year: today.getFullYear()
    onOpenChanged: if (open) { today = new Date(); month = today.getMonth(); year = today.getFullYear(); }
    function shift(delta) {
        const d = new Date(year, month + delta, 1);
        month = d.getMonth();
        year = d.getFullYear();
    }

    ColumnLayout {
        x: 18
        y: 18
        width: root.sheetWidth - 36
        spacing: 10

        Overline {
            text: Qt.formatDate(root.today, "dddd")
            color: Theme.violet
        }
        Label {
            text: Qt.formatDate(root.today, "d MMMM yyyy")
            font.family: Theme.display
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textHeading
            font.weight: Font.DemiBold
        }

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.topMargin: 4
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                text: Qt.formatDate(new Date(root.year, root.month, 1), "MMMM yyyy")
                font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                color: Theme.textSoft
            }
            Repeater {
                model: [{ glyph: "󰅁", delta: -1 }, { glyph: "󰅂", delta: 1 }]
                delegate: Rectangle {
                    required property var modelData
                    implicitWidth: 28
                    implicitHeight: 28
                    radius: Theme.corner(14)
                    color: navArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                    Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: modelData.glyph; color: Theme.textSoft }
                    MouseArea {
                        id: navArea
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: root.shift(modelData.delta)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        DayOfWeekRow {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            locale: grid.locale
            delegate: Label {
                required property string shortName
                text: shortName.slice(0, 2)
                horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                color: Theme.dim
            }
        }

        MonthGrid {
            id: grid
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.preferredHeight: 6 * 34 + 5 * 2
            month: root.month
            year: root.year
            locale: Qt.locale()
            spacing: 2
            delegate: Rectangle {
                required property var model
                readonly property bool isToday: model.today
                implicitWidth: 34
                implicitHeight: 34
                radius: Theme.corner(17)
                color: isToday ? Theme.violet : "transparent"
                Label {
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    text: model.day
                    font.weight: parent.isToday ? Font.Bold : Font.Normal
                    color: parent.isToday ? Theme.violetInk : model.month === root.month ? Theme.text : Theme.dim
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Capsule.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts

// A floating glass pill; children are laid out in a row.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    default property alias content: row.data
    property alias spacing: row.spacing
    property int padding: 13
    property string tip: ""
    property bool interactive: true
    property bool active: false
    readonly property bool hovered: mouse.containsMouse
    // Set instead of `visible`: the capsule fades and narrows away, and its neighbours glide.
    property bool shown: true
    property real reveal: shown ? 1 : 0
    Behavior on reveal { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
    visible: reveal > 0
    opacity: reveal
    Layout.preferredWidth: Math.round(implicitWidth * reveal)
    clip: reveal < 1

    signal clicked(var mouse)
    // One step per wheel notch: +1 up, -1 down. Touchpads send many small deltas, which add up
    // to a notch (120) before anything happens.
    signal scrolled(int step)
    property int wheelRest: 0

    implicitHeight: Theme.barHeight
    implicitWidth: row.implicitWidth + padding * 2
    radius: Theme.corner(height / 2)
    color: active ? Theme.violet : (interactive && hovered ? Theme.hover : Theme.glass)
    border.width: 1
    border.color: active ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.line
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
    Behavior on implicitWidth { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

    // Declared before the row so it sits underneath: children with their own
    // mouse handling (tray icons) receive their clicks, plain contents fall through.
    MouseArea {
        id: mouse
        anchors.fill: parent
        hoverEnabled: true
        acceptedButtons: Qt.LeftButton | Qt.RightButton | Qt.MiddleButton
        cursorShape: root.interactive ? Qt.PointingHandCursor : Qt.ArrowCursor
        onClicked: m => root.clicked(m)
        onWheel: w => {
            root.wheelRest += w.angleDelta.y;
            while (Math.abs(root.wheelRest) >= 120) {
                const step = root.wheelRest > 0 ? 1 : -1;
                root.wheelRest -= step * 120;
                root.scrolled(step);
            }
        }
        onExited: root.wheelRest = 0
    }

    // An active capsule takes the focused window's border: violet shading towards violetDeep.
    Rectangle {
        anchors.fill: parent
        radius: root.radius
        opacity: root.active ? 1 : 0
        visible: opacity > 0
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
        gradient: Gradient {
            orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
            GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violetBright }
            GradientStop { position: 1; color: Qt.tint(Theme.violet, Theme.alpha(Theme.violetDeep, 0.4)) }
        }
    }
    // The lit top edge the panels have, so the bar reads as the same glass.
    GlassEdge {
        radius: root.radius
        strength: root.active ? 0.55 : 0.2
    }

    RowLayout {
        id: row
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        spacing: 6
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Clock.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell

// Wall-clock time to the minute. Stands in for Quickshell's SystemClock, which during the hour
// repeated when DST ends schedules its next tick in the past and spins a 1ms timer, flooding the
// log. Checking every second also catches up straight away after suspend or a clock change.
Singleton {
    id: root

    property date date: new Date()

    Timer {
        interval: 1000
        running: true
        repeat: true
        onTriggered: {
            const now = new Date();
            if (Math.floor(now.getTime() / 60000) !== Math.floor(root.date.getTime() / 60000))
                root.date = now;
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/ControlCenter.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Effects
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Networking
import Quickshell.Services.Mpris
import Quickshell.Widgets

// Quick settings, sliders, media and notification history, under the status capsule.
Panel {
    id: root
    anchors { top: true; right: true }
    margins.right: Theme.edge - shadowRoom
    sheetWidth: 400
    open: Ui.controlOpen
    onDismissed: Ui.dismiss()

    readonly property var player: Mpris.players.values.find(p => p.isPlaying) ?? Mpris.players.values[0] ?? null
    property var expandedApps: []

    property string uptime: ""
    onOpenChanged: if (open) { uptimeFile.reload(); Sys.refreshBrightness(); }
    FileView {
        id: uptimeFile
        path: "/proc/uptime"
        onLoaded: {
            const s = Number(text().split(" ")[0]), h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.floor(s % 3600 / 60);
            root.uptime = h ? `up ${h} h ${m} min` : `up ${m} min`;
        }
    }

    // One page at a time: the main view, or Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, sound or shell settings.
    ColumnLayout {
        id: pages
        x: 18
        y: 18
        width: root.sheetWidth - 36
        spacing: 0

        Connections {
            target: Ui
            function onControlPageChanged() { pageIn.restart(); }
        }
        ParallelAnimation {
            id: pageIn
            NumberAnimation { target: pages; property: "opacity"; from: 0; to: 1; duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic }
            NumberAnimation { target: pages; property: "x"; from: Ui.controlPage === "main" ? -6 : 42; to: 18; duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo }
        }

        WifiPage {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: Ui.controlPage === "wifi"
            active: root.open && visible
        }

        BluetoothPage {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: Ui.controlPage === "bluetooth"
            active: root.open && visible
        }

        MixerPage {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: Ui.controlPage === "mixer"
            active: root.open && visible
        }

        SettingsPage {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: Ui.controlPage === "settings"
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: Ui.controlPage === "main"
            spacing: 14

            // Header: who, how long, and session shortcuts.
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 12
                Avatar { size: 42 }
                ColumnLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    spacing: 1
                    Label { text: Quickshell.env("USER"); font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge; font.weight: Font.DemiBold }
                    Label { text: root.uptime; font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; color: Theme.muted }
                }
                Repeater {
                    model: [
                        { glyph: "󰒓", tip: "Settings", run: () => Ui.controlPage = "settings" },
                        { glyph: "󰸉", tip: "Wallpaper", run: () => Ui.toggleWallpapers() },
                        { glyph: "󰌾", tip: "Lock", run: () => { Ui.close(); Ui.run("loginctl lock-session"); } },
                        { glyph: "󰐥", tip: "Session", run: () => Ui.openSession() }
                    ]
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property var modelData
                        implicitWidth: 36
                        implicitHeight: 36
                        radius: Theme.corner(18)
                        color: headerArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
                        border.color: Theme.line
                        Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: modelData.glyph; size: 15; color: Theme.violetBright }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: headerArea
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: modelData.run()
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            GridLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                columns: 2
                rowSpacing: 8
                columnSpacing: 8
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: Devices.networkGlyph
                    title: Devices.wifiDevice ? "Wi-Fi" : "Network"
                    subtitle: Devices.networkName
                    on: Devices.wifiDevice ? Networking.wifiEnabled : Devices.wired
                    hasMore: true
                    onToggled: Devices.wifiDevice ? Networking.wifiEnabled = !Networking.wifiEnabled : Ui.controlPage = "wifi"
                    onMore: Ui.controlPage = "wifi"
                }
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: Devices.bluetoothGlyph
                    title: "Bluetooth"
                    subtitle: Devices.bluetoothName
                    on: Devices.bluetoothOn
                    available: !!Devices.adapter
                    hasMore: true
                    onToggled: Devices.adapter.enabled = !Devices.adapter.enabled
                    onMore: Ui.controlPage = "bluetooth"
                }
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: Notifs.dnd ? "󰂛" : "󰂚"
                    title: "Do not disturb"
                    subtitle: Notifs.dnd ? "Only critical alerts" : "Off"
                    on: Notifs.dnd
                    onToggled: Notifs.dnd = !Notifs.dnd
                }
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: Ui.caffeine ? "󰅶" : "󰛊"
                    title: "Stay awake"
                    subtitle: Ui.caffeine ? "Idle lock paused" : "Off"
                    on: Ui.caffeine
                    onToggled: Ui.caffeine = !Ui.caffeine
                }
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: "󱩌"
                    title: "Night light"
                    subtitle: !Ui.hasNightLight ? "Needs hyprsunset" : Ui.nightLight ? `Warm · ${Ui.nightTemperature} K` : "Off"
                    on: Ui.nightLight
                    available: Ui.hasNightLight
                    onToggled: Ui.nightLight = !Ui.nightLight
                }
                Tile {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    glyph: "󰹑"
                    title: "Screenshot"
                    subtitle: "Picture or video"
                    onToggled: Ui.openShot()
                }
            }

            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 4
                RowLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    spacing: 2
                    SliderRow {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        glyph: Devices.volumeGlyph
                        value: Devices.volume
                        muted: Devices.muted
                        onMoved: v => Devices.setVolume(v)
                        onGlyphClicked: if (Devices.sink) Devices.sink.muted = !Devices.sink.muted
                    }
                    // Devices and per-app volume.
                    Rectangle {
                        implicitWidth: 26
                        implicitHeight: 36
                        radius: Theme.radiusSmall
                        color: mixerArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                        Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "󰅂"; color: Theme.muted }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: mixerArea
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: Ui.controlPage = "mixer"
                        }
                    }
                }
                SliderRow {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    Layout.rightMargin: 28
                    visible: !!Devices.source
                    glyph: Devices.micMuted ? "󰍭" : "󰍬"
                    value: Devices.source ? Math.round(Devices.source.volume * 100) : 0
                    muted: Devices.micMuted
                    onMoved: v => { Devices.source.muted = false; Devices.source.volume = v / 100; }
                    onGlyphClicked: Devices.source.muted = !Devices.source.muted
                }
                SliderRow {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    Layout.rightMargin: 28
                    visible: Sys.brightness >= 0
                    glyph: "󰃟"
                    value: Sys.brightness
                    onMoved: v => Sys.setBrightness(v)
                }
            }

            // Media: blurred cover art behind the track, controls and a seek bar.
            ClippingRectangle {
                id: media
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: !!root.player
                implicitHeight: mediaColumn.implicitHeight + 20
                radius: Theme.radius
                color: Theme.raised
                border.color: Theme.line

                Image {
                    id: artwork
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    source: root.player?.trackArtUrl ?? ""
                    sourceSize.width: 200
                    fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                    asynchronous: true
                    visible: false
                }
                MultiEffect {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    source: artwork
                    visible: artwork.status === Image.Ready
                    blurEnabled: true
                    blur: 1
                    blurMax: 48
                    saturation: 0.2
                    opacity: 0.45
                }
                Rectangle {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    gradient: Gradient {
                        orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
                        GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.alpha(Theme.surface, 0.55) }
                        GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.alpha(Theme.surface, 0.15) }
                    }
                }

                // MPRIS doesn't announce position changes; ask while it's on screen.
                Timer {
                    interval: 1000
                    repeat: true
                    running: root.open && !!root.player?.isPlaying
                    onTriggered: root.player.positionChanged()
                }
                ColumnLayout {
                    id: mediaColumn
                    anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 10 }
                    spacing: 8
                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        spacing: 12
                        ClippingRectangle {
                            implicitWidth: 56
                            implicitHeight: 56
                            radius: Theme.corner(12)
                            color: Theme.hover
                            Image {
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                source: root.player?.trackArtUrl ?? ""
                                fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                                asynchronous: true
                            }
                            Glyph {
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                visible: !root.player?.trackArtUrl
                                text: "󰎆"
                                size: 22
                                color: Theme.violetBright
                            }
                        }
                        ColumnLayout {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            spacing: 2
                            Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: root.player?.trackTitle || Media.appName(root.player); font.weight: Font.DemiBold }
                            RowLayout {
                                Layout.fillWidth: true
                                spacing: 6
                                // A live stream has nothing to seek through; say so instead.
                                Rectangle {
                                    visible: Media.isLive(root.player)
                                    implicitWidth: liveLabel.implicitWidth + 12
                                    implicitHeight: 16
                                    radius: Theme.corner(8)
                                    color: Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.18)
                                    border.color: Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.5)
                                    Label {
                                        id: liveLabel
                                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                                        text: "LIVE"
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                                        font.weight: Font.Bold
                                        font.letterSpacing: 0.8
                                        color: Theme.rose
                                    }
                                }
                                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: root.player?.trackArtist || Media.appName(root.player); font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; color: Theme.textSoft }
                            }
                        }
                        Repeater {
                            model: [
                                { glyph: "󰒮", run: () => root.player.previous(), ok: root.player?.canGoPrevious },
                                { glyph: root.player?.isPlaying ? "󰏤" : "󰐊", run: () => root.player.togglePlaying(), ok: root.player?.canTogglePlaying, main: true },
                                { glyph: "󰒭", run: () => root.player.next(), ok: root.player?.canGoNext }
                            ]
                            delegate: Rectangle {
                                required property var modelData
                                implicitWidth: modelData.main ? 38 : 30
                                implicitHeight: implicitWidth
                                radius: Theme.corner(implicitWidth / 2)
                                opacity: modelData.ok ? 1 : 0.4
                                color: modelData.main ? Theme.violet : mediaArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                                Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: modelData.glyph; size: 16; color: modelData.main ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text }
                                MouseArea {
                                    id: mediaArea
                                    anchors.fill: parent
                                    hoverEnabled: true
                                    enabled: !!modelData.ok
                                    cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                    onClicked: modelData.run()
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        visible: Media.hasLength(root.player)
                        spacing: 10
                        Label {
                            text: Media.time(root.player?.position ?? 0)
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                            color: Theme.textSoft
                        }
                        Item {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            implicitHeight: 14
                            readonly property real progress: root.player?.length > 0 ? Math.min(1, root.player.position / root.player.length) : 0
                            Rectangle {
                                anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                                width: parent.width
                                height: seekArea.containsMouse ? 6 : 4
                                radius: Theme.corner(height / 2)
                                color: Qt.rgba(1, 1, 1, 0.12)
                                Behavior on height { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                                Rectangle {
                                    width: parent.width * parent.parent.progress
                                    height: parent.height
                                    radius: Theme.corner(height / 2)
                                    color: Theme.violet
                                }
                            }
                            MouseArea {
                                id: seekArea
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                enabled: root.player?.canSeek ?? false
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                function seek(x) {
                                    root.player.position = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x / width)) * root.player.length;
                                }
                                onPressed: m => seek(m.x)
                                onPositionChanged: m => { if (pressed) seek(m.x); }
                            }
                        }
                        Label {
                            text: Media.time(root.player?.length ?? 0)
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                            color: Theme.textSoft
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Notifications
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.topMargin: 2
                Overline {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: Notifs.count ? `Notifications · ${Notifs.count}` : "Notifications"
                }
                Label {
                    visible: Notifs.count > 0
                    text: "Clear all"
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: clearArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.violet
                    MouseArea {
                        id: clearArea
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        anchors.margins: -6
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: Notifs.clear()
                    }
                }
            }

            // Grouped by app; each group shows its newest notification until expanded.
            ListView {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.preferredHeight: Notifs.count ? Math.min(contentHeight, 360) : 0
                visible: Notifs.count > 0
                clip: true
                spacing: 12
                model: Notifs.groups
                boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
                delegate: ColumnLayout {
                    id: group
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property bool expanded: root.expandedApps.includes(modelData.app)
                    width: ListView.view.width
                    spacing: 6

                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        Layout.leftMargin: 4
                        Layout.rightMargin: 4
                        Label {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            text: group.modelData.app + (group.modelData.items.length > 1 ? ` · ${group.modelData.items.length}` : "")
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                            font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                            color: Theme.textSoft
                        }
                        Label {
                            visible: group.modelData.items.length > 1
                            text: group.expanded ? "Show less" : "Show all"
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                            color: expandArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.violet
                            MouseArea {
                                id: expandArea
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                anchors.margins: -6
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: root.expandedApps = group.expanded
                                    ? root.expandedApps.filter(a => a !== group.modelData.app)
                                    : [...root.expandedApps, group.modelData.app]
                            }
                        }
                        Glyph {
                            Layout.leftMargin: 8
                            text: "󰎟"
                            size: 13
                            color: clearGroup.containsMouse ? Theme.text : Theme.dim
                            MouseArea {
                                id: clearGroup
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                anchors.margins: -6
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: Notifs.clearApp(group.modelData.app)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Repeater {
                        model: group.expanded ? group.modelData.items : group.modelData.items.slice(0, 1)
                        delegate: NotificationCard {
                            required property var modelData
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            notification: modelData
                            compact: true
                        }
                    }
                }
                displaced: Transition { NumberAnimation { property: "y"; duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
            }

            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.bottomMargin: 6
                visible: Notifs.count === 0
                spacing: 4
                Glyph { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: "󰂜"; size: 26; color: Theme.dim }
                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter; text: "All caught up"; color: Theme.muted }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/DawnScene.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Shapes
import "dawn/layers.js" as Dawn

// Dawn: stars twinkle, the glow breathes, light creeps along the planet's rim, a moonlet rides
// the ring and a shooting star crosses now and then.
WallpaperScene {
    id: root
    folder: "dawn"
    layers: Dawn.layers
    width: Dawn.width
    height: Dawn.height

    // Ring geometry from assets/wallpaper-dawn.svg.
    readonly property point planet: Qt.point(820, 1010)
    readonly property real ringX: 760
    readonly property real ringY: 172
    readonly property real ringTilt: 16 * Math.PI / 180

    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: root.clean ? "sky-clean" : "sky"; z: 0 }
    // Rendered brighter than the still image, so it can swell and ebb around it.
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "glow"
        z: 1
        opacity: (1 + 0.3 * root.wave(11)) / info.boost
    }
    Stars {
        scene: root
        stars: Dawn.stars
        z: 2
    }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "back"; z: 3 }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "planet"; z: 5 }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "rim"
        z: 6
        opacity: 0.85 + 0.15 * root.wave(7, 1)
        pivot: root.planet
        angle: 3 * root.wave(23)
    }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "front"; z: 7 }

    // A moonlet on the ring: behind the planet on the far side, brightest where the ring is lit.
    Item {
        readonly property real angle: 2 * Math.PI * root.t / 80 + 2.2
        readonly property real along: root.ringX * Math.cos(angle)
        readonly property real across: root.ringY * Math.sin(angle)
        x: root.planet.x + along * Math.cos(root.ringTilt) - across * Math.sin(root.ringTilt)
        y: root.planet.y + along * Math.sin(root.ringTilt) + across * Math.cos(root.ringTilt)
        z: across > 0 ? 8 : 4
        scale: 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(angle)
        opacity: Math.max(0.2, 1 - Math.abs(Math.cos(angle)))

        Shape {
            x: -24
            y: -24
            width: 48
            height: 48
            preferredRendererType: Shape.CurveRenderer
            ShapePath {
                strokeColor: "transparent"
                fillGradient: RadialGradient {
                    centerX: 24; centerY: 24; focalX: 24; focalY: 24
                    centerRadius: 24
                    GradientStop { position: 0; color: Qt.rgba(243 / 255, 180 / 255, 214 / 255, 0.55) }
                    GradientStop { position: 1; color: "transparent" }
                }
                PathAngleArc { centerX: 24; centerY: 24; radiusX: 24; radiusY: 24; sweepAngle: 360 }
            }
        }
        Rectangle {
            x: -2.5
            y: -2.5
            width: 5
            height: 5
            radius: 2.5
            color: "#ffe3f1"
        }
    }

    // Shooting stars cross the open sky to the right.
    Meteor {
        z: 9
        running: root.running
        area: Qt.rect(1700, 80, 700, 300)
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Devices.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Bluetooth
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Networking
import Quickshell.Services.Pipewire
import Quickshell.Services.UPower

// Live device state, with the glyphs the bar and control center show for it.
Singleton {
    id: root

    PwObjectTracker {
        objects: [Pipewire.defaultAudioSink, Pipewire.defaultAudioSource]
    }
    readonly property var sink: Pipewire.defaultAudioSink?.audio ?? null
    readonly property var source: Pipewire.defaultAudioSource?.audio ?? null
    readonly property int volume: sink ? Math.round(sink.volume * 100) : 0
    readonly property bool muted: sink?.muted ?? true
    readonly property string volumeGlyph: muted ? "󰝟" : volume > 60 ? "󰕾" : volume > 25 ? "󰖀" : "󰕿"
    readonly property bool micMuted: source?.muted ?? true
    function setVolume(percent) {
        if (!sink)
            return;
        sink.muted = false;
        sink.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent)) / 100;
    }

    // Volume and microphone keys: 5% steps between 0 and 100% ("up", "down"), "mute" toggles,
    // "current" only shows the popup. Held keys repeat faster than PipeWire reports back, so each
    // step starts from the last one asked for while it is recent.
    property var targets: ({})
    function adjust(control, action) {
        const node = control === "microphone" ? source : sink;
        if (!node)
            return false;
        const recent = targets[control] && Date.now() - targets[control].at < 600;
        let volume = recent ? targets[control].volume : node.volume;
        let muted = node.muted;
        if (action === "mute") {
            muted = !muted;
            node.muted = muted;
        } else if (action === "up" || action === "down") {
            volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.round(volume * 20 + (action === "up" ? 1 : -1)) / 20));
            node.volume = volume;
        }
        targets = Object.assign({}, targets, { [control]: { volume, at: Date.now() } });
        Ui.showOsd(control, Math.round(volume * 100), muted);
        return true;
    }

    readonly property var wifiDevice: Networking.devices.values.find(d => d.type === DeviceType.Wifi) ?? null
    readonly property var wiredDevice: Networking.devices.values.find(d => d.type === DeviceType.Wired) ?? null
    readonly property var wifiNetwork: wifiDevice?.networks.values.find(n => n.connected) ?? null
    readonly property bool wired: wiredDevice?.connected ?? false
    readonly property string networkName: wifiNetwork ? wifiNetwork.name : wired ? "Wired" : "Offline"
    // Signal strength as 0–1, whichever scale the backend reports.
    function strength(network) {
        return network.signalStrength > 1 ? network.signalStrength / 100 : network.signalStrength;
    }
    function signalGlyph(network) {
        const s = strength(network);
        return s > 0.75 ? "󰤨" : s > 0.5 ? "󰤥" : s > 0.25 ? "󰤢" : "󰤟";
    }
    readonly property string networkGlyph: {
        if (wifiNetwork)
            return signalGlyph(wifiNetwork);
        return wired ? "󰈀" : Networking.wifiEnabled ? "󰤯" : "󰤭";
    }

    readonly property var adapter: Bluetooth.defaultAdapter
    readonly property bool bluetoothOn: adapter?.enabled ?? false
    readonly property var bluetoothConnected: Bluetooth.devices.values.filter(d => d.connected)
    readonly property string bluetoothGlyph: !adapter ? "󰂲" : !bluetoothOn ? "󰂲" : bluetoothConnected.length ? "󰂱" : "󰂯"
    readonly property string bluetoothName: !adapter ? "No adapter" : !bluetoothOn ? "Off" : bluetoothConnected.length ? bluetoothConnected[0].name : "On"

    readonly property var battery: UPower.displayDevice
    readonly property bool hasBattery: battery?.isPresent && battery.type === UPowerDeviceType.Battery
    readonly property int batteryPercent: !hasBattery ? 0 : Math.round(battery.percentage > 1 ? battery.percentage : battery.percentage * 100)
    readonly property bool charging: hasBattery && (battery.state === UPowerDeviceState.Charging || battery.state === UPowerDeviceState.FullyCharged || battery.state === UPowerDeviceState.PendingCharge)
    readonly property string batteryGlyph: charging ? "󰂄" : ["󰁺", "󰁼", "󰁾", "󰂀", "󰁹"][Math.min(4, Math.floor(batteryPercent / 20))]
    readonly property color batteryColor: charging ? Theme.green : batteryPercent <= 10 ? Theme.rose : batteryPercent <= 25 ? Theme.amber : Theme.textSoft
    readonly property string batteryDetail: {
        if (!hasBattery)
            return "";
        const seconds = charging ? battery.timeToFull : battery.timeToEmpty;
        if (!seconds)
            return charging ? "Charging" : "On battery";
        const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600), m = Math.round(seconds % 3600 / 60);
        return `${h ? h + " h " : ""}${m} min ${charging ? "until full" : "left"}`;
    }

    // Keyboard layouts of the main keyboard, e.g. ["us", "fr"], and the one in use.
    property var layouts: []
    property int layoutIndex: 0
    property string layoutName: ""
    readonly property string layoutCode: (layouts[layoutIndex] ?? "").toUpperCase()
    function nextLayout() {
        Quickshell.execDetached(["hyprctl", "switchxkblayout", "all", "next"]);
    }
    Process {
        id: keyboards
        running: true
        command: ["hyprctl", "devices", "-j"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                try {
                    const all = JSON.parse(text).keyboards;
                    const main = all.find(k => k.main) ?? all[all.length - 1];
                    root.layouts = main.layout.split(",").map(l => l.trim()).filter(l => l);
                    root.layoutIndex = main.active_layout_index ?? 0;
                    root.layoutName = main.active_keymap ?? "";
                } catch (e) {
                    root.layouts = [];
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Connections {
        target: Hyprland
        function onRawEvent(event) {
            if (event.name === "activelayout" || event.name === "configreloaded")
                keyboards.running = true;
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/EclipseScene.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Shapes
import "eclipse/layers.js" as Eclipse

// Eclipse: the nebula drifts, stars twinkle and glint, the corona's streamers sway and shimmer,
// the diamond-ring flare breathes, two backlit moons circle the eclipse, an aurora ripples along
// the near world's horizon, a satellite blinks across the sky and the odd shooting star falls.
WallpaperScene {
    id: root
    folder: "eclipse"
    layers: Eclipse.layers
    width: Eclipse.width
    height: Eclipse.height

    // Geometry from assets/wallpaper-eclipse.svg.
    readonly property point eclipse: Qt.point(1660, 600)
    readonly property real orbitTilt: -18 * Math.PI / 180
    readonly property point world: Qt.point(380, 2950)
    readonly property real worldRadius: 1680

    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: root.clean ? "sky-clean" : "sky"; z: 0 }
    // Zoomed a touch so its cropped edges never come into view as it turns.
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "nebula"
        z: 1
        opacity: (1 + 0.25 * root.wave(17)) / info.boost
        zoom: 1.05 + 0.02 * root.wave(60)
        angle: 0.8 * root.wave(80)
    }
    Stars {
        scene: root
        stars: Eclipse.stars
        color: "#ddd0f5"
        z: 2
    }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "halo"
        z: 3
        pivot: root.eclipse
        opacity: (1 + 0.2 * root.wave(9)) / info.boost
        zoom: 1.02 + 0.02 * root.wave(9)
    }
    // Two sets of streamers swaying against each other read as a living corona.
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "corona"
        z: 4
        pivot: root.eclipse
        angle: 2.5 * root.wave(47)
        opacity: 0.85 + 0.15 * root.wave(6.3)
    }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "wisps"
        z: 5
        pivot: root.eclipse
        angle: -3.5 * root.wave(37, 1)
        zoom: 1 + 0.04 * root.wave(11)
        opacity: 0.7 + 0.3 * root.wave(5.1, 2)
    }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "orbits"; z: 6 }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "body"; z: 8 }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "limb"
        z: 9
        opacity: 0.85 + 0.15 * root.wave(4.3)
    }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "bead"
        z: 10
        opacity: 0.8 + 0.2 * root.wave(5.7)
        zoom: 0.85 + 0.25 * (0.5 + 0.5 * root.wave(5.7))
    }
    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "near"; z: 11 }

    // Moons on the dotted orbits, lit from behind: a rim on the side facing the eclipse that
    // closes into a ring as they cross it. On the far side they pass behind the eclipsing body.
    component Moon: Item {
        id: moon
        required property real orbitX
        required property real orbitY
        required property real period
        required property real phase
        required property real radius
        readonly property real angle: 2 * Math.PI * root.t / period + phase
        readonly property real along: orbitX * Math.cos(angle)
        readonly property real across: orbitY * Math.sin(angle)
        readonly property real toward: Math.atan2(root.eclipse.y - y, root.eclipse.x - x) * 180 / Math.PI
        readonly property real sweep: 110 + 250 * Math.max(0, 1 - Math.hypot(root.eclipse.x - x, root.eclipse.y - y) / 520)
        x: root.eclipse.x + along * Math.cos(root.orbitTilt) - across * Math.sin(root.orbitTilt)
        y: root.eclipse.y + along * Math.sin(root.orbitTilt) + across * Math.cos(root.orbitTilt)
        z: across > 0 ? 12 : 7

        Shape {
            preferredRendererType: Shape.CurveRenderer
            ShapePath {
                fillColor: "#0b0911"
                strokeColor: "#2a1d3a"
                strokeWidth: 1
                PathAngleArc { radiusX: moon.radius; radiusY: moon.radius; sweepAngle: 360 }
            }
            ShapePath {
                fillColor: "transparent"
                strokeColor: "#efe4ff"
                strokeWidth: 1.6
                capStyle: ShapePath.RoundCap
                PathAngleArc {
                    radiusX: moon.radius
                    radiusY: moon.radius
                    startAngle: moon.toward - moon.sweep / 2
                    sweepAngle: moon.sweep
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Moon { orbitX: 360; orbitY: 92; period: 55; phase: 0.6; radius: 13 }
    Moon { orbitX: 560; orbitY: 148; period: 95; phase: 3.4; radius: 8 }

    // A satellite crosses in a minute, then the sky stays empty for a while.
    Item {
        readonly property real progress: root.t % 140 / 60
        visible: progress < 1
        z: 13
        x: -40 + 2680 * progress
        y: 230 - 170 * progress
        Rectangle {
            x: -1.3
            y: -1.3
            width: 2.6
            height: 2.6
            radius: 1.3
            color: "#d8cbe8"
            opacity: 0.6
        }
        Rectangle {
            x: -2.5
            y: -2.5
            width: 5
            height: 5
            radius: 2.5
            color: "#ef8fa4"
            visible: root.t % 1.6 < 0.1
        }
    }

    Meteor {
        z: 14
        running: root.running
        area: Qt.rect(700, 60, 800, 250)
        heading: 150
    }

    SceneLayer { scene: root; name: "world"; z: 15 }
    SceneLayer {
        scene: root
        name: "air"
        z: 16
        opacity: 0.8 + 0.2 * root.wave(8)
    }

    // Aurora along the near world's horizon (eclipse/aurora.frag).
    ShaderEffect {
        z: 17
        y: 640
        width: 1600
        height: 960
        property real time: root.t
        property size size: Qt.size(width, height)
        property point center: Qt.point(root.world.x - x, root.world.y - y)
        property real radius: root.worldRadius
        // From the left edge of the scene to where the horizon leaves its bottom edge.
        property real arcFrom: -0.21
        property real arcTo: 0.545
        fragmentShader: "eclipse/aurora.frag.qsb"
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/GlassEdge.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Effects

// The lit top edge of a glass surface: a hairline bright at the top that fades out halfway down,
// over the surface's own border. It is what makes frosted glass read as glass. Fill the surface.
Item {
    id: root

    property real radius: 0
    property real strength: 0.22

    anchors.fill: parent

    Rectangle {
        id: light
        anchors.fill: parent
        visible: false
        gradient: Gradient {
            GradientStop { position: 0; color: Qt.rgba(1, 1, 1, root.strength) }
            GradientStop { position: 0.5; color: Qt.rgba(1, 1, 1, 0) }
        }
    }
    Rectangle {
        id: ring
        anchors.fill: parent
        visible: false
        layer.enabled: true
        radius: root.radius
        color: "transparent"
        border.width: 1
        border.color: "white"
    }
    MultiEffect {
        anchors.fill: parent
        source: light
        maskEnabled: true
        maskSource: ring
        maskThresholdMin: 0.25
        maskSpreadAtMin: 0.25
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Glyph.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// A Nerd Font icon.
Text {
    id: root
    property real size: 14
    color: Theme.textSoft
    font.family: Theme.mono
    font.pixelSize: size
    verticalAlignment: Text.AlignVCenter
    horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

    // The non-Mono Nerd Font draws most icons wider than their one-cell advance, hanging off to
    // the right, so centring the advance leaves the icon off-centre. Pad the short side so the
    // ink, not the advance, sits in the middle.
    TextMetrics {
        id: ink
        font: root.font
        text: root.text
    }
    readonly property real inkOffset: ink.tightBoundingRect.x + ink.tightBoundingRect.width / 2 - ink.advanceWidth / 2
    leftPadding: Math.max(0, -2 * inkOffset)
    rightPadding: Math.max(0, 2 * inkOffset)
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Graph.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Shapes

// A history graph, newest sample at the right edge: a line over a fill that fades downwards.
// While live, each new sample glides in over one sampling interval instead of jumping.
Item {
    id: root

    property var values: []
    // The value at the top edge; 0 scales to the largest value shown, but never below floor.
    property real maximum: 100
    property real floor: 1
    property color color: Theme.violet
    property real fillOpacity: 0.3
    property bool live: true

    readonly property real ceiling: maximum > 0 ? maximum : Math.max(floor, 1.15 * Math.max(0, ...values))
    // One point more than fits: the oldest sits just off the left edge, so the glide never
    // shows a gap there.
    readonly property real step: width / (Usage.historyLength - 2)
    // Inset by half the line, so a line along the top or bottom is drawn whole.
    readonly property var points: values.map((v, i) => Qt.point(width - (values.length - 1 - i) * step, 1 + (height - 2) * (1 - Math.max(0, Math.min(v, ceiling)) / ceiling)))

    clip: true
    onValuesChanged: if (live) glide.restart()

    Shape {
        id: shape
        width: root.width
        height: root.height
        visible: root.points.length > 1
        preferredRendererType: Shape.CurveRenderer

        NumberAnimation on x {
            id: glide
            running: false
            from: root.step
            to: 0
            duration: Usage.interval
        }

        ShapePath {
            strokeColor: "transparent"
            fillGradient: LinearGradient {
                y1: 0
                y2: root.height
                GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.alpha(root.color, root.fillOpacity) }
                GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.alpha(root.color, 0) }
            }
            PathPolyline {
                path: root.points.length ? [Qt.point(root.points[0].x, root.height)].concat(root.points, [Qt.point(root.width, root.height)]) : []
            }
        }
        ShapePath {
            strokeColor: root.color
            strokeWidth: 1.5
            fillColor: "transparent"
            joinStyle: ShapePath.RoundJoin
            capStyle: ShapePath.RoundCap
            PathPolyline { path: root.points }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/HotCorners.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Wayland

// Push the pointer into the top-right corner for the control center. A short dwell keeps a
// passing pointer from opening it; the corner rests while a window is fullscreen, or when it's
// turned off in settings.
Variants {
    model: Quickshell.screens

    delegate: PanelWindow {
        id: corner
        required property var modelData
        screen: modelData
        visible: Settings.hotCorner && !(Hyprland.monitorFor(modelData)?.activeWorkspace?.hasFullscreen ?? false)
        anchors { top: true; right: true }
        exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
        implicitWidth: 3
        implicitHeight: 3
        color: "transparent"
        WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
        // Not penumbra-*: nothing here to blur.
        WlrLayershell.namespace: "hot-corner"

        MouseArea {
            anchors.fill: parent
            hoverEnabled: true
            onEntered: dwell.restart()
            onExited: dwell.stop()
        }
        Timer {
            id: dwell
            interval: 160
            onTriggered: Ui.toggleControl()
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Launcher.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Wayland
import Quickshell.Widgets
import "symbols.js" as Symbols

// Applications, clipboard history, emoji and symbols, over a dimmed screen. Type to search; `>` runs
// a command, a sum (or `=`) is worked out and copied; Tab moves to the next mode.
PanelWindow {
    id: root
    screen: Ui.screen
    anchors { top: true; bottom: true; left: true; right: true }
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    visible: Ui.launcherOpen || card.opacity > 0
    color: "transparent"
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-launcher"
    WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: Ui.launcherOpen ? WlrKeyboardFocus.Exclusive : WlrKeyboardFocus.None

    readonly property var modes: [
        { mode: "apps", label: "Apps", glyph: "󰀻" },
        { mode: "clipboard", label: "Clipboard", glyph: "󰅍" },
        { mode: "emoji", label: "Emoji", glyph: "󰇵" },
        { mode: "symbols", label: "Symbols", glyph: "󰒠" }
    ]
    readonly property bool clipboard: Ui.launcherMode === "clipboard"
    // Emoji and symbols are a grid of characters.
    readonly property bool characters: Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" || Ui.launcherMode === "symbols"
    readonly property var apps: DesktopEntries.applications.values.filter(e => !e.noDisplay)
    readonly property var symbols: Symbols.groups.reduce((all, g) => all.concat(g[1].map(s => ({ char: s[0], name: s[1], group: g[0] }))), [])
    property var emoji: []
    property var results: []
    property var clips: []
    property var usage: ({}) // desktop entry id → launches
    property var picks: ({}) // emoji or symbol → times picked
    property var pointer: null // where the mouse last was, in window coordinates

    Connections {
        target: Ui
        function onLauncherOpenChanged() {
            if (Ui.launcherOpen) {
                root.pointer = null;
                root.reset();
            }
        }
        function onLauncherModeChanged() { if (Ui.launcherOpen) root.reset(); }
    }
    onAppsChanged: refresh()

    function reset() {
        search.text = "";
        search.forceActiveFocus();
        if (clipboard)
            clipList.running = true;
        refresh();
    }
```
