```qml
    // Hover follows the mouse only when it moves: opening under a still cursor, the card growing or
    // results shifting beneath it leave the selection alone. Positions are the pointer's own window
    // coordinates, never mapped back through the scaling card, so a still cursor reads the same each
    // time. The first sighting only records where it is.
    function pointerAt(x, y) {
        if (pointer === null)
            pointer = Qt.point(x, y);
        if (Math.hypot(x - pointer.x, y - pointer.y) < 2)
            return;
        pointer = Qt.point(x, y);
        const list = characters ? grid : view;
        const p = list.mapFromItem(null, x, y);
        if (!list.contains(p))
            return;
        const i = list.indexAt(p.x + list.contentX, p.y + list.contentY);
        if (i >= 0)
            list.currentIndex = i;
    }

    // ── Results ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function refresh() {
        const text = search.text, q = text.trim().toLowerCase();
        let list = [];
        if (characters) {
            const terms = q.split(/\s+/).filter(t => t);
            const pool = Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" ? emoji : symbols;
            const scored = pool.map((c, i) => ({ c, i, score: charScore(c, terms) })).filter(s => s.score > 0);
            scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.i - b.i);
            list = scored.map(s => ({ kind: "char", char: s.c }));
        } else if (clipboard) {
            list = clips.filter(c => !q || c.text.toLowerCase().includes(q)).map(c => ({ kind: "clip", clip: c }));
        } else if (text.startsWith(">")) {
            if (text.slice(1).trim())
                list = [{ kind: "run", command: text.slice(1).trim() }];
        } else {
            const sum = calculate(text);
            if (sum !== null)
                list.push({ kind: "calc", value: sum });
            const scored = apps.map(e => ({ entry: e, score: score(e, q) })).filter(s => s.score > 0);
            scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.entry.name.localeCompare(b.entry.name));
            list = list.concat(scored.slice(0, 60).map(s => ({ kind: "app", entry: s.entry })));
            // Nothing runs unless asked for with `>`: a typo like "reboot" must not reboot.
        }
        results = list;
        view.currentIndex = 0;
        view.positionViewAtBeginning();
        grid.currentIndex = 0;
        grid.positionViewAtBeginning();
    }

    // Every term in the name or group; names that start with the first term come first, and
    // often-picked characters rise. Everything matches no terms, most picked first.
    function charScore(c, terms) {
        const boost = Math.min(40, picks[c.char] ?? 0);
        if (!terms.length)
            return 1 + boost;
        const name = c.name.toLowerCase(), all = name + " " + c.group.toLowerCase();
        if (!terms.every(t => all.includes(t)))
            return 0;
        const first = terms[0];
        const s = name === terms.join(" ") ? 100 : name.startsWith(first) ? 60 : name.split(/[\s:\-]+/).some(w => w.startsWith(first)) ? 40 : 10;
        return s + boost;
    }

    // Name matches first, then initials and scattered letters, then descriptions; often-launched
    // apps rise a little. Everything matches an empty query.
    function score(entry, q) {
        const boost = Math.min(15, usage[entry.id] ?? 0);
        if (!q)
            return 1 + boost;
        const name = entry.name.toLowerCase();
        let s = 0;
        if (name === q)
            s = 120;
        else if (name.startsWith(q))
            s = 100;
        else if (name.split(/[\s\-_.]+/).some(w => w.startsWith(q)))
            s = 85;
        else if (name.includes(q))
            s = 70;
        else if (fuzzy(name, q))
            s = 30 + fuzzy(name, q);
        else if ([entry.genericName, entry.keywords.join(" "), entry.comment, entry.id].join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(q))
            s = 25;
        return s ? s + boost : 0;
    }
    // Letters of q in order inside name; fewer gaps score higher (1–30), no match is 0.
    function fuzzy(name, q) {
        let at = -1, gaps = 0;
        for (const c of q) {
            const next = name.indexOf(c, at + 1);
            if (next < 0)
                return 0;
            gaps += next - at - 1;
            at = next;
        }
        return Math.max(1, 30 - gaps);
    }

    // Arithmetic and Math functions only; anything else is not a sum.
    readonly property var mathNames: ({
        pi: "Math.PI", e: "Math.E", sqrt: "Math.sqrt", cbrt: "Math.cbrt", abs: "Math.abs", round: "Math.round",
        floor: "Math.floor", ceil: "Math.ceil", sin: "Math.sin", cos: "Math.cos", tan: "Math.tan",
        asin: "Math.asin", acos: "Math.acos", atan: "Math.atan", ln: "Math.log", log: "Math.log10",
        exp: "Math.exp", min: "Math.min", max: "Math.max", pow: "Math.pow"
    })
    function calculate(text) {
        const forced = text.startsWith("=");
        let expr = text.replace(/^=/, "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!expr || !/^[\d\s+\-*/%^().,a-z]+$/.test(expr) || !/\d|pi/.test(expr))
            return null;
        // A sum needs an operator or a function; a bare number is a search.
        if (!forced && !/[+\-*/%^]|[a-z]+\s*\(/.test(expr.replace(/^-/, "")))
            return null;
        // A comma is a decimal point (3,5), unless it separates a function's arguments.
        if (!/[a-z]\s*\(/.test(expr))
            expr = expr.replace(/(\d),(\d)/g, "$1.$2");
        let unknown = false;
        expr = expr.replace(/\^/g, "**")
            .replace(/[a-z]+/g, word => root.mathNames[word] ?? (unknown = true, word));
        if (unknown)
            return null;
        try {
            const value = Function(`"use strict"; return (${expr});`)();
            return typeof value === "number" && isFinite(value) ? Number(value.toPrecision(12)) : null;
        } catch (e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    // ── Actions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function activate(index, alternate) {
        const item = results[index];
        if (!item)
            return;
        Ui.launcherOpen = false;
        if (item.kind === "app") {
            launch(item.entry);
        } else if (item.kind === "run") {
            Ui.spawn(alternate ? ["kitty", "--hold", "bash", "-c", item.command] : ["bash", "-c", item.command]);
        } else if (item.kind === "calc") {
            Ui.spawn(["wl-copy", String(item.value)]);
        } else if (item.kind === "clip") {
            Ui.spawn(["bash", "-c", 'printf "%s" "$1" | cliphist decode | wl-copy', "_", item.clip.line]);
        } else if (item.kind === "char") {
            // Typed into the focused window once the launcher lets go of the keyboard, else copied.
            Ui.spawn(["bash", "-c",
                'if [ "$2" = type ] && command -v wtype >/dev/null; then sleep 0.15; printf "%s" "$1" | wtype -; else printf "%s" "$1" | wl-copy; fi',
                "_", item.char.char, alternate || !Ui.hasTyping ? "copy" : "type"]);
            picks = Object.assign({}, picks, { [item.char.char]: (picks[item.char.char] ?? 0) + 1 });
            picksFile.setText(JSON.stringify(picks));
        }
    }
    function launch(entry) {
        // Field codes (%u, %F…) stand for files and URLs; there are none to pass.
        const command = entry.command.filter(arg => !/^%[a-zA-Z]$/.test(arg));
        Ui.spawn({
            command: entry.runInTerminal ? ["kitty", "--", ...command] : command,
            workingDirectory: entry.workingDirectory || Quickshell.env("HOME")
        });
        usage = Object.assign({}, usage, { [entry.id]: (usage[entry.id] ?? 0) + 1 });
        usageFile.setText(JSON.stringify(usage));
    }
    function forget(index) {
        const item = results[index];
        if (item?.kind !== "clip")
            return;
        Quickshell.execDetached(["bash", "-c", 'printf "%s" "$1" | cliphist delete', "_", item.clip.line]);
        clips = clips.filter(c => c !== item.clip);
        const keep = view.currentIndex;
        refresh();
        view.currentIndex = Math.min(keep, results.length - 1);
    }

    function gridKey(key) {
        const page = grid.columns * 4;
        if (key === Qt.Key_Left)
            grid.moveCurrentIndexLeft();
        else if (key === Qt.Key_Right)
            grid.moveCurrentIndexRight();
        else if (key === Qt.Key_Up)
            grid.moveCurrentIndexUp();
        else if (key === Qt.Key_Down)
            grid.moveCurrentIndexDown();
        else if (key === Qt.Key_PageDown)
            grid.currentIndex = Math.min(results.length - 1, grid.currentIndex + page);
        else if (key === Qt.Key_PageUp)
            grid.currentIndex = Math.max(0, grid.currentIndex - page);
        else
            return false;
        return true;
    }

    FileView {
        id: usageFile
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/state/penumbra/launcher.json"
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: {
            try {
                root.usage = JSON.parse(text());
            } catch (e) {
                root.usage = {};
            }
        }
    }

    FileView {
        id: picksFile
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/state/penumbra/characters.json"
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: {
            try {
                root.picks = JSON.parse(text());
            } catch (e) {
                root.picks = {};
            }
        }
    }

    // Unicode's emoji list (the unicode-emoji package), one per line:
    // "1F600 ; fully-qualified # 😀 E1.0 grinning face", under "# group: …" headings.
    // Skin-tone variants are left out.
    FileView {
        path: "/usr/share/unicode/emoji/emoji-test.txt"
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: {
            let group = "";
            const list = [];
            for (const line of text().split("\n")) {
                if (line.startsWith("# group: ")) {
                    group = line.slice(9);
                    continue;
                }
                const m = line.match(/; fully-qualified\s+# (\S+) E[\d.]+ (.+)$/);
                if (m && !m[2].includes("skin tone"))
                    list.push({ char: m[1], name: m[2], group });
            }
            root.emoji = list;
            if (Ui.launcherOpen)
                root.refresh();
        }
    }

    // cliphist lists "id<TAB>preview", newest first; images preview as
    // "[[ binary data 52 KiB png 800x600 ]]".
    Process {
        id: clipList
        command: ["cliphist", "list"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                root.clips = text.split("\n").filter(line => line.includes("\t")).map(line => {
                    const tab = line.indexOf("\t"), preview = line.slice(tab + 1);
                    const image = preview.match(/^\[\[ binary data (.+) (\w+) (\d+)x(\d+) \]\]$/);
                    return {
                        line,
                        id: line.slice(0, tab),
                        text: image ? `${image[2].toUpperCase()} image · ${image[3]}×${image[4]} · ${image[1]}` : preview.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(),
                        image: image ? image[2] : ""
                    };
                });
                root.refresh();
            }
        }
    }
    readonly property string thumbs: (Quickshell.env("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") || "/tmp") + "/penumbra/clipboard"

    // ── Surface ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Faint enough that the glass rule leaves it unblurred; only the card is frosted.
    Rectangle {
        anchors.fill: parent
        color: Theme.alpha(Theme.scrim, 0.26)
        opacity: card.opacity
        MouseArea {
            anchors.fill: parent
            onClicked: Ui.launcherOpen = false
        }
    }

    Shadow { target: card }
    Rectangle {
        id: card
        width: 640
        x: (parent.width - width) / 2
        y: Math.round(parent.height * 0.17)
        implicitHeight: column.implicitHeight + 24
        height: implicitHeight
        radius: Theme.radiusLarge
        color: Theme.glassStrong
        border.color: Theme.lineStrong
        clip: true
        opacity: Ui.launcherOpen ? 1 : 0
        scale: Ui.launcherOpen ? 1 : 0.96
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
        Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo } }
        Behavior on height { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

        // Swallow clicks so they don't reach the backdrop.
        MouseArea { anchors.fill: parent }
        // Handlers hear hover even when a row's MouseArea takes it.
        HoverHandler {
            onPointChanged: if (hovered) root.pointerAt(point.scenePosition.x, point.scenePosition.y)
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            id: column
            x: 12
            y: 12
            width: parent.width - 24
            spacing: 8

            // Search field and mode switch.
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.leftMargin: 10
                Layout.preferredHeight: 48
                spacing: 12
                Glyph {
                    text: root.clipboard || root.characters ? root.modes.find(m => m.mode === Ui.launcherMode).glyph : search.text.startsWith(">") ? "󰆍" : "󰍉"
                    size: 20
                    color: Theme.violetBright
                }
                TextInput {
                    id: search
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    color: Theme.text
                    selectionColor: Theme.violetDeep
                    selectedTextColor: Theme.text
                    font.family: Theme.sans
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTitle
                    clip: true
                    focus: true
                    onTextChanged: root.refresh()
                    Label {
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        visible: !search.text
                        text: ({ clipboard: "Search clipboard history…", emoji: "Search emoji…", symbols: "Search symbols…" })[Ui.launcherMode]
                            ?? "Search applications, > run, = calculate…"
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textTitle
                        color: Theme.dim
                    }
                    Keys.onPressed: event => {
                        const ctrl = event.modifiers & Qt.ControlModifier;
                        if (event.key === Qt.Key_Escape) {
                            Ui.launcherOpen = false;
                        } else if (root.characters && root.gridKey(event.key)) {
                            // Arrows and pages move around the grid.
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Down || (ctrl && event.key === Qt.Key_N)) {
                            view.incrementCurrentIndex();
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Up || (ctrl && event.key === Qt.Key_P)) {
                            view.decrementCurrentIndex();
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_PageDown) {
                            view.currentIndex = Math.min(root.results.length - 1, view.currentIndex + 6);
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_PageUp) {
                            view.currentIndex = Math.max(0, view.currentIndex - 6);
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Tab || event.key === Qt.Key_Backtab) {
                            const at = root.modes.findIndex(m => m.mode === Ui.launcherMode), step = event.key === Qt.Key_Tab ? 1 : -1;
                            Ui.launcherMode = root.modes[(at + step + root.modes.length) % root.modes.length].mode;
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Return || event.key === Qt.Key_Enter) {
                            root.activate(root.characters ? grid.currentIndex : view.currentIndex, event.modifiers & Qt.ShiftModifier);
                        } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Delete && (event.modifiers & Qt.ShiftModifier)) {
                            root.forget(view.currentIndex);
                        } else {
                            return;
                        }
                        event.accepted = true;
                    }
                }
                // Only the current mode shows its name; the others are icons.
                Repeater {
                    model: root.modes
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property var modelData
                        readonly property bool current: Ui.launcherMode === modelData.mode
                        implicitWidth: current ? modeRow.implicitWidth + 22 : 30
                        implicitHeight: 30
                        radius: Theme.corner(15)
                        color: current ? Theme.violet : modeArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
                        border.color: current ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.line
                        Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                        RowLayout {
                            id: modeRow
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            spacing: 6
                            Glyph { text: modelData.glyph; size: 13; color: current ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.textSoft }
                            Label { visible: current; text: modelData.label; font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; font.weight: Font.DemiBold; color: current ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: modeArea
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: { Ui.launcherMode = modelData.mode; search.forceActiveFocus(); }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Rectangle {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                implicitHeight: 1
                color: Theme.line
            }

            ListView {
                id: view
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(contentHeight, 7 * 56)
                visible: root.results.length > 0 && !root.characters
                clip: true
                spacing: 2
                model: root.characters ? [] : root.results
                boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
                highlightMoveDuration: Theme.fast
                highlight: Rectangle {
                    radius: Theme.radiusSmall
                    color: Theme.line
                    border.color: Theme.lineStrong
                }
                delegate: Item {
                    id: row
                    required property var modelData
                    required property int index
                    readonly property bool current: ListView.isCurrentItem
                    readonly property bool isImage: modelData.kind === "clip" && modelData.clip.image !== ""
                    width: ListView.view.width
                    implicitHeight: isImage ? 84 : 54

                    MouseArea {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: root.activate(row.index, false)
                    }

                    RowLayout {
                        anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 12; rightMargin: 14 }
                        spacing: 14

                        // App icon, or a glyph for commands, sums and text clips.
                        Item {
                            visible: !row.isImage
                            implicitWidth: 32
                            implicitHeight: 32
                            IconImage {
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                visible: row.modelData.kind === "app"
                                source: row.modelData.kind === "app" ? Quickshell.iconPath(row.modelData.entry.icon, "application-x-executable") : ""
                                asynchronous: true
                            }
                            // Picked colors (#rrggbb) show as a swatch.
                            Rectangle {
                                readonly property bool swatch: row.modelData.kind === "clip" && /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(row.modelData.clip.text)
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                visible: row.modelData.kind !== "app"
                                radius: Theme.corner(10)
                                color: swatch ? row.modelData.clip.text : Theme.raised
                                border.color: swatch ? Theme.lineStrong : "transparent"
                                Glyph {
                                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                                    visible: !parent.swatch
                                    text: ({ run: "󰆍", calc: "󰃬", clip: "󰅍" })[row.modelData.kind] ?? ""
                                    size: 16
                                    color: Theme.violetBright
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Image clips show a thumbnail, decoded once into the runtime directory.
                        ClippingRectangle {
                            visible: row.isImage
                            implicitWidth: 112
                            implicitHeight: 68
                            radius: Theme.corner(10)
                            color: Theme.raised
                            Image {
                                id: thumb
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                                asynchronous: true
                                sourceSize: Qt.size(224, 136)
                            }
                            Process {
                                running: row.isImage
                                command: ["bash", "-c", 'f=$1; [ -s "$f" ] || { mkdir -p "${f%/*}"; printf "%s" "$2" | cliphist decode > "$f"; }',
                                    "_", `${root.thumbs}/${row.modelData.clip?.id}.${row.modelData.clip?.image}`, row.modelData.clip?.line ?? ""]
                                onExited: thumb.source = `file://${root.thumbs}/${row.modelData.clip.id}.${row.modelData.clip.image}`
                            }
                        }

                        ColumnLayout {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            spacing: 1
                            Label {
                                Layout.fillWidth: true
                                text: {
                                    const m = row.modelData;
                                    if (m.kind === "app")
                                        return m.entry.name;
                                    if (m.kind === "run")
                                        return m.command;
                                    if (m.kind === "calc")
                                        return "= " + m.value;
                                    return m.clip.text;
                                }
                                font.pixelSize: row.modelData.kind === "calc" ? 17 : 13
                                font.family: row.modelData.kind === "run" || row.modelData.kind === "calc" ? Theme.mono : Theme.sans
                                font.weight: row.current ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Normal
                                color: row.modelData.kind === "calc" ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.text
                            }
                            Label {
                                Layout.fillWidth: true
                                visible: text !== ""
                                text: {
                                    const m = row.modelData;
                                    if (m.kind === "app")
                                        return m.entry.genericName || m.entry.comment;
                                    if (m.kind === "run")
                                        return "Run command · Shift+Enter in a terminal";
                                    if (m.kind === "calc")
                                        return "Enter copies the result";
                                    return "";
                                }
                                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                color: Theme.muted
                            }
                        }

                        Glyph {
                            visible: row.current
                            text: "󰌑"
                            size: 14
                            color: Theme.violet
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            GridView {
                id: grid
                readonly property int columns: 10
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(Math.ceil(count / columns), 5) * cellHeight
                visible: root.results.length > 0 && root.characters
                clip: true
                cellWidth: Math.floor(width / columns)
                cellHeight: 56
                model: root.characters ? root.results : []
                boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
                highlightMoveDuration: Theme.fast
                highlight: Rectangle {
                    radius: Theme.radiusSmall
                    color: Theme.line
                    border.color: Theme.lineStrong
                }
                delegate: Item {
                    id: cell
                    required property var modelData
                    required property int index
                    width: grid.cellWidth
                    height: grid.cellHeight
                    Text {
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        text: cell.modelData.char?.char ?? ""
                        font.family: Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" ? "Noto Color Emoji" : Theme.sans
                        font.pixelSize: Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" ? 28 : 24
                        color: Theme.text
                    }
                    MouseArea {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: mouse => root.activate(cell.index, mouse.modifiers & Qt.ShiftModifier)
                    }
                }
            }

            // The chosen character's name.
            RowLayout {
                readonly property var current: root.characters ? root.results[grid.currentIndex]?.char : null
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.leftMargin: 10
                visible: !!current
                spacing: 10
                Label {
                    text: parent.current?.name ?? ""
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textMedium
                    font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                    Layout.maximumWidth: 420
                }
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: parent.current?.group ?? ""
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: Theme.muted
                }
            }

            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.topMargin: 14
                Layout.bottomMargin: 10
                visible: root.results.length === 0
                spacing: 4
                Glyph {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: root.clipboard || root.characters ? root.modes.find(m => m.mode === Ui.launcherMode).glyph : "󰍉"
                    size: 26
                    color: Theme.dim
                }
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                    text: {
                        if (root.clipboard)
                            return search.text ? "Nothing copied matches" : "Nothing copied yet";
                        if (Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" && root.emoji.length === 0)
                            return "Install the unicode-emoji package for the emoji list";
                        if (root.characters)
                            return "Nothing matches";
                        if (search.text.trim() && !search.text.startsWith(">"))
                            return "No application matches · start with > to run it as a command";
                        return "Type to search";
                    }
                    color: Theme.muted
                }
            }

            // Hints, and clearing the clipboard.
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.leftMargin: 10
                Layout.rightMargin: 4
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: {
                        if (root.clipboard)
                            return "↑↓ choose · Enter copy · Shift+Del forget · Tab emoji · Esc close";
                        if (root.characters)
                            return (Ui.hasTyping ? "Arrows choose · Enter type · Shift+Enter copy" : "Arrows choose · Enter copy")
                                + (Ui.launcherMode === "emoji" ? " · Tab symbols" : " · Tab apps") + " · Esc close";
                        return "↑↓ choose · Enter open · Tab clipboard · Esc close";
                    }
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: Theme.dim
                }
                PillButton {
                    visible: root.clipboard && root.clips.length > 0
                    text: "Clear history"
                    danger: true
                    onClicked: {
                        Ui.launcherOpen = false;
                        Ui.run(Ui.bin + "penumbra-clipboard clear");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    GlassEdge {
        anchors.fill: card
        radius: card.radius
        opacity: card.opacity
        scale: card.scale
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/ListRow.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts

// A device or network row; `expanded` reveals the extra content below it.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    property string glyph
    property string title
    property string subtitle
    property string trailing: "" // glyph on the right, e.g. a lock
    property bool highlighted: false
    property bool busy: false
    property bool expanded: false
    property bool danger: false
    default property alias extra: extraColumn.data
    signal clicked()

    implicitHeight: column.implicitHeight + 16
    radius: Theme.radiusSmall
    color: highlighted ? Theme.line : area.containsMouse || expanded ? Theme.raised : "transparent"
    border.color: highlighted ? Theme.lineStrong : expanded ? Theme.line : "transparent"
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
    Behavior on implicitHeight { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
    clip: true

    MouseArea {
        id: area
        anchors.fill: parent
        hoverEnabled: true
        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
        onClicked: root.clicked()
    }

    ColumnLayout {
        id: column
        anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 8; leftMargin: 10; rightMargin: 10 }
        spacing: 8

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 11
            Glyph {
                Layout.preferredWidth: 22
                text: root.glyph
                size: 17
                color: root.highlighted ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.textSoft
            }
            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 0
                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: root.title; font.weight: root.highlighted ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Normal }
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    visible: text !== ""
                    text: root.subtitle
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: root.danger ? Theme.rose : root.highlighted ? Theme.violet : Theme.muted
                }
            }
            Glyph {
                visible: root.busy
                text: "󰔟"
                size: 14
                color: Theme.violet
                RotationAnimation on rotation { running: root.busy; from: 0; to: 360; duration: 1200; loops: Animation.Infinite }
            }
            Glyph {
                visible: root.trailing !== "" && !root.busy
                text: root.trailing
                size: 13
                color: Theme.dim
            }
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            id: extraColumn
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.leftMargin: 33
            visible: root.expanded
            spacing: 8
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/LockContent.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Services.Mpris
import Quickshell.Widgets

// One monitor of the lock screen: the wordmark-free wallpaper (dawn and eclipse keep moving),
// the time, and the password field. Every monitor shows the same password dots.
Rectangle {
    id: surface
    required property var lock
    property real pixelRatio: 1
    color: Theme.base

    readonly property var player: Mpris.players.values.find(p => p.isPlaying) ?? null

    // ── Wallpaper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    Image {
        anchors.fill: parent
        source: "file://" + Ui.wallpapers + Ui.wallpaper + "-clean.png"
        fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
        asynchronous: true
    }
    // Cover the screen like `awww img --resize crop`.
    Loader {
        id: scene
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        // Not before the surface has a size: the scene decodes its layers for it.
        active: surface.width > 0 && ["wallpaper-dawn", "wallpaper-eclipse"].includes(Ui.wallpaper)
        scale: item ? Math.max(surface.width / item.width, surface.height / item.height) : 1
        sourceComponent: Ui.wallpaper === "wallpaper-eclipse" ? eclipse : dawn
        Component {
            id: dawn
            DawnScene {
                clean: true
                pixelScale: width > 0 && height > 0 ? Math.max(surface.width / width, surface.height / height) * surface.pixelRatio : 1
            }
        }
        Component {
            id: eclipse
            EclipseScene {
                clean: true
                pixelScale: width > 0 && height > 0 ? Math.max(surface.width / width, surface.height / height) * surface.pixelRatio : 1
            }
        }
    }
    // Darker toward the bottom, where the password field sits.
    Rectangle {
        anchors.fill: parent
        gradient: Gradient {
            GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.alpha(Theme.base, 0.25) }
            GradientStop { position: 0.55; color: Theme.alpha(Theme.base, 0.35) }
            GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.alpha(Theme.base, 0.78) }
        }
    }

    MouseArea {
        anchors.fill: parent
        onClicked: input.forceActiveFocus()
    }

    // ── Content ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    Item {
        id: content
        anchors.fill: parent
        opacity: 0
        Component.onCompleted: { opacity = 1; input.forceActiveFocus(); }
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow * 2; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

        // Battery and network, top right.
        RowLayout {
            anchors { top: parent.top; right: parent.right; margins: 28 }
            spacing: 16
            Glyph { text: Devices.networkGlyph; size: 16; color: Theme.textSoft }
            RowLayout {
                visible: Devices.hasBattery
                spacing: 6
                Glyph { text: Devices.batteryGlyph; size: 16; color: Devices.batteryColor }
                Label { text: Devices.batteryPercent + "%"; color: Devices.batteryColor }
            }
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
            y: Math.round(parent.height * 0.2)
            spacing: 0
            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                text: Qt.formatDateTime(Clock.date, "HH:mm")
                font.family: Theme.display
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textDisplay
                font.weight: Font.Light
                font.letterSpacing: -1
                color: Theme.text
            }
            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                text: Qt.formatDateTime(Clock.date, "dddd d MMMM")
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                font.letterSpacing: 3
                font.capitalization: Font.AllUppercase
                color: Theme.violet
            }
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            id: login
            anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
            y: Math.round(parent.height * 0.58)
            spacing: 14

            Avatar {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                size: 76
                ring: true
            }
            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                text: Quickshell.env("USER")
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                font.weight: Font.DemiBold
            }

            // The typed password shows as dots; the real field is invisible behind them.
            Rectangle {
                id: field
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                Layout.topMargin: 4
                implicitWidth: 320
                implicitHeight: 52
                radius: Theme.corner(26)
                color: Theme.glassStrong
                border.width: input.activeFocus ? 2 : 1
                border.color: surface.lock.message && !surface.lock.busy ? Theme.rose : input.activeFocus ? Theme.violet : Theme.lineStrong
                Behavior on border.color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

                TextInput {
                    id: input
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    opacity: 0
                    echoMode: TextInput.Password
                    enabled: !surface.lock.busy
                    onTextChanged: if (surface.lock.password !== text) surface.lock.password = text
                    Keys.onReturnPressed: surface.lock.submit()
                    Keys.onEnterPressed: surface.lock.submit()
                    Keys.onEscapePressed: text = ""
                    Keys.onPressed: event => {
                        surface.lock.checkCaps();
                        event.accepted = false;
                    }
                }
                Connections {
                    target: surface.lock
                    function onPasswordChanged() {
                        if (input.text !== surface.lock.password)
                            input.text = surface.lock.password;
                    }
                    function onRejected() { shake.restart(); }
                }

                Label {
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    visible: surface.lock.password === "" && !surface.lock.busy
                    text: "Enter password"
                    color: Theme.dim
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textMedium
                }
                Row {
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    spacing: 8
                    Repeater {
                        model: Math.min(surface.lock.password.length, 22)
                        delegate: Rectangle {
                            width: 9
                            height: 9
                            radius: Theme.corner(4.5)
                            color: Theme.violetBright
                            scale: 0
                            Component.onCompleted: scale = 1
                            Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutBack } }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Glyph {
                    anchors { right: parent.right; rightMargin: 18; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
                    text: surface.lock.busy ? "󰔟" : "󰌆"
                    size: 15
                    color: surface.lock.busy ? Theme.violet : Theme.dim
                    RotationAnimation on rotation { running: surface.lock.busy; from: 0; to: 360; duration: 1200; loops: Animation.Infinite }
                }

                SequentialAnimation {
                    id: shake
                    NumberAnimation { target: field; property: "Layout.leftMargin"; to: 18; duration: 50 }
                    NumberAnimation { target: field; property: "Layout.leftMargin"; to: -14; duration: 70 }
                    NumberAnimation { target: field; property: "Layout.leftMargin"; to: 10; duration: 60 }
                    NumberAnimation { target: field; property: "Layout.leftMargin"; to: 0; duration: 60 }
                }
            }

            // With several keyboard layouts, the one the password is typed in; click to switch.
            Rectangle {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                visible: Devices.layouts.length > 1
                implicitWidth: layoutRow.implicitWidth + 24
                implicitHeight: 28
                radius: Theme.corner(14)
                color: layoutArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.glass
                border.color: Theme.line
                RowLayout {
                    id: layoutRow
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    spacing: 6
                    Glyph { text: "󰌌"; size: 13; color: Theme.textSoft }
                    Label { text: Devices.layoutCode; font.weight: Font.Bold; color: Theme.textSoft }
                }
                MouseArea {
                    id: layoutArea
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    hoverEnabled: true
                    cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                    onClicked: { Devices.nextLayout(); input.forceActiveFocus(); }
                }
            }

            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                Layout.preferredHeight: 16
                text: surface.lock.message || (surface.lock.capsLock ? "Caps Lock is on" : "")
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                color: surface.lock.message ? Theme.rose : Theme.amber
            }
        }

        // What's playing, with its controls.
        Rectangle {
            anchors { horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter; bottom: parent.bottom; bottomMargin: 40 }
            visible: !!surface.player
            implicitWidth: 360
            implicitHeight: 64
            radius: Theme.corner(32)
            color: Theme.glassStrong
            border.color: Theme.line
            RowLayout {
                anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 8; rightMargin: 12 }
                spacing: 12
                ClippingRectangle {
                    implicitWidth: 48
                    implicitHeight: 48
                    radius: Theme.corner(24)
                    color: Theme.raised
                    Image {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        source: surface.player?.trackArtUrl ?? ""
                        fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                        asynchronous: true
                    }
                    Glyph {
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        visible: !surface.player?.trackArtUrl
                        text: "󰎆"
                        size: 19
                        color: Theme.violetBright
                    }
                }
                ColumnLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    spacing: 1
                    Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: surface.player?.trackTitle || Media.appName(surface.player); font.weight: Font.DemiBold }
                    Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: surface.player?.trackArtist || Media.appName(surface.player); font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; color: Theme.muted }
                }
                Repeater {
                    model: [
                        { glyph: "󰒮", run: () => surface.player.previous() },
                        { glyph: surface.player?.isPlaying ? "󰏤" : "󰐊", run: () => surface.player.togglePlaying(), main: true },
                        { glyph: "󰒭", run: () => surface.player.next() }
                    ]
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property var modelData
                        implicitWidth: modelData.main ? 36 : 28
                        implicitHeight: implicitWidth
                        radius: Theme.corner(implicitWidth / 2)
                        color: modelData.main ? Theme.violet : lockMedia.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                        Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: modelData.glyph; size: 15; color: modelData.main ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: lockMedia
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: { modelData.run(); input.forceActiveFocus(); }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/LockScreen.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Services.Pam
import Quickshell.Wayland

// The lock screen, on every monitor. hypridle locks through `ipc call lock lock` (which
// `loginctl lock-session` triggers); hyprlock takes over when the shell is not running.
Scope {
    id: root

    property string password: ""
    property bool busy: false
    property string message: ""
    property bool capsLock: false
    readonly property bool locked: sessionLock.locked
    signal rejected()

    function lock() {
        if (sessionLock.locked)
            return;
        Ui.closeOverlays();
        capsLeds.running = true;
        password = "";
        message = "";
        checkCaps();
        lockState.setText("locked\n");
        sessionLock.locked = true;
    }
    function submit() {
        if (busy || password === "")
            return;
        busy = true;
        message = "";
        pam.start();
    }
    // Re-reads the Caps Lock LEDs; cheap enough for every keypress.
    function checkCaps() {
        for (const led of leds.instances)
            led.reload();
    }

    // The default "login" PAM stack, the same one hyprlock includes.
    PamContext {
        id: pam
        onPamMessage: {
            if (responseRequired)
                respond(root.password);
            else if (message)
                root.message = message;
        }
        onCompleted: result => {
            root.busy = false;
            root.password = "";
            if (result === PamResult.Success) {
                sessionLock.locked = false;
                lockState.setText("");
                return;
            }
            if (result === PamResult.MaxTries)
                root.message = "Too many attempts; wait a moment";
            else if (!root.message)
                root.message = "Wrong password";
            root.rejected();
        }
        onError: error => {
            root.busy = false;
            root.password = "";
            root.message = "Could not check the password (" + PamError.toString(error) + ")";
            root.rejected();
        }
    }

    // Whether the screen is locked, kept outside the shell: if the shell dies while locked,
    // systemd starts it again (penumbra-shell.service) and it locks straight away. The runtime
    // directory is emptied at logout, so a stale "locked" never outlives the session.
    FileView {
        id: lockState
        path: (Quickshell.env("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") || "/tmp") + "/penumbra-locked"
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: if (text().trim() === "locked") root.lock()
    }

    // Caps Lock lights an LED on every keyboard that has one. The LEDs are listed when locking
    // (a keyboard may have been plugged in since), then read directly.
    property var capsPaths: []
    Process {
        id: capsLeds
        running: true
        command: ["bash", "-c", "printf '%s\\n' /sys/class/leds/*::capslock/brightness"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: root.capsPaths = text.split("\n").filter(p => p.startsWith("/") && !p.includes("*"))
        }
    }
    Variants {
        id: leds
        model: root.capsPaths
        delegate: FileView {
            required property string modelData
            path: modelData
            printErrors: false
            onLoaded: root.capsLock = leds.instances.some(led => led.loaded && parseInt(led.text()) > 0)
        }
    }

    WlSessionLock {
        id: sessionLock
        WlSessionLockSurface {
            id: surface
            color: Theme.base
            LockContent {
                anchors.fill: parent
                lock: root
                pixelRatio: surface.screen?.devicePixelRatio ?? 1
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Media.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Services.Mpris

// What's playing, and a small spectrum of it from cava while it plays.
Singleton {
    id: root

    // The player that is playing, else the last one that played, while it lasts.
    readonly property var playing: Mpris.players.values.find(p => p.isPlaying) ?? null
    property var recent: null
    onPlayingChanged: if (playing) recent = playing
    readonly property var player: playing ?? (Mpris.players.values.includes(recent) ? recent : null)
    readonly property int count: 8
    property var bars: new Array(count).fill(0)

    // The app's own name ("Firefox"), not its MPRIS identity ("Mozilla firefox").
    function appName(p) {
        if (!p)
            return "";
        return DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(p.desktopEntry || p.identity)?.name || p.identity;
    }
    // Live streams have no real length: Firefox reports an endless one (about 292,000 years).
    function isLive(p) {
        return !!p && p.lengthSupported && p.length > 100 * 3600;
    }
    function hasLength(p) {
        return !!p && p.lengthSupported && p.length > 0 && !isLive(p);
    }
    // 4:05, or 1:02:03 past an hour.
    function time(seconds) {
        const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds)), h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.floor(s % 3600 / 60);
        const ss = String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
        return h ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${ss}` : `${m}:${ss}`;
    }

    readonly property string config: [
        "[general]", `bars = ${count}`, "framerate = 30", "sleep_timer = 2",
        "[input]", "method = pipewire",
        "[output]", "method = raw", "raw_target = /dev/stdout", "data_format = ascii",
        "ascii_max_range = 100", "bar_delimiter = 59", "frame_delimiter = 10",
        "[smoothing]", "noise_reduction = 60", ""
    ].join("\n")

    // cava prints one frame per line: "12;40;7;…;".
    Process {
        running: Ui.hasVisualizer && !!root.playing
        command: ["bash", "-c", 'f="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/tmp}/penumbra/cava.conf"; mkdir -p "${f%/*}"; printf "%s" "$1" > "$f"; exec cava -p "$f"', "_", root.config]
        stdout: SplitParser {
            onRead: data => root.bars = data.split(";").filter(v => v !== "").map(v => Math.min(1, parseInt(v) / 100))
        }
        onRunningChanged: if (!running) root.bars = new Array(root.count).fill(0)
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Meteor.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// Now and then a shooting star starts somewhere in `area` (scene coordinates) and falls along `heading`.
Item {
    id: root
    property bool running: true
    property rect area
    property real heading: 155
    property real distance: 560

    Rectangle {
        id: streak
        property real startX: 0
        property real startY: 0
        property real progress: 0
        width: 260
        height: 2
        radius: 1
        visible: falling.running
        transformOrigin: Item.Right
        rotation: root.heading
        x: startX + root.distance * progress * Math.cos(root.heading * Math.PI / 180) - width
        y: startY + root.distance * progress * Math.sin(root.heading * Math.PI / 180) - height / 2
        opacity: Math.sin(Math.PI * progress)
        gradient: Gradient {
            orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
            GradientStop { position: 0; color: "transparent" }
            GradientStop { position: 1; color: "#f7e6ff" }
        }
        NumberAnimation on progress {
            id: falling
            running: false
            from: 0
            to: 1
            duration: 1100
            easing.type: Easing.InQuad
        }
    }

    Timer {
        interval: 20000 + Math.random() * 30000
        repeat: true
        running: root.running
        onTriggered: {
            interval = 20000 + Math.random() * 30000;
            streak.startX = root.area.x + Math.random() * root.area.width;
            streak.startY = root.area.y + Math.random() * root.area.height;
            falling.restart();
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/MixerPage.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Services.Pipewire

// Sound: pick the output and input device, and set each app's own volume.
ColumnLayout {
    id: root

    property bool active: false
    readonly property var audioNodes: Pipewire.nodes.values.filter(n => n.audio)
    // A laptop's HDMI and DisplayPort outputs exist even with nothing plugged in; list them only
    // while an external screen is connected, or while one is in use.
    readonly property bool externalScreen: Quickshell.screens.some(s => !/^(eDP|LVDS|DSI)/.test(s.name))
    readonly property var outputs: audioNodes.filter(n => n.isSink && !n.isStream
        && (externalScreen || !isDisplayOutput(n) || n === Pipewire.defaultAudioSink))
    readonly property var inputs: audioNodes.filter(n => !n.isSink && !n.isStream)
    // Apps playing sound. Quickshell flags playback streams as sinks (AudioOutStream =
    // Audio | Sink | Stream); recording streams are sources and are left out.
    readonly property var streams: audioNodes.filter(n => n.isStream && n.isSink)

    spacing: 12

    // Volume, mute and properties are only live on tracked nodes.
    PwObjectTracker {
        objects: root.active ? [...root.outputs, ...root.inputs, ...root.streams] : []
    }

    function isDisplayOutput(node) {
        return /hdmi|displayport/i.test(node.name + " " + node.description);
    }
    // The port ("Speaker") tells a card's devices apart, a display output goes by its short name
    // ("HDMI 1"), and Bluetooth devices by their own name.
    function deviceName(node) {
        if (isDisplayOutput(node) && node.nickname)
            return node.nickname;
        const port = node.name.startsWith("bluez") ? "" : node.properties["device.profile.description"];
        return port || node.description || node.nickname || node.name;
    }
    function deviceGlyph(node, input) {
        const name = (node.name + " " + node.description).toLowerCase();
        if (name.includes("bluez") || name.includes("headset") || name.includes("headphone"))
            return input ? "󰋎" : "󰋋";
        if (name.includes("hdmi") || name.includes("displayport"))
            return "󰡁";
        return input ? "󰍬" : "󰓃";
    }
    function appName(node) {
        const p = node.properties;
        return p["application.name"] || node.description || node.name;
    }
    function appIcon(node) {
        const p = node.properties;
        const entry = DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(p["application.process.binary"] ?? p["application.name"] ?? "");
        return Quickshell.iconPath(p["application.icon-name"] || entry?.icon || p["application.process.binary"] || "", "audio-volume-high");
    }

    PageHeader {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        title: "Sound"
        status: Pipewire.defaultAudioSink ? root.deviceName(Pipewire.defaultAudioSink) : "No output"
        showSwitch: false
        onBack: Ui.controlPage = "main"
    }

    Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
        text: "Output"
    }
    Repeater {
        model: root.outputs
        delegate: ListRow {
            required property var modelData
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            glyph: root.deviceGlyph(modelData, false)
            title: root.deviceName(modelData)
            highlighted: Pipewire.defaultAudioSink === modelData
            subtitle: highlighted ? "In use" : ""
            onClicked: Pipewire.preferredDefaultAudioSink = modelData
        }
    }

    Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
        visible: root.inputs.length > 0
        text: "Input"
    }
    Repeater {
        model: root.inputs
        delegate: ListRow {
            required property var modelData
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            glyph: root.deviceGlyph(modelData, true)
            title: root.deviceName(modelData)
            highlighted: Pipewire.defaultAudioSource === modelData
            subtitle: highlighted ? "In use" : ""
            onClicked: Pipewire.preferredDefaultAudioSource = modelData
        }
    }

    Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
        text: "Applications"
    }
    Repeater {
        model: root.streams
        delegate: ColumnLayout {
            id: stream
            required property var modelData
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 0
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.leftMargin: 46
                text: root.appName(stream.modelData) + (stream.modelData.properties["media.name"] ? " · " + stream.modelData.properties["media.name"] : "")
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                color: Theme.textSoft
            }
            SliderRow {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                icon: root.appIcon(stream.modelData)
                value: Math.round((stream.modelData.audio?.volume ?? 0) * 100)
                muted: stream.modelData.audio?.muted ?? false
                onMoved: v => { stream.modelData.audio.muted = false; stream.modelData.audio.volume = v / 100; }
                onGlyphClicked: stream.modelData.audio.muted = !stream.modelData.audio.muted
            }
        }
    }
    ColumnLayout {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.bottomMargin: 4
        visible: root.streams.length === 0
        spacing: 4
        Glyph { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: "󰝚"; size: 26; color: Theme.dim }
        Label {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
            text: "No app is playing sound"
            color: Theme.muted
        }
    }

    Label {
        Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignRight
        text: "Advanced sound settings"
        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
        color: advancedArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.muted
        MouseArea {
            id: advancedArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            anchors.margins: -4
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: { Ui.close(); Ui.run("pavucontrol"); }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/MonitorPanel.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Effects
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Widgets

// The system monitor, under the system capsule: live graphs of the processor, memory,
// graphics, disks and network with storage, or the busiest apps.
Panel {
    id: root
    anchors { top: true; right: true }
    margins.right: Theme.edge - shadowRoom
    sheetWidth: 440
    open: Ui.monitorOpen
    onDismissed: Ui.dismiss()

    readonly property var pages: [
        { page: "overview", label: "Overview", glyph: "󰕮" },
        { page: "processes", label: "Processes", glyph: "󰉹" }
    ]
    property string page: "overview"
    readonly property bool listing: open && page === "processes"

    readonly property int shown: 10
    property string sortBy: "cpu" // cpu or memory
    property string expanded: ""
    property var groups: []

    // The graphics card shows the GPU picked by clicking it, or else the discrete GPU while it
    // is awake and the integrated one otherwise.
    property int gpuChoice: -1
    readonly property int gpuDefault: Math.max(0, Usage.gpus.findIndex(g => !g.integrated && !g.asleep), Usage.gpus.findIndex(g => g.integrated))
    readonly property var gpu: Usage.gpus[gpuChoice >= 0 ? gpuChoice : gpuDefault] ?? null

    onOpenChanged: if (!open) expanded = ""
    onPageChanged: pageIn.restart()
    onSortByChanged: refreshList()

    // Two batch iterations: the second measures CPU over the one-second delay.
    // The C locale keeps decimal points out of the user's locale.
    Process {
        id: top
        command: ["env", "LC_ALL=C", "top", "-b", "-n", "2", "-d", "1", "-w", "512", "-c", "-e", "k"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: root.parse(text)
        }
    }
    Timer {
        interval: 2000
        running: root.listing
        repeat: true
        triggeredOnStart: true
        onTriggered: if (!top.running) top.running = true
    }

    // RES is in KiB, but top shortens values too wide for the column (e.g. 10.2g).
    function kib(value) {
        const scale = { m: 1024, g: 1048576, t: 1073741824 }[value.slice(-1)];
        return scale ? parseFloat(value) * scale : Number(value);
    }
    // Processes are grouped by executable, so a browser counts once.
    function parse(out) {
        const lines = out.split("\n");
        let start = lines.length;
        for (let i = lines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (/^\s*PID\s+USER/.test(lines[i])) {
                start = i + 1;
                break;
            }
        }
        const found = {};
        for (const line of lines.slice(start)) {
            // PID USER PR NI VIRT RES SHR S %CPU %MEM TIME+ COMMAND
            const m = line.match(/^\s*(\d+)\s+(\S+)\s+\S+\s+\S+\s+\S+\s+(\S+)\s+\S+\s+\S\s+([\d.]+)\s+\S+\s+\S+\s+(.+)$/);
            if (!m || m[5].startsWith("top -b -n 2"))
                continue;
            const key = m[5].startsWith("[") ? "kernel" : m[5].split(" ")[0].split("/").pop().replace(/^-/, "");
            if (!key)
                continue;
            if (!found[key])
                found[key] = { key, cpu: 0, mem: 0, pids: [], user: m[2] };
            const g = found[key];
            g.cpu += Number(m[4]);
            g.mem += kib(m[3]);
            g.pids.push(m[1]);
            if (g.user !== m[2])
                g.user = "";
        }
        groups = Object.values(found);
        refreshList();
    }
    // Apps whose desktop file is named after something else (com.anthropic.Claude runs
    // claude-desktop) are found by the program they launch; wrappers say nothing about the app.
    readonly property var launchers: {
        const wrappers = ["env", "flatpak", "sh", "bash", "python", "python3", "java", "sudo", "pkexec"];
        const map = {};
        for (const e of DesktopEntries.applications.values) {
            const exe = (e.command[0] ?? "").split("/").pop();
            if (exe && !wrappers.includes(exe) && (!map[exe] || map[exe].noDisplay))
                map[exe] = e;
        }
        return map;
    }
    // Updated in place, so rows keep their state between samples.
    function refreshList() {
        const byCpu = (a, b) => (b.cpu - a.cpu) || (b.mem - a.mem);
        const byMem = (a, b) => b.mem - a.mem;
        const top = groups.slice().sort(sortBy === "cpu" ? byCpu : byMem).slice(0, shown);
        top.forEach((g, i) => {
            const entry = g.key === "kernel" ? null : DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(g.key) ?? launchers[g.key];
            const row = {
                key: g.key,
                name: g.key === "kernel" ? "Kernel" : entry?.name || g.key,
                icon: entry?.icon ? Quickshell.iconPath(entry.icon, true) : "",
                cpu: g.cpu / Usage.cores,
                mem: g.mem,
                pids: g.pids.join(" "),
                count: g.pids.length,
                user: g.user
            };
            if (i < rows.count)
                rows.set(i, row);
            else
                rows.append(row);
        });
        while (rows.count > top.length)
            rows.remove(rows.count - 1);
    }
    function percent(value) {
        return value < 0.05 ? "0%" : value < 10 ? value.toFixed(1) + "%" : Math.round(value) + "%";
    }
    function size(kib) {
        return kib < 512 ? "—" : kib >= 1048576 ? (kib / 1048576).toFixed(1) + " GiB" : Math.round(kib / 1024) + " MiB";
    }
    function gib(kib) {
        return (kib / 1048576).toFixed(1) + " GiB";
    }
    // Decimal units, as drives and connections are sold.
    function bytes(n) {
        const units = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
        let i = 0;
        for (; n >= 1000 && i < units.length - 1; i++)
            n /= 1000;
        return `${i === 0 || n >= 10 ? Math.round(n) : n.toFixed(1)} ${units[i]}`;
    }
    function rate(n) {
        return bytes(n) + "/s";
    }
    // Calm, then amber, then rose as something fills up.
    function level(value, calm) {
        return value >= 90 ? Theme.rose : value >= 75 ? Theme.amber : calm;
    }
    function end(pids, force) {
        Quickshell.execDetached(["kill", force ? "-KILL" : "-TERM"].concat(pids.split(" ")));
        expanded = "";
        refreshSoon.restart();
    }
    Timer {
        id: refreshSoon
        interval: 400
        onTriggered: if (!top.running) top.running = true
    }

    ListModel { id: rows }

    // A figure over its history graph, which fills the card's lower part edge to edge.
    component Meter: Rectangle {
        id: meter
        property string glyph
        property string title
        property color accent: Theme.violet
        property bool interactive: false
        // The graphs; the header's right side; the figures under the title.
        default property alias graphs: graphArea.data
        property alias corner: cornerSlot.data
        property alias figures: figureSlot.data
        signal clicked()

        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.preferredWidth: 1
        implicitHeight: 132
        radius: Theme.radius
        color: interactive && meterArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
        border.color: Theme.line
        Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

        MouseArea {
            id: meterArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            enabled: meter.interactive
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: meter.clicked()
        }

        Item {
            id: graphArea
            anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; bottom: parent.bottom; margins: 1 }
            height: 54
            layer.enabled: true
            layer.effect: MultiEffect {
                maskEnabled: true
                maskSource: graphMask
                maskThresholdMin: 0.25
                maskSpreadAtMin: 0.25
            }
        }
        // The card's lower corners, for the graphs.
        Rectangle {
            id: graphMask
            anchors.fill: graphArea
            visible: false
            layer.enabled: true
            bottomLeftRadius: Math.max(0, meter.radius - 1)
            bottomRightRadius: Math.max(0, meter.radius - 1)
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 12; leftMargin: 14; rightMargin: 14 }
            spacing: 4
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.preferredHeight: 16
                spacing: 7
                Glyph { text: meter.glyph; size: 13; color: meter.accent }
                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: meter.title; font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; color: Theme.muted }
                RowLayout { id: cornerSlot; spacing: 6 }
            }
            ColumnLayout {
                id: figureSlot
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 0
            }
        }
    }

    // A big figure and a line of detail under it.
    component Figure: ColumnLayout {
        id: figure
        property string value
        property string detail
        property color color: Theme.text
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        spacing: 0
        Label {
            text: figure.value
            font.family: Theme.display
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textHeading
            font.weight: Font.DemiBold
            color: figure.color
        }
        Label {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            visible: text !== ""
            text: figure.detail
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
            color: Theme.subtle
        }
    }

    // A column title that sorts the list.
    component SortHeader: Overline {
        id: header
        property string mode
        readonly property bool current: root.sortBy === mode
        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignRight
        font.weight: current ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Medium
        color: current ? Theme.violetBright : headerArea.containsMouse ? Theme.textSoft : Theme.muted
        MouseArea {
            id: headerArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            anchors.margins: -6
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: root.sortBy = header.mode
        }
    }

    ColumnLayout {
        x: 18
        y: 18
        width: root.sheetWidth - 36
        spacing: 12

        // Header: the machine, and the two pages. Only the current page shows its name.
        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.bottomMargin: 2
            spacing: 6
            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 1
                Label { text: "System"; font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge; font.weight: Font.DemiBold }
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: Usage.cpuModel || `${Usage.cores} threads`
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: Theme.muted
                }
            }
            Repeater {
                model: root.pages
                delegate: Rectangle {
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property bool current: root.page === modelData.page
                    implicitWidth: current ? pageRow.implicitWidth + 24 : 32
                    implicitHeight: 32
                    radius: Theme.corner(16)
                    color: current ? Theme.violet : pageArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
                    border.color: current ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.line
                    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                    Behavior on implicitWidth { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                    RowLayout {
                        id: pageRow
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        spacing: 6
                        Glyph { text: modelData.glyph; size: 13; color: current ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.textSoft }
                        Label { visible: current; text: modelData.label; font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; font.weight: Font.DemiBold; color: Theme.violetInk }
                    }
                    MouseArea {
                        id: pageArea
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: root.page = modelData.page
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            id: pageContent
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 12

            ParallelAnimation {
                id: pageIn
                NumberAnimation { target: pageContent; property: "opacity"; from: 0; to: 1; duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic }
                NumberAnimation { target: pageContent; property: "Layout.topMargin"; from: 8; to: 0; duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo }
            }

            // Overview
            GridLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: root.page === "overview"
                columns: 2
                rowSpacing: 8
                columnSpacing: 8

                Meter {
                    glyph: "󰻠"
                    title: "Processor"
                    accent: Theme.violet
                    // One bar per thread.
                    corner: Row {
                        readonly property int count: Usage.coreLoads.length
                        readonly property real bar: Math.max(1, (84 - (count - 1)) / count)
                        spacing: 1
                        Repeater {
                            model: parent.count
                            delegate: Rectangle {
                                required property int index
                                width: parent.bar
                                height: 14
                                radius: Theme.corner(1)
                                color: Theme.alpha(Theme.violet, 0.16)
                                Rectangle {
                                    anchors.bottom: parent.bottom
                                    width: parent.width
                                    height: Math.max(1, parent.height * (Usage.coreLoads[index] ?? 0) / 100)
                                    radius: parent.radius
                                    color: root.level(Usage.coreLoads[index] ?? 0, Theme.violet)
                                    Behavior on height { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    figures: Figure {
                        value: Usage.cpu + "%"
                        detail: [Usage.cpuTemp >= 0 ? Usage.cpuTemp + " °C" : "", Usage.cpuGhz ? Usage.cpuGhz.toFixed(1) + " GHz" : "", Usage.loadAverage ? "load " + Usage.loadAverage : ""].filter(s => s).join(" · ")
                    }
                    Graph {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: Usage.cpuHistory
                        color: Theme.violet
                        live: root.open
                    }
                }

                Meter {
                    glyph: "󰍛"
                    title: "Memory"
                    accent: Theme.pink
                    corner: Label {
                        visible: Usage.swapUsed >= 1024
                        text: "Swap " + root.gib(Usage.swapUsed)
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                        color: Theme.dim
                    }
                    figures: Figure {
                        value: Usage.memory + "%"
                        detail: `${root.gib(Usage.memoryUsed)} of ${root.gib(Usage.memoryTotal)}`
                    }
                    Graph {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: Usage.memoryHistory
                        color: Theme.pink
                        live: root.open
                    }
                }

                Meter {
                    glyph: "󰢮"
                    title: root.gpu?.name ?? "Graphics"
                    accent: Theme.blue
                    interactive: Usage.gpus.length > 1
                    onClicked: root.gpuChoice = (Usage.gpus.indexOf(root.gpu) + 1) % Usage.gpus.length
                    // Which GPU of several this is.
                    corner: Row {
                        visible: Usage.gpus.length > 1
                        spacing: 4
                        Repeater {
                            model: Usage.gpus.length
                            delegate: Rectangle {
                                required property int index
                                readonly property bool current: Usage.gpus[index] === root.gpu
                                width: current ? 12 : 5
                                height: 5
                                radius: Theme.corner(2.5)
                                color: current ? Theme.blue : Theme.alpha(Theme.blue, 0.3)
                                Behavior on width { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    figures: Figure {
                        readonly property var g: root.gpu
                        value: !g ? "—" : g.asleep ? "Asleep" : g.busy >= 0 ? Math.round(g.busy) + "%" : "…"
                        color: g?.asleep ? Theme.textSoft : Theme.text
                        detail: {
                            if (!g)
                                return "No GPU found";
                            if (g.asleep)
                                return "Wakes when an app needs it";
                            return [g.temp >= 0 ? g.temp + " °C" : "",
                                    g.memTotal > 0 ? `${(g.memUsed / 1024).toFixed(1)} of ${(g.memTotal / 1024).toFixed(1)} GiB` : "",
                                    g.power >= 0 ? g.power + " W" : "",
                                    g.freq >= 0 ? `${g.freq} MHz` : ""].filter(s => s).join(" · ");
                        }
                    }
                    Graph {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: root.gpu?.history ?? []
                        color: Theme.blue
                        live: root.open
                    }
                }

                Meter {
                    glyph: "󰋊"
                    title: "Disks"
                    accent: Theme.green
                    figures: Figure {
                        value: root.rate(Usage.diskRead + Usage.diskWrite)
                        detail: `Read ${root.rate(Usage.diskRead)} · Write ${root.rate(Usage.diskWrite)}`
                    }
                    Graph {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: Usage.diskHistory
                        maximum: 0
                        floor: 1e6
                        color: Theme.green
                        live: root.open
                    }
                }

                Meter {
```
