                    Layout.columnSpan: 2
                    glyph: Devices.networkGlyph
                    title: Devices.networkName
                    accent: Theme.cyan
                    corner: Label {
                        text: Usage.netInterface
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                        color: Theme.dim
                    }
                    figures: RowLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        spacing: 22
                        Repeater {
                            model: [
                                { glyph: "󰇚", value: Usage.netDown, total: Usage.netReceived, what: "received", color: Theme.cyan },
                                { glyph: "󰕒", value: Usage.netUp, total: Usage.netSent, what: "sent", color: Theme.violetBright }
                            ]
                            delegate: RowLayout {
                                required property var modelData
                                spacing: 8
                                Glyph { text: modelData.glyph; size: 16; color: modelData.color }
                                Figure {
                                    value: root.rate(modelData.value)
                                    detail: `${root.bytes(modelData.total)} ${modelData.what}`
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Item { Layout.fillWidth: true }
                    }
                    // Both directions on one scale.
                    Graph {
                        id: download
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: Usage.downHistory
                        maximum: Math.max(1e5, 1.15 * Math.max(0, ...Usage.downHistory, ...Usage.upHistory))
                        color: Theme.cyan
                        live: root.open
                    }
                    Graph {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        values: Usage.upHistory
                        maximum: download.maximum
                        color: Theme.violetBright
                        fillOpacity: 0.16
                        live: root.open
                    }
                }

                // Storage, one row per filesystem.
                Rectangle {
                    Layout.columnSpan: 2
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    visible: Usage.filesystems.length > 0
                    implicitHeight: storage.implicitHeight + 24
                    radius: Theme.radius
                    color: Theme.raised
                    border.color: Theme.line
                    ColumnLayout {
                        id: storage
                        anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 12; leftMargin: 14; rightMargin: 14 }
                        spacing: 10
                        Repeater {
                            model: Usage.filesystems
                            delegate: ColumnLayout {
                                id: fs
                                required property var modelData
                                readonly property real used: 100 * modelData.used / modelData.size
                                Layout.fillWidth: true
                                spacing: 6
                                RowLayout {
                                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                                    spacing: 7
                                    Glyph { text: "󰋊"; size: 13; color: Theme.green }
                                    Label { text: fs.modelData.name; font.weight: Font.Medium }
                                    Label {
                                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                                        text: fs.modelData.mount
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                        color: Theme.dim
                                    }
                                    Label {
                                        text: `${root.bytes(fs.modelData.size - fs.modelData.used)} free of ${root.bytes(fs.modelData.size)}`
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                        color: Theme.subtle
                                    }
                                }
                                Rectangle {
                                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                                    implicitHeight: 6
                                    radius: Theme.corner(3)
                                    color: Theme.hover
                                    Rectangle {
                                        width: Math.max(height, parent.width * Math.min(fs.used, 100) / 100)
                                        height: parent.height
                                        radius: parent.radius
                                        color: root.level(fs.used, Theme.green)
                                        Behavior on width { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Processes
            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: root.page === "processes"
                spacing: 8

                RowLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    Layout.leftMargin: 10
                    Layout.rightMargin: 10
                    spacing: 0
                    Overline {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        text: "Apps & processes"
                    }
                    SortHeader { Layout.preferredWidth: 56; text: "CPU"; mode: "cpu" }
                    SortHeader { Layout.preferredWidth: 76; text: "Memory"; mode: "memory" }
                }

                ColumnLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    spacing: 2

                    Label {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        Layout.preferredHeight: 40
                        visible: rows.count === 0
                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                        text: "Measuring…"
                        color: Theme.muted
                    }

                    Repeater {
                        model: rows
                        delegate: Rectangle {
                            id: row
                            required property string key
                            required property string name
                            required property string icon
                            required property real cpu
                            required property real mem
                            required property string pids
                            required property int count
                            required property string user
                            readonly property bool expanded: root.expanded === key
                            readonly property bool mine: user === Quickshell.env("USER")

                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            implicitHeight: column.implicitHeight + 14
                            radius: Theme.radiusSmall
                            color: area.containsMouse || expanded ? Theme.raised : "transparent"
                            border.color: expanded ? Theme.line : "transparent"
                            clip: true
                            Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                            Behavior on implicitHeight { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

                            MouseArea {
                                id: area
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                hoverEnabled: true
                                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                                onClicked: root.expanded = row.expanded ? "" : row.key
                            }

                            ColumnLayout {
                                id: column
                                anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 7; leftMargin: 10; rightMargin: 10 }
                                spacing: 8

                                RowLayout {
                                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                                    spacing: 11
                                    Item {
                                        implicitWidth: 22
                                        implicitHeight: 22
                                        IconImage {
                                            anchors.fill: parent
                                            visible: row.icon !== ""
                                            source: row.icon
                                            asynchronous: true
                                        }
                                        Glyph {
                                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                                            visible: row.icon === ""
                                            text: row.key === "kernel" ? "󰻠" : "󰣆"
                                            size: 17
                                            color: Theme.textSoft
                                        }
                                    }
                                    ColumnLayout {
                                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                                        spacing: 0
                                        Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: row.name }
                                        Label {
                                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                                            text: (row.count > 1 ? `${row.count} processes` : `PID ${row.pids}`) + (row.user && !row.mine ? ` · ${row.user}` : "")
                                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                            color: Theme.muted
                                        }
                                    }
                                    Label {
                                        Layout.preferredWidth: 56
                                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignRight
                                        text: root.percent(row.cpu)
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                                        color: root.sortBy === "cpu" ? Theme.text : Theme.muted
                                    }
                                    Label {
                                        Layout.preferredWidth: 76
                                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignRight
                                        text: root.size(row.mem)
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                                        color: root.sortBy === "memory" ? Theme.text : Theme.muted
                                    }
                                }

                                RowLayout {
                                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                                    Layout.leftMargin: 33
                                    visible: row.expanded
                                    spacing: 8
                                    Label {
                                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                                        text: row.mine ? `Running as ${row.user}` : row.key === "kernel" ? "Kernel threads" : "Owned by another user"
                                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                        color: Theme.dim
                                    }
                                    PillButton {
                                        visible: row.mine
                                        text: "End"
                                        onClicked: root.end(row.pids, false)
                                    }
                                    PillButton {
                                        visible: row.mine
                                        text: "Force quit"
                                        danger: true
                                        onClicked: root.end(row.pids, true)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.topMargin: 2
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                text: "Updates every 2 s"
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                color: Theme.dim
            }
            PillButton {
                text: "Advanced view"
                primary: true
                onClicked: {
                    Ui.close();
                    Ui.run("kitty --class penumbra-monitor htop");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/NotificationCard.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Services.Notifications
import Quickshell.Widgets

// One notification: icon, app, summary, body and actions. Click runs the default action.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    required property var notification
    property bool compact: false
    readonly property bool critical: notification?.urgency === NotificationUrgency.Critical
    readonly property var actions: (notification?.actions ?? []).filter(a => a.identifier !== "default")
    // Big images (screenshots, photos) show under the text; small ones (avatars) are the icon.
    readonly property bool largeImage: probe.status === Image.Ready && probe.implicitWidth >= 240
    readonly property string appIconSource: {
        const icon = notification?.appIcon ?? "";
        if (!icon)
            return "";
        return icon.startsWith("/") || icon.includes("://") ? icon : Quickshell.iconPath(icon, true);
    }
    readonly property string iconSource: notification?.image && !largeImage ? notification.image : appIconSource
    Image {
        id: probe
        visible: false
        source: root.notification?.image ?? ""
        asynchronous: true
    }

    implicitHeight: layout.implicitHeight + 28
    radius: Theme.radius
    color: compact ? (area.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised) : Theme.glassStrong
    border.color: critical ? Theme.rose : compact ? Theme.line : Theme.lineStrong
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

    Shadow {
        z: -1
        target: root
        visible: !root.compact
        // A child already takes the card's fade and scale.
        opacity: 1
        scale: 1
    }
    GlassEdge {
        visible: !root.compact
        radius: root.radius
    }

    MouseArea {
        id: area
        anchors.fill: parent
        hoverEnabled: true
        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
        onClicked: {
            const n = root.notification;
            const action = n.actions.find(a => a.identifier === "default");
            if (action)
                action.invoke();
            n.dismiss();
        }
    }

    RowLayout {
        id: layout
        anchors { left: parent.left; right: parent.right; top: parent.top; margins: 14 }
        spacing: 12

        Item {
            Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignTop
            implicitWidth: 36
            implicitHeight: 36
            ClippingRectangle {
                anchors.fill: parent
                radius: Theme.corner(10)
                color: Theme.raised
                visible: root.iconSource !== ""
                IconImage {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    source: root.iconSource
                }
            }
            Rectangle {
                anchors.fill: parent
                radius: Theme.corner(10)
                color: Theme.raised
                visible: root.iconSource === ""
                Glyph {
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    text: root.critical ? "󰀦" : "󰂚"
                    size: 17
                    color: root.critical ? Theme.rose : Theme.violetBright
                }
            }
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 3

            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: root.notification?.appName || "Notification"
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                    font.letterSpacing: 1.2
                    font.capitalization: Font.AllUppercase
                    color: root.critical ? Theme.rose : Theme.violet
                }
                Label {
                    text: Notifs.age(root.notification, Clock.date.getTime())
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                    color: Theme.dim
                }
                Glyph {
                    text: "󰅖"
                    size: 13
                    color: close.containsMouse ? Theme.text : Theme.dim
                    MouseArea {
                        id: close
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        anchors.margins: -6
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: root.notification.dismiss()
                    }
                }
            }
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                text: root.notification?.summary ?? ""
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textMedium
                font.weight: Font.DemiBold
            }
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: text !== ""
                text: root.notification?.body ?? ""
                textFormat: Text.StyledText
                wrapMode: Text.Wrap
                maximumLineCount: root.compact ? 2 : 4
                color: Theme.textSoft
                linkColor: Theme.violetBright
                onLinkActivated: link => Qt.openUrlExternally(link)
            }
            ClippingRectangle {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.topMargin: 6
                Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(root.compact ? 120 : 180, width * probe.implicitHeight / Math.max(1, probe.implicitWidth))
                visible: root.largeImage
                radius: Theme.corner(10)
                color: Theme.raised
                Image {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    source: root.largeImage ? root.notification.image : ""
                    fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                    asynchronous: true
                }
            }

            // Inline reply, for chat apps that offer it.
            Rectangle {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.topMargin: 6
                visible: root.notification?.hasInlineReply ?? false
                implicitHeight: 34
                radius: Theme.corner(17)
                color: Theme.raised
                border.color: reply.activeFocus ? Theme.violet : Theme.line
                TextInput {
                    id: reply
                    anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 14; rightMargin: 40 }
                    verticalAlignment: TextInput.AlignVCenter
                    color: Theme.text
                    font.family: Theme.sans
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                    selectionColor: Theme.violetDeep
                    clip: true
                    function send() {
                        if (!text.trim())
                            return;
                        root.notification.sendInlineReply(text);
                        root.notification.dismiss();
                    }
                    Keys.onReturnPressed: send()
                    Keys.onEnterPressed: send()
                    Label {
                        anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                        visible: !reply.text
                        text: root.notification?.inlineReplyPlaceholder || "Reply…"
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                        color: Theme.dim
                    }
                }
                Rectangle {
                    anchors { right: parent.right; rightMargin: 3; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
                    width: 28
                    height: 28
                    radius: Theme.corner(14)
                    color: reply.text ? Theme.violet : "transparent"
                    Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "󰒊"; size: 13; color: reply.text ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.dim }
                    MouseArea {
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: reply.send()
                    }
                }
            }

            Flow {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.topMargin: 6
                visible: root.actions.length > 0
                spacing: 6
                Repeater {
                    model: root.actions
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property var modelData
                        implicitWidth: actionLabel.implicitWidth + 24
                        implicitHeight: 28
                        radius: Theme.corner(14)
                        color: actionArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violet : Theme.raised
                        border.color: Theme.line
                        Label {
                            id: actionLabel
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            text: modelData.text
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                            color: actionArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: actionArea
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: {
                                modelData.invoke();
                                root.notification.dismiss();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/NotificationPopups.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Wayland

// New notifications slide in at the top right, then retire to the control center.
PanelWindow {
    id: root
    screen: Ui.screen
    anchors { top: true; right: true }
    // Room around the cards for their shadows.
    readonly property int shadowRoom: Theme.edge
    margins { top: Theme.barHeight + 10 + Theme.gap - shadowRoom; right: Theme.edge - shadowRoom }
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    implicitWidth: 360 + 2 * shadowRoom
    implicitHeight: Math.max(1, column.height + 2 * shadowRoom)
    visible: Notifs.popups.length > 0 && !Ui.controlOpen
    color: "transparent"
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-popups"
    // A click gives the popup the keyboard, for inline replies.
    WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: WlrKeyboardFocus.OnDemand
    // Only the cards take input; the rest of the column passes clicks through.
    mask: Region { item: column }

    Column {
        id: column
        x: root.shadowRoom
        y: root.shadowRoom
        width: parent.width - 2 * root.shadowRoom
        spacing: 8
        move: Transition { NumberAnimation { property: "y"; duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

        Repeater {
            model: Notifs.popups
            delegate: NotificationCard {
                id: card
                required property var modelData
                width: column.width
                notification: modelData
                opacity: 0
                x: 40
                Component.onCompleted: { opacity = 1; x = 0; }
                Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal } }
                Behavior on x { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo } }

                Timer {
                    interval: Notifs.timeout(card.modelData)
                    running: interval > 0 && !card.hoveredCard
                    onTriggered: Notifs.retire(card.modelData)
                }
                readonly property bool hoveredCard: hover.hovered
                HoverHandler { id: hover }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Notifs.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Services.Notifications

// Notification daemon: popups for new notifications, history for the control center.
Singleton {
    id: root

    property bool dnd: false
    property var popups: []
    // The history keeps this many notifications; older ones expire.
    readonly property int limit: 50
    // When each notification arrived (Date.now()), by id.
    property var received: ({})
    // Newest first.
    readonly property var history: server.trackedNotifications.values.slice().reverse()
    readonly property int count: history.length
    // History by app, newest first: [{ app, items }].
    readonly property var groups: {
        const groups = [];
        for (const n of history) {
            const app = n.appName || "Notification";
            let group = groups.find(g => g.app === app);
            if (!group)
                groups.push(group = { app, items: [] });
            group.items.push(n);
        }
        return groups;
    }

    function clear() {
        for (const n of server.trackedNotifications.values.slice())
            n.dismiss();
        popups = [];
    }
    function clearApp(app) {
        for (const n of history.filter(n => (n.appName || "Notification") === app))
            n.dismiss();
    }
    function hidePopup(n) {
        popups = popups.filter(p => p !== n);
    }
    // A popup timed out: transient notifications go away instead of into the history.
    function retire(n) {
        hidePopup(n);
        if (n.transient)
            n.expire();
    }
    // "now", "5 min", "2 h", "yesterday" or the date; `now` is passed in so callers update with the clock.
    function age(n, now) {
        const at = received[n.id];
        if (!at)
            return "";
        const minutes = Math.floor((now - at) / 60000);
        if (minutes < 1)
            return "now";
        if (minutes < 60)
            return `${minutes} min`;
        if (minutes < 24 * 60)
            return `${Math.floor(minutes / 60)} h`;
        return minutes < 48 * 60 ? "yesterday" : Qt.formatDate(new Date(at), "d MMM");
    }
    // Milliseconds a popup stays up (expireTimeout is already in ms); critical ones wait for the user.
    function timeout(n) {
        if (n.urgency === NotificationUrgency.Critical)
            return 0;
        return n.expireTimeout > 0 ? Math.min(n.expireTimeout, 20000) : 6000;
    }

    NotificationServer {
        id: server
        keepOnReload: true
        actionsSupported: true
        bodyMarkupSupported: true
        bodyHyperlinksSupported: true
        imageSupported: true
        inlineReplySupported: true
        persistenceSupported: true

        onNotification: n => {
            n.tracked = true;
            root.received = Object.assign({}, root.received, { [n.id]: Date.now() });
            n.closed.connect(() => root.hidePopup(n));
            const shown = !root.dnd || n.urgency === NotificationUrgency.Critical;
            if (shown)
                root.popups = [n, ...root.popups].slice(0, 4);
            else if (n.transient)
                Qt.callLater(() => n.expire());
            // Oldest first; keep the newest `limit`.
            const all = server.trackedNotifications.values;
            for (const old of all.slice(0, Math.max(0, all.length - root.limit)))
                old.expire();
        }
    }
}
```

import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Wayland

// Volume / microphone / brightness feedback, floating above the bottom edge.
PanelWindow {
    id: root
    screen: Ui.screen
    anchors.bottom: true
    // Room around the pill for its shadow.
    readonly property int shadowRoom: 16
    margins.bottom: 80 - shadowRoom
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    implicitWidth: 300 + 2 * shadowRoom
    implicitHeight: 58 + 2 * shadowRoom
    visible: Ui.osdVisible || pill.opacity > 0
    color: "transparent"
    mask: Region {}
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-osd"

    readonly property string glyph: {
        if (Ui.osdKind === "brightness")
            return Ui.osdValue > 66 ? "󰃠" : Ui.osdValue > 33 ? "󰃟" : "󰃞";
        if (Ui.osdKind === "microphone")
            return Ui.osdMuted ? "󰍭" : "󰍬";
        return Ui.osdMuted ? "󰝟" : Ui.osdValue > 60 ? "󰕾" : Ui.osdValue > 25 ? "󰖀" : "󰕿";
    }

    Shadow { target: pill }
    Rectangle {
        id: pill
        anchors { fill: parent; margins: root.shadowRoom }
        radius: Theme.corner(height / 2)
        color: Theme.glassStrong
        border.color: Theme.line
        opacity: Ui.osdVisible ? 1 : 0
        scale: Ui.osdVisible ? 1 : 0.92
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal } }
        Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutBack } }

        GlassEdge { radius: pill.radius }

        RowLayout {
            anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 20; rightMargin: 20 }
            spacing: 14
            Glyph {
                text: root.glyph
                size: 19
                color: Ui.osdMuted ? Theme.dim : Theme.violetBright
                Layout.preferredWidth: 22
            }
            Rectangle {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                implicitHeight: 8
                radius: Theme.corner(4)
                color: Theme.raised
                Rectangle {
                    width: parent.width * (Ui.osdMuted ? 0 : Ui.osdValue / 100)
                    height: parent.height
                    radius: Theme.corner(4)
                    gradient: Gradient {
                        orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
                        GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violetDeep }
                        GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.violet }
                    }
                    Behavior on width { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                }
            }
            Label {
                Layout.preferredWidth: 38
                horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignRight
                text: Ui.osdMuted ? "Muted" : Ui.osdValue + "%"
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                color: Theme.textSoft
            }
        }
    }
}

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Panel.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Wayland

// A glass sheet that drops in under the bar and closes on Escape or a click outside.
PanelWindow {
    id: panel

    property bool open: false
    property int sheetWidth: 380
    default property alias content: sheet.data
    property alias sheet: sheet
    signal dismissed()

    // Room around the sheet for its shadow; panels anchor with Theme.edge - shadowRoom, so the
    // sheet itself still sits Theme.edge from the screen's edge.
    readonly property int shadowRoom: Theme.edge

    screen: Ui.screen
    visible: open || sheet.opacity > 0
    color: "transparent"
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    implicitWidth: sheetWidth + 2 * shadowRoom
    // A fixed-height surface; only the sheet takes input, so it can grow and shrink smoothly.
    implicitHeight: (screen?.height ?? 1080) - margins.top - Theme.edge
    mask: Region { item: sheet }
    margins.top: Theme.barHeight + 10 + Theme.gap - shadowRoom
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Top
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-panel"
    WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: open ? WlrKeyboardFocus.OnDemand : WlrKeyboardFocus.None

    HyprlandFocusGrab {
        windows: [panel]
        active: panel.open
        onCleared: panel.dismissed()
    }

    Shadow { target: sheet }

    Rectangle {
        id: sheet
        x: panel.shadowRoom
        width: panel.sheetWidth
        // Content sits at x/y = 18; keep the same padding below it.
        implicitHeight: childrenRect.y + childrenRect.height + 18
        height: implicitHeight
        clip: true
        radius: Theme.radiusLarge
        color: Theme.glassStrong
        border.color: Theme.line
        opacity: panel.open ? 1 : 0
        y: panel.shadowRoom + (panel.open ? 0 : -14)
        focus: panel.open
        Keys.onEscapePressed: panel.dismissed()
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
        Behavior on y { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo } }
        Behavior on height { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
    }
    // Over the sheet rather than inside it, where it would count towards the sheet's height.
    GlassEdge {
        anchors.fill: sheet
        radius: sheet.radius
        opacity: sheet.opacity
    }
}
```
