## `config/quickshell/penumbra/PolkitDialog.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Services.Polkit
import Quickshell.Wayland

// The polkit agent: when an app asks for administrator rights, a password card over the dimmed screen.
Scope {
    id: root

    readonly property var flow: agent.flow
    property bool shown: false
    property string error: ""

    PolkitAgent {
        id: agent
        onAuthenticationRequestStarted: {
            root.error = "";
            root.shown = true;
        }
    }
    Connections {
        target: root.flow
        ignoreUnknownSignals: true
        function onAuthenticationFailed() {
            root.error = "Wrong password";
            shake.restart();
        }
        function onIsCompletedChanged() {
            if (root.flow?.isCompleted)
                root.shown = false;
        }
    }
    onFlowChanged: {
        input.text = "";
        if (!flow)
            shown = false;
    }

    function submit() {
        if (flow && flow.isResponseRequired) {
            error = "";
            flow.submit(input.text);
            input.text = "";
        }
    }
    function cancel() {
        flow?.cancelAuthenticationRequest();
        shown = false;
    }

    PanelWindow {
        screen: Ui.screen
        anchors { top: true; bottom: true; left: true; right: true }
        exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
        visible: root.shown || card.opacity > 0
        color: "transparent"
        WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
        WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-polkit"
        WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: root.shown ? WlrKeyboardFocus.Exclusive : WlrKeyboardFocus.None

        Rectangle {
            anchors.fill: parent
            color: Theme.alpha(Theme.scrim, 0.62)
            opacity: card.opacity
        }

        Shadow { target: card }
        Rectangle {
            id: card
            width: 420
            height: column.implicitHeight + 48
            anchors.centerIn: parent
            radius: Theme.radiusLarge
            color: Theme.glassStrong
            border.color: Theme.lineStrong
            opacity: root.shown ? 1 : 0
            scale: root.shown ? 1 : 0.94
            Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal } }
            Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo } }

            GlassEdge { radius: card.radius }

            SequentialAnimation {
                id: shake
                NumberAnimation { target: card; property: "anchors.horizontalCenterOffset"; to: 14; duration: 50 }
                NumberAnimation { target: card; property: "anchors.horizontalCenterOffset"; to: -12; duration: 70 }
                NumberAnimation { target: card; property: "anchors.horizontalCenterOffset"; to: 8; duration: 60 }
                NumberAnimation { target: card; property: "anchors.horizontalCenterOffset"; to: 0; duration: 60 }
            }

            ColumnLayout {
                id: column
                x: 24
                y: 24
                width: parent.width - 48
                spacing: 14

                RowLayout {
                    spacing: 14
                    Rectangle {
                        implicitWidth: 48
                        implicitHeight: 48
                        radius: Theme.corner(24)
                        gradient: Gradient {
                            GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violet }
                            GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.violetDeep }
                        }
                        Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "󰒃"; size: 22; color: Theme.violetInk }
                    }
                    ColumnLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        spacing: 2
                        Label {
                            text: "A U T H E N T I C A T E"
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                            color: Theme.violet
                        }
                        Label {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            text: "Administrator rights needed"
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                            font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                        }
                    }
                }

                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    text: root.flow?.message ?? ""
                    wrapMode: Text.Wrap
                    color: Theme.textSoft
                }
                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    visible: text !== ""
                    text: root.flow?.actionId ?? ""
                    font.family: Theme.mono
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                    color: Theme.dim
                }

                Rectangle {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    Layout.topMargin: 4
                    implicitHeight: 48
                    radius: Theme.corner(24)
                    color: Theme.raised
                    border.width: input.activeFocus ? 2 : 1
                    border.color: root.error ? Theme.rose : input.activeFocus ? Theme.violet : Theme.lineStrong
                    Behavior on border.color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

                    TextInput {
                        id: input
                        anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 20; rightMargin: 44 }
                        verticalAlignment: TextInput.AlignVCenter
                        echoMode: root.flow?.responseVisible ? TextInput.Normal : TextInput.Password
                        passwordCharacter: "•"
                        color: Theme.text
                        font.family: Theme.sans
                        font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                        font.letterSpacing: echoMode === TextInput.Password ? 2 : 0
                        selectionColor: Theme.violetDeep
                        enabled: !!root.flow?.isResponseRequired
                        focus: root.shown
                        onVisibleChanged: if (visible) forceActiveFocus()
                        Keys.onReturnPressed: root.submit()
                        Keys.onEnterPressed: root.submit()
                        Keys.onEscapePressed: root.cancel()
                        Label {
                            anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
                            visible: !input.text
                            text: (root.flow?.inputPrompt ?? "").replace(/:\s*$/, "") || "Password"
                            color: Theme.dim
                            font.pixelSize: Theme.textMedium
                        }
                    }
                    Glyph {
                        anchors { right: parent.right; rightMargin: 18; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
                        text: root.flow?.isResponseRequired === false ? "󰔟" : "󰌆"
                        size: 15
                        color: Theme.dim
                    }
                }

                Label {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    visible: text !== ""
                    text: root.error || (root.flow?.supplementaryMessage ?? "")
                    wrapMode: Text.Wrap
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                    color: root.error || root.flow?.supplementaryIsError ? Theme.rose : Theme.muted
                }

                RowLayout {
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    Layout.topMargin: 4
                    spacing: 8
                    Item { Layout.fillWidth: true }
                    PillButton {
                        text: "Cancel"
                        implicitHeight: 36
                        implicitWidth: 96
                        onClicked: root.cancel()
                    }
                    PillButton {
                        text: "Authenticate"
                        primary: true
                        implicitHeight: 36
                        implicitWidth: 124
                        available: !!root.flow?.isResponseRequired
                        onClicked: root.submit()
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Recorder.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

// Screen recordings and capture countdowns. penumbra-record does the recording and keeps it in
// $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/penumbra/recording ("started-at\nfile"), so a recording outlives the shell; the
// bar shows it from there. A capture with a timer counts down here first, shown in the bar too.
Singleton {
    id: root

    property bool recording: false
    property real startedAt: 0 // seconds since the epoch
    property string file: ""
    property int elapsed: 0 // seconds
    readonly property string elapsedText: `${Math.floor(elapsed / 60)}:${String(elapsed % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`

    function refresh() {
        state.reload();
    }
    function stop() {
        Quickshell.execDetached([Ui.bin + "penumbra-record", "stop"]);
    }

    FileView {
        id: state
        path: (Quickshell.env("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") || "/tmp") + "/penumbra/recording"
        watchChanges: true
        printErrors: false
        onFileChanged: reload()
        onLoaded: {
            const [started, file] = text().split("\n");
            root.startedAt = Number(started) || 0;
            root.file = file ?? "";
            root.recording = root.startedAt > 0;
        }
        onLoadFailed: root.recording = false
    }
    Timer {
        interval: 1000
        repeat: true
        running: root.recording
        triggeredOnStart: true
        onTriggered: root.elapsed = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000 - root.startedAt))
    }

    // Run `command` after `delay` seconds of countdown. The countdown leaves the bar a moment before
    // the command runs, so a screenshot of the bar doesn't show it.
    property int countdown: 0
    property var pending: []
    function after(delay, command) {
        pending = command;
        countdown = delay;
        if (delay > 0)
            tick.restart();
        else
            go.restart();
    }
    function cancel() {
        tick.stop();
        countdown = 0;
        pending = [];
    }
    Timer {
        id: tick
        interval: 1000
        repeat: true
        onTriggered: {
            root.countdown -= 1;
            if (root.countdown <= 0) {
                tick.stop();
                go.restart();
            }
        }
    }
    // Also lets the compositor draw a frame without the overlay before the capture starts.
    Timer {
        id: go
        interval: 90
        onTriggered: {
            if (root.pending.length)
                Ui.spawn(root.pending);
            root.pending = [];
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/SceneLayer.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// One pre-rendered layer of a scene, placed where tools/build-wallpapers.py cropped it.
// `angle` turns it and `zoom` scales it around `pivot`, in scene coordinates.
Image {
    id: root
    required property WallpaperScene scene
    required property string name
    readonly property var info: scene.layers.find(layer => layer.name === name)
    property point pivot: Qt.point(info.x + info.width / 2, info.y + info.height / 2)
    property real angle: 0
    property real zoom: 1

    x: info.x
    y: info.y
    width: info.width
    height: info.height
    source: scene.folder + "/" + name + ".png"
    sourceSize: Qt.size(Math.ceil(info.width * scene.pixelScale), Math.ceil(info.height * scene.pixelScale))
    smooth: true
    transform: [
        Scale {
            origin.x: root.pivot.x - root.x
            origin.y: root.pivot.y - root.y
            xScale: root.zoom
            yScale: root.zoom
        },
        Rotation {
            origin.x: root.pivot.x - root.x
            origin.y: root.pivot.y - root.y
            angle: root.angle
        }
    ]
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/ScreenshotOverlay.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Wayland

// Print: the screen freezes; drag a region, click a window or click for the whole screen.
// The overlay then steps aside (no closing animation) and penumbra-shot captures the area, or
// penumbra-record records it (Video); a timer counts down in the bar first.
PanelWindow {
    id: root
    screen: Ui.screen
    anchors { top: true; bottom: true; left: true; right: true }
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    visible: Ui.shotOpen
    color: "transparent"
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-shot"
    WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: Ui.shotOpen ? WlrKeyboardFocus.Exclusive : WlrKeyboardFocus.None

    property string mode: "region" // region, window or screen
    property bool annotate: false
    property bool record: false
    property bool sound: false // kept between recordings
    property int delay: 0 // seconds
    readonly property var delays: [0, 3, 5, 10]
    property rect selection: Qt.rect(0, 0, 0, 0)
    property point origin
    property bool dragging: false
    property var hovered: null // window geometry under the pointer, in window mode

    readonly property var monitor: Hyprland.monitorFor(screen)
    // Windows on this screen's workspace in local coordinates, floating ones first (they're on top).
    readonly property var windows: (monitor?.activeWorkspace?.toplevels.values ?? [])
        .filter(t => t.lastIpcObject.size)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.lastIpcObject.floating ?? false) - (a.lastIpcObject.floating ?? false))
        .map(t => ({
            title: t.title,
            rect: Qt.rect(t.lastIpcObject.at[0] - monitor.x, t.lastIpcObject.at[1] - monitor.y, t.lastIpcObject.size[0], t.lastIpcObject.size[1])
        }))
    // What gets captured if the user confirms now.
    readonly property rect target: mode === "screen" ? Qt.rect(0, 0, width, height)
        : mode === "window" ? (hovered?.rect ?? Qt.rect(0, 0, 0, 0)) : selection

    function open(withAnnotate, withRecord) {
        Ui.openShot();
        annotate = withAnnotate;
        record = withRecord;
    }
    function nextDelay() {
        delay = delays[(delays.indexOf(delay) + 1) % delays.length];
    }
    // Start fresh however it opens (Print, or the control center's tile).
    Connections {
        target: Ui
        function onShotOpenChanged() {
            if (!Ui.shotOpen)
                return;
            Hyprland.refreshToplevels();
            root.annotate = false;
            root.record = false;
            root.delay = 0;
            root.mode = "region";
            root.selection = Qt.rect(0, 0, 0, 0);
            root.hovered = null;
            root.dragging = false;
        }
    }
    function capture() {
        const r = target;
        if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4)
            return;
        const geometry = `${Math.round(r.x + monitor.x)},${Math.round(r.y + monitor.y)} ${Math.round(r.width)}x${Math.round(r.height)}`;
        Ui.shotOpen = false;
        Recorder.after(delay, record
            ? [Ui.bin + "penumbra-record", "start", "--geometry", geometry, ...(sound ? ["--sound"] : [])]
            : [Ui.bin + "penumbra-shot", ...(annotate ? ["annotate"] : []), "--geometry", geometry]);
    }
    function windowAt(x, y) {
        return windows.find(w => x >= w.rect.x && x < w.rect.x + w.rect.width && y >= w.rect.y && y < w.rect.y + w.rect.height) ?? null;
    }

    // The frozen frame, taken once as the overlay opens.
    ScreencopyView {
        id: frame
        anchors.fill: parent
        captureSource: Ui.shotOpen ? root.screen : null
        live: false
    }

    Item {
        id: content
        anchors.fill: parent
        visible: frame.hasContent
        focus: Ui.shotOpen
        Keys.onPressed: event => {
            if (event.key === Qt.Key_Escape)
                Ui.shotOpen = false;
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_R)
                root.mode = "region";
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_W)
                root.mode = "window";
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_S)
                root.mode = "screen";
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_E && !root.record)
                root.annotate = !root.annotate;
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_V)
                root.record = !root.record;
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_A && root.record)
                root.sound = !root.sound;
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_T)
                root.nextDelay();
            else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Return || event.key === Qt.Key_Enter)
                root.capture();
            else
                return;
            event.accepted = true;
        }

        // Dim everything but the target: above, below, left and right of it.
        component Shade: Rectangle {
            color: Theme.alpha(Theme.scrim, 0.5)
        }
        Shade { width: root.width; height: Math.max(0, root.target.y) }
        Shade {
            y: root.target.y + root.target.height
            width: root.width
            height: Math.max(0, root.height - y)
        }
        Shade {
            y: root.target.y
            width: Math.max(0, root.target.x)
            height: root.target.height
        }
        Shade {
            x: root.target.x + root.target.width
            y: root.target.y
            width: Math.max(0, root.width - x)
            height: root.target.height
        }
        Rectangle {
            visible: root.target.width > 0 && root.target.height > 0 && root.mode !== "screen"
            x: root.target.x - 2
            y: root.target.y - 2
            width: root.target.width + 4
            height: root.target.height + 4
            color: "transparent"
            radius: Theme.corner(4)
            border.width: 2
            border.color: root.record ? Theme.rose : Theme.violet
            Rectangle {
                anchors { left: parent.left; top: parent.bottom; topMargin: 8 }
                visible: parent.width > 60
                implicitWidth: size.implicitWidth + 16
                implicitHeight: 22
                radius: Theme.corner(11)
                color: Theme.glassStrong
                Label {
                    id: size
                    anchors.centerIn: parent
                    text: `${Math.round(root.target.width)} × ${Math.round(root.target.height)}`
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                    color: Theme.textSoft
                }
            }
        }

        MouseArea {
            anchors.fill: parent
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: root.mode === "region" ? Qt.CrossCursor : Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onPressed: m => {
                if (root.mode !== "region")
                    return;
                root.origin = Qt.point(m.x, m.y);
                root.selection = Qt.rect(m.x, m.y, 0, 0);
                root.dragging = true;
            }
            onPositionChanged: m => {
                if (root.mode === "window")
                    root.hovered = root.windowAt(m.x, m.y);
                else if (root.dragging)
                    root.selection = Qt.rect(Math.min(m.x, root.origin.x), Math.min(m.y, root.origin.y),
                        Math.abs(m.x - root.origin.x), Math.abs(m.y - root.origin.y));
            }
            onReleased: {
                const wasDragging = root.dragging;
                root.dragging = false;
                if (root.mode !== "region" || wasDragging)
                    root.capture();
            }
        }

        // A toggle on the toolbar, lit in `tint` while on.
        component Chip: Rectangle {
            id: chip
            property string glyph
            property string text
            property bool on: false
            property color tint: Theme.violet
            signal clicked()
            implicitWidth: chipRow.implicitWidth + 24
            implicitHeight: 38
            radius: Theme.corner(19)
            color: on ? Theme.alpha(tint, 0.2) : chipArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
            border.color: on ? tint : "transparent"
            Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
            RowLayout {
                id: chipRow
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                spacing: 7
                Glyph { text: chip.glyph; size: 15; color: chip.on ? chip.tint : Theme.textSoft }
                Label { text: chip.text; color: chip.on ? chip.tint : Theme.text }
            }
            MouseArea {
                id: chipArea
                anchors.fill: parent
                hoverEnabled: true
                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                onClicked: chip.clicked()
            }
        }

        // Mode and after-capture choices.
        Rectangle {
            id: toolbar
            anchors { horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter; bottom: parent.bottom; bottomMargin: 48 }
            visible: !root.dragging
            implicitWidth: tools.implicitWidth + 16
            implicitHeight: 52
            radius: Theme.corner(26)
            color: Theme.glassStrong
            border.color: Theme.lineStrong

            // Clicks between the buttons stay on the toolbar.
            MouseArea { anchors.fill: parent }

            RowLayout {
                id: tools
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                spacing: 6
                Repeater {
                    model: [
                        { mode: "region", glyph: "󰆞", label: "Region", key: "R" },
                        { mode: "window", glyph: "󰖯", label: "Window", key: "W" },
                        { mode: "screen", glyph: "󰍹", label: "Screen", key: "S" }
                    ]
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        required property var modelData
                        readonly property bool current: root.mode === modelData.mode
                        implicitWidth: toolRow.implicitWidth + 24
                        implicitHeight: 38
                        radius: Theme.corner(19)
                        color: current ? Theme.violet : toolArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                        Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
                        RowLayout {
                            id: toolRow
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            spacing: 7
                            Glyph { text: modelData.glyph; size: 15; color: current ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.textSoft }
                            Label { text: modelData.label; font.weight: Font.DemiBold; color: current ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.text }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: toolArea
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: { root.mode = modelData.mode; root.hovered = null; }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Rectangle {
                    Layout.leftMargin: 4
                    Layout.rightMargin: 4
                    implicitWidth: 1
                    implicitHeight: 24
                    color: Theme.line
                }
                Chip {
                    glyph: "󰕧"
                    text: "Video"
                    on: root.record
                    tint: Theme.rose
                    onClicked: root.record = !root.record
                }
                Chip {
                    visible: !root.record
                    glyph: "󰏫"
                    text: "Annotate"
                    on: root.annotate
                    onClicked: root.annotate = !root.annotate
                }
                Chip {
                    visible: root.record
                    glyph: root.sound ? "󰕾" : "󰖁"
                    text: "Sound"
                    on: root.sound
                    tint: Theme.rose
                    onClicked: root.sound = !root.sound
                }
                Chip {
                    glyph: "󰔛"
                    text: root.delay ? `${root.delay} s` : "Timer"
                    on: root.delay > 0
                    onClicked: root.nextDelay()
                }
                Rectangle {
                    implicitWidth: 38
                    implicitHeight: 38
                    radius: Theme.corner(19)
                    color: closeArea.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : "transparent"
                    Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "󰅖"; size: 15; color: Theme.textSoft }
                    MouseArea {
                        id: closeArea
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        hoverEnabled: true
                        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                        onClicked: Ui.shotOpen = false
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Label {
            anchors { horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter; bottom: toolbar.top; bottomMargin: 12 }
            visible: !root.dragging
            text: (root.mode === "region" ? "Drag to select" : root.mode === "window" ? "Click a window" : "Click or press Enter for the whole screen")
                + (root.record ? " · records until you click the bar's red capsule or press Ctrl+Print"
                    : root.annotate ? " · opens in Satty" : " · saved and copied")
                + (root.delay ? ` · starts after ${root.delay} s` : "") + " · Esc to cancel"
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
            color: Theme.textSoft
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/SessionMenu.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Wayland

// Full-screen session overlay. Destructive choices need a second press to confirm.
PanelWindow {
    id: root
    screen: Ui.screen
    anchors { top: true; bottom: true; left: true; right: true }
    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
    visible: Ui.sessionOpen || backdrop.opacity > 0
    color: "transparent"
    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
    WlrLayershell.namespace: "penumbra-session"
    WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: Ui.sessionOpen ? WlrKeyboardFocus.Exclusive : WlrKeyboardFocus.None

    readonly property var entries: [
        { glyph: "󰌾", label: "Lock", command: "loginctl lock-session" },
        { glyph: "󰤄", label: "Suspend", command: "systemctl suspend" },
        { glyph: "󰍃", label: "Log out", command: "hyprctl dispatch 'hl.dsp.exit()'", confirm: true },
        { glyph: "󰜉", label: "Restart", command: "systemctl reboot", confirm: true },
        { glyph: "󰐥", label: "Shut down", command: "systemctl poweroff", confirm: true }
    ]
    property int selected: 0
    property int armed: -1

    Connections {
        target: Ui
        function onSessionOpenChanged() {
            root.selected = 0;
            root.armed = -1;
        }
    }

    function choose(index) {
        const entry = entries[index];
        if (entry.confirm && armed !== index) {
            armed = index;
            selected = index;
            return;
        }
        Ui.sessionOpen = false;
        Ui.run(entry.command);
    }

    Rectangle {
        id: backdrop
        anchors.fill: parent
        color: Theme.alpha(Theme.scrim, 0.62)
        opacity: Ui.sessionOpen ? 1 : 0
        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal } }

        focus: Ui.sessionOpen
        Keys.onEscapePressed: Ui.sessionOpen = false
        Keys.onLeftPressed: { root.selected = (root.selected + root.entries.length - 1) % root.entries.length; root.armed = -1; }
        Keys.onRightPressed: { root.selected = (root.selected + 1) % root.entries.length; root.armed = -1; }
        Keys.onTabPressed: { root.selected = (root.selected + 1) % root.entries.length; root.armed = -1; }
        Keys.onReturnPressed: root.choose(root.selected)
        Keys.onEnterPressed: root.choose(root.selected)
        Keys.onSpacePressed: root.choose(root.selected)

        MouseArea {
            anchors.fill: parent
            onClicked: Ui.sessionOpen = false
        }

        ColumnLayout {
            anchors.centerIn: parent
            spacing: 26
            scale: Ui.sessionOpen ? 1 : 0.94
            Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.slow; easing.type: Easing.OutExpo } }

            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                text: "S E S S I O N"
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                color: Theme.violet
            }

            RowLayout {
                spacing: 14
                Repeater {
                    model: root.entries
                    delegate: Rectangle {
                        id: tile
                        required property var modelData
                        required property int index
                        readonly property bool current: root.selected === index
                        readonly property bool isArmed: root.armed === index
                        implicitWidth: 132
                        implicitHeight: 140
                        radius: Theme.radiusLarge
                        color: isArmed ? Theme.alpha(Theme.rose, 0.22) : current ? Theme.raised : Theme.glassStrong
                        border.width: current ? 2 : 1
                        border.color: isArmed ? Theme.rose : current ? Theme.violet : Theme.line
                        scale: current ? 1.04 : 1
                        Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                        Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

                        ColumnLayout {
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            spacing: 12
                            Glyph {
                                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                                text: tile.modelData.glyph
                                size: 34
                                color: tile.isArmed ? Theme.rose : tile.current ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.textSoft
                            }
                            Label {
                                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                                text: tile.isArmed ? "Confirm" : tile.modelData.label
                                font.pixelSize: Theme.textMedium
                                font.weight: tile.current ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Normal
                                color: tile.isArmed ? Theme.rose : Theme.text
                            }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onEntered: if (root.armed !== tile.index) { root.selected = tile.index; root.armed = -1; }
                            onClicked: root.choose(tile.index)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Label {
                Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                text: root.armed >= 0 ? `Press again to ${root.entries[root.armed].label.toLowerCase()} · Esc to cancel` : "← → to choose · Enter to select · Esc to close"
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                color: root.armed >= 0 ? Theme.rose : Theme.muted
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Settings.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

// Shell preferences from the settings page, kept in ~/.config/penumbra/shell.json. The corner
// style is penumbra-corners' instead (see Theme), since Hyprland and other apps follow it too.
Singleton {
    property alias animatedWallpapers: adapter.animatedWallpapers
    property alias hotCorner: adapter.hotCorner

    FileView {
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.config/penumbra/shell.json"
        watchChanges: true
        printErrors: false
        onFileChanged: reload()
        onAdapterUpdated: writeAdapter()
        // The first run has no file yet: write the defaults.
        onLoadFailed: error => {
            if (error === FileViewError.FileNotFound)
                writeAdapter();
        }

        JsonAdapter {
            id: adapter
            property bool animatedWallpapers: true
            property bool hotCorner: true
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/SettingsPage.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts

// Settings: the corner style, the wallpaper and a few shell behaviours.
ColumnLayout {
    id: root

    readonly property var wallpaperNames: ({ "wallpaper": "Night", "wallpaper-dawn": "Dawn", "wallpaper-eclipse": "Eclipse" })

    spacing: 12

    component Section: Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
    }

    // A labelled on/off switch; the whole row toggles.
    component SwitchRow: Rectangle {
        id: row
        property string glyph
        property string title
        property string subtitle
        property bool checked: false
        signal toggled()

        Layout.fillWidth: true
        implicitHeight: 52
        radius: Theme.radiusSmall
        color: rowArea.containsMouse ? Theme.raised : "transparent"
        Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

        MouseArea {
            id: rowArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: row.toggled()
        }
        RowLayout {
            anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 10; rightMargin: 10 }
            spacing: 11
            Glyph { Layout.preferredWidth: 22; text: row.glyph; size: 17 }
            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 0
                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: row.title }
                Label { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: row.subtitle; font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall; color: Theme.muted }
            }
            Switch {
                checked: row.checked
                onToggled: row.toggled()
            }
        }
    }

    PageHeader {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        title: "Settings"
        status: "Super+, opens this page"
        showSwitch: false
        onBack: Ui.controlPage = "main"
    }

    Section { text: "Corners" }

    // Each card draws a little bar and window in its own style.
    RowLayout {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        spacing: 8
        Repeater {
            model: [
                { name: "rounded", title: "Rounded", round: true },
                { name: "sharp", title: "Sharp", round: false }
            ]
            delegate: Rectangle {
                id: choice
                required property var modelData
                readonly property bool current: Theme.corners === modelData.name
                readonly property color ink: current ? Theme.violet : Theme.lineStrong
                function r(px) { return modelData.round ? px : 0; }

                Layout.fillWidth: true
                Layout.preferredWidth: 1 // equal halves
                implicitHeight: 118
                radius: Theme.radius
                color: current ? Theme.alpha(Theme.violet, 0.12) : area.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
                border.width: current ? 2 : 1
                border.color: current ? Theme.violet : Theme.line
                Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

                Item {
                    id: preview
                    anchors { top: parent.top; topMargin: 16; horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter }
                    width: 116
                    height: 62
                    Rectangle {
                        width: parent.width
                        height: 10
                        radius: choice.r(5)
                        color: Theme.glass
                        border.color: choice.ink
                        Rectangle { x: 4; y: 3; width: 14; height: 4; radius: choice.r(2); color: choice.ink }
                    }
                    Rectangle {
                        y: 16
                        width: parent.width
                        height: parent.height - y
                        radius: choice.r(9)
                        color: Theme.surface
                        border.color: choice.ink
                        Rectangle { x: 9; y: 10; width: 44; height: 5; radius: choice.r(2.5); color: choice.ink }
                        Rectangle { x: 9; y: 21; width: 70; height: 4; radius: choice.r(2); color: Theme.line }
                        Rectangle { x: 9; y: 30; width: 56; height: 4; radius: choice.r(2); color: Theme.line }
                    }
                }
                RowLayout {
                    anchors { bottom: parent.bottom; bottomMargin: 12; horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter }
                    spacing: 6
                    Rectangle {
                        visible: choice.current
                        implicitWidth: 6
                        implicitHeight: 6
                        radius: Theme.corner(3)
                        color: Theme.violet
                    }
                    Label {
                        text: choice.modelData.title
                        font.weight: choice.current ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Normal
                        color: choice.current ? Theme.text : Theme.textSoft
                    }
                }
                MouseArea {
                    id: area
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    hoverEnabled: true
                    cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                    onClicked: {
                        if (!choice.current)
                            Ui.run(Ui.bin + "penumbra-corners " + choice.modelData.name);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Label {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        text: "The shell, windows, launcher and lock screen follow it"
        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
        color: Theme.dim
    }

    Section { text: "Desktop" }

    ColumnLayout {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        spacing: 2
        ListRow {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            glyph: "󰸉"
            title: "Wallpaper"
            subtitle: root.wallpaperNames[Ui.wallpaper] ?? Ui.wallpaper
            trailing: "󰅂"
            onClicked: Ui.toggleWallpapers()
        }
        SwitchRow {
            glyph: "󰐊"
            title: "Animated wallpapers"
            subtitle: Settings.animatedWallpapers ? "Dawn and eclipse move" : "Still images"
            checked: Settings.animatedWallpapers
            onToggled: Settings.animatedWallpapers = !Settings.animatedWallpapers
        }
        SwitchRow {
            glyph: "󰆾"
            title: "Hot corner"
            subtitle: "Top-right corner opens the control center"
            checked: Settings.hotCorner
            onToggled: Settings.hotCorner = !Settings.hotCorner
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Shadow.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Effects

// The soft shadow under a floating surface. Hyprland only shadows windows, so shell surfaces
// draw their own. Declare it just before the surface it lies under.
RectangularShadow {
    required property Item target

    anchors.fill: target
    radius: target.radius
    offset.y: 6
    blur: 26
    spread: -4
    color: Qt.rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)
    opacity: target.opacity
    scale: target.scale
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/SliderRow.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell.Widgets

// Glyph (or app icon) button for mute, plus a soft violet slider, 0–100.
RowLayout {
    id: root

    property string glyph
    property string icon: ""
    property real value: 0
    property bool muted: false
    signal moved(real value)
    signal glyphClicked()

    spacing: 10

    Rectangle {
        implicitWidth: 36
        implicitHeight: 36
        radius: Theme.corner(18)
        color: button.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
        Glyph {
            anchors.centerIn: parent
            visible: root.icon === ""
            text: root.glyph
            size: 16
            color: root.muted ? Theme.dim : Theme.violetBright
        }
        IconImage {
            anchors.centerIn: parent
            visible: root.icon !== ""
            implicitSize: 20
            source: root.icon
            opacity: root.muted ? 0.4 : 1
        }
        MouseArea {
            id: button
            anchors.fill: parent
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: root.glyphClicked()
        }
    }

    Slider {
        id: slider
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        // The custom background and handle give no implicit height, which would leave a 0 px hit area.
        implicitHeight: 36
        from: 0
        to: 100
        value: root.value
        onMoved: root.moved(value)
        background: Rectangle {
            x: slider.leftPadding
            y: slider.topPadding + slider.availableHeight / 2 - height / 2
            width: slider.availableWidth
            height: 8
            radius: Theme.corner(4)
            color: Theme.raised
            Rectangle {
                width: slider.visualPosition * parent.width
                height: parent.height
                radius: Theme.corner(4)
                gradient: Gradient {
                    orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
                    GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violetDeep }
                    GradientStop { position: 1; color: root.muted ? Theme.dim : Theme.violet }
                }
            }
        }
        handle: Rectangle {
            x: slider.leftPadding + slider.visualPosition * (slider.availableWidth - width)
            y: slider.topPadding + slider.availableHeight / 2 - height / 2
            width: slider.pressed ? 18 : 16
            height: width
            radius: Theme.corner(width / 2)
            color: Theme.white
            border.color: Theme.violet
            border.width: 2
            Behavior on width { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
        }
    }

    Label {
        Layout.preferredWidth: 34
        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignRight
        text: Math.round(root.value) + "%"
        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
        color: Theme.muted
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Stars.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// A scene's stars, each twinkling on its own rhythm; glint stars flash a four-point sparkle at their brightest.
Item {
    id: root
    required property WallpaperScene scene
    required property var stars
    property color color: "#e9c8f2"

    Repeater {
        model: root.stars

        delegate: Item {
            id: star
            required property var modelData
            required property int index
            readonly property real twinkle: 0.8 + 0.45 * root.scene.wave(3.5 + index * 0.73 % 4, index * 2.4)
            readonly property real flash: Math.max(0, twinkle - 0.85) / 0.4
            x: modelData.x
            y: modelData.y

            Rectangle {
                x: -width / 2
                y: -height / 2
                width: star.modelData.r * 2
                height: width
                radius: width / 2
                color: root.color
                opacity: star.modelData.opacity * star.twinkle
            }

            Item {
                visible: star.modelData.glint && star.flash > 0
                opacity: star.flash
                scale: 0.6 + 0.6 * star.flash
                Rectangle {
                    x: -width / 2
                    y: -height / 2
                    width: 56
                    height: 1.2
                    gradient: Gradient {
                        orientation: Gradient.Horizontal
                        GradientStop { position: 0; color: "transparent" }
                        GradientStop { position: 0.5; color: root.color }
                        GradientStop { position: 1; color: "transparent" }
                    }
                }
                Rectangle {
                    x: -width / 2
                    y: -height / 2
                    width: 1.2
                    height: 40
                    gradient: Gradient {
                        GradientStop { position: 0; color: "transparent" }
                        GradientStop { position: 0.5; color: root.color }
                        GradientStop { position: 1; color: "transparent" }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Switch.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// Pill switch.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    property bool checked: false
    signal toggled()

    implicitWidth: 44
    implicitHeight: 24
    radius: Theme.corner(12)
    color: checked ? Theme.violet : Theme.raised
    border.color: checked ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.lineStrong
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

    Rectangle {
        width: 18
        height: 18
        radius: Theme.corner(9)
        y: 3
        x: root.checked ? root.width - width - 3 : 3
        color: root.checked ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.textSoft
        Behavior on x { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.fast; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
    }

    MouseArea {
        anchors.fill: parent
        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
        onClicked: root.toggled()
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Sys.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

// Polled system state: backlight and pending updates. Resource usage is in Usage.
Singleton {
    id: root

    property int brightness: -1 // -1 when the machine has no backlight
    property int updates: 0
    property string updatesDetail: ""
    property var updateSources: [] // [{ name, packages: ["name old -> new" or a Flatpak ref] }]
    property bool checkingUpdates: updateCheck.running

    // brightnessctl -m prints: device,class,current,percent%,max
    Process {
        id: backlight
        command: ["brightnessctl", "-m", "--class=backlight", "info"]
        running: true
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                const parts = text.trim().split(",");
                root.brightness = parts.length >= 4 ? parseInt(parts[3]) : -1;
            }
        }
    }
    function refreshBrightness() {
        backlight.running = true;
    }
    // Brightness keys: 5% steps, never down to black; the popup shows the result.
    Process {
        id: brightnessStep
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                const parts = text.trim().split(",");
                if (parts.length < 4)
                    return;
                root.brightness = parseInt(parts[3]);
                Ui.showOsd("brightness", root.brightness, false);
            }
        }
    }
    function stepBrightness(action) {
        if (root.brightness < 0)
            return false;
        if (action === "current") {
            Ui.showOsd("brightness", root.brightness, false);
            return true;
        }
        brightnessStep.command = ["brightnessctl", "-m", "--class=backlight", "--min-value=1", "set", action === "up" ? "5%+" : "5%-"];
        brightnessStep.running = true;
        return true;
    }
    function setBrightness(percent) {
        root.brightness = Math.max(1, Math.round(percent));
        brightnessDebounce.restart();
    }
    Timer {
        id: brightnessDebounce
        interval: 60
        onTriggered: Quickshell.execDetached(["brightnessctl", "--class=backlight", "--min-value=1", "set", root.brightness + "%"])
    }

    Process {
        id: updateCheck
        command: [Ui.bin + "penumbra-updates", "status"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                try {
                    const status = JSON.parse(text);
                    root.updates = parseInt(status.text.replace(/\D/g, "")) || 0;
                    root.updatesDetail = status.tooltip.replace(/&amp;/g, "&").replace(/&lt;/g, "<").replace(/&gt;/g, ">");
                    root.updateSources = status.sources ?? [];
                } catch (e) {
                    root.updates = 0;
                    root.updateSources = [];
                }
            }
        }
    }
    function refreshUpdates() {
        updateCheck.running = true;
    }
    Timer {
        interval: 15 * 60 * 1000
        running: true
        repeat: true
        triggeredOnStart: true
        onTriggered: root.refreshUpdates()
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Theme.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import "palette.js" as P

// PENUMBRA palette, type and geometry, shared by every shell surface.
Singleton {
    id: root

    // Colours come from palette.js, which tools/build-palette.py writes from palette/palette.toml.
    readonly property color base: P.base
    readonly property color surface: P.surface
    readonly property color glass: alpha(P.surface, 0.72)
    readonly property color glassStrong: alpha(P.surface, 0.88)
    readonly property color raised: alpha(P.glassRaised, 0.55)
    readonly property color hover: P.hover
    readonly property color line: alpha(P.violet, 0.16)
    readonly property color lineStrong: alpha(P.violet, 0.34)
    // Behind full-screen overlays (launcher, session menu, polkit, screenshots).
    readonly property color scrim: P.shade
    readonly property color violet: P.violet
    readonly property color violetBright: P.violetBright
    readonly property color violetDeep: P.violetDeep
    readonly property color violetInk: P.violetInk
    readonly property color white: P.white
    readonly property color text: P.text
    readonly property color textSoft: P.textSoft
    readonly property color subtle: P.subtle
    readonly property color muted: P.muted
    readonly property color dim: P.dim
    readonly property color rose: P.rose
    readonly property color green: P.green
    readonly property color amber: P.amber
    // The system monitor's series.
    readonly property color blue: P.blue
    readonly property color pink: P.pink
    readonly property color cyan: P.cyan

    // A palette colour made translucent.
    function alpha(c, a) {
        const q = Qt.lighter(c, 1);
        return Qt.rgba(q.r, q.g, q.b, a);
    }

    readonly property string sans: "Inter"
    // Inter's optical size for large text: tighter spacing and finer detail from about 20 px up.
    readonly property string display: "Inter Display"
    readonly property string mono: "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font"
    // The distribution's logo on the bar's launcher button, set by install.sh from distro/NAME/distro.sh.
    readonly property string distroGlyph: "@@DISTRO_GLYPH@@"

    // The type scale. Every text size in the shell is one of these.
    readonly property int textTiny: 10     // badges, timestamps
    readonly property int textSmall: 11    // subtitles, section labels
    readonly property int textBody: 12     // the default
    readonly property int textMedium: 13   // notification titles, emphasized rows
    readonly property int textLarge: 15    // page titles, the bar clock
    readonly property int textTitle: 18    // the launcher's search, big figures
    readonly property int textHeading: 22  // panel headlines, in the display face
    readonly property int textDisplay: 124 // the lock screen clock

    // Rounded or sharp corners, as penumbra-corners records them. Every radius goes through
    // corner(), so the whole shell follows the choice at once.
    property string corners: "rounded"
    readonly property bool sharp: corners === "sharp"
    function corner(px) { return sharp ? 0 : px; }
    FileView {
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/state/penumbra/corners"
        watchChanges: true
        printErrors: false
        onFileChanged: reload()
        onLoaded: root.corners = text().trim() || "rounded"
    }

    readonly property int radiusLarge: corner(22)
    readonly property int radius: corner(16)
    readonly property int radiusSmall: corner(12)
    readonly property int barHeight: 40
    readonly property int gap: 8
    readonly property int edge: 14

    readonly property int fast: 160
    readonly property int normal: 260
    readonly property int slow: 420
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Tile.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts

// Quick-settings toggle: click the body to toggle, the chevron for more.
Rectangle {
    id: root

    property string glyph
    property string title
    property string subtitle
    property bool on: false
    property bool available: true
    property bool hasMore: false
    signal toggled()
    signal more()

    implicitHeight: 62
    radius: Theme.radius
    // On is a violet tint with the accent on the icon's disc; a solid fill outweighed the panel.
    color: on ? Theme.alpha(Theme.violet, body.containsMouse ? 0.3 : 0.22) : body.containsMouse ? Theme.hover : Theme.raised
    border.color: on ? Theme.alpha(Theme.violetBright, 0.55) : Theme.line
    opacity: available ? 1 : 0.45
    Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }

    MouseArea {
        id: body
        anchors.fill: parent
        hoverEnabled: true
        enabled: root.available
        cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
        onClicked: root.toggled()
    }

    RowLayout {
        anchors { fill: parent; leftMargin: 12; rightMargin: root.hasMore ? 6 : 14 }
        spacing: 11
        Rectangle {
            implicitWidth: 36
            implicitHeight: 36
            radius: Theme.corner(18)
            color: root.on ? Theme.violet : Theme.alpha(Theme.violet, 0.12)
            Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: Theme.fast } }
            Glyph {
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                text: root.glyph
                size: 17
                color: root.on ? Theme.violetInk : Theme.violetBright
            }
        }
        ColumnLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 1
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                text: root.title
                font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                color: Theme.text
            }
            Label {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                text: root.subtitle
                font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                color: root.on ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.muted
            }
        }
        Rectangle {
            visible: root.hasMore
            implicitWidth: 30
            implicitHeight: 44
            radius: Theme.radiusSmall
            color: more.containsMouse ? Qt.rgba(1, 1, 1, 0.08) : "transparent"
            Glyph {
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                text: "󰅂"
                color: root.on ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.muted
            }
            MouseArea {
                id: more
                anchors.fill: parent
                hoverEnabled: true
                enabled: root.available
                cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                onClicked: root.more()
            }
        }
    }
}
```
