## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Ui.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Io

// Which surfaces are open, and the screen they open on.
Singleton {
    id: root

    property bool controlOpen: false
    property string controlPage: "main" // main, wifi, bluetooth, mixer or settings
    property bool calendarOpen: false
    property bool monitorOpen: false
    property bool updatesOpen: false
    property bool wallpapersOpen: false
    property bool sessionOpen: false
    property bool launcherOpen: false
    property string launcherMode: "apps" // apps, clipboard, emoji or symbols
    property bool shotOpen: false
    property bool caffeine: false
    property bool nightLight: false

    property string osdKind: "volume"
    property int osdValue: 0
    property bool osdMuted: false
    property bool osdVisible: false

    // Panels follow the monitor that has focus.
    readonly property var screen: Quickshell.screens.find(s => s.name === Hyprland.focusedMonitor?.name) ?? Quickshell.screens[0]

    function close() {
        controlOpen = false;
        calendarOpen = false;
        monitorOpen = false;
        updatesOpen = false;
        wallpapersOpen = false;
    }
    // Full-screen overlays replace each other and any open panel.
    function closeOverlays() {
        close();
        sessionOpen = false;
        launcherOpen = false;
        shotOpen = false;
    }
    // A click outside a panel closes it; ignore that same click reopening it from the bar.
    property double closedAt: 0
    function dismiss() {
        closedAt = Date.now();
        close();
    }
    function toggleControl() {
        const reopen = !controlOpen && Date.now() - closedAt > 250;
        close();
        if (reopen)
            controlPage = "main";
        controlOpen = reopen;
    }
    function openControl(page) {
        close();
        controlPage = page;
        controlOpen = true;
    }
    function toggleCalendar() {
        const reopen = !calendarOpen && Date.now() - closedAt > 250;
        close();
        calendarOpen = reopen;
    }
    function toggleMonitor() {
        const reopen = !monitorOpen && Date.now() - closedAt > 250;
        close();
        monitorOpen = reopen;
    }
    function toggleUpdates() {
        const reopen = !updatesOpen && Date.now() - closedAt > 250;
        close();
        updatesOpen = reopen;
    }
    function toggleWallpapers() {
        const reopen = !wallpapersOpen && Date.now() - closedAt > 250;
        close();
        wallpapersOpen = reopen;
    }
    function openSession() {
        closeOverlays();
        sessionOpen = true;
    }
    function toggleLauncher(mode) {
        const reopen = !(launcherOpen && launcherMode === mode);
        closeOverlays();
        launcherMode = mode;
        launcherOpen = reopen;
    }
    function openShot() {
        closeOverlays();
        shotOpen = true;
    }

    function showOsd(kind, value, muted) {
        osdKind = kind;
        osdValue = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value));
        osdMuted = muted;
        osdVisible = true;
        osdTimer.restart();
    }
    Timer {
        id: osdTimer
        interval: 1500
        onTriggered: root.osdVisible = false
    }

    // The chosen wallpaper (wallpaper, wallpaper-dawn…), as penumbra-wallpaper records it.
    property string wallpaper: "wallpaper"
    readonly property string wallpapers: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/share/penumbra/"
    FileView {
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/state/penumbra/wallpaper"
        watchChanges: true
        printErrors: false
        onFileChanged: reload()
        onLoaded: root.wallpaper = text().trim() || "wallpaper"
    }

    // Optional tools, found once at startup.
    property bool hasNightLight: false
    property bool hasVisualizer: false
    property bool hasTyping: false // wtype types emoji and symbols into the focused window
    Process {
        running: true
        command: ["bash", "-c", "command -v hyprsunset; command -v cava; command -v wtype"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                root.hasNightLight = text.includes("/hyprsunset");
                root.hasVisualizer = text.includes("/cava");
                root.hasTyping = text.includes("/wtype");
            }
        }
    }
    // Night light: hyprsunset warms the screen for as long as it runs.
    readonly property int nightTemperature: 4500
    Process {
        running: root.nightLight && root.hasNightLight
        command: ["hyprsunset", "--temperature", String(root.nightTemperature)]
    }

    // Start a program in its own systemd scope (under app.slice), so it outlives the shell: the
    // shell runs as penumbra-shell.service, and restarting it must not take the apps along.
    // `command` is an argument list, or { command, workingDirectory }.
    function spawn(command) {
        const scope = ["systemd-run", "--user", "--scope", "--slice=app.slice", "--collect", "--quiet", "--"];
        if (Array.isArray(command))
            Quickshell.execDetached(scope.concat(command));
        else
            Quickshell.execDetached({ command: scope.concat(command.command), workingDirectory: command.workingDirectory });
    }
    function run(command) {
        spawn(["bash", "-c", command]);
    }
    readonly property string bin: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/bin/"
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/UpdatesPanel.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts

// Pending updates by source, under the updates capsule; "Update now" opens the Kitty session.
Panel {
    id: root
    anchors { top: true; right: true }
    margins.right: Theme.edge - shadowRoom
    sheetWidth: 400
    open: Ui.updatesOpen
    onDismissed: Ui.dismiss()

    ColumnLayout {
        x: 18
        y: 18
        width: root.sheetWidth - 36
        spacing: 12

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 12
            Rectangle {
                implicitWidth: 42
                implicitHeight: 42
                radius: Theme.corner(21)
                gradient: Gradient {
                    GradientStop { position: 0; color: Theme.violet }
                    GradientStop { position: 1; color: Theme.violetDeep }
                }
                Glyph { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "󰚰"; size: 19; color: Theme.violetInk }
            }
            ColumnLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                spacing: 1
                Label {
                    text: Sys.updates === 1 ? "1 update" : `${Sys.updates} updates`
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
                    font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                }
                Label {
                    text: Sys.checkingUpdates ? "Checking…" : "Checked every 15 minutes"
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                    color: Theme.muted
                }
            }
        }

        Flickable {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(contentHeight, 380)
            contentHeight: list.implicitHeight
            clip: true
            boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds

            ColumnLayout {
                id: list
                width: parent.width
                spacing: 6

                Repeater {
                    model: Sys.updateSources
                    delegate: ColumnLayout {
                        id: source
                        required property var modelData
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        spacing: 2

                        Overline {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            Layout.topMargin: 4
                            Layout.bottomMargin: 2
                            text: `${source.modelData.name} · ${source.modelData.packages.length}`
                        }
                        Repeater {
                            model: source.modelData.packages
                            // "name old -> new", or just a Flatpak ref.
                            delegate: RowLayout {
                                required property string modelData
                                readonly property var parts: modelData.match(/^(\S+)\s+(\S+)\s+->\s+(\S+)$/)
                                Layout.fillWidth: true
                                Layout.leftMargin: 4
                                spacing: 8
                                Label {
                                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                                    text: parent.parts ? parent.parts[1] : parent.modelData
                                    font.family: Theme.mono
                                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
                                }
                                Label {
                                    visible: !!parent.parts
                                    text: parent.parts ? `${parent.parts[2]} → ${parent.parts[3]}` : ""
                                    Layout.maximumWidth: 190
                                    font.family: Theme.mono
                                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                                    color: Theme.violet
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            Layout.topMargin: 4
            spacing: 8
            Item { Layout.fillWidth: true }
            PillButton {
                text: "Check again"
                implicitHeight: 34
                available: !Sys.checkingUpdates
                onClicked: Sys.refreshUpdates()
            }
            PillButton {
                text: "Update now"
                primary: true
                implicitHeight: 34
                onClicked: {
                    Ui.close();
                    Ui.run(Ui.bin + "penumbra-updates open");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Usage.qml`

```qml
pragma Singleton
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

// Resource usage: CPU, memory, GPUs, disks and network, with two minutes of history for the
// monitor's graphs. The /proc counters are cheap and always read, so the graphs already have a
// past when the monitor opens; temperatures, clocks, GPUs and filesystems are only read while
// it is open.
Singleton {
    id: root

    readonly property int interval: 2000
    readonly property int historyLength: 60
    readonly property bool watching: Ui.monitorOpen

    property int cpu: 0
    property int cores: 1
    property var coreLoads: [] // percent, one per thread
    property string cpuModel: ""
    property real cpuGhz: 0
    property int cpuTemp: -1
    property string loadAverage: ""
    property var cpuHistory: []

    // KiB, as /proc/meminfo counts.
    property int memory: 0
    property real memoryUsed: 0
    property real memoryCache: 0 // page cache the kernel gives back when asked
    property real memoryTotal: 0
    property real swapUsed: 0
    property real swapTotal: 0
    property string memoryDetail: ""
    property var memoryHistory: []

    // Bytes per second across the physical disks, and bytes for filesystems.
    property real diskRead: 0
    property real diskWrite: 0
    property var diskHistory: []
    property var filesystems: [] // [{ name, mount, used, size }]

    // The interface with the default route; bytes per second, and bytes since it came up.
    property string netInterface: ""
    property real netDown: 0
    property real netUp: 0
    property real netReceived: 0
    property real netSent: 0
    property var downHistory: []
    property var upHistory: []

    // [{ card, driver, name, integrated, asleep, busy, memUsed, memTotal, temp, power, freq, maxFreq, history }]
    // Memory in MiB, temperature in °C, power in W, clocks in MHz; -1 where the driver says nothing.
    property var gpus: []

    // Previous counters, for rates.
    property var lastCpu: null
    property var lastCores: []
    property var lastDisk: null
    property var lastNet: null
    property var lastGpu: ({})
    property string cpuTempPath: ""

    function push(list, value) {
        return list.concat([value]).slice(-historyLength);
    }

    Timer {
        interval: root.interval
        running: true
        repeat: true
        triggeredOnStart: true
        onTriggered: {
            stat.reload();
            meminfo.reload();
            route.reload();
            netdev.reload();
            diskstats.reload();
            if (root.watching)
                root.readDetails();
        }
    }
    function readDetails() {
        cpuinfo.reload();
        loadavg.reload();
        if (cpuTempPath)
            cpuTemp.reload();
        if (gpus.length && !gpuSample.running)
            gpuSample.running = true;
    }
    // Filesystems change rarely; ask on opening, then every half minute. Intel's GPU busy time
    // is a difference between samples, so a stale sample from the last opening is dropped.
    onWatchingChanged: {
        lastGpu = {};
        if (watching) {
            readDetails();
            df.running = true;
        }
    }
    Timer {
        interval: 30000
        running: root.watching
        repeat: true
        onTriggered: df.running = true
    }

    FileView {
        id: stat
        path: "/proc/stat"
        onLoaded: {
            const lines = text().split("\n").filter(l => l.startsWith("cpu"));
            const times = lines.map(l => {
                const f = l.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(1).map(Number);
                return { idle: f[3] + f[4], total: f.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) };
            });
            const busy = (now, before) => {
                const dt = now.total - before.total;
                return dt > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 * (1 - (now.idle - before.idle) / dt))) : 0;
            };
            root.cores = times.length - 1 || 1;
            if (root.lastCpu) {
                root.cpu = Math.round(busy(times[0], root.lastCpu));
                root.cpuHistory = root.push(root.cpuHistory, root.cpu);
            }
            if (root.lastCores.length === times.length - 1)
                root.coreLoads = times.slice(1).map((t, i) => busy(t, root.lastCores[i]));
            root.lastCpu = times[0];
            root.lastCores = times.slice(1);
        }
    }

    FileView {
        id: cpuinfo
        path: "/proc/cpuinfo"
        onLoaded: {
            const mhz = (text().match(/^cpu MHz\s*:\s*([\d.]+)/gm) ?? []).map(l => Number(l.split(":")[1]));
            root.cpuGhz = mhz.length ? mhz.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / mhz.length / 1000 : 0;
            if (!root.cpuModel) {
                const model = text().match(/^model name\s*:\s*(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? "";
                root.cpuModel = model.replace(/\((R|TM)\)/g, "").replace(/ CPU @.*$| \d+-Core Processor$/, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
            }
        }
    }

    FileView {
        id: loadavg
        path: "/proc/loadavg"
        onLoaded: root.loadAverage = text().split(" ")[0]
    }

    // The package sensor: coretemp on Intel, k10temp or zenpower on AMD.
    Process {
        running: true
        command: ["sh", "-c", "for h in /sys/class/hwmon/hwmon*; do case $(cat $h/name) in coretemp|k10temp|zenpower|cpu_thermal) echo $h/temp1_input; exit;; esac; done"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: root.cpuTempPath = text.trim()
        }
    }
    FileView {
        id: cpuTemp
        path: root.cpuTempPath
        printErrors: false
        onLoaded: root.cpuTemp = Math.round(Number(text()) / 1000)
    }

    FileView {
        id: meminfo
        path: "/proc/meminfo"
        onLoaded: {
            const kb = key => Number(text().match(new RegExp("^" + key + ":\\s+(\\d+)", "m"))?.[1] ?? 0);
            const total = kb("MemTotal"), available = kb("MemAvailable"), used = total - available;
            root.memoryTotal = total;
            root.memoryUsed = used;
            root.memoryCache = Math.max(0, available - kb("MemFree"));
            root.swapTotal = kb("SwapTotal");
            root.swapUsed = root.swapTotal - kb("SwapFree");
            root.memory = Math.round(100 * used / total);
            root.memoryDetail = `${(used / 1048576).toFixed(1)} / ${(total / 1048576).toFixed(1)} GiB`;
            root.memoryHistory = root.push(root.memoryHistory, 100 * used / total);
        }
    }

    // Whole disks only: partitions would count the same bytes twice, and zram is memory.
    FileView {
        id: diskstats
        path: "/proc/diskstats"
        onLoaded: {
            let read = 0, written = 0;
            for (const line of text().split("\n")) {
                const f = line.trim().split(/\s+/);
                if (/^(nvme\d+n\d+|sd[a-z]+|vd[a-z]+|xvd[a-z]+|hd[a-z]+|mmcblk\d+)$/.test(f[2] ?? "")) {
                    read += Number(f[5]) * 512;
                    written += Number(f[9]) * 512;
                }
            }
            const now = Date.now(), last = root.lastDisk;
            if (last) {
                const dt = (now - last.time) / 1000;
                root.diskRead = Math.max(0, (read - last.read) / dt);
                root.diskWrite = Math.max(0, (written - last.written) / dt);
                root.diskHistory = root.push(root.diskHistory, root.diskRead + root.diskWrite);
            }
            root.lastDisk = { read, written, time: now };
        }
    }

    Process {
        id: df
        command: ["env", "LC_ALL=C", "df", "--local", "-B1", "--output=source,size,used,target"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                // Btrfs subvolumes and bind mounts list one device several times; keep its
                // shortest mount point. Boot partitions are not worth a row.
                const found = {};
                for (const line of text.trim().split("\n").slice(1)) {
                    const m = line.match(/^(\/dev\/\S+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(.+)$/);
                    if (!m || /^\/(boot|efi)(\/|$)/.test(m[4]))
                        continue;
                    if (!found[m[1]] || m[4].length < found[m[1]].mount.length)
                        found[m[1]] = { mount: m[4], size: Number(m[2]), used: Number(m[3]) };
                }
                root.filesystems = Object.values(found).sort((a, b) => a.mount.length - b.mount.length).map(f => Object.assign(f, {
                    name: f.mount === "/" ? "System" : f.mount === "/home" ? "Home" : f.mount.split("/").pop()
                }));
            }
        }
    }

    // The default route's interface: Destination 00000000, lowest metric.
    FileView {
        id: route
        path: "/proc/net/route"
        onLoaded: {
            const routes = text().trim().split("\n").slice(1).map(l => l.trim().split(/\s+/)).filter(f => f[1] === "00000000");
            routes.sort((a, b) => Number(a[6]) - Number(b[6]));
            root.netInterface = routes[0]?.[0] ?? "";
        }
    }
    FileView {
        id: netdev
        path: "/proc/net/dev"
        onLoaded: {
            let rx = 0, tx = 0;
            for (const line of text().split("\n").slice(2)) {
                const [name, rest] = line.split(":");
                const iface = name?.trim(), f = rest?.trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
                if (!f || iface === "lo" || (root.netInterface && iface !== root.netInterface))
                    continue;
                rx += f[0];
                tx += f[8];
            }
            const now = Date.now(), last = root.lastNet;
            root.netReceived = rx;
            root.netSent = tx;
            // A new interface starts from its own counters; skip the jump between them.
            if (last && last.iface === root.netInterface) {
                const dt = (now - last.time) / 1000;
                root.netDown = Math.max(0, (rx - last.rx) / dt);
                root.netUp = Math.max(0, (tx - last.tx) / dt);
            } else {
                root.netDown = root.netUp = 0;
            }
            if (last) {
                root.downHistory = root.push(root.downHistory, root.netDown);
                root.upHistory = root.push(root.upHistory, root.netUp);
            }
            root.lastNet = { rx, tx, time: now, iface: root.netInterface };
        }
    }

    // GPUs come from bin/penumbra-gpus, which never wakes a sleeping one.
    Process {
        running: true
        command: [Ui.bin + "penumbra-gpus", "list"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                root.gpus = text.trim().split("\n").filter(l => l).map(line => {
                    const [card, driver, model] = line.split("\t");
                    // "AD107M [GeForce RTX 4060 Max-Q / Mobile]" → "GeForce RTX 4060 Max-Q"
                    const name = (model?.match(/\[(.+)\]/)?.[1] ?? model ?? "").split(" / ")[0].trim();
                    return {
                        card, driver,
                        name: name || "Graphics",
                        integrated: driver === "i915" || driver === "xe",
                        asleep: false, busy: -1, memUsed: -1, memTotal: -1, temp: -1, power: -1, freq: -1, maxFreq: -1,
                        history: []
                    };
                });
            }
        }
    }
    Process {
        id: gpuSample
        command: [Ui.bin + "penumbra-gpus", "sample"]
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: {
                const lines = text.trim().split("\n");
                const now = Number(lines[0]?.split(" ")[1]) * 1000;
                const readings = {};
                for (const line of lines.slice(1)) {
                    const [card, ...pairs] = line.split("\t");
                    readings[card] = {};
                    for (const pair of pairs) {
                        const at = pair.indexOf("=");
                        readings[card][pair.slice(0, at)] = pair.slice(at + 1);
                    }
                }
                const seen = {};
                root.gpus = root.gpus.map(gpu => {
                    const r = readings[gpu.card];
                    if (!r)
                        return gpu;
                    const num = key => r[key] === undefined || r[key] === "" || isNaN(Number(r[key])) ? -1 : Number(r[key]);
                    let busy = num("busy");
                    // Intel counts the time spent idle (RC6); busy is the rest.
                    if (num("idle_ms") >= 0) {
                        const before = root.lastGpu[gpu.card];
                        busy = before && now > before.now ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 * (1 - (num("idle_ms") - before.idle) / (now - before.now)))) : -1;
                        seen[gpu.card] = { idle: num("idle_ms"), now };
                    }
                    const asleep = r.state === "asleep";
                    return Object.assign({}, gpu, {
                        asleep,
                        busy: asleep ? -1 : busy,
                        memUsed: num("mem_used"), memTotal: num("mem_total"),
                        temp: num("temp"), power: num("power"),
                        freq: num("freq"), maxFreq: num("max_freq"),
                        history: busy >= 0 || asleep ? root.push(gpu.history, Math.max(0, busy)) : gpu.history
                    });
                });
                root.lastGpu = seen;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/Wallpaper.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Hyprland
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Wayland

// Animated wallpapers. Each scene sits on the bottom layer over awww's still copy of the same
// image, so fading it in or out changes nothing at rest; night has no animated scene.
// penumbra-wallpaper says when the wallpaper changes; the settings page can keep them still.
Scope {
    id: root

    readonly property var scenes: ["wallpaper-dawn", "wallpaper-eclipse"]
    // The chosen wallpaper; the animated scene that should be up, and the one loaded now (they
    // differ while one fades out).
    property string chosen: ""
    readonly property string target: Settings.animatedWallpapers && scenes.includes(chosen) ? chosen : ""
    property string scene: ""
    // awww's transition is still running.
    property bool waiting: false
    readonly property bool shown: target !== "" && scene === target && !waiting

    property real fade: shown ? 1 : 0
    Behavior on fade { NumberAnimation { duration: root.shown ? 900 : 350; easing.type: Easing.InOutQuad } }
    onFadeChanged: if (fade === 0) scene = target
    onTargetChanged: if (fade === 0) scene = target

    // Wait out awww's transition before covering it.
    function show(name, delay) {
        chosen = name;
        waiting = delay > 0;
        if (waiting) {
            reveal.interval = delay;
            reveal.restart();
        }
    }
    Timer {
        id: reveal
        onTriggered: root.waiting = false
    }

    FileView {
        path: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/.local/state/penumbra/wallpaper"
        onLoaded: root.show(text().trim(), 0)
    }

    Variants {
        model: Quickshell.screens

        delegate: Scope {
            id: output
            required property var modelData

            LazyLoader {
                active: root.fade > 0

                PanelWindow {
                    id: window
                    screen: output.modelData
                    anchors { top: true; bottom: true; left: true; right: true }
                    exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
                    color: "transparent"
                    mask: Region {}
                    WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Bottom
                    // Not penumbra-*: the glass layer rule would blur it.
                    WlrLayershell.namespace: "animated-wallpaper"

                    // Nothing to see under a fullscreen window; stop drawing.
                    readonly property bool running: !(Hyprland.monitorFor(screen)?.activeWorkspace?.hasFullscreen ?? false)

                    // Cover the screen like `awww img --resize crop`.
                    Loader {
                        id: scene
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        scale: item ? Math.max(window.width / item.width, window.height / item.height) : 1
                        opacity: root.fade
                        sourceComponent: root.scene === "wallpaper-eclipse" ? eclipse : dawn

                        Component {
                            id: dawn
                            DawnScene {
                                running: window.running
                                pixelScale: Math.max(window.width / width, window.height / height) * window.devicePixelRatio
                            }
                        }
                        Component {
                            id: eclipse
                            EclipseScene {
                                running: window.running
                                pixelScale: Math.max(window.width / width, window.height / height) * window.devicePixelRatio
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/WallpaperPicker.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell.Widgets

// The three wallpapers side by side; click one to switch with penumbra-wallpaper's transition.
Panel {
    id: root
    anchors { top: true; right: true }
    margins.right: Theme.edge - shadowRoom
    sheetWidth: 620
    open: Ui.wallpapersOpen
    onDismissed: Ui.dismiss()

    readonly property var choices: [
        { name: "wallpaper", title: "Night", animated: false },
        { name: "wallpaper-dawn", title: "Dawn", animated: true },
        { name: "wallpaper-eclipse", title: "Eclipse", animated: true }
    ]

    ColumnLayout {
        x: 18
        y: 18
        width: root.sheetWidth - 36
        spacing: 12

        Label {
            text: "Wallpaper"
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textLarge
            font.weight: Font.DemiBold
        }

        RowLayout {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            spacing: 10
            Repeater {
                model: root.choices
                delegate: ColumnLayout {
                    id: choice
                    required property var modelData
                    readonly property bool current: Ui.wallpaper === modelData.name
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    // Equal thirds, whatever the labels' widths.
                    Layout.preferredWidth: 1
                    spacing: 6

                    ClippingRectangle {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        Layout.preferredHeight: width * 9 / 16
                        radius: Theme.radiusSmall
                        color: Theme.raised
                        border.width: choice.current ? 2 : 1
                        border.color: choice.current ? Theme.violet : area.containsMouse ? Theme.lineStrong : Theme.line
                        contentUnderBorder: true
                        Image {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            source: "file://" + Ui.wallpapers + choice.modelData.name + ".png"
                            sourceSize.width: 360
                            fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop
                            asynchronous: true
                            scale: area.containsMouse ? 1.04 : 1
                            Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: Theme.normal; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                        }
                        Rectangle {
                            visible: choice.modelData.animated
                            anchors { left: parent.left; top: parent.top; margins: 8 }
                            implicitWidth: badge.implicitWidth + 14
                            implicitHeight: 20
                            radius: Theme.corner(10)
                            color: Theme.glassStrong
                            Label {
                                id: badge
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                text: "Animated"
                                font.pixelSize: Theme.textTiny
                                font.weight: Font.DemiBold
                                color: Theme.violetBright
                            }
                        }
                        MouseArea {
                            id: area
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: {
                                if (!choice.current)
                                    Ui.run(Ui.bin + "penumbra-wallpaper " + choice.modelData.name);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                        spacing: 6
                        Rectangle {
                            visible: choice.current
                            implicitWidth: 6
                            implicitHeight: 6
                            radius: Theme.corner(3)
                            color: Theme.violet
                        }
                        Label {
                            text: choice.modelData.title
                            font.weight: choice.current ? Font.DemiBold : Font.Normal
                            color: choice.current ? Theme.text : Theme.muted
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Label {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            text: "Super+W cycles through them · the lock screen follows your choice"
            font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
            color: Theme.dim
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/WallpaperScene.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick

// Base for an animated wallpaper scene, laid out in the SVG's own coordinates. Its layers are
// PNGs and a layers.js written by tools/build-wallpapers.py into `folder`, next to this file.
Item {
    id: root
    required property string folder
    required property var layers
    property bool running: true
    // The lock screen leaves out the wordmark.
    property bool clean: false
    // Device pixels per scene unit, so layers decode no larger than they are shown.
    property real pixelScale: 1

    // Seconds of animation; ticking at 30 Hz keeps the redraws cheap.
    property real t: 0
    Timer {
        interval: 33
        repeat: true
        running: root.running
        onTriggered: root.t += interval / 1000
    }
    function wave(period, phase = 0) {
        return Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * root.t / period + phase);
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/WifiPage.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts
import Quickshell.Networking

// Wi-Fi networks: scan, connect (with a password when needed), disconnect and forget.
ColumnLayout {
    id: root

    property bool active: false
    readonly property var device: Devices.wifiDevice
    readonly property var networks: (device?.networks.values ?? [])
        .filter(n => n.name)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.connected - a.connected) || (b.known - a.known) || (Devices.strength(b) - Devices.strength(a)))
    property string expanded: ""
    property var failures: ({}) // network name → message

    spacing: 12

    // Scan only while the page is on screen.
    function updateScanner() {
        if (device)
            device.scannerEnabled = active && Networking.wifiEnabled;
    }
    onActiveChanged: { updateScanner(); if (!active) expanded = ""; }
    onDeviceChanged: updateScanner()
    Connections {
        target: Networking
        function onWifiEnabledChanged() { root.updateScanner(); }
    }

    function fail(name, message) {
        failures = Object.assign({}, failures, { [name]: message });
    }
    function clearFailure(name) {
        const next = Object.assign({}, failures);
        delete next[name];
        failures = next;
    }

    PageHeader {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        title: "Wi-Fi"
        status: !root.device ? "No Wi-Fi adapter" : !Networking.wifiEnabled ? "Off" : Devices.wifiNetwork ? `Connected to ${Devices.wifiNetwork.name}` : "Not connected"
        showSwitch: !!root.device
        checked: Networking.wifiEnabled
        onBack: Ui.controlPage = "main"
        onToggled: Networking.wifiEnabled = !Networking.wifiEnabled
    }

    // Wired connection, while a cable is plugged in.
    ListRow {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        visible: Devices.wired
        glyph: "󰈀"
        title: "Ethernet"
        subtitle: "Connected"
        highlighted: true
    }

    Overline {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 2
        visible: !!root.device && Networking.wifiEnabled
        text: root.networks.length ? "Networks" : "Searching for networks…"
    }

    ListView {
        id: list
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.preferredHeight: Math.min(contentHeight, 380)
        visible: !!root.device && Networking.wifiEnabled
        clip: true
        spacing: 4
        boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
        model: root.networks
        ScrollBar.vertical: ScrollBar { policy: list.contentHeight > list.height ? ScrollBar.AsNeeded : ScrollBar.AlwaysOff }

        delegate: ListRow {
            id: row
            required property var modelData
            readonly property var net: modelData
            readonly property bool secured: net.security !== WifiSecurityType.Open
            readonly property bool connecting: net.stateChanging || net.state === ConnectionState.Connecting
            readonly property string failure: root.failures[net.name] ?? ""

            width: ListView.view.width
            glyph: Devices.signalGlyph(net)
            title: net.name
            subtitle: failure !== "" ? failure : net.connected ? "Connected" : connecting ? "Connecting…" : net.known ? "Saved" : secured ? "Secured" : "Open"
            danger: failure !== ""
            trailing: secured ? "󰌾" : ""
            highlighted: net.connected
            busy: connecting
            expanded: root.expanded === net.name

            function connect() {
                root.clearFailure(net.name);
                if (net.known || !secured)
                    net.connect();
                else if (password.text !== "")
                    net.connectWithPsk(password.text);
            }

            onClicked: {
                const open = root.expanded === net.name;
                root.expanded = open ? "" : net.name;
                // Saved and open networks connect straight away; the row stays open for Forget.
                if (!open && !net.connected && (net.known || !secured))
                    connect();
                if (!open && !net.known && secured)
                    password.forceActiveFocus();
            }

            Connections {
                target: row.net
                function onConnectionFailed(reason) {
                    const wrongKey = reason === ConnectionFailReason.NoSecrets || reason === ConnectionFailReason.WifiAuthTimeout || reason === ConnectionFailReason.WifiClientFailed;
                    root.fail(row.net.name, wrongKey ? "Wrong password, or the network refused the connection" : "Couldn't connect");
                    root.expanded = row.net.name;
                }
                function onConnectedChanged() {
                    if (row.net.connected) {
                        root.clearFailure(row.net.name);
                        password.text = "";
                    }
                }
            }

            // Password entry for new secured networks.
            RowLayout {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: !row.net.known && row.secured
                spacing: 8
                TextField {
                    id: password
                    Layout.fillWidth: true
                    placeholderText: "Password"
                    echoMode: reveal.checked ? TextInput.Normal : TextInput.Password
                    color: Theme.text
                    placeholderTextColor: Theme.dim
                    selectionColor: Theme.violet
                    selectedTextColor: Theme.violetInk
                    font.family: Theme.sans
                    font.pixelSize: Theme.textBody
                    leftPadding: 12
                    rightPadding: 34
                    background: Rectangle {
                        implicitHeight: 32
                        radius: Theme.corner(10)
                        color: Theme.glassStrong
                        border.color: password.activeFocus ? Theme.violet : Theme.lineStrong
                    }
                    onAccepted: row.connect()
                    Glyph {
                        id: reveal
                        property bool checked: false
                        anchors { right: parent.right; rightMargin: 10; verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter }
                        text: checked ? "󰈈" : "󰈉"
                        size: 14
                        color: Theme.muted
                        MouseArea {
                            anchors.fill: parent
                            anchors.margins: -4
                            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
                            onClicked: reveal.checked = !reveal.checked
                        }
                    }
                }
                PillButton {
                    text: "Connect"
                    primary: true
                    available: password.text !== "" && !row.connecting
                    onClicked: row.connect()
                }
            }

            Flow {
                Layout.fillWidth: true
                visible: row.net.known || row.net.connected || !row.secured
                spacing: 6
                PillButton {
                    visible: !row.net.connected
                    text: row.connecting ? "Connecting…" : "Connect"
                    primary: true
                    available: !row.connecting
                    onClicked: row.connect()
                }
                PillButton {
                    visible: row.net.connected
                    text: "Disconnect"
                    onClicked: row.net.disconnect()
                }
                PillButton {
                    visible: row.net.known
                    text: "Forget"
                    danger: true
                    onClicked: { row.net.forget(); root.expanded = ""; }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    ColumnLayout {
        Layout.fillWidth: true
        Layout.topMargin: 6
        Layout.bottomMargin: 6
        visible: !root.device || !Networking.wifiEnabled
        spacing: 4
        Glyph { Layout.fillWidth: true; text: "󰤭"; size: 28; color: Theme.dim }
        Label {
            Layout.fillWidth: true
            horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
            text: !root.device ? "This computer has no Wi-Fi adapter" : "Wi-Fi is off"
            color: Theme.muted
        }
    }

    Label {
        Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignRight
        text: "Advanced network settings"
        font.pixelSize: Theme.textSmall
        color: advancedArea.containsMouse ? Theme.violetBright : Theme.muted
        MouseArea {
            id: advancedArea
            anchors.fill: parent
            anchors.margins: -4
            hoverEnabled: true
            cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
            onClicked: { Ui.close(); Ui.run("nm-connection-editor"); }
        }
    }
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/eclipse/aurora.frag`

```glsl
#version 440
// Aurora over the near world's horizon: curtains that fold and drift along the arc, fine rays,
// violet at the base turning rose higher up, and a faint glow on the night side below.
// Compiled to aurora.frag.qsb by tools/build-wallpapers.py.
layout(location = 0) in vec2 qt_TexCoord0;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
layout(std140, binding = 0) uniform buf {
    mat4 qt_Matrix;
    float qt_Opacity;
    float time;
    vec2 size;      // item size, scene units
    vec2 center;    // world center, relative to the item
    float radius;   // world radius
    float arcFrom;  // where the aurora starts and ends, radians from the top of the world
    float arcTo;
};

void main() {
    vec2 p = qt_TexCoord0 * size;
    vec2 d = p - center;
    float h = length(d) - radius;
    float a = atan(d.x, -d.y);
    float u = (a - arcFrom) / (arcTo - arcFrom);
    float s = a * radius;

    float envelope = smoothstep(0.0, 0.22, u) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.7, u);
    float fold = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(s * 0.011 - time * 0.45 + 1.8 * sin(s * 0.0037 + time * 0.12));
    float ripple = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(s * 0.029 + time * 0.9 + 2.0 * sin(s * 0.006 - time * 0.3));
    float light = fold * (0.35 + 0.65 * ripple);
    float rays = 0.74 + 0.16 * sin(s * 0.21 + 4.0 * sin(s * 0.012 - time * 0.55)) + 0.1 * sin(s * 0.083 - time * 0.3);

    float top = 150.0 + 130.0 * (0.5 + 0.5 * sin(s * 0.005 + time * 0.2));
    // Curtains thin out well before the top of the item, so its edge never shows.
    float curtain = smoothstep(0.0, 22.0, h) * exp(-max(h, 0.0) / top) * (1.0 - smoothstep(top, top * 2.0, h)) * rays;
    float below = exp(min(h, 0.0) / 40.0) * (1.0 - step(0.0, h)) * 0.35;

    float alpha = 0.55 * envelope * light * (curtain + below) * smoothstep(0.0, 160.0, p.y);
    vec3 color = mix(vec3(0.77, 0.65, 1.0), vec3(0.89, 0.54, 0.75), smoothstep(30.0, 260.0, h));
    fragColor = vec4(color * alpha, alpha) * qt_Opacity;
}
```

## `config/quickshell/penumbra/shell.qml`

```qml
//@ pragma UseQApplication
//@ pragma IconTheme Penumbra
// PENUMBRA shell: animated wallpapers, bar, control center, notifications, OSD, calendar, session
// overlay, launcher, lock screen, polkit agent and screenshot/recording overlay.
// Run with `quickshell -c penumbra`; scripts talk to it with `quickshell -c penumbra ipc call …`.
import QtQuick
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io

ShellRoot {
    Wallpaper {
        id: wallpaper
    }

    Variants {
        model: Quickshell.screens
        delegate: Bar {}
    }

    ControlCenter {}
    CalendarPanel {}
    MonitorPanel {}
    UpdatesPanel {}
    WallpaperPicker {}
    NotificationPopups {}
    Osd {}
    SessionMenu {}
    Launcher {}
    HotCorners {}
    PolkitDialog {}
    ScreenshotOverlay {
        id: shot
    }
    LockScreen {
        id: lockScreen
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "osd"
        function flash(kind: string, value: int, muted: bool): void {
            Ui.showOsd(kind, value, muted);
        }
        // What the volume, microphone and brightness keys do: penumbra-osd calls this first.
        // `action` is up, down, mute or current (just show the popup).
        function adjust(control: string, action: string): bool {
            return control === "brightness" ? Sys.stepBrightness(action) : Devices.adjust(control, action);
        }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "panel"
        function control(): void { Ui.toggleControl(); }
        function wifi(): void { Ui.openControl("wifi"); }
        function bluetooth(): void { Ui.openControl("bluetooth"); }
        function mixer(): void { Ui.openControl("mixer"); }
        function settings(): void { Ui.openControl("settings"); }
        function calendar(): void { Ui.toggleCalendar(); }
        function monitor(): void { Ui.toggleMonitor(); }
        function session(): void { Ui.openSession(); }
        function updates(): void { Ui.toggleUpdates(); }
        function wallpapers(): void { Ui.toggleWallpapers(); }
        function close(): void { Ui.closeOverlays(); }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "launcher"
        function apps(): void { Ui.toggleLauncher("apps"); }
        function clipboard(): void { Ui.toggleLauncher("clipboard"); }
        function emoji(): void { Ui.toggleLauncher("emoji"); }
        function symbols(): void { Ui.toggleLauncher("symbols"); }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "shot"
        function open(annotate: bool): void { shot.open(annotate, false); }
        // The same overlay, set to record a video.
        function record(): void { shot.open(false, true); }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "recorder"
        // penumbra-record calls this when a recording starts or ends.
        function refresh(): void { Recorder.refresh(); }
        function stop(): void { Recorder.stop(); }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "lock"
        function lock(): void { lockScreen.lock(); }
        function isLocked(): bool { return lockScreen.locked; }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "notifications"
        function clear(): void { Notifs.clear(); }
        function dnd(): void { Notifs.dnd = !Notifs.dnd; }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "wallpaper"
        // `delay` is how long awww's transition takes, in milliseconds.
        function set(name: string, delay: int): void { wallpaper.show(name, delay); }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "updates"
        function refresh(): void { Sys.refreshUpdates(); }
    }
}
```

## `config/rofi/penumbra.rasi`

```css
/* Colours come from colors.rasi, generated from palette/palette.toml. */
@import "colors"
* { background-color: transparent; text-color: @fg; border-color: @line; }
window { width: 640px; padding: 26px; border: 1px; border-radius: 22px; background-color: @bg; }
mainbox { spacing: 22px; children: [ inputbar, listview, mode-switcher ]; }
inputbar { padding: 16px; border-radius: 12px; background-color: @field; spacing: 14px; children: [ prompt, entry ]; }
prompt { text-color: @violet; font: "Inter Bold 11"; vertical-align: 0.5; }
entry { placeholder: "Search your applications…"; placeholder-color: @muted; }
listview { lines: 6; columns: 1; spacing: 5px; fixed-height: true; }
element { padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; spacing: 14px; }
element selected.normal { background-color: @selection; text-color: @violet; }
element-icon { size: 28px; }
element-text { vertical-align: 0.5; text-color: inherit; }
mode-switcher { spacing: 10px; }
button { padding: 8px; border-radius: 8px; text-color: @muted; font: "Inter 10"; }
button selected { text-color: @violet; background-color: @field; }
message { padding: 10px; }
/* Corner style: penumbra-corners rewrites this to square everything off. */
@import "corners"
```

## `config/satty/config.toml`

```toml
# PENUMBRA — screenshot annotation (Shift+Print). Generated by tools/build-palette.py from
# palette/templates/config/satty/config.toml and palette/palette.toml; edit those.
[general]
initial-tool = "arrow"
copy-command = "wl-copy"
annotation-size-factor = 1.5
corner-roundness = 12
early-exit = ["copy", "save"]
actions-on-enter = ["save-to-clipboard", "save-to-file"]
actions-on-escape = ["exit"]
primary-highlighter = "block"
default-round-caps = true
no-window-decoration = true
notification-thumbnail = "screenshot"

[font]
family = "Inter"
style = "Bold"

[color-palette]
palette = [
    "#b69af7ff",
    "#ef8fa4ff",
    "#a5ccb3ff",
    "#e5c89aff",
    "#9ccbd2ff",
    "#faf5ffff",
]
```

## `config/starship.toml`

```toml
# PENUMBRA prompt. Generated by tools/build-palette.py from palette/templates/config/starship.toml
# and palette/palette.toml; edit those.
add_newline = true
format = "[󰣇 ](bold #b69af7)$directory$git_branch$git_status$cmd_duration$character"
right_format = "$time"
[directory]
style = "bold #b69af7"
truncation_length = 3
truncation_symbol = "…/"
[character]
success_symbol = "[❯](bold #b69af7)"
error_symbol = "[❯](bold #ef8fa4)"
[git_branch]
symbol = " "
style = "#9ccbd2"
[git_status]
style = "#e5c89a"
[cmd_duration]
min_time = 2000
format = "[ · $duration](#8f809f)"
[time]
disabled = false
time_format = "%H:%M"
format = "[󰥔 $time](#71637f)"
```

## `config/systemd/user/penumbra-shell.service`

```ini
[Unit]
Description=Penumbra shell: bar, notifications, launcher, lock screen and polkit agent
PartOf=graphical-session.target
After=graphical-session.target
# A shell that keeps crashing stops being restarted.
StartLimitIntervalSec=60
StartLimitBurst=5

[Service]
Type=exec
# Only while the compositor is up: after logging out its socket is gone, and the unit is skipped
# rather than restarted in a loop.
ExecCondition=/bin/sh -c 'test -S "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$WAYLAND_DISPLAY"'
ExecStart=/usr/bin/quickshell -c penumbra
# If the shell dies (even while the screen is locked) it comes back, and locks again if it was.
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=1
# Apps started from the shell run in their own scopes (Ui.spawn), so they survive a restart.
```

## `system/usr/share/sddm/themes/penumbra/Main.qml`

```qml
import QtQuick 2.15
import QtQuick.Controls 2.15
import QtQuick.Layouts 1.15
import "palette.js" as P

// The sign-in screen. It is laid out like the shell's lock screen (the clock high in the centre,
// you and the password field below), so signing in and unlocking look like one place.
Rectangle {
 id: root
 width: 1280; height: 800; color: P.shade
 // Every size is designed for a 1280×800 screen and grows with larger ones.
 readonly property real u: Math.max(1, Math.min(width / 1280, height / 800))
 property date now: new Date()
 property bool busy: false
 property string error: ""
 function login() { if (!busy && username.text.length) { busy = true; error = ""; sddm.login(username.text, password.text, session.currentIndex); } }
 // #rrggbb with an alpha, as #aarrggbb.
 function alpha(color, a) { return "#" + ("0" + Math.round(a * 255).toString(16)).slice(-2) + color.slice(1); }

 Image { anchors.fill: parent; source: "wallpaper.png"; fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectCrop }
 // Darker toward the bottom, where the password field sits, as on the lock screen.
 Rectangle {
  anchors.fill: parent
  gradient: Gradient {
   GradientStop { position: 0; color: root.alpha(P.base, 0.25) }
   GradientStop { position: 0.55; color: root.alpha(P.base, 0.35) }
   GradientStop { position: 1; color: root.alpha(P.base, 0.78) }
  }
 }
 Timer { interval: 1000; repeat: true; running: true; onTriggered: root.now = new Date() }

 Item {
  id: content
  anchors.fill: parent
  opacity: 0
  Component.onCompleted: entrance.start()
  NumberAnimation { id: entrance; target: content; property: "opacity"; from: 0; to: 1; duration: 840; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic }

  // ── Clock ──
  ColumnLayout {
   anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
   y: Math.round(parent.height * 0.2)
   spacing: 0
   Text {
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    text: Qt.formatTime(root.now, "HH:mm")
    color: P.text; font.family: "Inter Display"; font.weight: Font.Light; font.pixelSize: 112 * root.u; font.letterSpacing: -1
    font.features: { "tnum": 1 }
   }
   Text {
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    text: Qt.formatDate(root.now, "dddd d MMMM")
    color: P.violet; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 14 * root.u; font.letterSpacing: 3 * root.u; font.capitalization: Font.AllUppercase
   }
  }

  // ── You and your password ──
  ColumnLayout {
   anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
   y: Math.round(parent.height * 0.56)
   spacing: 14 * root.u

   // The initial of whoever signs in, on the violet disc the lock screen shows.
   Rectangle {
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    width: 76 * root.u; height: width; radius: width / 2
    border.width: 2; border.color: P.violet
    gradient: Gradient { GradientStop { position: 0; color: P.violet } GradientStop { position: 1; color: P.violetDeep } }
    Text {
     anchors.centerIn: parent
     text: username.text.charAt(0).toUpperCase()
     color: P.violetInk; font.family: "Inter Display"; font.bold: true; font.pixelSize: 30 * root.u
    }
   }
   TextField {
    id: username
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    implicitWidth: 240 * root.u; implicitHeight: 34 * root.u
    text: userModel.lastUser; placeholderText: "Username"; horizontalAlignment: TextInput.AlignHCenter
    color: P.text; placeholderTextColor: P.muted; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 15 * root.u; font.weight: Font.DemiBold
    background: Rectangle {
     radius: height / 2
     color: username.activeFocus || username.hovered ? root.alpha(P.surface, 0.7) : "transparent"
     border.color: username.activeFocus ? P.violet : "transparent"
    }
    onAccepted: password.forceActiveFocus()
   }
   TextField {
    id: password
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    Layout.topMargin: 4 * root.u
    implicitWidth: 320 * root.u; implicitHeight: 52 * root.u
    placeholderText: "Password"; echoMode: TextInput.Password; passwordCharacter: "●"; horizontalAlignment: TextInput.AlignHCenter
    color: P.text; placeholderTextColor: P.muted; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 15 * root.u
    leftPadding: rightPadding
    rightPadding: (layoutButton.visible ? layoutButton.width + 10 * root.u : 0) + 54 * root.u
    enabled: !root.busy
    background: Rectangle {
     radius: height / 2
     color: root.alpha(P.surface, 0.88)
     border.width: password.activeFocus ? 2 : 1
     border.color: root.error !== "" ? P.rose : password.activeFocus ? P.violet : root.alpha(P.violet, 0.34)
    }
    onAccepted: root.login()
    // With several keyboard layouts, the one the password is typed in; click to switch.
    Rectangle {
     id: layoutButton
     visible: keyboard.layouts.length > 1
     anchors.right: submit.left; anchors.rightMargin: 8 * root.u; anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
     width: layoutText.implicitWidth + 16 * root.u; height: 26 * root.u; radius: height / 2
     color: layoutArea.containsMouse ? P.hover : "transparent"; border.color: P.border
     Text {
      id: layoutText; anchors.centerIn: parent
      text: (keyboard.layouts[keyboard.currentLayout] ? keyboard.layouts[keyboard.currentLayout].shortName : "").toUpperCase()
      color: P.textSoft; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 11 * root.u; font.bold: true
     }
     MouseArea {
      id: layoutArea; anchors.fill: parent; hoverEnabled: true; cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor
      onClicked: { keyboard.currentLayout = (keyboard.currentLayout + 1) % keyboard.layouts.length; password.forceActiveFocus(); }
     }
    }
    // Sign in.
    Rectangle {
     id: submit
     anchors.right: parent.right; anchors.rightMargin: 6 * root.u; anchors.verticalCenter: parent.verticalCenter
     width: 40 * root.u; height: width; radius: width / 2
     color: submitArea.containsMouse ? P.violetBright : P.violet
     opacity: root.busy ? 0.5 : 1
     Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: 160 } }
     Text { anchors.centerIn: parent; text: "→"; color: P.violetInk; font.family: "Inter"; font.bold: true; font.pixelSize: 18 * root.u }
     MouseArea { id: submitArea; anchors.fill: parent; hoverEnabled: true; cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor; onClicked: root.login() }
    }
   }
   // Signing in, a sign-in error, else a Caps Lock warning.
   Text {
    Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
    Layout.minimumHeight: 16 * root.u
    text: root.busy ? "Signing in…" : root.error !== "" ? root.error : keyboard.capsLock ? "Caps Lock is on" : ""
    color: root.busy ? P.textSoft : root.error !== "" ? P.rose : P.amber
    font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 12 * root.u
   }
  }

  // ── Footer: the wordmark, the session, and power ──
  Text {
   anchors.left: parent.left; anchors.bottom: parent.bottom; anchors.margins: 32 * root.u
   text: "PENUMBRA"
   color: root.alpha(P.violet, 0.8); font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 11 * root.u; font.weight: Font.DemiBold; font.letterSpacing: 5 * root.u
  }
  RowLayout {
   anchors.right: parent.right; anchors.bottom: parent.bottom; anchors.margins: 24 * root.u
   spacing: 6 * root.u
   ComboBox {
    id: session; implicitHeight: 34 * root.u; implicitWidth: 220 * root.u
    model: sessionModel; textRole: "name"; currentIndex: sessionModel.lastIndex
    palette.button: P.raised; palette.buttonText: P.textSoft; palette.text: P.text; palette.base: P.raised; palette.highlight: P.selection; palette.highlightedText: P.selectionText; palette.window: P.surfaceAlt
    font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 12 * root.u
    background: Rectangle { color: session.hovered ? root.alpha(P.hover, 0.8) : root.alpha(P.surface, 0.6); radius: height / 2; border.color: root.alpha(P.violet, 0.16) }
   }
   Repeater {
    model: [
     { label: "Suspend", show: sddm.canSuspend, run: function() { sddm.suspend() } },
     { label: "Restart", show: true, run: function() { powerDialog.action = "restart"; powerDialog.open() } },
     { label: "Shut down", show: true, run: function() { powerDialog.action = "shutdown"; powerDialog.open() } }
    ]
    delegate: Rectangle {
     visible: modelData.show
     implicitWidth: powerText.implicitWidth + 28 * root.u; implicitHeight: 34 * root.u; radius: height / 2
     color: powerArea.containsMouse ? root.alpha(P.hover, 0.8) : "transparent"
     Text { id: powerText; anchors.centerIn: parent; text: modelData.label; color: powerArea.containsMouse ? P.text : P.muted; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 12 * root.u }
     MouseArea { id: powerArea; anchors.fill: parent; hoverEnabled: true; cursorShape: Qt.PointingHandCursor; onClicked: modelData.run() }
    }
   }
  }
 }

 MouseArea { anchors.fill: parent; z: -1; onClicked: password.forceActiveFocus() }

 Dialog {
  id: powerDialog; property string action: ""; anchors.centerIn: parent; title: action === "restart" ? "Restart computer?" : "Shut down computer?"; modal: true
  standardButtons: Dialog.Ok | Dialog.Cancel; font.family: "Inter"; font.pixelSize: 13 * root.u
  palette.window: P.surfaceAlt; palette.windowText: P.text; palette.button: P.hover; palette.buttonText: P.violet
  onAccepted: { if (action === "restart") sddm.reboot(); else sddm.powerOff(); }
 }
 Connections {
  target: sddm
  function onLoginFailed() { root.busy = false; password.text = ""; root.error = "Sign-in failed. Please try again."; password.forceActiveFocus(); }
  function onLoginSucceeded() { root.error = ""; }
 }
 Component.onCompleted: { if (username.text.length) password.forceActiveFocus(); else username.forceActiveFocus(); }
}
```

## `boot/grub/theme.txt`

```text
# Penumbra — original orbital artwork, using GRUB's bundled Unicode font.
desktop-image: "background.png"
desktop-image-scale-method: "crop"
desktop-color: "#0d0a12"
title-text: ""
message-color: "#e4dced"
message-bg-color: "#100d18"
terminal-font: "Unifont Regular 16"
+ label {
    left = 8%
    top = 18%
    width = 60%
    height = 32
    text = "P E N U M B R A"
    font = "Unifont Regular 16"
    color = "#b69af7"
}
+ boot_menu {
    left = 8%
    top = 28%
    width = 58%
    height = 45%
    item_font = "Unifont Regular 16"
    item_color = "#b6a8c7"
    selected_item_color = "#f3eaff"
    item_height = 36
    item_padding = 10
    item_spacing = 8
    icon_width = 0
    icon_height = 0
    selected_item_pixmap_style = "select_*.png"
}
+ label {
    left = 8%
    top = 78%
    width = 84%
    height = 24
    text = "Arrow keys: select   Enter: boot   e: edit   c: console"
    color = "#8f809f"
    font = "Unifont Regular 16"
}
+ label {
    id = "__timeout__"
    left = 8%
    top = 83%
    width = 84%
    height = 24
    text = "Booting selected entry in %d seconds"
    color = "#b69af7"
    font = "Unifont Regular 16"
}
```

## `boot/plymouth/penumbra.plymouth`

```ini
[Plymouth Theme]
Name=Penumbra
Description=A quieter orbit. The wallpaper's ringed planet with a moonlet, native password dialogs and update progress.
ModuleName=two-step

[two-step]
# Arch's mkinitcpio hook copies the file behind Font into the initramfs; the other labels reuse it.
Font=Inter Variable 12
TitleFont=Inter Variable 20
MonospaceFont=monospace 11
ImageDir=/usr/share/plymouth/themes/penumbra
# The planet and the password card share one spot; update titles sit above it.
HorizontalAlignment=.5
VerticalAlignment=.45
DialogHorizontalAlignment=.5
DialogVerticalAlignment=.45
TitleHorizontalAlignment=.5
TitleVerticalAlignment=.2
WatermarkHorizontalAlignment=.5
WatermarkVerticalAlignment=.9
MessageBelowAnimation=true
Transition=none
TransitionDuration=0.0
# background.png is the sky glow; it is stretched to the screen over this edge colour.
ScaleBackgroundImage=true
BackgroundStartColor=0x07070b
BackgroundEndColor=0x07070b
ProgressBarBackgroundColor=0x231a31
ProgressBarForegroundColor=0xb69af7
ProgressBarWidth=320
ProgressBarHeight=4
ProgressBarHorizontalAlignment=.5
ProgressBarVerticalAlignment=.64
ConsoleLogBackgroundColor=0x0d0a12
ConsoleLogTextColor=0xe4dced

[boot-up]
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false

[shutdown]
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false

[reboot]
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false

[updates]
SuppressMessages=true
UseProgressBar=true
ProgressBarShowPercentComplete=true
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false
Title=Installing updates
SubTitle=Do not turn off your computer

[system-upgrade]
SuppressMessages=true
UseProgressBar=true
ProgressBarShowPercentComplete=true
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false
Title=Upgrading the system
SubTitle=Do not turn off your computer

[firmware-upgrade]
SuppressMessages=true
UseProgressBar=true
ProgressBarShowPercentComplete=true
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false
Title=Upgrading firmware
SubTitle=Do not turn off your computer

[system-reset]
SuppressMessages=true
UseProgressBar=true
ProgressBarShowPercentComplete=true
UseEndAnimation=false
UseFirmwareBackground=false
Title=Resetting the system
SubTitle=Do not turn off your computer
```
